1:1	bismi alahi arahmani arahim
1:2	alhamdu lilahi rabi alalamin
1:3	arahmani arahim
1:4	maliki yawmi adin
1:5	iyaka nabudu wa iyaka nastain
1:6	ihdina asirata almustaqim
1:7	sirata alathina anamta alayhim ghayri almaghdubi alayhim wala adalin
2:1	alif lam mim
2:2	thalika alkitabu larayba fihi hudan lilmutaqin
2:3	alathina yu minuna bilghaybiwayuqimuna asalata wamima razaqnahumyunfiqun
2:4	walathina yu minuna bimaunzila ilayka wama unzila min qablika wabil akhiratihum yuqinun
2:5	ula ika ala hudan minrabihim waula ika humu almuflihun
2:6	ina alathina kafaru sawaunalayhim anthartahum am lam tunthirhum layu minun
2:7	khatama alahu ala qulubihimwala samaihim wala absarihim ghishawatunwalahum athabun athim
2:8	wamina anasi man yaqulu amanabialahi wabilyawmi al akhiri wamahum bimu minin
2:9	yukhadiauna alaha walathinamanu wama yakhdauna ila anfusahum wamayashaurun
2:10	fi qulubihim maradun fazadahumualahu maradan walahum athabun alimun bimakanu yakthibun
2:11	wa itha qila lahum latufsidu fi al ardi qalu inama nahnumuslihun
2:12	ala inahum humu almufsiduna walakinla yashaurun
2:13	wa itha qila lahum aminu kamamana anasu qalu anu minu kamamana asufahau ala inahum humu asufahauwalakin la yalamun
2:14	wa itha laqu alathina amanuqalu amana wa itha khalaw ilashayatinihim qalu ina makum inamanahnu mustahzi un
2:15	alahu yastahzi u bihim wayamuduhumfi tughyanihim yamahun
2:16	ula ika alathina ishtarawuadalalata bilhuda famarabihat tijaratuhum wama kanumuhtadin
2:17	mathaluhum kamathali alathistawqada naran falama adat ma hawlahuthahaba alahu binurihim watarakahum fi thulumatinla yubsirun
2:18	sumun bukmun aumyun fahum layarjiaun
2:19	aw kasayibin mina asama ifihi thulumatun waradun wabarqunyajaluna asabiahum fi athanihim mina asawaiqihathara almawti walahu muhitunbilkafirin
2:20	yakadu albarqu yakhtafu absarahumkulama ada lahum mashaw fihi wa itha athlamalayhim qamu walaw sha alahu lathahababisamaihim wabsarihim ina alaha alakuli shay in qadir
2:21	ya ayuha anasuabudu rabakumu alathi khalaqakum walathinamin qablikum lalakum tataqun
2:22	alathi jala lakumu al ardafirashan wasama binan wanzalamina asama i man fakhraja bihi mina aththamaratirizqan lakum fala tajalu lilahi andadanwantum talamun
2:23	wa in kuntum fi raybin mima nazalnala abdina fa tu bisuratin min mithlihi wadaushuhadakum min duni alahi in kuntum sadiqin
2:24	fa in lam tafalu walan tafalu fataquanara alati waquduha anasuwalhijaratu uaidat lilkafirin
2:25	wabashshiri alathina amanuwamilu asalihati ana lahum janatintajri min tahtiha al anharu kulamaruziqu minha min thamaratin rizqan qalu hathalathi ruziqna min qablu wautu bihi mutashabihanwalahum fiha azwajun mutaharatun wahumfiha khalidun
2:26	ina alaha la yastahyian yadriba mathalan ma baudatan famafawqaha fama alathina amanufayalamuna anahu alhaqu min rabihim wamalathina kafaru fayaquluna matha aradalahu bihatha mathalan yudilu bihikathiran wayahdi bihi kathiran wama yudilu bihila alfasiqin
2:27	alathina yanquduna ahdalahi min badi mithaqihi wayaqtauna mamara alahu bihi an yusala wayufsiduna fi al ardiula ika humu alkhasirun
2:28	kayfa takfuruna bilahiwakuntum amwatan fahyakum thuma yumitukumthuma yuhyikum thuma ilayhi turjaun
2:29	huwa alathi khalaqa lakum mafi al ardi jamian thuma istawa ila asama ifasawahuna saba samawatin wahuwa bikulishay in alim
2:30	wa ith qala rabuka lilmala ikatini jailun fi al ardi khalifatan qaluatajalu fiha man yufsidu fiha wayasfiku adimawanahnu nusabihu bihamdika wanuqadisu lakaqala ini alamu ma la talamun
2:31	walama adama al asmakulaha thuma aradahum ala almala ikatifaqala anbi uni bi asma i haula in kuntum sadiqin
2:32	qalu subhanaka lailma lana ila ma alamtanainaka anta alalimu alhakim
2:33	qala ya adamu anbi humbi asma ihim falama anbahum bi asma ihim qalalam aqul lakum ini alamu ghayba asamawatiwal ardi walamu ma tubduna wamakuntum taktumun
2:34	wa ith qulna lilmala ikatiusjudu li adama fasajadu ila iblisa abawastakbara wakana mina alkafirin
2:35	waqulna ya adamu uskunanta wazawjuka aljanata wakula minha raghadan haythushi tuma wala taqraba hathihi ashshajaratafatakuna mina aththalimin
2:36	fazalahuma ashshaytanuanha fakhrajahuma mima kanafihi waqulna ihbitu badukum libadinaduwun walakum fi al ardi mustaqarun wamataunila hin
2:37	fatalaqa adamu min rabihikalimatin fataba alayhi inahu huwa atawabuarahim
2:38	qulna ihbitu minhajamian fa ima ya tiyanakum mini hudan faman tabiahudaya fala khawfun alayhim wala hum yahzanun
2:39	walathina kafaru wakaththabubi ayatina ula ika as habuanari hum fiha khalidun
2:40	ya bani isra ila uthkuruniamatiya alati anamtu alaykum wawfu biahdi ufibiahdikum wa iyaya farhabun
2:41	waminu bima anzaltu musadiqanlima makum wala takunu awala kafirinbihi wala tashtaru bi ayati thamananqalilan wa iyaya fataqun
2:42	wala talbisu alhaqa bilbatiliwataktumu alhaqa wantum talamun
2:43	waqimu asalata watuazakata warkau ma arakiain
2:44	ata muruna anasa bilbiriwatansawna anfusakum wantum tatluna alkitaba afalataqilun
2:45	wastainu bisabriwasalati wa inaha lakabiratunila ala alkhashiain
2:46	alathina yathununanahum mulaqu rabihim wanahum ilayhi rajiaun
2:47	ya bani isra ila uthkuruniamatiya alati anamtu alaykum wani fadaltukumala alalamin
2:48	wataqu yawman la tajzinafsun an nafsin shay an wala yuqbalu minha shafatunwala yu khathu minha adlun wala humyunsarun
2:49	wa ith najaynakum min alifirawna yasumunakum su a alathabi yuthabihunabnakum wayastahyuna nisakum wafi thalikumbalaun min rabikum athim
2:50	wa ith faraqna bikumu albahrafanjaynakum waghraqna ala firawnawantum tanthurun
2:51	wa ith wadna musarbaina laylatan thuma itakhathtumu alaijla minbadihi wantum thalimun
2:52	thuma afawna ankum min badi thalikalalakum tashkurun
2:53	wa ith atayna musalkitaba walfurqana lalakum tahtadun
2:54	wa ith qala musaliqawmihi ya qawmi inakum thalamtumanfusakum bitikhathikumu alaijla fatubu ilabari ikum faqtulu anfusakum thalikumkhayrun lakum ainda bari ikum fataba alaykuminahu huwa atawabu arahim
2:55	wa ith qultum ya musalan nu mina laka hata nara alahajahratan fakhathatkumu asaiqatu wantumtanthurun
2:56	thuma bathnakum min badimawtikum lalakum tashkurun
2:57	wathalalna alaykumualghamama wanzalna alaykumu almana wasalwakulu min tayibati ma razaqnakum wamathalamuna walakin kanuanfusahum yathlimun
2:58	wa ith qulna udkhulu hathihialqaryata fakulu minha haythu shi tum raghadan wadkhulualbaba sujadan waqulu hitatun naghfirlakum khatayakum wasanazidu almuhsinin
2:59	fabadala alathina thalamuqawlan ghayra alathi qila lahum fanzalna alalathina thalamu rijzan mina asama ibima kanu yafsuqun
2:60	wa ithi istasqa musaliqawmihi faqulna idrib biasaka alhajarafanfajarat minhu ithnata ashrata aynan qadalima kulu unasin mashrabahum kulu washrabumin rizqi alahi wala tathaw fi al ardimufsidin
2:61	wa ith qultum ya musalan nasbira ala tamin wahidinfadau lana rabaka yukhrij lana mimatunbitu al ardu min baqliha waqiththa ihawafumiha wadasiha wabasaliha qalatastabdiluna alathi huwa adna bilathihuwa khayrun ihbitu misran fa ina lakum masaltum waduribat alayhimu aththilatu walmaskanatuwabau bighadabin mina alahi thalikabi anahum kanu yakfuruna bi ayati alahiwayaqtuluna anabiyina bighayri alhaqi thalikabima asaw wakanu yatadun
2:62	ina alathina amanu walathinahadu wanasara wasabi inaman amana bialahi walyawmi al akhiriwamila salihan falahum ajruhum ainda rabihimwala khawfun alayhim wala hum yahzanun
2:63	wa ith akhathna mithaqakumwarafana fawqakumu atura khuthuma ataynakum biquwatin wathkuruma fihi lalakum tataqun
2:64	thuma tawalaytum min badi thalikafalawla fadlu alahi alaykum warahmatuhulakuntum mina alkhasirin
2:65	walaqad alimtumu alathinaiatadaw minkum fi asabti faqulna lahum kunuqiradatan khasi in
2:66	fajalnaha nakalanlima bayna yadayha wama khalfahawamawaithatan lilmutaqin
2:67	wa ith qala musaliqawmihi ina alaha ya murukum an tathbahubaqaratan qalu atatakhithuna huzuwan qalauthu bialahi an akuna mina aljahilin
2:68	qalu udau lana rabakayubayin lana ma hiya qala inahu yaquluinaha baqaratun la faridun walabikrun awanun bayna thalika fafalu matu marun
2:69	qalu udau lana rabakayubayin lana ma lawnuha qala inahuyaqulu inaha baqaratun safrau faqiaunlawnuha tasuru anathirin
2:70	qalu udau lana rabakayubayin lana ma hiya ina albaqara tashabahalayna wa ina in sha alahulamuhtadun
2:71	qala inahu yaqulu inahabaqaratun la thalulun tuthiru al arda walatasqi alhartha musalamatun la shiyata fihaqalu al ana ji ta bilhaqi fathabahuhawama kadu yafalun
2:72	wa ith qataltum nafsan fadara tumfiha walahu mukhrijun ma kuntumtaktumun
2:73	faqulna idribuhu bibadihakathalika yuhyi alahu almawtawayurikum ayatihi lalakum taqilun
2:74	thuma qasat qulubukum min badi thalikafahiya kalhijarati aw ashadu qaswatanwa ina mina alhijarati lama yatafajaruminhu al anharu wa ina minha lamayashshaqaqu fayakhruju minhu almau wa ina minhalama yahbitu min khashyati alahi wamalahu bighafilin ama tamalun
2:75	afatatmauna an yu minu lakumwaqad kana fariqun minhum yasmauna kalama alahithuma yuharifunahu min badi ma aqaluhuwahum yalamun
2:76	wa itha laqu alathina amanuqalu amana wa itha khalabaduhum ila badin qalu atuhadithunahumbima fataha alahu alaykum liyuhajukumbihi ainda rabikum afala taqilun
2:77	awa la yalamuna ana alahayalamu ma yusiruna wama yualinun
2:78	waminhum umiyuna la yalamunalkitaba ila amaniya wa in hum ilayathunun
2:79	fawaylun lilathina yaktubuna alkitababi aydihim thuma yaquluna hatha min aindi alahiliyashtaru bihi thamanan qalilan fawaylun lahum mimakatabat aydihim wawaylun lahum mima yaksibun
2:80	waqalu lan tamasana anaruila ayaman madudatan qul atakhathtumainda alahi ahdan falan yukhlifa alahuahdahu am taquluna ala alahi ma latalamun
2:81	bala man kasaba sayi atan wahatatbihi khati atuhu faula ika as habu anarihum fiha khalidun
2:82	walathina amanuwamilu asalihati ula ika as habualjanati hum fiha khalidun
2:83	wa ith akhathna mithaqabani isra ila la tabuduna ila alahawabilwalidayni ihsanan wathialqurba walyatama walmasakiniwaqulu linasi husnan waqimu asalatawatu azakata thuma tawalaytum ilaqalilan minkum wantum muaridun
2:84	wa ith akhathna mithaqakumla tasfikuna dimakum wala tukhrijunanfusakum min diyarikum thuma aqrartum wantum tashadun
2:85	thuma antum haula itaqtuluna anfusakum watukhrijuna fariqan minkum min diyarihimtathaharuna alayhim bil ithmi walaudwaniwa in ya tukum usara tufaduhum wahuwa muharamunalaykum ikhrajuhum afatu minuna bibadi alkitabiwatakfuruna bibadin fama jazau manyafalu thalika minkum ila khizyun fi alhayatiadunya wayawma alqiyamati yuraduna ilashadi alathabi wama alahu bighafilinama tamalun
2:86	ula ika alathina ishtarawualhayata adunya bil akhiratifala yukhafafu anhumu alathabu wala humyunsarun
2:87	walaqad atayna musalkitaba waqafayna min badihi birusuliwatayna aisa ibna maryama albayinatiwayadnahu biruhi alqudusi afakulama jakumrasulun bima la tahwa anfusukumuistakbartum fafariqan kaththabtum wafariqan taqtulun
2:88	waqalu qulubuna ghulfun balanahumu alahu bikufrihim faqalilan mayu minun
2:89	walama jahum kitabunmin aindi alahi musadiqun lima mahumwakanu min qablu yastaftihuna ala alathinakafaru falama jahum ma arafu kafarubihi falanatu alahi ala alkafirin
2:90	bi sama ishtaraw bihi anfusahum anyakfuru bima anzala alahu baghyan an yunazilalahu min fadlihi ala man yashaumin aibadihi fabau bighadabin alaghadabin walilkafirina athabun muhin
2:91	wa itha qila lahum aminu bimanzala alahu qalu nu minu bima unzilalayna wayakfuruna bima warahu wahuwa alhaqumusadiqan lima mahum qul falima taqtulunanbiya alahi min qablu in kuntum mu minin
2:92	walaqad jakum musa bilbayinatithuma itakhathtumu alaijla min badihi wantum thalimun
2:93	wa ith akhathna mithaqakumwarafana fawqakumu atura khuthuma ataynakum biquwatin wasmau qalusamiana wasayna waushribu fiqulubihimu alaijla bikufrihim qul bi sama ya murukumbihi imanukum in kuntum mu minin
2:94	qul in kanat lakumu adarual akhiratu ainda alahi khalisatanmin duni anasi fatamanawu almawta in kuntum sadiqin
2:95	walan yatamanawhu abadan bimaqadamat aydihim walahu alimun biaththalimin
2:96	walatajidanahum ahrasa anasiala hayatin wamina alathinashraku yawadu ahaduhum law yuamaru alfa sanatin wamahuwa bimuzahzihihi mina alathabi anyuamara walahu basirun bimayamalun
2:97	qul man kana aduwan lijibrilafa inahu nazalahu ala qalbika bi ithni alahimusadiqan lima bayna yadayhi wahudan wabushralilmu minin
2:98	man kana aduwan lilahiwamala ikatihi warusulihi wajibrila wamikalafa ina alaha aduwun lilkafirin
2:99	walaqad anzalna ilayka ayatinbayinatin wama yakfuru biha ila alfasiqun
2:100	awa kulama ahadu ahdanabathahu fariqun minhum bal aktharuhum layu minun
2:101	walama jahum rasulun minaindi alahi musadiqun lima mahum nabathafariqun mina alathina utu alkitaba kitabalahi wara thuhurihim kanahum layalamun
2:102	watabau ma tatlu ashshayatinuala mulki sulaymana wama kafara sulaymanuwalakina ashshayatina kafaruyualimuna anasa asihra wamaunzila ala almalakayni bibabila harutawamaruta wama yualimani min ahadinhata yaqula inama nahnufitnatun fala takfur fayatalamuna minhuma mayufariquna bihi bayna almar i wazawjihi wama hum bidarinabihi min ahadin ila bi ithni alahiwayatalamuna ma yaduruhum walayanfauhum walaqad alimu lamani ishtarahu malahu fi al akhirati min khalaqin walabi sa masharaw bihi anfusahum law kanu yalamun
2:103	walaw anahum amanu wataqawlamathubatun min aindi alahi khayrun law kanuyalamun
2:104	ya ayuha alathina amanula taqulu raina waqulu unthurnawasmau walilkafirina athabun alim
2:105	ma yawadu alathina kafarumin ahli alkitabi wala almushrikina an yunazalalaykum min khayrin min rabikum walahu yakhtasubirahmatihi man yashau walahu thualfadli alathim
2:106	ma nansakh min ayatin awnunsiha na ti bikhayrin minha aw mithlihalam talam ana alaha ala kuli shay in qadir
2:107	alam talam ana alaha lahu mulkuasamawati wal ardi wamalakum min duni alahi min waliyin wala nasir
2:108	am turiduna an tas alu rasulakum kamasu ila musa min qablu waman yatabadali alkufra bil imanifaqad dala sawa asabil
2:109	wada kathirun min ahli alkitabilaw yarudunakum min badi imanikum kufaran hasadanmin aindi anfusihim min badi ma tabayana lahumu alhaqufafu wasfahu hataya tiya alahu bi amrihi ina alaha alakuli shay in qadir
2:110	waqimu asalata watuazakata wama tuqadimu li anfusikum minkhayrin tajiduhu ainda alahi ina alaha bimatamaluna basir
2:111	waqalu lan yadkhula aljanata ilaman kana hudan aw nasara tilka amaniyuhumqul hatu burhanakum in kuntum sadiqin
2:112	bala man aslama wajhahu lilahiwahuwa muhsinun falahu ajruhu ainda rabihi walakhawfun alayhim wala hum yahzanun
2:113	waqalati alyahudu laysati anasarala shay in waqalati anasaralaysati alyahudu ala shay in wahum yatluna alkitabakathalika qala alathina layalamuna mithla qawlihim falahu yahkumubaynahum yawma alqiyamati fima kanu fihiyakhtalifun
2:114	waman athlamu miman manamasajida alahi an yuthkara fihaismuhu wasa fi kharabiha ula ikama kana lahum an yadkhuluha ila kha ifinalahum fi adunya khizyun walahum fi al akhiratiathabun athim
2:115	walilahi almashriqu walmaghribufaynama tuwalu fathama wajhu alahi ina alahawasiaun alim
2:116	waqalu itakhatha alahuwaladan subhanahu bal lahu ma fi asamawatiwal ardi kulun lahu qanitun
2:117	badiau asamawatiwal ardi wa itha qada amran fa inamayaqulu lahu kun fayakun
2:118	waqala alathina layalamuna lawla yukalimuna alahu awta tina ayatun kathalika qala alathinamin qablihim mithla qawlihim tashabahat qulubuhum qadbayana al ayati liqawmin yuqinun
2:119	ina arsalnaka bilhaqibashiran wanathiran wala tus alu an as habialjahim
2:120	walan tarda anka alyahudu walanasara hata tatabiamilatahum qul ina huda alahi huwa alhudawala ini itabata ahwahum bada alathi jakamina alailmi ma laka mina alahi min waliyin walanasir
2:121	alathina ataynahumualkitaba yatlunahu haqa tilawatihi ula ikayu minuna bihi waman yakfur bihi faula ika humu alkhasirun
2:122	ya bani isra ila uthkuruniamatiya alati anamtu alaykum wani fadaltukumala alalamin
2:123	wataqu yawman la tajzinafsun an nafsin shay an wala yuqbalu minhadlun wala tanfauha shafatun walahum yunsarun
2:124	wa ithi ibtala ibrahimarabuhu bikalimatin fatamahuna qala ini jailukalinasi imaman qala wamin thuriyatiqala la yanalu ahdi aththalimin
2:125	wa ith jalna albayta mathabatanlinasi wamnan watakhithu minmaqami ibrahima musalan wahidnaila ibrahima wa ismaila an tahirabaytiya lita ifina walakifinawarukai asujud
2:126	wa ith qala ibrahimurabi ijal hatha baladan aminan warzuqahlahu mina aththamarati man amana minhumbilahi walyawmi al akhiri qalawaman kafara faumatiauhu qalilan thuma adtaruhu ilathabi anari wabi sa almasir
2:127	wa ith yarfau ibrahimualqawaida mina albayti wa ismailu rabanataqabal mina inaka anta asamiau alalim
2:128	rabana wajalnamuslimayni laka wamin thuriyatina umatanmuslimatan laka warina manasikana watubalayna inaka anta atawabu arahim
2:129	rabana wabath fihimrasulan minhum yatlu alayhim ayatikawayualimuhumu alkitaba walhikmatawayuzakihim inaka anta alazizu alhakim
2:130	waman yarghabu an milati ibrahimaila man safiha nafsahu walaqadi istafaynahufi adunya wa inahu fi al akhiratilamina asalihin
2:131	ith qala lahu rabuhu aslim qalaslamtu lirabi alalamin
2:132	wawasa biha ibrahimubanihi wayaqubu ya baniya ina alaha istafalakumu adina fala tamutuna ila wantumuslimun
2:133	am kuntum shuhada ith hadarayaquba almawtu ith qala libanihi matabuduna min badi qalu nabudu ilahakawa ilaha aba ika ibrahima wa ismailawa ishaqa ilahan wahidan wanahnu lahumuslimun
2:134	tilka umatun qad khalat laha makasabat walakum ma kasabtum wala tus aluna amakanu yamalun
2:135	waqalu kunu hudan aw nasaratahtadu qul bal milata ibrahima hanifan wamakana mina almushrikin
2:136	qulu amana bilahiwama unzila ilayna wama unzila ilaibrahima wa ismaila wa ishaqawayaquba wal asbati wama utiya musawaisa wama utiya anabiyuna minrabihim la nufariqu bayna ahadin minhum wanahnulahu muslimun
2:137	fa in amanu bimithli ma amantumbihi faqadi ihtadaw wa in tawalaw fa inama hum fi shiqaqinfasayakfikahumu alahu wahuwa asamiaualalim
2:138	sibghata alahi waman ahsanumina alahi sibghatan wanahnu lahu abidun
2:139	qul atuhajunana fi alahiwahuwa rabuna warabukum walana amalunawalakum amalukum wanahnu lahu mukhlisun
2:140	am taquluna ina ibrahima wa ismailawa ishaqa wayaquba wal asbata kanuhudan aw nasara qul antum alamu ami alahuwaman athlamu miman katama shahadatanaindahu mina alahi wama alahu bighafilinama tamalun
2:141	tilka umatun qad khalat laha makasabat walakum ma kasabtum wala tus aluna amakanu yamalun
2:142	sayaqulu asufahau mina anasima walahum an qiblatihimu alati kanualayha qul lilahi almashriqu walmaghribuyahdi man yashau ila siratinmustaqim
2:143	wakathalika jalnakumumatan wasatan litakunu shuhada ala anasiwayakuna arasulu alaykum shahidan wamajalna alqiblata alati kunta alayha ilalinalama man yatabiau arasula miman yanqalibu alaqibayhi wa in kanat lakabiratan ila alalathina hada alahu wama kanalahu liyudia imanakum ina alahabinasi laraufun rahim
2:144	qad nara taqaluba wajhika fi asama ifalanuwaliyanaka qiblatan tardaha fawali wajhakashatra almasjidi alharami wahaythu makuntum fawalu wujuhakum shatrahu wa ina alathinautu alkitaba layalamuna anahu alhaqu minrabihim wama alahu bighafilin amayamalun
2:145	wala in atayta alathina utualkitaba bikuli ayatin ma tabiauqiblataka wama anta bitabiain qiblatahum wamabaduhum bitabiain qiblata badinwala ini itabata ahwahum min badi ma jakamina alailmi inaka ithan lamina aththalimin
2:146	alathina ataynahumualkitaba yarifunahu kama yarifuna abnahumwa ina fariqan minhum layaktumuna alhaqa wahumyalamun
2:147	alhaqu min rabika falatakunana mina almumtarin
2:148	walikulin wijhatun huwa muwalihafastabiqu alkhayrati aynama takunu ya tibikumu alahu jamian ina alaha alakuli shay in qadir
2:149	wamin haythu kharajta fawaliwajhaka shatra almasjidi alharami wa inahulalhaqu min rabika wama alahu bighafilinama tamalun
2:150	wamin haythu kharajta fawaliwajhaka shatra almasjidi alharami wahaythuma kuntum fawalu wujuhakum shatrahu li alayakuna linasi alaykum hujatun ilalathina thalamu minhum falatakhshawhum wakhshawni wali utima niamati alaykumwalalakum tahtadun
2:151	kama arsalna fikum rasulanminkum yatlu alaykum ayatinawayuzakikum wayualimukumu alkitaba walhikmatawayualimukum ma lam takunu talamun
2:152	fathkuruni athkurkumwashkuru li wala takfurun
2:153	ya ayuha alathina amanuistainu bisabri wasalatina alaha ma asabirin
2:154	wala taqulu liman yuqtalu fisabili alahi amwatun bal ahyaun walakinla tashaurun
2:155	walanabluwanakum bishay in mina alkhawfiwaljuai wanaqsin mina al amwali wal anfusiwaththamarati wabashshiri asabirin
2:156	alathina itha asabat humusibatun qalu ina lilahi wa inailayhi rajiaun
2:157	ula ika alayhim salawatunmin rabihim warahmatun waula ika humu almuhtadun
2:158	ina asafa walmarwatamin sha iri alahi faman haja albaytawi iatamara fala junaha alayhi an yatawafabihima waman tatawa khayran fa ina alahashakirun alim
2:159	ina alathina yaktumuna manzalna mina albayinati walhuda minbadi ma bayanahu linasi fialkitabi ula ika yalanuhumu alahuwayalanuhumu alainun
2:160	ila alathina tabuwaslahu wabayanu faula ika atubualayhim wana atawabu arahim
2:161	ina alathina kafaru wamatuwahum kufarun ula ika alayhim lanatu alahiwalmala ikati wanasi ajmain
2:162	khalidina fiha layukhafafu anhumu alathabu wala hum yuntharun
2:163	wa ilahukum ilahun wahidunla ilaha ila huwa arahmanuarahim
2:164	ina fi khalqi asamawatiwal ardi wakhtilafi alayli wanahariwalfulki alati tajri fi albahri bimayanfau anasa wama anzala alahumina asama i min ma in fahyabihi al arda bada mawtiha wabaththa fihamin kuli dabatin watasrifi ariyahiwasahabi almusakhkhari bayna asama iwal ardi layatin liqawminyaqilun
2:165	wamina anasi man yatakhithumin duni alahi andadan yuhibunahum kahubialahi walathina amanu ashadu hubanlilahi walaw yara alathina thalamuith yarawna alathaba ana alquwata lilahijamian wana alaha shadidu alathab
2:166	ith tabara alathinaitubiau mina alathina itabau warawu alathabawataqatat bihimu al asbab
2:167	waqala alathina itabaulaw ana lana karatan fanatabara minhum kamatabarau mina kathalika yurihimu alahuamalahum hasaratin alayhim wamahum bikharijina mina anar
2:168	ya ayuha anasukulu mima fi al ardi halalan tayibanwala tatabiau khutuwati ashshaytaninahu lakum aduwun mubin
2:169	inama ya murukum bisu iwalfahsha i wan taqulu ala alahima la talamun
2:170	wa itha qila lahumu itabiau manzala alahu qalu bal natabiau ma alfaynalayhi abana awa law kana abauhumla yaqiluna shay an wala yahtadun
2:171	wamathalu alathina kafarukamathali alathi yanaiqu bima la yasmauila duan wanidan sumun bukmunaumyun fahum la yaqilun
2:172	ya ayuha alathina amanukulu min tayibati ma razaqnakum washkurulilahi in kuntum iyahu tabudun
2:173	inama harama alaykumualmaytata wadama walahma alkhinziri wamauhila bihi lighayri alahi famani idtura ghayra baghinwala adin fala ithma alayhi ina alahaghafurun rahim
2:174	ina alathina yaktumuna manzala alahu mina alkitabi wayashtaruna bihithamanan qalilan ula ika ma ya kuluna fi butunihimila anara wala yukalimuhumu alahuyawma alqiyamati wala yuzakihim walahum athabunalim
2:175	ula ika alathina ishtarawuadalalata bilhuda walathababilmaghfirati fama asbarahum ala anar
2:176	thalika bi ana alahanazala alkitaba bilhaqi wa ina alathinaikhtalafu fi alkitabi lafi shiqaqin baid
2:177	laysa albira an tuwalu wujuhakum qibalalmashriqi walmaghribi walakina albira man amanabilahi walyawmi al akhiri walmala ikatiwalkitabi wanabiyina watalmala ala hubihi thawi alqurbawalyatama walmasakina wabnalsabili wasa ilina wafi ariqabiwaqama asalata watazakata walmufuna biahdihim ithahadu wasabirina fi alba sa iwadara i wahina alba si ula ikalathina sadaqu waula ika humualmutaqun
2:178	ya ayuha alathina amanukutiba alaykumu alqisasu fi alqatla alhurubilhuri walabdu bilabdi walunthabiluntha faman aufiya lahu min akhihi shay un fatibaunbilmarufi wadaun ilayhi bi ihsaninthalika takhfifun min rabikum warahmatun famaniatada bada thalika falahu athabunalim
2:179	walakum fi alqisasi hayatunya uli al albabi lalakum tataqun
2:180	kutiba alaykum itha hadarahadakumu almawtu in taraka khayran alwasiyatulilwalidayni wal aqrabina bilmarufi haqanala almutaqin
2:181	faman badalahu bada ma samiahufa inama ithmuhu ala alathinayubadilunahu ina alaha samiaun alim
2:182	faman khafa min musinjanafan aw ithman faslaha baynahum falaithma alayhi ina alaha ghafurun rahim
2:183	ya ayuha alathina amanukutiba alaykumu asiyamu kamakutiba ala alathina min qablikum lalakumtataqun
2:184	ayaman madudatin faman kanaminkum maridan aw ala safarin faidatun minayamin ukhara wala alathina yutiqunahufidyatun tamu miskinin faman tatawakhayran fahuwa khayrun lahu wan tasumu khayrun lakum inkuntum talamun
2:185	shahru ramadana alathiunzila fihi alqur anu hudan linasiwabayinatin mina alhuda walfurqanifaman shahida minkumu ashshahra falyasumhu waman kanamaridan aw ala safarin faidatun min ayaminukhara yuridu alahu bikumu alyusra wala yuridubikumu alausra walitukmilu alaidata walitukabiru alahala ma hadakum walalakum tashkurun
2:186	wa itha salaka aibadiani fa ini qaribun ujibu dawata adaitha dani falyastajibu li walyu minu bilalahum yarshudun
2:187	uhila lakum laylata asiyamiarafathu ila nisa ikum huna libasunlakum wantum libasun lahuna alima alahuanakum kuntum takhtanuna anfusakum fatabalaykum wafa ankum fal ana bashiruhunawabtaghu ma kataba alahu lakum wakulu washrabuhata yatabayana lakumu alkhaytu al abyadumina alkhayti al aswadi mina alfajri thuma atimu asiyamaila alayli wala tubashiruhuna wantum akifunafi almasajidi tilka hududu alahi falataqrabuha kathalika yubayinu alahu ayatihilinasi lalahum yataqun
2:188	wala ta kulu amwalakumbaynakum bilbatili watudlu biha ilalhukami lita kulu fariqan min amwali anasibil ithmi wantum talamun
2:189	yas alunaka ani al ahilati qul hiya mawaqitulinasi walhaji walaysa albirubi an ta tu albuyuta min thuhuriha walakinalbira mani itaqa wa tu albuyuta min abwabihawataqu alaha lalakum tuflihun
2:190	waqatilu fi sabili alahialathina yuqatilunakum wala tataduina alaha la yuhibu almuatadin
2:191	waqtuluhum haythuthaqiftumuhum wakhrijuhum min haythu akhrajukum walfitnatuashadu mina alqatli wala tuqatiluhum aindalmasjidi alharami hata yuqatilukumfihi fa in qatalukum faqtuluhum kathalikajazau alkafirin
2:192	fa ini intahaw fa ina alahaghafurun rahim
2:193	waqatiluhum hata latakuna fitnatun wayakuna adinu lilahi fa inintahaw fala audwana ila ala aththalimin
2:194	ashshahru alharamu bishshahrialharami walhurumatu qisasunfamani iatada alaykum fatadu alayhibimithli ma iatada alaykum wataqu alahawalamu ana alaha ma almutaqin
2:195	wanfiqu fi sabili alahi walatulqu bi aydikum ila atahlukati wahsinuina alaha yuhibu almuhsinin
2:196	watimu alhaja walaumratalilahi fa in uhsirtum fama istaysara minalhadyi wala tahliqu rusakum hatayablugha alhadyu mahilahu faman kana minkum maridanaw bihi athan min ra sihi fafidyatun min siyaminaw sadaqatin aw nusukin fa itha amintum famantamata bilaumrati ila alhaji famaistaysara mina alhadyi faman lam yajid fasiyamuthalathati ayamin fi alhaji wasabatin itharajatum tilka asharatun kamilatun thalika limanlam yakun ahluhu hadiri almasjidi alharamiwataqu alaha walamu ana alahashadidu alaiqab
2:197	alhaju ashurun malumatunfaman farada fihina alhaja fala rafathawala fusuqa wala jidala fi alhajiwama tafalu min khayrin yalamhu alahuwatazawadu fa ina khayra azadi ataqwawataquni ya uli al albab
2:198	laysa alaykum junahun an tabtaghufadlan min rabikum fa itha afadtum minarafatin fathkuru alaha aindalmashari alharami wathkuruhu kamahadakum wa in kuntum min qablihi lamina adalin
2:199	thuma afidu min haythu afadanasu wastaghfiru alaha ina alahaghafurun rahim
2:200	fa itha qadaytum manasikakumfathkuru alaha kathikrikum abakumaw ashada thikran famina anasi manyaqulu rabana atina fi adunyawama lahu fi al akhirati min khalaq
2:201	waminhum man yaqulu rabana atinafi adunya hasanatan wafi al akhiratihasanatan waqina athaba anar
2:202	ula ika lahum nasibun mimakasabu walahu sariau alhisab
2:203	wathkuru alaha fiayamin madudatin faman tajala fi yawmaynifala ithma alayhi waman takhkhara fala ithmalayhi limani itaqa wataqu alaha walamuanakum ilayhi tuhsharun
2:204	wamina anasi man yuajibukaqawluhu fi alhayati adunyawayushidu alaha ala ma fi qalbihiwahuwa aladu alkhisam
2:205	wa itha tawala safi al ardi liyufsida fiha wayuhlika alharthawanasla walahu la yuhibualfasad
2:206	wa itha qila lahu itaqi alahakhathat hu alaizatu bil ithmi fahasbuhujahanamu walabi sa almihad
2:207	wamina anasi man yashrinafsahu ibtigha mardati alahi walahuraufun bilaibad
2:208	ya ayuha alathina amanudkhulu fi asilmi kafatan walatatabiau khutuwati ashshaytaninahu lakum aduwun mubin
2:209	fa in zalaltum min badi ma jatkumualbayinatu falamu ana alaha azizunhakim
2:210	hal yanthuruna ilan ya tiyahumu alahu fi thulalin minalghamami walmala ikatu waqudiyal amru wa ila alahi turjau al umur
2:211	sal bani isra ila kam ataynahumin ayatin bayinatin waman yubadil niamata alahimin badi ma jat hu fa ina alahashadidu alaiqab
2:212	zuyina lilathina kafaru alhayatuadunya wayaskharuna mina alathina amanuwalathina itaqaw fawqahum yawma alqiyamatiwalahu yarzuqu man yashau bighayri hisab
2:213	kana anasu umatan wahidatanfabatha alahu anabiyina mubashshirinawamunthirina wanzala mahumu alkitaba bilhaqiliyahkuma bayna anasi fimaikhtalafu fihi wama ikhtalafa fihi ila alathinautuhu min badi ma jat humu albayinatubaghyan baynahum fahada alahu alathina amanulima ikhtalafu fihi mina alhaqi bi ithnihiwalahu yahdi man yashau ila siratinmustaqim
2:214	am hasibtum an tadkhulu aljanatawalama ya tikum mathalu alathina khalaw minqablikum masat humu alba sau wadarauwazulzilu hata yaqula arasulu walathinamanu mahu mata nasru alahi alaina nasra alahi qarib
2:215	yas alunaka matha yunfiquna qul manfaqtum min khayrin falilwalidayni wal aqrabinawalyatama walmasakini wabniasabili wama tafalu min khayrin fa ina alahabihi alim
2:216	kutiba alaykumu alqitalu wahuwakurhun lakum wasa an takrahu shay an wahuwa khayrunlakum wasa an tuhibu shay an wahuwa sharunlakum walahu yalamu wantum latalamun
2:217	yas alunaka ani ashshahri alharamiqitalin fihi qul qitalun fihi kabirun wasadunan sabili alahi wakufrun bihi walmasjidi alharamiwa ikhraju ahlihi minhu akbaru ainda alahi walfitnatuakbaru mina alqatli wala yazaluna yuqatilunakumhata yarudukum an dinikum ini istatauwaman yartadid minkum an dinihi fayamut wahuwa kafirunfaula ika habitat amaluhum fi adunyawal akhirati waula ika as habuanari hum fiha khalidun
2:218	ina alathina amanu walathinahajaru wajahadu fi sabili alahi ula ikayarjuna rahmata alahi walahughafurun rahim
2:219	yas alunaka ani alkhamri walmaysiriqul fihima ithmun kabirun wamanafiau linasiwa ithmuhuma akbaru min nafaihima wayas alunaka mathayunfiquna quli alafwa kathalika yubayinu alahulakumu al ayati lalakum tatafakarun
2:220	fi adunya wal akhiratiwayas alunaka ani alyatama qul islahunlahum khayrun wa in tukhalituhum fa ikhwanukumwalahu yalamu almufsida mina almuslihiwalaw sha alahu lanatakum ina alahazizun hakim
2:221	wala tankihu almushrikatihata yu mina walamatun mu minatun khayrun minmushrikatin walaw ajabatkum wala tunkihualmushrikina hata yu minu walabdun mu minunkhayrun min mushrikin walaw ajabakum ula ika yadaunaila anari walahu yadauila aljanati walmaghfirati bi ithnihiwayubayinu ayatihi linasilalahum yatathakarun
2:222	wayas alunaka ani almahidiqul huwa athan fatazilu anisafi almahidi wala taqrabuhuna hatayathurna fa itha tataharna fa tuhuna min haythuamarakumu alahu ina alaha yuhibu atawabinawayuhibu almutatahirin
2:223	nisaukum harthun lakum fa tuharthakum ana shi tum waqadimu li anfusikum wataqualaha walamu anakum mulaquhuwabashshiri almu minin
2:224	wala tajalu alaha aurdatanli aymanikum an tabaru watataqu watuslihubayna anasi walahu samiaunalim
2:225	la yu akhithukumu alahubilaghwi fi aymanikum walakin yu akhithukumbima kasabat qulubukum walahu ghafurun halim
2:226	lilathina yu luna min nisa ihimtarabusu arbati ashurin fa in fau fa ina alahaghafurun rahim
2:227	wa in azamu atalaqafa ina alaha samiaun alim
2:228	walmutalaqatuyatarabasna bi anfusihina thalathata quru in walayahilu lahuna an yaktumna ma khalaqa alahufi arhamihina in kuna yu mina bilahiwalyawmi al akhiri wabuaulatuhuna ahaqubiradihina fi thalika in aradu islahanwalahuna mithlu alathi alayhina bilmarufiwalirijali alayhina darajatun walahuazizun hakim
2:229	atalaqu maratanifa imsakun bimarufin aw tasrihun bi ihsaninwala yahilu lakum an ta khuthu mimataytumuhuna shay an ila an yakhafala yuqima hududa alahi fa inkhiftum ala yuqima hududa alahifala junaha alayhima fima iftadatbihi tilka hududu alahi fala tataduhawaman yatada hududa alahi faula ikahumu aththalimun
2:230	fa in talaqaha falatahilu lahu min badu hata tankihazawjan ghayrahu fa in talaqaha fala junahalayhima an yataraja in thanan yuqima hududa alahi watilka hududualahi yubayinuha liqawmin yalamun
2:231	wa itha talaqtumu anisafabalaghna ajalahuna famsikuhuna bimarufin aw sarihuhunabimarufin wala tumsikuhuna diraranlitatadu waman yafal thalika faqad thalamanafsahu wala tatakhithu ayati alahihuzuwan wathkuru niamata alahi alaykumwama anzala alaykum mina alkitabi walhikmatiyaithukum bihi wataqu alaha walamuana alaha bikuli shay in alim
2:232	wa itha talaqtumu anisafabalaghna ajalahuna fala taduluhuna an yankihnazwajahuna itha taradaw baynahum bilmarufithalika yuathu bihi man kanaminkum yu minu bilahi walyawmi al akhirithalikum azka lakum watharu walahuyalamu wantum la talamun
2:233	walwalidatu yurdianawladahuna hawlayni kamilayni liman aradan yutima aradata wala almawludilahu rizquhuna wakiswatuhuna bilmarufi latukalafu nafsun ila wusaha la tudarawalidatun biwaladiha wala mawludun lahubiwaladihi wala alwarithi mithlu thalikafa in arada fisalan an taradinminhuma watashawurin fala junahalayhima wa in aradtum an tastardiau awladakumfala junaha alaykum itha salamtum mataytum bilmarufi wataqu alahawalamu ana alaha bima tamaluna basir
2:234	walathina yutawafawnaminkum wayatharuna azwajan yatarabasnabi anfusihina arbata ashurin washran fa ithabalaghna ajalahuna fala junaha alaykum fimafalna fi anfusihina bilmarufi walahubima tamaluna khabir
2:235	wala junaha alaykum fimaradtum bihi min khitbati anisa iaw aknantum fi anfusikum alima alahu anakum satathkurunahunawalakin la tuwaiduhuna siran ilan taqulu qawlan marufan wala tazimu auqdata anikahihata yablugha alkitabu ajalahu walamuana alaha yalamu ma fi anfusikum fahtharuhuwalamu ana alaha ghafurun halim
2:236	la junaha alaykum in talaqtumuanisa ma lam tamasuhuna aw tafridulahuna faridatan wamatiauhuna alalmusiai qadaruhu wala almuqtiri qadaruhu matanbilmarufi haqan ala almuhsinin
2:237	wa in talaqtumuhuna min qabli antamasuhuna waqad faradtum lahuna faridatanfanisfu ma faradtum ila an yafunaw yafuwa alathi biyadihi auqdatu anikahiwan tafu aqrabu litaqwa wala tansawualfadla baynakum ina alaha bimatamaluna basir
2:238	hafithu alasalawati wasalatialwusta waqumu lilahi qanitin
2:239	fa in khiftum farijalan aw rukbananfa itha amintum fathkuru alaha kamalamakum ma lam takunu talamun
2:240	walathina yutawafawnaminkum wayatharuna azwajan wasiyatanli azwajihim matan ila alhawlighayra ikhrajin fa in kharajna fala junahalaykum fi ma falna fi anfusihina min marufinwalahu azizun hakim
2:241	walilmutalaqati mataunbilmarufi haqan ala almutaqin
2:242	kathalika yubayinu alahulakum ayatihi lalakum taqilun
2:243	alam tara ila alathinakharaju min diyarihim wahum ulufun hatharalmawti faqala lahumu alahu mutu thuma ahyahumina alaha lathu fadlin ala anasiwalakina akthara anasi layashkurun
2:244	waqatilu fi sabili alahiwalamu ana alaha samiaun alim
2:245	man tha alathi yuqridualaha qardan hasanan fayudaifahulahu adafan kathiratan walahuyaqbidu wayabsutu wa ilayhi turjaun
2:246	alam tara ila almala i min bani isra ilamin badi musa ith qalu linabiyin lahumuibath lana malikan nuqatil fi sabili alahiqala hal asaytum in kutiba alaykumu alqitaluala tuqatilu qalu wama lanala nuqatila fi sabili alahi waqadukhrijna min diyarina wabna inafalama kutiba alayhimu alqitalu tawalaw ilaqalilan minhum walahu alimun biththalimin
2:247	waqala lahum nabiyuhum ina alahaqad batha lakum taluta malikan qalu anayakunu lahu almulku alayna wanahnu ahaqubilmulki minhu walam yu ta satan mina almali qalaina alaha istafahu alaykum wazadahubastatan fi alailmi waljismi walahuyu ti mulkahu man yashau walahu wasiaunalim
2:248	waqala lahum nabiyuhum ina ayatamulkihi an ya tiyakumu atabutu fihi sakinatunmin rabikum wabaqiyatun mima taraka alu musawalu haruna tahmiluhu almala ikatuina fi thalika layatan lakum in kuntum mu minin
2:249	falama fasala talutubiljunudi qala ina alaha mubtalikumbinaharin faman shariba minhu falaysa mini waman lam yatamhufa inahu mini ila mani ightarafa ghurfatan biyadihifasharibu minhu ila qalilan minhum falama jawazahuhuwa walathina amanu mahu qalula taqata lana alyawma bijalutawajunudihi qala alathina yathununanahum mulaqu alahi kam min fi atin qalilatinghalabat fi atan kathiratan bi ithni alahi walahuma asabirin
2:250	walama barazu lijalutawajunudihi qalu rabana afrigh alayna sabranwathabit aqdamana wansurnala alqawmi alkafirin
2:251	fahazamuhum bi ithni alahiwaqatala dawudu jaluta watahu alahualmulka walhikmata walamahu mima yashauwalawla dafau alahi anasa badahumbibadin lafasadati al ardu walakina alahathu fadlin ala alalamin
2:252	tilka ayatu alahinatluha alayka bilhaqi wa inaka laminalmursalin
2:253	tilka arusulu fadalnabadahum ala badin minhum man kalamalahu warafa badahum darajatin wataynaisa ibna maryama albayinati wayadnahubiruhi alqudusi walaw sha alahu maiqtatala alathina min badihim min badi ma jat humualbayinatu walakini ikhtalafu faminhum man amanawaminhum man kafara walaw sha alahu maiqtatalu walakina alaha yafalu mayurid
2:254	ya ayuha alathina amanuanfiqu mima razaqnakum min qabli an ya tiyayawmun la bayaun fihi wala khulatun walashafatun walkafiruna humu aththalimun
2:255	alahu la ilaha ilahuwa alhayu alqayumu la ta khuthuhusinatun wala nawmun lahu ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi man tha alathiyashfau aindahu ila bi ithnihi yalamu mabayna aydihim wama khalfahum wala yuhitunabishay in min ailmihi ila bima shawasia kursiyuhu asamawati wal ardawala yauduhu hifthuhumawahuwa alaliyu alathim
2:256	la ikraha fi adiniqad tabayana arushdu mina alghayi faman yakfur bitaghutiwayu min bilahi faqadi istamsaka bilaurwatialwuthqa la infisama laha walahusamiaun alim
2:257	alahu waliyu alathina amanuyukhrijuhum mina aththulumati ilanuri walathina kafaru awliyauhumuataghutu yukhrijunahum mina anuri ilaththulumati ula ika as habuanari hum fiha khalidun
2:258	alam tara ila alathi hajaibrahima fi rabihi an atahu alahualmulka ith qala ibrahimu rabiya alathiyuhyi wayumitu qala ana uhyiwaumitu qala ibrahimu fa ina alahaya ti bishshamsi mina almashriqi fa ti biha minalmaghribi fabuhita alathi kafara walahula yahdi alqawma aththalimin
2:259	aw kalathi mara alaqaryatin wahiya khawiyatun ala aurushihaqala ana yuhyi hathihi alahubada mawtiha famatahu alahu mi ata aminthuma bathahu qala kam labithta qala labithtuyawman aw bada yawmin qala bal labithta mi ata aminfanthur ila tamikawasharabika lam yatasanah wanthurila himarika walinajalaka ayatan linasiwanthur ila alaithamikayfa nunshizuha thuma naksuha lahmanfalama tabayana lahu qala alamu ana alahala kuli shay in qadir
2:260	wa ith qala ibrahimurabi arini kayfa tuhyi almawta qala awalam tu min qala bala walakin liyatma inaqalbi qala fakhuth arbatan mina atayrifasurhuna ilayka thuma ijal ala kuli jabalinminhuna juz an thuma udauhuna ya tinaka sayan walamana alaha azizun hakim
2:261	mathalu alathina yunfiquna amwalahumfi sabili alahi kamathali habatin anbatatsaba sanabila fi kuli sunbulatin mi atu habatinwalahu yudaifu liman yashau walahuwasiaun alim
2:262	alathina yunfiquna amwalahumfi sabili alahi thuma la yutbiauna manfaqu manan wala athan lahum ajruhum aindarabihim wala khawfun alayhim wala hum yahzanun
2:263	qawlun marufun wamaghfiratun khayrun minsadaqatin yatbauha athan walahughaniyun halim
2:264	ya ayuha alathina amanula tubtilu sadaqatikum bilmaniwal atha kalathi yunfiqu malahuri a anasi wala yu minu bilahiwalyawmi al akhiri famathaluhu kamathali safwaninalayhi turabun fasabahu wabilunfatarakahu saldan la yaqdiruna alashay in mima kasabu walahu layahdi alqawma alkafirin
2:265	wamathalu alathina yunfiquna amwalahumuibtigha mardati alahi watathbitan minanfusihim kamathali janatin birabwatin asabaha wabilunfatat ukulaha diafayni fa in lam yusibhawabilun fatalun walahu bimatamaluna basir
2:266	ayawadu ahadukum an takuna lahujanatun min nakhilin wanabin tajri min tahtihal anharu lahu fiha min kuli aththamaratiwasabahu alkibaru walahu thuriyatun duafaufasabaha iasarun fihi narun fahtaraqatkathalika yubayinu alahu lakumu al ayatilalakum tatafakarun
2:267	ya ayuha alathina amanuanfiqu min tayibati ma kasabtum wamimakhrajna lakum mina al ardi wala tayamamualkhabitha minhu tunfiquna walastum bi akhithihila an tughmidu fihi walamu ana alahaghaniyun hamid
2:268	ashshaytanu yaidukumualfaqra waya murukum bilfahsha i walahuyaidukum maghfiratan minhu wafadlan walahuwasiaun alim
2:269	yu ti alhikmata man yashauwaman yu ta alhikmata faqad utiya khayran kathiran wamayaththakaru ila ulu al albab
2:270	wama anfaqtum min nafaqatin aw nathartumin nathrin fa ina alaha yalamuhu wamaliththalimina min ansar
2:271	in tubdu asadaqatifaniaima hiya wa in tukhfuha watu tuhalfuqara fahuwa khayrun lakum wayukafiru ankum minsayi atikum walahu bimatamaluna khabir
2:272	laysa alayka hudahum walakinalaha yahdi man yashau wama tunfiqu minkhayrin fali anfusikum wama tunfiquna ila ibtighawajhi alahi wama tunfiqu min khayrin yuwafailaykum wantum la tuthlamun
2:273	lilfuqara i alathina uhsirufi sabili alahi la yastatiauna darbanfi al ardi yahsabuhumu aljahilu aghniyamina atafufi tarifuhum bisimahum layas aluna anasa ilhafan wamatunfiqu min khayrin fa ina alaha bihi alim
2:274	alathina yunfiquna amwalahumbilayli wanahari siran walaniyatanfalahum ajruhum ainda rabihim wala khawfun alayhim walahum yahzanun
2:275	alathina ya kuluna aribala yaqumuna ila kama yaqumu alathiyatakhabatuhu ashshaytanu mina almasi thalikabi anahum qalu inama albayau mithlu aribawahala alahu albaya waharama aribafaman jahu mawaithatun min rabihi fantahafalahu ma salafa wamruhu ila alahi wamanada faula ika as habu anarihum fiha khalidun
2:276	yamhaqu alahu aribawayurbi asadaqati walahula yuhibu kula kafarin athim
2:277	ina alathina amanuwamilu asalihati waqamu asalatawatawu azakata lahum ajruhum aindarabihim wala khawfun alayhim wala hum yahzanun
2:278	ya ayuha alathina amanuitaqu alaha watharu ma baqiya mina aribain kuntum mu minin
2:279	fa in lam tafalu fa thanu biharbinmina alahi warasulihi wa in tubtum falakum rusu amwalikumla tathlimuna wala tuthlamun
2:280	wa in kana thu ausratinfanathiratun ila maysaratin wan tasadaqukhayrun lakum in kuntum talamun
2:281	wataqu yawman turjauna fihila alahi thuma tuwafa kulu nafsin makasabat wahum la yuthlamun
2:282	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha tadayantum bidaynin ila ajalin musamanfaktubuhu walyaktub baynakum katibun biladliwala ya ba katibun an yaktuba kamalamahu alahu falyaktub walyumlili alathialayhi alhaqu walyataqi alaha rabahu walayabkhas minhu shay an fa in kana alathi alayhialhaqu safihan aw daifan aw la yastatiauan yumila huwa falyumlil waliyuhu biladli wastashidushahidayni min rijalikum fa in lam yakunarajulayni farajulun wamratani miman tardawnamina ashshuhada i an tadila ihdahumafatuthakira ihdahuma al ukhrawala ya ba ashshuhadau itha maduau wala tas amu an taktubuhu saghiran awkabiran ila ajalihi thalikum aqsatu aindalahi waqwamu lishshahadati wadnala tartabu ila an takuna tijaratanhadiratan tudirunaha baynakum falaysa alaykumjunahun ala taktubuha washidu ithatabayatum wala yudara katibun walashahidun wa in tafalu fa inahu fusuqun bikum wataqualaha wayualimukumu alahu walahubikuli shay in alim
2:283	wa in kuntum ala safarin walamtajidu katiban farihanun maqbudatun fa inamina badukum badan falyu adialathi tumina amanatahu walyataqi alaha rabahuwala taktumu ashshahadata waman yaktumhafa inahu athimun qalbuhu walahu bimatamaluna alim
2:284	lilahi ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi wa in tubdu ma fianfusikum aw tukhfuhu yuhasibkum bihi alahufayaghfiru liman yashau wayuaththibu man yashauwalahu ala kuli shay in qadir
2:285	amana arasulu bimaunzila ilayhi min rabihi walmu minuna kulun amanabilahi wamala ikatihi wakutubihiwarusulihi la nufariqu bayna ahadin min rusulihiwaqalu samiana watana ghufranakarabana wa ilayka almasir
2:286	la yukalifu alahu nafsanila wusaha laha ma kasabatwalayha ma iktasabat rabana latu akhithna in nasina aw akhta narabana wala tahmil alayna isrankama hamaltahu ala alathina minqablina rabana wala tuhamilnama la taqata lana bihi wafuana waghfir lana warhamnanta mawlana fansurna alalqawmi alkafirin
3:1	alif lam mim
3:2	alahu la ilaha ilahuwa alhayu alqayum
3:3	nazala alayka alkitaba bilhaqimusadiqan lima bayna yadayhi wanzala atawratawal injil
3:4	min qablu hudan linasiwanzala alfurqana ina alathina kafaru bi ayatialahi lahum athabun shadidun walahuazizun thu intiqam
3:5	ina alaha la yakhfalayhi shay un fi al ardi wala fi asama 
3:6	huwa alathi yusawirukum fial arhami kayfa yashau la ilaha ilahuwa alazizu alhakim
3:7	huwa alathi anzala alayka alkitabaminhu ayatun muhkamatun huna umualkitabi waukharu mutashabihatun famalathina fi qulubihim zayghun fayatabiauna matashabaha minhu ibtigha alfitnati wabtighata wilihi wama yalamu ta wilahu ila alahuwarasikhuna fi alailmi yaquluna amanabihi kulun min aindi rabina wama yaththakaruila ulu al albab
3:8	rabana la tuzigh qulubanabada ith hadaytana wahab lana min ladunkarahmatan inaka anta alwahab
3:9	rabana inaka jamiau anasiliyawmin la rayba fihi ina alaha layukhlifu almiad
3:10	ina alathina kafaru lan tughniyanhum amwaluhum wala awladuhum mina alahishay an waula ika hum waqudu anar
3:11	kada bi ali firawna walathinamin qablihim kaththabu bi ayatinafakhathahumu alahu bithunubihim walahushadidu alaiqab
3:12	qul lilathina kafaru satughlabunawatuhsharuna ila jahanama wabi sa almihad
3:13	qad kana lakum ayatun fifi atayni iltaqata fi atun tuqatilu fi sabili alahiwaukhra kafiratun yarawnahum mithlayhim ra yalayni walahu yu ayidu binasrihi manyashau ina fi thalika laibratan li uli al absar
3:14	zuyina linasi hubuashshahawati mina anisa i walbaninawalqanatiri almuqantarati mina aththahabiwalfidati walkhayli almusawamati wal anamiwalharthi thalika matau alhayatiadunya walahu aindahu husnualmab
3:15	qul aunabi ukum bikhayrin min thalikumlilathina itaqaw ainda rabihim janatun tajrimin tahtiha al anharu khalidina fihawazwajun mutaharatun waridwanunmina alahi walahu basirun bilaibad
3:16	alathina yaquluna rabanainana amana faghfir lana thunubanawaqina athaba anar
3:17	asabirina wasadiqinawalqanitina walmunfiqina walmustaghfirinabil ashar
3:18	shahida alahu anahu la ilahaila huwa walmala ikatu waulu alailmi qa imanbilqisti la ilaha ila huwalazizu alhakim
3:19	ina adina ainda alahial islamu wama ikhtalafa alathina utualkitaba ila min badi ma jahumualailmu baghyan baynahum waman yakfur bi ayati alahifa ina alaha sariau alhisab
3:20	fa in hajuka faqul aslamtu wajhiyalilahi wamani itabani waqul lilathina utualkitaba walumiyina aslamtum fa inaslamu faqadi ihtadaw wa in tawalaw fa inama alaykalbalaghu walahu basirun bilaibad
3:21	ina alathina yakfuruna bi ayatialahi wayaqtuluna anabiyina bighayri haqinwayaqtuluna alathina ya muruna bilqistimina anasi fabashshirhum biathabin alim
3:22	ula ika alathina habitatamaluhum fi adunya wal akhiratiwama lahum min nasirin
3:23	alam tara ila alathina utunasiban mina alkitabi yudawna ila kitabialahi liyahkuma baynahum thuma yatawalafariqun minhum wahum muaridun
3:24	thalika bi anahum qalu lantamasana anaru ila ayamanmadudatin wagharahum fi dinihim ma kanuyaftarun
3:25	fakayfa itha jamanahumliyawmin la rayba fihi wawufiyat kulu nafsin makasabat wahum la yuthlamun
3:26	quli alahuma malika almulkitu ti almulka man tashau watanziau almulka miman tashauwatuaizu man tashau watuthilu man tashaubiyadika alkhayru inaka ala kuli shay in qadir
3:27	tuliju alayla fi anahariwatuliju anahara fi alayli watukhriju alhayamina almayiti watukhriju almayita mina alhayi watarzuquman tashau bighayri hisab
3:28	la yatakhithi almu minunalkafirina awliya min duni almu minina wamanyafal thalika falaysa mina alahi fi shay in ilan tataqu minhum tuqatan wayuhaththirukumualahu nafsahu wa ila alahi almasir
3:29	qul in tukhfu ma fi sudurikumaw tubduhu yalamhu alahu wayalamu ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi walahu alakuli shay in qadir
3:30	yawma tajidu kulu nafsin ma amilatmin khayrin muhdaran wama amilat min su intawadu law ana baynaha wabaynahu amadan baidan wayuhaththirukumualahu nafsahu walahu raufun bilaibad
3:31	qul in kuntum tuhibuna alahafatabiauni yuhbibkumu alahu wayaghfirlakum thunubakum walahu ghafurun rahim
3:32	qul atiau alaha warasulafa in tawalaw fa ina alaha la yuhibu alkafirin
3:33	ina alaha istafa adamawanuhan wala ibrahima wala aimranala alalamin
3:34	thuriyatan baduhamin badin walahu samiaun alim
3:35	ith qalati imratu aimranarabi ini nathartu laka ma fi batni muhararanfataqabal mini inaka anta asamiau alalim
3:36	falama wadat ha qalatrabi ini wadatuha untha walahualamu bima wadat walaysa aththakarukaluntha wa ini samaytuha maryamawa ini uaithuha bika wathuriyatahamina ashshaytani arajim
3:37	fataqabalaha rabuhabiqabulin hasanin wanbataha nabatan hasananwakafalaha zakariya kulama dakhalalayha zakariya almihraba wajadaindaha rizqan qala ya maryamu analaki hatha qalat huwa min aindi alahi inalaha yarzuqu man yashau bighayri hisab
3:38	hunalika da zakariyarabahu qala rabi hab li min ladunka thuriyatantayibatan inaka samiau adua 
3:39	fanadat hu almala ikatu wahuwaqa imun yusali fi almihrabi analaha yubashshiruka biyahya musadiqanbikalimatin mina alahi wasayidan wahasuranwanabiyan mina asalihin
3:40	qala rabi ana yakunu lighulamun waqad balaghaniya alkibaru wamrati aqirunqala kathalika alahu yafalu mayasha 
3:41	qala rabi ijal li ayatan qalayatuka ala tukalima anasa thalathatayamin ila ramzan wathkur rabakakathiran wasabih bilashiyi wal ibkar
3:42	wa ith qalati almala ikatuya maryamu ina alaha istafaki wataharakiwastafaki ala nisa i alalamin
3:43	ya maryamu uqnuti lirabiki wasjudiwarkai ma arakiain
3:44	thalika min anba i alghaybinuhihi ilayka wama kunta ladayhim ithyulquna aqlamahum ayuhum yakfulu maryama wamakunta ladayhim ith yakhtasimun
3:45	ith qalati almala ikatuya maryamu ina alaha yubashshiruki bikalimatinminhu ismuhu almasihu aisa ibnu maryama wajihanfi adunya wal akhirati waminalmuqarabin
3:46	wayukalimu anasa fialmahdi wakahlan wamina asalihin
3:47	qalat rabi ana yakunu liwaladun walam yamsasni basharun qala kathaliki alahuyakhluqu ma yashau itha qada amranfa inama yaqulu lahu kun fayakun
3:48	wayualimuhu alkitaba walhikmatawatawrata wal injil
3:49	warasulan ila bani isra ilani qad ji tukum bi ayatin min rabikum ani akhluqulakum mina atini kahay ati atayrifanfukhu fihi fayakunu tayran bi ithni alahiwaubri u al akmaha wal abrasa wauhyialmawta bi ithni alahi waunabi ukum bimata kuluna wama tadakhiruna fi buyutikum ina fi thalikalayatan lakum in kuntum mu minin
3:50	wamusadiqan lima baynayadaya mina atawrati wali uhila lakumbada alathi hurima alaykum waji tukumbi ayatin min rabikum fataqu alaha watiaun
3:51	ina alaha rabi warabukum fabuduhuhatha siratun mustaqim
3:52	falama ahasa aisaminhumu alkufra qala man ansari ila alahiqala alhawariyuna nahnu ansarualahi amana bilahi washadbi ana muslimun
3:53	rabana amana bimanzalta watabana arasula faktubnama ashshahidin
3:54	wamakaru wamakara alahu walahukhayru almakirin
3:55	ith qala alahu yaisa ini mutawafika warafiauka ilaya wamutahirukamina alathina kafaru wajailu alathinaitabauka fawqa alathina kafaru ila yawmialqiyamati thuma ilaya marjiaukum fahkumubaynakum fima kuntum fihi takhtalifun
3:56	fama alathina kafarufauaththibuhum athaban shadidan fi adunyawal akhirati wama lahum min nasirin
3:57	wama alathina amanuwamilu asalihati fayuwafihimujurahum walahu la yuhibu aththalimin
3:58	thalika natluhu alayka mina al ayatiwaththikri alhakim
3:59	ina mathala aisa ainda alahikamathali adama khalaqahu min turabin thuma qalalahu kun fayakun
3:60	alhaqu min rabika fala takunmina almumtarin
3:61	faman hajaka fihi min badi majaka mina alailmi faqul talaw nadau abnanawabnakum wanisana wanisakumwanfusana wanfusakum thuma nabtahil fanajal lanatalahi ala alkathibin
3:62	ina hatha lahuwa alqasasualhaqu wama min ilahin ila alahuwa ina alaha lahuwa alazizu alhakim
3:63	fa in tawalaw fa ina alahalimun bilmufsidin
3:64	qul ya ahla alkitabi talawila kalimatin sawa in baynana wabaynakum alanabuda ila alaha wala nushrika bihishay an wala yatakhitha badunabadan arbaban min duni alahi fa intawalaw faqulu ishadu bi ana muslimun
3:65	ya ahla alkitabi lima tuhajunafi ibrahima wama unzilati atawratuwal injilu ila min badihi afalataqilun
3:66	ha antum haula i hajajtumfima lakum bihi ailmun falima tuhajuna fimalaysa lakum bihi ailmun walahu yalamu wantumla talamun
3:67	ma kana ibrahimuyahudiyan wala nasraniyan walakinkana hanifan musliman wama kana minalmushrikin
3:68	ina awla anasibi ibrahima lalathina itabauhu wahathanabiyu walathina amanu walahuwaliyu almu minin
3:69	wadat ta ifatun min ahli alkitabilaw yudilunakum wama yudiluna ilanfusahum wama yashaurun
3:70	ya ahla alkitabi limatakfuruna bi ayati alahi wantumtashadun
3:71	ya ahla alkitabi limatalbisuna alhaqa bilbatili wataktumunalhaqa wantum talamun
3:72	waqalat ta ifatun min ahlialkitabi aminu bilathi unzilala alathina amanu wajha anahariwakfuru akhirahu lalahum yarjiaun
3:73	wala tu minu ila limantabia dinakum qul ina alhuda huda alahian yu ta ahadun mithla ma utitum aw yuhajukumainda rabikum qul ina alfadla biyadi alahiyu tihi man yashau walahu wasiaunalim
3:74	yakhtasu birahmatihi man yashauwalahu thu alfadli alathim
3:75	wamin ahli alkitabi man in ta manhubiqintarin yu adihi ilayka waminhum man in ta manhubidinarin la yu adihi ilayka ila madumta alayhi qa iman thalika bi anahum qalulaysa alayna fi al umiyina sabilun wayaqulunala alahi alkathiba wahum yalamun
3:76	bala man awfa biahdihi wataqafa ina alaha yuhibu almutaqin
3:77	ina alathina yashtaruna biahdialahi waymanihim thamanan qalilan ula ikala khalaqa lahum fi al akhirati walayukalimuhumu alahu wala yanthuruilayhim yawma alqiyamati wala yuzakihim walahumathabun alim
3:78	wa ina minhum lafariqan yalwunalsinatahum bilkitabi litahsabuhu minalkitabi wama huwa mina alkitabiwayaquluna huwa min aindi alahi wama huwa minaindi alahi wayaquluna ala alahi alkathibawahum yalamun
3:79	ma kana libasharin anyu tiyahu alahu alkitaba walhukmawanubuwata thuma yaqula linasikunu aibadan li min duni alahi walakinkunu rabaniyina bima kuntum tualimunalkitaba wabima kuntum tadrusun
3:80	wala ya murakum an tatakhithualmala ikata wanabiyina arbabanaya murukum bilkufri bada ith antum muslimun
3:81	wa ith akhatha alahumithaqa anabiyina lama ataytukumin kitabin wahikmatin thuma jakumrasulun musadiqun lima makum latu minuna bihiwalatansurunahu qala aqrartum wakhathtumala thalikum isri qalu aqrarnaqala fashadu wana makum mina ashshahidin
3:82	faman tawala bada thalikafaula ika humu alfasiqun
3:83	afaghayra dini alahi yabghunawalahu aslama man fi asamawati wal arditawan wakarhan wa ilayhi yurjaun
3:84	qul amana bilahiwama unzila alayna wama unzila alaibrahima wa ismaila wa ishaqawayaquba wal asbati wama utiya musawaisa wanabiyuna min rabihim lanufariqu bayna ahadin minhum wanahnu lahumuslimun
3:85	waman yabtaghi ghayra al islamidinan falan yuqbala minhu wahuwa fi al akhirati minalkhasirin
3:86	kayfa yahdi alahu qawman kafarubada imanihim washahidu ana arasula haqunwajahumu albayinatu walahu layahdi alqawma aththalimin
3:87	ula ika jazauhum analayhim lanata alahi walmala ikati wanasiajmain
3:88	khalidina fiha layukhafafu anhumu alathabu wala hum yuntharun
3:89	ila alathina tabumin badi thalika waslahu fa ina alahaghafurun rahim
3:90	ina alathina kafaru bada imanihimthuma izdadu kufran lan tuqbala tawbatuhum waula ikahumu adalun
3:91	ina alathina kafaru wamatuwahum kufarun falan yuqbala min ahadihim milual ardi thahaban walawi iftada bihi ula ikalahum athabun alimun wama lahum min nasirin
3:92	lan tanalu albira hatatunfiqu mima tuhibuna wama tunfiqu minshay in fa ina alaha bihi alim
3:93	kulu atami kanahilan libani isra ila ila ma haramaisra ilu ala nafsihi min qabli an tunazala atawratuqul fa tu bitawrati fatluha inkuntum sadiqin
3:94	famani iftara ala alahialkathiba min badi thalika faula ika humuathalimun
3:95	qul sadaqa alahu fatabiaumilata ibrahima hanifan wama kanamina almushrikin
3:96	ina awala baytin wudia linasilalathi bibakata mubarakan wahudan lilalamin
3:97	fihi ayatun bayinatunmaqamu ibrahima waman dakhalahu kana aminanwalilahi ala anasi hijualbayti mani istata ilayhi sabilan waman kafarafa ina alaha ghaniyun ani alalamin
3:98	qul ya ahla alkitabi limatakfuruna bi ayati alahi walahushahidun ala ma tamalun
3:99	qul ya ahla alkitabi lima tasudunan sabili alahi man amana tabghunahaiwajan wantum shuhadau wama alahu bighafilinama tamalun
3:100	ya ayuha alathina amanuin tutiau fariqan mina alathina utu alkitabayarudukum bada imanikum kafirin
3:101	wakayfa takfuruna wantum tutlalaykum ayatu alahi wafikum rasuluhuwaman yatasim bilahi faqad hudiya ilasiratin mustaqim
3:102	ya ayuha alathina amanuitaqu alaha haqa tuqatihi walatamutuna ila wantum muslimun
3:103	watasimu bihablialahi jamian wala tafaraqu wathkuruniamata alahi alaykum ith kuntum adanfalafa bayna qulubikum fasbahtum biniamatihikhwanan wakuntum ala shafa hufratinmina anari fanqathakum minha kathalikayubayinu alahu lakum ayatihi lalakumtahtadun
3:104	waltakun minkum umatun yadauna ilalkhayri waya muruna bilmarufi wayanhawna anialmunkari waula ika humu almuflihun
3:105	wala takunu kalathinatafaraqu wakhtalafu min badi ma jahumualbayinatu waula ika lahum athabun athim
3:106	yawma tabyadu wujuhun wataswaduwujuhun fama alathina iswadat wujuhuhumakafartum bada imanikum fathuqu alathababima kuntum takfurun
3:107	wama alathina ibyadatwujuhuhum fafi rahmati alahi hum fiha khalidun
3:108	tilka ayatu alahinatluha alayka bilhaqi wama alahuyuridu thulman lilalamin
3:109	walilahi ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi wa ila alahiturjau al umur
3:110	kuntum khayra umatin ukhrijat linasita muruna bilmarufi watanhawna ani almunkariwatu minuna bilahi walaw amana ahlu alkitabilakana khayran lahum minhumu almu minuna waktharuhumualfasiqun
3:111	lan yadurukum ila athanwa in yuqatilukum yuwalukumu al adbara thuma layunsarun
3:112	duribat alayhimu aththilatuayna ma thuqifu ila bihablin mina alahiwahablin mina anasi wabau bighadabinmina alahi waduribat alayhimu almaskanatu thalikabi anahum kanu yakfuruna bi ayati alahiwayaqtuluna al anbiya bighayri haqin thalikabima asaw wakanu yatadun
3:113	laysu sawan min ahli alkitabiumatun qa imatun yatluna ayati alahiana alayli wahum yasjudun
3:114	yu minuna bilahi walyawmial akhiri waya muruna bilmarufi wayanhawnani almunkari wayusariauna fi alkhayrati waula ikamina asalihin
3:115	wama yafalu min khayrin falanyukfaruhu walahu alimun bilmutaqin
3:116	ina alathina kafaru lan tughniyanhum amwaluhum wala awladuhum mina alahishay an waula ika as habu anarihum fiha khalidun
3:117	mathalu ma yunfiquna fi hathihialhayati adunya kamathali rihinfiha sirun asabat hartha qawmin thalamuanfusahum fahlakat hu wama thalamahumu alahuwalakin anfusahum yathlimun
3:118	ya ayuha alathina amanula tatakhithu bitanatan min dunikum laya lunakum khabalan wadu ma anitum qad badatialbaghdau min afwahihim wama tukhfi suduruhumakbaru qad bayana lakumu al ayati inkuntum taqilun
3:119	ha antum ula i tuhibunahumwala yuhibunakum watu minuna bilkitabikulihi wa itha laqukum qalu amanawa itha khalaw adu alaykumu al anamilamina alghaythi qul mutu bighaythikumina alaha alimun bithati asudur
3:120	in tamsaskum hasanatun tasu humwa in tusibkum sayi atun yafrahu bihawa in tasbiru watataqu la yadurukumkayduhum shay an ina alaha bima yamaluna muhit
3:121	wa ith ghadawta min ahlika tubawi ualmu minina maqaida lilqitali walahusamiaun alim
3:122	ith hamat ta ifataniminkum an tafshala walahu waliyuhumawala alahi falyatawakali almu minun
3:123	walaqad nasarakumu alahubibadrin wantum athilatun fataqu alahalalakum tashkurun
3:124	ith taqulu lilmu minina alanyakfiyakum an yumidakum rabukum bithalathati alafinmina almala ikati munzalin
3:125	bala in tasbiru watataquwaya tukum min fawrihim hatha yumdidkum rabukumbikhamsati alafin mina almala ikatimusawimin
3:126	wama jalahu alahu ilabushra lakum walitatma ina qulubukum bihi wamanasru ila min aindi alahialazizi alhakim
3:127	liyaqta tarafan mina alathinakafaru aw yakbitahum fayanqalibu kha ibin
3:128	laysa laka mina al amri shay un aw yatubalayhim aw yuaththibahum fa inahum thalimun
3:129	walilahi ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi yaghfiru liman yashauwayuaththibu man yashau walahughafurun rahim
3:130	ya ayuha alathina amanula ta kulu ariba adafanmudafatan wataqu alaha lalakumtuflihun
3:131	wataqu anaralati uaidat lilkafirin
3:132	watiau alaha warasulalalakum turhamun
3:133	wasariau ila maghfiratinmin rabikum wajanatin arduha asamawatuwal ardu uaidat lilmutaqin
3:134	alathina yunfiquna fi asara iwadara i walkathiminalghaytha walafina ani anasiwalahu yuhibu almuhsinin
3:135	walathina ithafalu fahishatan aw thalamu anfusahum thakarualaha fastaghfaru lithunubihim wamanyaghfiru aththunuba ila alahuwalam yusiru ala ma falu wahumyalamun
3:136	ula ika jazauhum maghfiratunmin rabihim wajanatun tajri min tahtihal anharu khalidina fiha waniama ajrualamilin
3:137	qad khalat min qablikum sunanun fasirufi al ardi fanthuru kayfa kanaqibatu almukaththibin
3:138	hatha bayanun linasiwahudan wamawaithatun lilmutaqin
3:139	wala tahinu wala tahzanuwantumu al alawna in kuntum mu minin
3:140	in yamsaskum qarhun faqad masalqawma qarhun mithluhu watilka al ayamu nudawiluhabayna anasi waliyalama alahu alathinamanu wayatakhitha minkum shuhada walahula yuhibu aththalimin
3:141	waliyumahisa alahualathina amanu wayamhaqa alkafirin
3:142	am hasibtum an tadkhulu aljanatawalama yalami alahu alathina jahaduminkum wayalama asabirin
3:143	walaqad kuntum tamanawna almawta min qablian talqawhu faqad raytumuhu wantum tanthurun
3:144	wama muhamadun ilarasulun qad khalat min qablihi arusulu afa in mataw qutila inqalabtum ala aqabikum wamanyanqalib ala aqibayhi falan yadura alahashay an wasayajzi alahu ashshakirin
3:145	wama kana linafsin an tamutaila bi ithni alahi kitabanmu ajalan waman yurid thawaba adunyanu tihi minha waman yurid thawaba al akhiratinu tihi minha wasanajzi ashshakirin
3:146	wakayin min nabiyin qatalamahu ribiyuna kathirun fama wahanu lima asabahumfi sabili alahi wama daufu wamaistakanu walahu yuhibu asabirin
3:147	wama kana qawlahum ilan qalu rabana ighfir lana thunubanawa israfana fi amrina wathabit aqdamanawansurna ala alqawmi alkafirin
3:148	fatahumu alahu thawabadunya wahusna thawabi al akhiratiwalahu yuhibu almuhsinin
3:149	ya ayuha alathina amanuin tutiau alathina kafaru yarudukum alaqabikum fatanqalibu khasirin
3:150	bali alahu mawlakum wahuwakhayru anasirin
3:151	sanulqi fi qulubi alathinakafaru aruaba bima ashraku bilahima lam yunazil bihi sultanan wama wahumu anaruwabi sa mathwa aththalimin
3:152	walaqad sadaqakumu alahuwadahu ith tahusunahum bi ithnihi hataitha fashiltum watanazatum fi al amri wasaytumin badi ma arakum ma tuhibunaminkum man yuridu adunya waminkum man yuridual akhirata thuma sarafakum anhum liyabtaliyakumwalaqad afa ankum walahu thufadlin ala almu minin
3:153	ith tusaiduna walatalwuna ala ahadin warasuluyadaukum fi ukhrakum fathabakum ghamanbighamin likay la tahzanu ala mafatakum wala ma asabakum walahukhabirun bima tamalun
3:154	thuma anzala alaykum min badi alghamiamanatan nuasan yaghsha ta ifatan minkumwata ifatun qad ahamat hum anfusuhum yathununabilahi ghayra alhaqi thanaljahiliyati yaquluna hal lana mina al amri minshay in qul ina al amra kulahu lilahi yukhfuna fianfusihim ma la yubduna laka yaquluna law kanalana mina al amri shay un ma qutilna hahunaqul law kuntum fi buyutikum labaraza alathina kutibalayhimu alqatlu ila madajiaihim waliyabtaliyalahu ma fi sudurikum waliyumahisama fi qulubikum walahu alimun bithatiasudur
3:155	ina alathina tawalaw minkumyawma iltaqa aljamani inama istazalahumuashshaytanu bibadi ma kasabuwalaqad afa alahu anhum ina alahaghafurun halim
3:156	ya ayuha alathina amanula takunu kalathina kafaru waqaluli ikhwanihim itha darabu fi al ardiaw kanu ghuzan law kanu aindana mamatu wama qutilu liyajala alahu thalikahasratan fi qulubihim walahu yuhyiwayumitu walahu bima tamaluna basir
3:157	wala in qutiltum fi sabili alahiaw mutum lamaghfiratun mina alahi warahmatunkhayrun mima yajmaun
3:158	wala in mutum aw qutiltum la ilalahi tuhsharun
3:159	fabima rahmatin mina alahilinta lahum walaw kunta faththan ghalithalqalbi lanfadu min hawlika fafuanhum wastaghfir lahum washawirhum fi al amrifa itha azamta fatawakal ala alahina alaha yuhibu almutawakilin
3:160	in yansurkumu alahu falaghaliba lakum wa in yakhthulkum faman thalathi yansurukum min badihi wala alahifalyatawakali almu minun
3:161	wama kana linabiyin anyaghula waman yaghlul ya ti bima ghala yawma alqiyamatithuma tuwafa kulu nafsin ma kasabat wahum layuthlamun
3:162	afamani itaba ridwana alahikaman ba bisakhatin mina alahi wama wahujahanamu wabi sa almasir
3:163	hum darajatun ainda alahiwalahu basirun bima yamalun
3:164	laqad mana alahu alalmu minina ith batha fihim rasulan min anfusihimyatlu alayhim ayatihi wayuzakihimwayualimuhumu alkitaba walhikmata wa inkanu min qablu lafi dalalin mubin
3:165	awa lama asabatkum musibatunqad asabtum mithlayha qultum ana hathaqul huwa min aindi anfusikum ina alaha alakuli shay in qadir
3:166	wama asabakum yawma iltaqaljamani fabi ithni alahi waliyalamalmu minin
3:167	waliyalama alathina nafaquwaqila lahum talaw qatilu fi sabili alahiawi idfau qalu law nalamu qitalan latabanakumhum lilkufri yawma ithin aqrabu minhum lil imaniyaquluna bi afwahihim ma laysa fi qulubihim walahualamu bima yaktumun
3:168	alathina qalu li ikhwanihimwaqadu law atauna ma qutilu qul fadrauan anfusikumu almawta in kuntum sadiqin
3:169	wala tahsabana alathinaqutilu fi sabili alahi amwatan bal ahyaunainda rabihim yurzaqun
3:170	farihina bima atahumualahu min fadlihi wayastabshiruna bilathinalam yalhaqu bihim min khalfihim ala khawfunalayhim wala hum yahzanun
3:171	yastabshiruna biniamatin mina alahiwafadlin wana alaha la yudiauajra almu minin
3:172	alathina istajabu lilahiwarasuli min badi ma asabahumu alqarhulilathina ahsanu minhum wataqaw ajrunathim
3:173	alathina qala lahumu anasuina anasa qad jamau lakum fakhshawhumfazadahum imanan waqalu hasbunalahu waniama alwakil
3:174	fanqalabu biniamatin mina alahiwafadlin lam yamsas hum su un watabau ridwanalahi walahu thu fadlinathim
3:175	inama thalikumu ashshaytanuyukhawifu awliyahu fala takhafuhum wakhafunin kuntum mu minin
3:176	wala yahzunka alathinayusariauna fi alkufri inahum lan yaduru alahashay an yuridu alahu ala yajala lahum haththanfi al akhirati walahum athabun athim
3:177	ina alathina ishtarawu alkufrabil imani lan yaduru alahashay an walahum athabun alim
3:178	wala yahsabana alathinakafaru anama numli lahum khayrun li anfusihim inamanumli lahum liyazdadu ithman walahum athabunmuhin
3:179	ma kana alahu liyatharalmu minina ala ma antum alayhi hatayamiza alkhabitha mina atayibi wama kanalahu liyutliakum ala alghaybi walakinalaha yajtabi min rusulihi man yashau faminubilahi warusulihi wa in tu minu watataqufalakum ajrun athim
3:180	wala yahsabana alathinayabkhaluna bima atahumu alahu minfadlihi huwa khayran lahum bal huwa sharun lahum sayutawaqunama bakhilu bihi yawma alqiyamati walilahimirathu asamawati wal ardiwalahu bima tamaluna khabir
3:181	laqad samia alahu qawla alathinaqalu ina alaha faqirun wanahnu aghniyaunsanaktubu ma qalu waqatlahumu al anbiyabighayri haqin wanaqulu thuqu athabalhariq
3:182	thalika bima qadamataydikum wana alaha laysa bithalaminlilabid
3:183	alathina qalu ina alahahida ilayna ala nu mina lirasulin hataya tiyana biqurbanin ta kuluhu anaruqul qad jakum rusulun min qabli bilbayinatiwabilathi qultum falima qataltumuhum in kutum sadiqin
3:184	fa in kaththabuka faqad kuththibarusulun min qablika jau bilbayinati wazuburiwalkitabi almunir
3:185	kulu nafsin tha iqatu almawtiwa inama tuwafawna ujurakum yawma alqiyamatifaman zuhziha ani anari waudkhilaljanata faqad faza wama alhayatu adunyaila matau alghurur
3:186	latublawuna fi amwalikumwanfusikum walatasmauna mina alathina utu alkitabamin qablikum wamina alathina ashraku athankathiran wa in tasbiru watataqu fa ina thalikamin azmi al umur
3:187	wa ith akhatha alahumithaqa alathina utu alkitabalatubayinunahu linasi wala taktumunahufanabathuhu wara thuhurihim washtarawbihi thamanan qalilan fabi sa ma yashtarun
3:188	la tahsabana alathinayafrahuna bima ataw wayuhibuna an yuhmadubima lam yafalu fala tahsabanahum bimafazatinmina alathabi walahum athabun alim
3:189	walilahi mulku asamawatiwal ardi walahu ala kulishay in qadir
3:190	ina fi khalqi asamawatiwal ardi wakhtilafi alayli wanaharilayatin li uli al albab
3:191	alathina yathkuruna alahaqiyaman waquaudan wala junubihimwayatafakaruna fi khalqi asamawati wal ardirabana ma khalaqta hatha batilan subhanakafaqina athaba anar
3:192	rabana inaka man tudkhili anarafaqad akhzaytahu wama liththaliminamin ansar
3:193	rabana inana samianamunadiyan yunadi lil imani an aminubirabikum famana rabana faghfirlana thunubana wakafir anasayi atina watawafana ma al abrar
3:194	rabana watina mawadtana ala rusulika wala tukhzinayawma alqiyamati inaka la tukhlifu almiad
3:195	fastajaba lahum rabuhumani la udiau amala amilin minkum minthakarin aw untha badukum min badinfalathina hajaru waukhriju min diyarihimwauthu fi sabili waqatalu waqutilulaukafirana anhum sayi atihim walaudkhilanahum janatintajri min tahtiha al anharu thawabanmin aindi alahi walahu aindahu husnuaththawab
3:196	la yaghuranaka taqalubu alathinakafaru fi albilad
3:197	mataun qalilun thuma ma wahumjahanamu wabi sa almihad
3:198	lakini alathina itaqawrabahum lahum janatun tajri min tahtihal anharu khalidina fiha nuzulan minaindi alahi wama ainda alahi khayrunlil abrar
3:199	wa ina min ahli alkitabi lamanyu minu bilahi wama unzila ilaykum wamaunzila ilayhim khashiaina lilahi layashtaruna bi ayati alahi thamananqalilan ula ika lahum ajruhum ainda rabihim ina alahasariau alhisab
3:200	ya ayuha alathina amanuisbiru wasabiru warabitu wataqualaha lalakum tuflihun
4:1	ya ayuha anasuitaqu rabakumu alathi khalaqakum min nafsin wahidatinwakhalaqa minha zawjaha wabaththa minhumarijalan kathiran wanisan wataqu alahalathi tasaluna bihi wal arhamaina alaha kana alaykum raqib
4:2	watu alyatama amwalahumwala tatabadalu alkhabitha bitayibiwala ta kulu amwalahum ila amwalikuminahu kana huban kabira
4:3	wa in khiftum ala tuqsitu fialyatama fankihu ma tabalakum mina anisa i mathna wathulathawaruba fa in khiftum ala tadilu fawahidatanaw ma malakat aymanukum thalika adnala taulu
4:4	watu anisa saduqatihinanihlatan fa in tibna lakum an shay in minhunafsan fakuluhu hani an mari a
4:5	wala tu tu asufahamwalakumu alati jala alahu lakum qiyamanwarzuquhum fiha waksuhum waqulu lahumqawlan marufa
4:6	wabtalu alyatama hataitha balaghu anikaha fa in anastuminhum rushdan fadfau ilayhim amwalahum walata kuluha israfan wabidaran an yakbaruwaman kana ghaniyan falyastafif waman kanafaqiran falya kul bilmarufi fa itha dafatumilayhim amwalahum fashidu alayhim wakafa bilahihasiba
4:7	lirijali nasibun mimataraka alwalidani wal aqrabuna walinisa inasibun mima taraka alwalidani wal aqrabunamima qala minhu aw kathura nasiban mafruda
4:8	wa itha hadara alqismataulu alqurba walyatama walmasakinufarzuquhum minhu waqulu lahum qawlan marufa
4:9	walyakhsha alathina law taraku minkhalfihim thuriyatan diafan khafualayhim falyataqu alaha walyaqulu qawlan sadida
4:10	ina alathina ya kuluna amwalalyatama thulman inamaya kuluna fi butunihim naran wasayaslawnasaira
4:11	yusikumu alahu fi awladikumliththakari mithlu haththialunthayayni fa in kuna nisan fawqa ithnatayni falahunathulutha ma taraka wa in kanat wahidatanfalaha anisfu wali abawayhi likuli wahidinminhuma asudusu mima taraka in kanalahu waladun fa in lam yakun lahu waladun wawarithahu abawahufali umihi aththuluthu fa in kana lahu ikhwatunfali umihi asudusu min badi wasiyatin yusibiha aw daynin abaukum wabnaukum latadruna ayuhum aqrabu lakum nafan faridatan mina alahina alaha kana aliman hakima
4:12	walakum nisfu ma taraka azwajukumin lam yakun lahuna waladun fa in kana lahuna waladunfalakumu arubuau mima tarakna min badi wasiyatinyusina biha aw daynin walahuna arubuaumima taraktum in lam yakun lakum waladun fa in kanalakum waladun falahuna aththumunu mima taraktumin badi wasiyatin tusuna biha awdaynin wa in kana rajulun yurathu kalalatan awimratun walahu akhun aw ukhtun falikuli wahidin minhumasudusu fa in kanu akthara min thalikafahum shurakau fi aththuluthi min badi wasiyatinyusa biha aw daynin ghayra mudarin wasiyatanmina alahi walahu alimun halim
4:13	tilka hududu alahi waman yutiaialaha warasulahu yudkhilhu janatin tajri min tahtihal anharu khalidina fiha wathalikalfawzu alathim
4:14	waman yasi alahawarasulahu wayatada hududahu yudkhilhu narankhalidan fiha walahu athabun muhin
4:15	walati ya tina alfahishatamin nisa ikum fastashidu alayhina arbatanminkum fa in shahidu famsikuhuna fi albuyuti hatayatawafahuna almawtu aw yajala alahu lahunasabila
4:16	walathani ya tiyanihaminkum fathuhuma fa in taba waslahafaridu anhuma ina alaha kanatawaban rahima
4:17	inama atawbatu alalahi lilathina yamaluna asu abijahalatin thuma yatubuna min qaribin faula ikayatubu alahu alayhim wakana alahualiman hakima
4:18	walaysati atawbatu lilathinayamaluna asayi ati hata ithahadara ahadahumu almawtu qala initubtu al ana wala alathina yamutunawahum kufarun ula ika atadna lahum athabanalima
4:19	ya ayuha alathina amanula yahilu lakum an tarithu anisakarhan wala taduluhuna litathabubibadi ma ataytumuhuna ila anya tina bifahishatin mubayinatin washiruhunabilmarufi fa in karihtumuhuna fasa antakrahu shay an wayajala alahu fihi khayran kathira
4:20	wa in aradtumu istibdala zawjin makanazawjin wataytum ihdahuna qintaranfala ta khuthu minhu shay an ata khuthunahubuhtanan wa ithman mubina
4:21	wakayfa ta khuthunahu waqad afdabadukum ila badin wakhathnaminkum mithaqan ghalitha
4:22	wala tankihu ma nakahabaukum mina anisa i ila maqad salafa inahu kana fahishatan wamaqtan wasasabila
4:23	hurimat alaykum umahatukumwabanatukum wakhawatukum wamatukum wakhalatukumwabanatu al akhi wabanatu al ukhti waumahatukumualati ardanakum wakhawatukum mina aradatiwaumahatu nisa ikum waraba ibukumu alatifi hujurikum min nisa ikumu alatidakhaltum bihina fa in lam takunu dakhaltum bihina falajunaha alaykum wahala ilu abna ikumualathina min aslabikum wan tajmaubayna al ukhtayni ila ma qad salafa ina alahakana ghafuran rahima
4:24	walmuhsanatu mina anisa ila ma malakat aymanukum kitaba alahialaykum wauhila lakum ma wara thalikuman tabtaghu bi amwalikum muhsinina ghayra musafihinafama istamtatum bihi minhuna fatuhunaujurahuna faridatan wala junaha alaykumfima taradaytum bihi min badi alfaridatina alaha kana aliman hakima
4:25	waman lam yastatia minkum tawlanan yankiha almuhsanati almu minatifamin ma malakat aymanukum min fatayatikumualmu minati walahu alamu bi imanikumbadukum min badin fankihuhunabi ithni ahlihina watuhuna ujurahuna bilmarufimuhsanatin ghayra masafihatin walamutakhithati akhdanin fa itha uhsinafa in atayna bifahishatin falayhina nisfu mala almuhsanati mina alathabi thalikaliman khashiya alanata minkum wan tasbiru khayrunlakum walahu ghafurun rahim
4:26	yuridu alahu liyubayina lakumwayahdiyakum sunana alathina min qablikum wayatubalaykum walahu alimun hakim
4:27	walahu yuridu an yatubalaykum wayuridu alathina yatabiauna ashshahawatian tamilu maylan athim
4:28	yuridu alahu an yukhafifa ankumwakhuliqa al insanu daifa
4:29	ya ayuha alathina amanula ta kulu amwalakum baynakum bilbatilila an takuna tijaratan an taradinminkum wala taqtulu anfusakum ina alaha kanabikum rahima
4:30	waman yafal thalika audwananwathulman fasawfa nuslihi naran wakanathalika ala alahi yasira
4:31	in tajtanibu kaba ira matunhawna anhu nukafir ankum sayi atikum wanudkhilkumudkhalan karima
4:32	wala tatamanaw ma fadalalahu bihi badakum ala badinlirijali nasibun mima iktasabuwalinisa i nasibun mima iktasabnawas alu alaha min fadlihi ina alahakana bikuli shay in alima
4:33	walikulin jalna mawaliyamima taraka alwalidani wal aqrabunawalathina aqadat aymanukum fatuhumnasibahum ina alaha kana alakuli shay in shahida
4:34	arijalu qawamunala anisa i bima fadalalahu badahum ala badin wabimanfaqu min amwalihim fasalihatu qanitatunhafithatun lilghaybi bima hafithalahu walati takhafunanushuzahuna faithuhuna wahjuruhunafi almadajiai wadribuhuna fa in atanakumfala tabghu alayhina sabilan ina alaha kanaliyan kabira
4:35	wa in khiftum shiqaqa baynihimafabathu hakaman min ahlihi wahakaman minahliha in yurida islahan yuwafiqialahu baynahuma ina alaha kanaliman khabira
4:36	wabudu alaha walatushriku bihi shay an wabilwalidayni ihsananwabithi alqurba walyatama walmasakiniwaljari thi alqurba waljarialjunubi wasahibi biljanbi wabniasabili wama malakat aymanukum ina alahala yuhibu man kana mukhtalan fakhura
4:37	alathina yabkhaluna waya muruna anasabilbukhli wayaktumuna ma atahumualahu min fadlihi watadna lilkafirinathaban muhina
4:38	walathina yunfiquna amwalahumri a anasi wala yu minuna bilahiwala bilyawmi al akhiri waman yakuni ashshaytanulahu qarinan fasa qarina
4:39	wamatha alayhim law amanubilahi walyawmi al akhiriwanfaqu mima razaqahumu alahu wakana alahubihim alima
4:40	ina alaha la yathlimumithqala tharatin wa in taku hasanatan yudaifhawayu ti min ladunhu ajran athima
4:41	fakayfa itha ji na min kuliumatin bishahidin waji na bika ala haula ishahida
4:42	yawma ithin yawadu alathinakafaru wasawu arasula law tusawabihimu al ardu wala yaktumuna alaha haditha
4:43	ya ayuha alathina amanula taqrabu asalata wantum sukarahata talamu ma taquluna walajunuban ila abiri sabilin hatataghtasilu wa in kuntum marda aw ala safarin awja ahadun minkum mina algha iti aw lamastumuanisa falam tajidu man fatayamamu saidantayiban famsahu biwujuhikum waydikumina alaha kana afuwan ghafura
4:44	alam tara ila alathina utunasiban mina alkitabi yashtaruna adalalatawayuriduna an tadilu asabil
4:45	walahu alamu bi ada ikumwakafa bilahi waliyan wakafa bilahinasira
4:46	mina alathina hadu yuharifunalkalima an mawadiaihi wayaquluna samianawasayna wasma ghayra musmain warainalayan bi alsinatihim watanan fi adini walawanahum qalu samiana watana wasmawanthurna lakana khayranlahum waqwama walakin lanahumu alahubikufrihim fala yu minuna ila qalila
4:47	ya ayuha alathinautu alkitaba aminu bima nazalnamusadiqan lima makum min qabli an natmisawujuhan fanarudaha ala adbarihaw nalanahum kama lana as habasabti wakana amru alahi mafaula
4:48	ina alaha la yaghfiru anyushraka bihi wayaghfiru ma duna thalika limanyashau waman yushrik bilahi faqadi iftaraithman athima
4:49	alam tara ila alathinayuzakuna anfusahum bali alahu yuzaki man yashauwala yuthlamuna fatila
4:50	unthur kayfa yaftaruna alalahi alkathiba wakafa bihi ithman mubina
4:51	alam tara ila alathina utunasiban mina alkitabi yu minuna biljibtiwataghuti wayaquluna lilathinakafaru haula i ahda mina alathina amanusabila
4:52	ula ika alathina lanahumualahu waman yalani alahu falan tajida lahu nasira
4:53	am lahum nasibun mina almulki fa ithanla yu tuna anasa naqira
4:54	am yahsuduna anasala ma atahumu alahu min fadlihifaqad atayna ala ibrahima alkitabawalhikmata wataynahum mulkan athima
4:55	faminhum man amana bihi waminhum man sadanhu wakafa bijahanama saira
4:56	ina alathina kafaru bi ayatinasawfa nuslihim naran kulama nadijatjuluduhum badalnahum juludan ghayraha liyathuqualathaba ina alaha kana azizan hakima
4:57	walathina amanuwamilu asalihati sanudkhiluhum janatintajri min tahtiha al anharu khalidinafiha abadan lahum fiha azwajun mutaharatunwanudkhiluhum thilan thalila
4:58	ina alaha ya murukum an tu adual amanati ila ahliha wa itha hakamtumbayna anasi an tahkumu biladlina alaha niaima yaithukum bihina alaha kana samian basira
