4:59	ya ayuha alathina amanuatiau alaha watiau arasulawauli al amri minkum fa in tanazatum fi shay infaruduhu ila alahi warasuli in kuntumtu minuna bilahi walyawmi al akhirithalika khayrun wahsanu ta wila
4:60	alam tara ila alathinayazaumuna anahum amanu bima unzila ilayka wamaunzila min qablika yuriduna an yatahakamu ila ataghutiwaqad umiru an yakfuru bihi wayuridu ashshaytanuan yudilahum dalalan baida
4:61	wa itha qila lahum talawila ma anzala alahu wa ila arasulirayta almunafiqina yasuduna anka sududa
4:62	fakayfa itha asabat hum musibatunbima qadamat aydihim thuma jauka yahlifunabilahi in aradna ila ihsananwatawfiqa
4:63	ula ika alathina yalamualahu ma fi qulubihim farid anhumwaithum waqul lahum fi anfusihim qawlan baligha
4:64	wama arsalna min rasulin ilaliyuta bi ithni alahi walaw anahum iththalamu anfusahum jauka fastaghfarualaha wastaghfara lahumu arasululawajadu alaha tawaban rahima
4:65	fala warabika la yu minuna hatayuhakimuka fima shajara baynahum thuma layajidu fi anfusihim harajan mima qadaytawayusalimu taslima
4:66	walaw ana katabna alayhimani uqtulu anfusakum awi ukhruju min diyarikum mafaluhu ila qalilun minhum walaw anahum falu mayuathuna bihi lakana khayran lahumwashada tathbita
4:67	wa ithan lataynahumin laduna ajran athima
4:68	walahadaynahum siratanmustaqima
4:69	waman yutiai alaha warasulafaula ika ma alathina anama alahualayhim mina anabiyina wasidiqinawashshuhada i wasalihinawahasuna ula ika rafiqa
4:70	thalika alfadlu mina alahiwakafa bilahi alima
4:71	ya ayuha alathina amanukhuthu hithrakum fanfiru thubatinawi infiru jamia
4:72	wa ina minkum laman layubati anafa in asabatkum musibatun qala qad anamalahu alaya ith lam akun mahum shahida
4:73	wala in asabakum fadlun minalahi layaqulana kan lam takun baynakum wabaynahumawadatun ya laytani kuntu mahum fafuza fawzan athima
4:74	falyuqatil fi sabili alahialathina yashruna alhayata adunyabil akhirati waman yuqatil fi sabili alahifayuqtal aw yaghlib fasawfa nu tihi ajran athima
4:75	wama lakum la tuqatilunafi sabili alahi walmustadafina mina arijaliwanisa i walwildani alathinayaquluna rabana akhrijna min hathihialqaryati aththalimi ahluhawajal lana min ladunka waliyan wajalana min ladunka nasira
4:76	alathina amanu yuqatilunafi sabili alahi walathina kafaru yuqatilunafi sabili ataghuti faqatilu awliyashshaytani ina kayda ashshaytanikana daifa
4:77	alam tara ila alathina qilalahum kufu aydiyakum waqimu asalatawatu azakata falama kutibalayhimu alqitalu itha fariqun minhum yakhshawnanasa kakhashyati alahi aw ashadakhashyatan waqalu rabana lima katabta alaynalqitala lawla akhkhartana ila ajalinqaribin qul matau adunya qalilun wal akhiratukhayrun limani itaqa wala tuthlamunafatila
4:78	aynama takunu yudrikumu almawtuwalaw kuntum fi burujin mushayadatin wa in tusibhum hasanatunyaqulu hathihi min aindi alahi wa in tusibhumsayi atun yaqulu hathihi min aindika qul kulun minaindi alahi famali haula i alqawmila yakaduna yafqahuna haditha
4:79	ma asabaka min hasanatinfamina alahi wama asabaka min sayi atinfamin nafsika warsalnaka linasi rasulanwakafa bilahi shahidan
4:80	man yutiai arasula faqad atalaha waman tawala fama arsalnakalayhim hafitha
4:81	wayaquluna tatun fa ithabarazu min aindika bayata ta ifatun minhum ghayra alathitaqulu walahu yaktubu ma yubayitunafarid anhum watawakal ala alahiwakafa bilahi wakila
4:82	afala yatadabaruna alqur anawalaw kana min aindi ghayri alahi lawajadu fihikhtilafan kathira
4:83	wa itha jahum amrun minal amni awi alkhawfi athau bihi walaw raduhu ilarasuli wa ila uli al amri minhum lalimahualathina yastanbitunahu minhum walawla fadlualahi alaykum warahmatuhu latabatumuashshaytana ila qalila
4:84	faqatil fi sabili alahi latukalafu ila nafsaka waharidialmu minina asa alahu an yakufa ba sa alathinakafaru walahu ashadu ba san washadu tankila
4:85	man yashfa shafatan hasanatanyakun lahu nasibun minha waman yashfa shafatansayi atan yakun lahu kiflun minha wakana alahuala kuli shay in muqita
4:86	wa itha huyitum bitahiyatinfahayu bi ahsana minha aw ruduhaina alaha kana ala kuli shay in hasiba
4:87	alahu la ilaha ilahuwa layajmanakum ila yawmi alqiyamati larayba fihi waman asdaqu mina alahi haditha
4:88	fama lakum fi almunafiqinafi atayni walahu arkasahum bima kasabuaturiduna an tahdu man adala alahu waman yudlilialahu falan tajida lahu sabila
4:89	wadu law takfuruna kama kafarufatakununa sawan fala tatakhithu minhumawliya hata yuhajiru fi sabilialahi fa in tawalaw fakhuthuhum waqtuluhumhaythu wajadtumuhum wala tatakhithuminhum waliyan wala nasira
4:90	ila alathina yasilunaila qawmin baynakum wabaynahum mithaqun aw jaukumhasirat suduruhum an yuqatilukum awyuqatilu qawmahum walaw sha alahu lasalatahumalaykum falaqatalukum fa ini iatazalukum falam yuqatilukumwalqaw ilaykumu asalama fama jala alahulakum alayhim sabila
4:91	satajiduna akharina yuriduna anya manukum waya manu qawmahum kula ma rudu ilalfitnati urkisu fiha fa in lam yatazilukum wayulquilaykumu asalama wayakufu aydiyahum fakhuthuhumwaqtuluhum haythu thaqiftumuhum waula ikumjalna lakum alayhim sultanan mubina
4:92	wama kana limu minin anyaqtula mu minan ila khatan waman qatala mu minankhatan fatahriru raqabatin mu minatin wadiyatunmusalamatun ila ahlihi ila an yasadaqufa in kana min qawmin aduwin lakum wahuwa mu minun fatahriruraqabatin mu minatin wa in kana min qawmin baynakumwabaynahum mithaqun fadiyatun musalamatun ilahlihi watahriru raqabatin mu minatin faman lam yajid fasiyamushahrayni mutatabiayni tawbatan mina alahi wakanalahu aliman hakima
4:93	waman yaqtul mu minan mutamidan fajazauhujahanamu khalidan fiha waghadiba alahualayhi walanahu wada lahu athaban athima
4:94	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha darabtum fi sabili alahifatabayanu wala taqulu liman alqa ilaykumu asalamalasta mu minan tabtaghuna arada alhayatiadunya fainda alahi maghanimukathiratun kathalika kuntum min qablu famana alahualaykum fatabayanu ina alaha kana bimatamaluna khabira
4:95	la yastawi alqaiduna minalmu minina ghayru uli adarari walmujahidunafi sabili alahi bi amwalihim wanfusihim fadalalahu almujahidina bi amwalihimwanfusihim ala alqaidina darajatan wakulanwada alahu alhusna wafadala alahualmujahidina ala alqaidina ajran athima
4:96	darajatin minhu wamaghfiratan warahmatanwakana alahu ghafuran rahima
4:97	ina alathina tawafahumualmala ikatu thalimi anfusihim qalufima kuntum qalu kuna mustadafina fial ardi qalu alam takun ardu alahiwasiatan fatuhajiru fiha faula ikama wahum jahanamu wasat masira
4:98	ila almustadafina mina arijaliwanisa i walwildani layastatiauna hilatan wala yahtadunasabila
4:99	faula ika asa alahuan yafuwa anhum wakana alahu afuwan ghafura
4:100	waman yuhajir fi sabili alahiyajid fi al ardi muraghaman kathiran wasatanwaman yakhruj min baytihi muhajiran ila alahiwarasulihi thuma yudrik hu almawtu faqad waqa ajruhu alalahi wakana alahu ghafuran rahima
4:101	wa itha darabtum fi al ardifalaysa alaykum junahun an taqsuru mina asalatin khiftum an yaftinakumu alathina kafaru ina alkafirinakanu lakum aduwan mubina
4:102	wa itha kunta fihim faqamta lahumuasalata faltaqum ta ifatun minhumaka walya khuthu aslihatahum fa ithasajadu falyakunu min wara ikum walta ti ta ifatunukhra lam yusalu falyusalu makawalya khuthu hithrahum waslihatahumwada alathina kafaru law taghfuluna an aslihatikumwamtiatikum fayamiluna alaykum maylatan wahidatanwala junaha alaykum in kana bikum athanmin matarin aw kuntum marda an tadau aslihatakumwakhuthu hithrakum ina alahada lilkafirina athaban muhina
4:103	fa itha qadaytumu asalatafathkuru alaha qiyaman waquaudanwala junubikum fa itha itma nantumfaqimu asalata ina asalatakanat ala almu minina kitaban mawquta
4:104	wala tahinu fi ibtigha ialqawmi in takunu ta lamuna fa inahum ya lamuna kamata lamuna watarjuna mina alahi ma layarjuna wakana alahu aliman hakima
4:105	ina anzalna ilayka alkitababilhaqi litahkuma bayna anasibima araka alahu wala takun lilkha ininakhasima
4:106	wastaghfiri alaha ina alahakana ghafuran rahima
4:107	wala tujadil ani alathinayakhtanuna anfusahum ina alaha la yuhibuman kana khawanan athima
4:108	yastakhfuna mina anasi walayastakhfuna mina alahi wahuwa mahum ithyubayituna ma la yarda mina alqawli wakanalahu bima yamaluna muhita
4:109	hantum haula i jadaltumanhum fi alhayati adunya famanyujadilu alaha anhum yawma alqiyamati aman yakunu alayhim wakila
4:110	waman yamal su an aw yathlimnafsahu thuma yastaghfiri alaha yajidi alahaghafuran rahima
4:111	waman yaksib ithman fa inamayaksibuhu ala nafsihi wakana alahualiman hakima
4:112	waman yaksib khati atan aw ithmanthuma yarmi bihi bari an faqadi ihtamala buhtananwa ithman mubina
4:113	walawla fadlu alahialayka warahmatuhu lahamat ta ifatun minhum anyudiluka wama yudiluna ilanfusahum wama yadurunaka min shay in wanzalalahu alayka alkitaba walhikmatawalamaka ma lam takun talamu wakana fadlualahi alayka athima
4:114	la khayra fi kathirin min najwahumila man amara bisadaqatin aw marufin aw islahinbayna anasi waman yafal thalika ibtighamardati alahi fasawfa nu tihi ajran athima
4:115	waman yushaqiqi arasulamin badi ma tabayana lahu alhuda wayatabiaghayra sabili almu minina nuwalihi ma tawalawanuslihi jahanama wasat masira
4:116	ina alaha la yaghfiru anyushraka bihi wayaghfiru ma duna thalika limanyashau waman yushrik bilahi faqad daladalalan baida
4:117	in yadauna min dunihi ila inathanwa in yadauna ila shaytanan marida
4:118	lanahu alahu waqalalatakhithana min aibadika nasibanmafruda
4:119	walaudilanahum walaumaniyanahumwalamuranahum falayubatikuna athana al anamiwalamuranahum falayughayiruna khalqa alahiwaman yatakhithi ashshaytana waliyan minduni alahi faqad khasira khusranan mubina
4:120	yaiduhum wayumanihim wamayaiduhumu ashshaytanu ila ghurura
4:121	ula ika ma wahum jahanamuwala yajiduna anha mahisa
4:122	walathina amanuwamilu asalihati sanudkhiluhum janatintajri min tahtiha al anharu khalidinafiha abadan wada alahi haqan waman asdaqumina alahi qila
4:123	laysa bi amaniyikum wala amaniyiahli alkitabi man yamal su an yujza bihi walayajid lahu min duni alahi waliyan wala nasira
4:124	waman yamal mina asalihatimin thakarin aw untha wahuwa mu minun faula ikayadkhuluna aljanata wala yuthlamunanaqira
4:125	waman ahsanu dinan miman aslamawajhahu lilahi wahuwa muhsinun watabamilata ibrahima hanifan watakhathalahu ibrahima khalila
4:126	walilahi ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi wakana alahubikuli shay in muhita
4:127	wayastaftunaka fi anisa iquli alahu yuftikum fihina wama yutlalaykum fi alkitabi fi yatama anisa ialati la tu tunahuna ma kutiba lahunawatarghabuna an tankihuhuna walmustadafinamina alwildani wan taqumu lilyatama bilqistiwama tafalu min khayrin fa ina alaha kanabihi alima
4:128	wa ini imratun khafat min balihanushuzan aw iaradan fala junaha alayhiman yusliha baynahuma sulhan wasulhukhayrun wauhdirati al anfusu ashshuhawa in tuhsinu watataqu fa ina alaha kanabima tamaluna khabira
4:129	walan tastatiau an tadilubayna anisa i walaw harastum falatamilu kula almayli fatatharuha kalmualaqatiwa in tuslihu watataqu fa ina alaha kanaghafuran rahima
4:130	wa in yatafaraqa yughni alahukulan min satihi wakana alahu wasian hakima
4:131	walilahi ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi walaqad wasaynalathina utu alkitaba min qablikum wa iyakumani itaqu alaha wa in takfuru fa ina lilahi mafi asamawati wama fi al ardiwakana alahu ghaniyan hamida
4:132	walilahi ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi wakafa bilahiwakila
4:133	in yasha yuthibkum ayuha anasuwaya ti bi akharina wakana alahu alathalika qadira
4:134	man kana yuridu thawaba adunyafainda alahi thawabu adunya wal akhiratiwakana alahu samian basira
4:135	ya ayuha alathina amanukunu qawamina bilqisti shuhadalilahi walaw ala anfusikum awi alwalidayniwal aqrabina in yakun ghaniyan aw faqiran falahuawla bihima fala tatabiau alhawan tadilu wa in talwu aw tuaridu fa ina alahakana bima tamaluna khabira
4:136	ya ayuha alathina amanuaminu bilahi warasulihi walkitabialathi nazala ala rasulihi walkitabialathi anzala min qablu waman yakfur bilahiwamala ikatihi wakutubihi warusulihi walyawmi al akhirifaqad dala dalalan baida
4:137	ina alathina amanu thumakafaru thuma amanu thuma kafaru thuma izdadukufran lam yakuni alahu liyaghfira lahum walaliyahdiyahum sabila
4:138	bashshiri almunafiqina bi analahum athaban alima
4:139	alathina yatakhithuna alkafirinawliya min duni almu minina ayabtaghuna aindahumualaizata fa ina alaizata lilahi jamia
4:140	waqad nazala alaykum fi alkitabian itha samiatum ayati alahiyukfaru biha wayustahzau biha fala taqaudumahum hata yakhudu fi hadithinghayrihi inakum ithan mithluhum ina alaha jamiaualmunafiqina walkafirina fi jahanamajamia
4:141	alathina yatarabasunabikum fa in kana lakum fathun mina alahi qalualam nakun makum wa in kana lilkafirina nasibunqalu alam nastahwith alaykum wanamnakumina almu minina falahu yahkumu baynakumyawma alqiyamati walan yajala alahu lilkafirinala almu minina sabila
4:142	ina almunafiqina yukhadiaunalaha wahuwa khadiauhum wa itha qamuila asalati qamu kusalayurauna anasa wala yathkurunalaha ila qalila
4:143	muthabthabina bayna thalikala ila haula i wala ilahaula i waman yudlili alahu falantajida lahu sabila
4:144	ya ayuha alathina amanula tatakhithu alkafirina awliyamin duni almu minina aturiduna an tajalu lilahialaykum sultanan mubina
4:145	ina almunafiqina fi adarkial asfali mina anari walan tajida lahum nasira
4:146	ila alathina tabuwaslahu watasamu bilahiwakhlasu dinahum lilahi faula ika malmu minina wasawfa yu ti alahu almu minina ajran athima
4:147	ma yafalu alahu biathabikumin shakartum wamantum wakana alahu shakiranalima
4:148	la yuhibu alahualjahra bisu i mina alqawli ila man thulimawakana alahu samian alima
4:149	in tubdu khayran aw tukhfuhu aw tafuan su in fa ina alaha kana afuwan qadira
4:150	ina alathina yakfuruna bilahiwarusulihi wayuriduna an yufariqu bayna alahiwarusulihi wayaquluna nu minu bibadin wanakfuru bibadinwayuriduna an yatakhithu bayna thalika sabila
4:151	ula ika humu alkafiruna haqanwatadna lilkafirina athaban muhina
4:152	walathina amanu bilahiwarusulihi walam yufariqu bayna ahadin minhum ula ikasawfa yu tihim ujurahum wakana alahu ghafuranrahima
4:153	yas aluka ahlu alkitabi an tunazilalayhim kitaban mina asama i faqadsalu musa akbara min thalika faqalu arinalaha jahratan fakhathat humu asaiqatubithulmihim thuma itakhathu alaijla minbadi ma jat humu albayinatu fafawnan thalika watayna musa sultananmubina
4:154	warafana fawqahumu aturabimithaqihim waqulna lahumu udkhulu albabasujadan waqulna lahum la tadu fi asabtiwakhathna minhum mithaqan ghalitha
4:155	fabima naqdihim mithaqahumwakufrihim bi ayati alahi waqatlihimual anbiya bighayri haqin waqawlihim qulubunaghulfun bal taba alahu alayhabikufrihim fala yu minuna ila qalila
4:156	wabikufrihim waqawlihim alamaryama buhtanan athima
4:157	waqawlihim ina qatalnalmasiha aisa ibna maryama rasula alahiwama qataluhu wama salabuhu walakinshubiha lahum wa ina alathina ikhtalafu fihi lafishakin minhu ma lahum bihi min ailmin ila itibaththani wama qataluhu yaqina
4:158	bal rafahu alahu ilayhi wakanalahu azizan hakima
4:159	wa in min ahli alkitabi ilalayu minana bihi qabla mawtihi wayawma alqiyamati yakunualayhim shahida
4:160	fabithulmin mina alathinahadu haramna alayhim tayibatinuhilat lahum wabisadihim an sabili alahikathira
4:161	wakhthihimu aribawaqad nuhu anhu waklihim amwala anasibilbatili watadna lilkafirinaminhum athaban alima
4:162	lakini arasikhunafi alailmi minhum walmu minuna yu minuna bimaunzila ilayka wama unzila min qablika walmuqiminasalata walmu tuna azakatawalmu minuna bilahi walyawmi al akhiriula ika sanu tihim ajran athima
4:163	ina awhayna ilaykakama awhayna ila nuhin wanabiyinamin badihi wawhayna ila ibrahimawa ismaila wa ishaqa wayaquba wal asbatiwaisa wayuba wayunusa waharuna wasulaymanawatayna dawuda zabura
4:164	warusulan qad qasasnahumalayka min qablu warusulan lam naqsushum alaykawakalama alahu musa taklima
4:165	rusulan mubashshirina wamunthirinali ala yakuna linasi ala alahihujatun bada arusuli wakana alahuazizan hakima
4:166	lakini alahu yashadu bimanzala ilayka anzalahu biailmihi walmala ikatuyashaduna wakafa bilahi shahida
4:167	ina alathina kafaru wasaduan sabili alahi qad dalu dalalanbaida
4:168	ina alathina kafaru wathalamulam yakuni alahu liyaghfira lahum walaliyahdiyahum tariqa
4:169	ila tariqa jahanama khalidinafiha abadan wakana thalika ala alahiyasira
4:170	ya ayuha anasuqad jakumu arasulu bilhaqi minrabikum faminu khayran lakum wa in takfuru fa inalilahi ma fi asamawati wal ardiwakana alahu aliman hakima
4:171	ya ahla alkitabi lataghlu fi dinikum wala taqulu ala alahila alhaqa inama almasihu aisaibnu maryama rasulu alahi wakalimatuhu alqahaila maryama waruhun minhu faminu bilahiwarusulihi wala taqulu thalathatun intahukhayran lakum inama alahu ilahun wahidunsubhanahu an yakuna lahu waladun lahu ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi wakafa bilahiwakila
4:172	lan yastankifa almasihu an yakunabdan lilahi wala almala ikatualmuqarabuna waman yastankif an aibadatihiwayastakbir fasayahshuruhum ilayhi jamia
4:173	fama alathina amanuwamilu asalihati fayuwafihimujurahum wayaziduhum min fadlihi wama alathinaistankafu wastakbaru fayuaththibuhum athabanaliman wala yajiduna lahum min duni alahiwaliyan wala nasira
4:174	ya ayuha anasuqad jakum burhanun min rabikum wanzalnailaykum nuran mubina
4:175	fama alathina amanubilahi watasamu bihifasayudkhiluhum fi rahmatin minhu wafadlinwayahdihim ilayhi siratan mustaqima
4:176	yastaftunaka quli alahu yuftikumfi alkalalati ini imrun halaka laysa lahu waladun walahukhtun falaha nisfu ma taraka wahuwayarithuha in lam yakun laha waladun fa in kanataithnatayni falahuma aththuluthani mimataraka wa in kanu ikhwatan rijalan wanisanfaliththakari mithlu haththialunthayayni yubayinu alahu lakum an tadilu walahubikuli shay in alim
5:1	ya ayuha alathina amanuawfu bilauqudi uhilat lakum bahimatu al anamila ma yutla alaykum ghayra muhiliasaydi wantum hurumun ina alahayahkumu ma yurid
5:2	ya ayuha alathina amanula tuhilu sha ira alahi walashshahra alharama wala alhadya walalqala ida wala amina albayta alharamayabtaghuna fadlan min rabihim waridwananwa itha halaltum fastadu walayajrimanakum shananu qawmin an sadukum anialmasjidi alharami an tatadu watawanuala albiri wataqwa wala tawanuala al ithmi walaudwani wataqualaha ina alaha shadidu alaiqab
5:3	hurimat alaykumu almaytatu wadamuwalahmu alkhinziri wama uhila lighayri alahibihi walmunkhaniqatu walmawquthatu walmutaradiyatuwanatihatu wama akala asabuauila ma thakaytum wama thubihala anusubi wan tastaqsimu bil azlamithalikum fisqun alyawma ya isa alathina kafarumin dinikum fala takhshawhum wakhshawni alyawmakmaltu lakum dinakum watmamtu alaykum niamati waraditulakumu al islama dinan famani idtura fi makhmasatinghayra mutajanifin li ithmin fa ina alahaghafurun rahim
5:4	yas alunaka matha uhila lahumqul uhila lakumu atayibatu wamalamtum mina aljawarihi mukalibinatualimunahuna mima alamakumu alahufakulu mima amsakna alaykum wathkuruisma alahi alayhi wataqu alaha inalaha sariau alhisab
5:5	alyawma uhila lakumu atayibatuwatamu alathina utu alkitaba hilunlakum watamukum hilun lahum walmuhsanatumina almu minati walmuhsanatu minalathina utu alkitaba min qablikum itha ataytumuhunaujurahuna muhsinina ghayra musafihinawala mutakhithi akhdanin waman yakfur bil imanifaqad habita amaluhu wahuwa fi al akhiratimina alkhasirin
5:6	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha qumtum ila asalati faghsiluwujuhakum waydiyakum ila almarafiqi wamsahubirusikum warjulakum ila alkabayni wa in kuntumjunuban fataharu wa in kuntum marda awala safarin aw ja ahadun minkum mina algha itiaw lamastumu anisa falam tajidu manfatayamamu saidan tayiban famsahubiwujuhikum waydikum minhu ma yuridu alahuliyajala alaykum min harajin walakin yuriduliyutahirakum waliyutima niamatahu alaykumlalakum tashkurun
5:7	wathkuru niamata alahialaykum wamithaqahu alathi wathaqakumbihi ith qultum samiana watana wataqualaha ina alaha alimun bithati asudur
5:8	ya ayuha alathina amanukunu qawamina lilahi shuhada bilqistiwala yajrimanakum shananu qawmin ala alatadilu iadilu huwa aqrabu litaqwa wataqualaha ina alaha khabirun bima tamalun
5:9	wada alahu alathina amanuwamilu asalihati lahum maghfiratunwajrun athim
5:10	walathina kafaru wakaththabubi ayatina ula ika as habualjahim
5:11	ya ayuha alathina amanuthkuru niamata alahi alaykum ith hamaqawmun an yabsutu ilaykum aydiyahum fakafa aydiyahumankum wataqu alaha wala alahifalyatawakali almu minun
5:12	walaqad akhatha alahu mithaqabani isra ila wabathna minhumu ithnay asharanaqiban waqala alahu ini makum la inaqamtumu asalata wataytumu azakatawamantum birusuli wazartumuhum waqradtumualaha qardan hasanan laukafirana ankumsayi atikum walaudkhilanakum janatin tajri mintahtiha al anharu faman kafara bada thalikaminkum faqad dala sawa asabil
5:13	fabima naqdihim mithaqahumlanahum wajalna qulubahum qasiyatanyuharifuna alkalima an mawadiaihi wanasu haththanmima thukiru bihi wala tazalu tataliauala kha inatin minhum ila qalilan minhumfafu anhum wasfah ina alahayuhibu almuhsinin
5:14	wamina alathina qalu inanasara akhathna mithaqahumfanasu haththan mima thukirubihi faghrayna baynahumu aladawata walbaghdaila yawmi alqiyamati wasawfa yunabi uhumu alahubima kanu yasnaun
5:15	ya ahla alkitabi qad jakumrasuluna yubayinu lakum kathiran mima kuntumtukhfuna mina alkitabi wayafu an kathirin qad jakumina alahi nurun wakitabun mubin
5:16	yahdi bihi alahu mani itaba ridwanahusubula asalami wayukhrijuhum mina aththulumatila anuri bi ithnihi wayahdihim ilasiratin mustaqim
5:17	laqad kafara alathina qaluina alaha huwa almasihu ibnu maryama qul famanyamliku mina alahi shay an in arada an yuhlikalmasiha ibna maryama waumahu waman fi al ardijamian walilahi mulku asamawatiwal ardi wama baynahuma yakhluqu mayashau walahu ala kuli shay inqadir
5:18	waqalati alyahudu wanasaranahnu abnau alahi wahibauhuqul falima yuaththibukum bithunubikum bal antumbasharun miman khalaqa yaghfiru liman yashau wayuaththibuman yashau walilahi mulku asamawatiwal ardi wama baynahuma wa ilayhialmasir
5:19	ya ahla alkitabi qad jakumrasuluna yubayinu lakum ala fatratin mina arusulian taqulu ma jana min bashirin walanathirin faqad jakum bashirun wanathirunwalahu ala kuli shay in qadir
5:20	wa ith qala musaliqawmihi ya qawmi uthkuru niamata alahialaykum ith jala fikum anbiya wajalakumulukan watakum ma lam yu ti ahadanmina alalamin
5:21	ya qawmi udkhulu al ardalmuqadasata alati kataba alahu lakum walatartadu ala adbarikum fatanqalibu khasirin
5:22	qalu ya musa inafiha qawman jabarina wa ina lanadkhulaha hata yakhruju minhafa in yakhruju minha fa ina dakhilun
5:23	qala rajulani mina alathinayakhafuna anama alahu alayhimaudkhulu alayhimu albaba fa itha dakhaltumuhufa inakum ghalibuna wala alahifatawakalu in kuntum mu minin
5:24	qalu ya musa inalan nadkhulaha abadan ma damu fihafa ithab anta warabuka faqatila inahahuna qaidun
5:25	qala rabi ini la amliku ilanafsi wakhi fafruq baynana wabayna alqawmi alfasiqin
5:26	qala fa inaha muharamatunalayhim arbaina sanatan yatihuna fi al ardi falata sa ala alqawmi alfasiqin
5:27	watlu alayhim naba ibnay adamabilhaqi ith qaraba qurbananfatuqubila min ahadihima walam yutaqabal mina al akhariqala laqtulanaka qala inama yataqabalualahu mina almutaqin
5:28	la in basata ilaya yadakalitaqtulani ma ana bibasitin yadiyailayka li aqtulaka ini akhafu alaha raba alalamin
5:29	ini uridu an tabu a bi ithmi wa ithmikafatakuna min as habi anari wathalikajazau aththalimin
5:30	fatawat lahu nafsuhu qatlakhihi faqatalahu fasbaha mina alkhasirin
5:31	fabatha alahu ghuraban yabhathufi al ardi liyuriyahu kayfa yuwari saw atakhihi qala ya waylata ajaztu an akunamithla hatha alghurabi fauwariya saw atakhi fasbaha mina anadimin
5:32	min ajli thalika katabna alabani isra ila anahu man qatala nafsan bighayri nafsinaw fasadin fi al ardi fakanama qatala anasajamian waman ahyaha fakanama ahyanasa jamian walaqad jat hum rusulunabilbayinati thuma ina kathiran minhum bada thalikafi al ardi lamusrifun
5:33	inama jazau alathinayuharibuna alaha warasulahu wayasawna fial ardi fasadan an yuqatalu aw yusalabuaw tuqata aydihim warjuluhum min khilafin awyunfaw mina al ardi thalika lahum khizyun fi adunyawalahum fi al akhirati athabun athim
5:34	ila alathina tabumin qabli an taqdiru alayhim falamu ana alahaghafurun rahim
5:35	ya ayuha alathina amanuitaqu alaha wabtaghu ilayhi alwasilata wajahidufi sabilihi lalakum tuflihun
5:36	ina alathina kafaru law analahum ma fi al ardi jamian wamithlahu mahuliyaftadu bihi min athabi yawmi alqiyamati matuqubila minhum walahum athabun alim
5:37	yuriduna an yakhruju mina anariwama hum bikharijina minha walahum athabunmuqim
5:38	wasariqu wasariqatufaqtau aydiyahuma jazan bimakasaba nakalan mina alahi walahuazizun hakim
5:39	faman taba min badi thulmihiwaslaha fa ina alaha yatubu alayhina alaha ghafurun rahim
5:40	alam talam ana alaha lahu mulku asamawatiwal ardi yuaththibu man yashauwayaghfiru liman yashau walahu alakuli shay in qadir
5:41	ya ayuha arasulu layahzunka alathina yusariauna fi alkufrimina alathina qalu amana bi afwahihimwalam tu min qulubuhum wamina alathina hadusamauna lilkathibi samauna liqawmin akharinalam ya tuka yuharifuna alkalima min badi mawadiaihiyaquluna in utitum hatha fakhuthuhu wa in lamtu tawhu fahtharu waman yuridi alahufitnatahu falan tamlika lahu mina alahi shay an ula ikalathina lam yuridi alahu an yutahiraqulubahum lahum fi adunya khizyun walahum fial akhirati athabun athim
5:42	samauna lilkathibi akalunalisuhti fa in jauka fahkumbaynahum aw arid anhum wa in tuarid anhumfalan yaduruka shay an wa in hakamta fahkumbaynahum bilqisti ina alaha yuhibualmuqsitin
5:43	wakayfa yuhakimunaka waindahumu atawratufiha hukmu alahi thuma yatawalawna minbadi thalika wama ula ika bilmu minin
5:44	ina anzalna atawratafiha hudan wanurun yahkumu biha anabiyunalathina aslamu lilathina hadu warabaniyunawal ahbaru bima istuhfithumin kitabi alahi wakanu alayhi shuhadafala takhshawu anasa wakhshawniwala tashtaru bi ayati thamanan qalilanwaman lam yahkum bima anzala alahu faula ikahumu alkafirun
5:45	wakatabna alayhim fiha ananafsa binafsi walayna bilayniwal anfa bil anfi waluthuna biluthuniwasina bisini waljuruha qisasunfaman tasadaqa bihi fahuwa kafaratun lahu wamanlam yahkum bima anzala alahu faula ikahumu aththalimun
5:46	waqafayna ala atharihimbiaisa ibni maryama musadiqan lima baynayadayhi mina atawrati wataynahual injila fihi hudan wanurun wamusadiqan limabayna yadayhi mina atawrati wahudan wamawaithatanlilmutaqin
5:47	walyahkum ahlu al injili bimanzala alahu fihi waman lam yahkum bimanzala alahu faula ika humu alfasiqun
5:48	wanzalna ilayka alkitaba bilhaqimusadiqan lima bayna yadayhi mina alkitabiwamuhayminan alayhi fahkum baynahum bimanzala alahu wala tatabia ahwahum amajaka mina alhaqi likulin jalna minkumshiratan waminhajan walaw sha alahulajalakum umatan wahidatan walakin liyabluwakumfima atakum fastabiqu alkhayratila alahi marjiaukum jamian fayunabi ukum bimakuntum fihi takhtalifun
5:49	wani uhkum baynahum bimanzala alahu wala tatabia ahwahum wahtharhuman yaftinuka an badi ma anzala alahuilayka fa in tawalaw falam anama yuridu alahuan yusibahum bibadi thunubihim wa inakathiran mina anasi lafasiqun
5:50	afahukma aljahiliyatiyabghuna waman ahsanu mina alahu hukmanliqawmin yuqinun
5:51	ya ayuha alathina amanula tatakhithu alyahuda wanasarawliya baduhum awliyau badinwaman yatawalahum minkum fa inahu minhum ina alaha layahdi alqawma aththalimin
5:52	fatara alathina fiqulubihim maradun yusariauna fihim yaqulunanakhsha an tusibana da iratun fasalahu an ya tiya bilfathi aw amrin minaindihi fayusbihu ala ma asarufi anfusihim nadimin
5:53	wayaqulu alathina amanu ahaula ialathina aqsamu bilahi jahda aymanihiminahum lamakum habitat amaluhum fasbahukhasirin
5:54	ya ayuha alathina amanuman yartada minkum an dinihi fasawfa ya ti alahubiqawmin yuhibuhum wayuhibunahu athilatinala almu minina aizatin ala alkafirinayujahiduna fi sabili alahi wala yakhafunalawmata la imin thalika fadlu alahiyu tihi man yashau walahu wasiaunalim
5:55	inama waliyukumu alahuwarasuluhu walathina amanu alathinayuqimuna asalata wayu tuna azakatawahum rakiaun
5:56	waman yatawala alaha warasulahu walathinamanu fa ina hizba alahi humu alghalibun
5:57	ya ayuha alathina amanula tatakhithu alathina itakhathudinakum huzuwan walaiban mina alathina utu alkitabamin qablikum walkufara awliya wataqualaha in kuntum mu minin
5:58	wa itha nadaytum ila asalatitakhathuha huzuwan walaiban thalikabi anahum qawmun la yaqilun
5:59	qul ya ahla alkitabi haltanqimuna mina ila an amana bilahiwama unzila ilayna wama unzila min qabluwana aktharakum fasiqun
5:60	qul hal unabi ukum bisharin min thalikamathubatan ainda alahi man lanahu alahuwaghadiba alayhi wajala minhumu alqiradata walkhanazirawabada ataghuti ula ika sharun makananwadalu an sawa i asabil
5:61	wa itha jaukum qalu amanawaqad dakhalu bilkufri wahum qad kharaju bihi walahualamu bima kanu yaktumun
5:62	watara kathiran minhum yusariaunafi al ithmi walaudwani waklihimu asuhtalabi sa ma kanu yamalun
5:63	lawla yanhahumu arabaniyunawal ahbaru an qawlihimu al ithmawaklihimu asuhta labi sa ma kanuyasnaun
5:64	waqalati alyahudu yadu alahimaghlulatun ghulat aydihim waluainu bima qalubal yadahu mabsutatani yunfiqu kayfa yashauwalayazidana kathiran minhum ma unzila ilayka minrabika tughyanan wakufran walqaynabaynahumu aladawata walbaghda ilayawmi alqiyamati kulama awqadu naran lilharbiatfaha alahu wayasawna fi al ardifasadan walahu la yuhibualmufsidin
5:65	walaw ana ahla alkitabi amanuwataqaw lakafarna anhum sayi atihimwaladkhalnahum janati anaim
5:66	walaw anahum aqamu atawratawal injila wama unzila ilayhim min rabihimlakalu min fawqihim wamin tahti arjulihim minhum umatunmuqtasidatun wakathirun minhum sa mayamalun
5:67	ya ayuha arasulubaligh ma unzila ilayka min rabika wa in lam tafal famabalaghta risalatahu walahu yasimukamina anasi ina alaha la yahdialqawma alkafirin
5:68	qul ya ahla alkitabi lastumala shay in hata tuqimu atawratawal injila wama unzila ilaykum min rabikumwalayazidana kathiran minhum ma unzila ilayka minrabika tughyanan wakufran fala ta sa alalqawmi alkafirin
5:69	ina alathina amanu walathinahadu wasabi una wanasaraman amana bilahi walyawmi al akhiriwamila salihan fala khawfun alayhim walahum yahzanun
5:70	laqad akhathna mithaqabani isra ila warsalna ilayhim rusulan kulamajahum rasulun bima la tahwanfusuhum fariqan kaththabu wafariqan yaqtulun
5:71	wahasibu ala takunafitnatun famu wasamu thuma taba alahualayhim thuma amu wasamu kathirun minhum walahubasirun bima yamalun
5:72	laqad kafara alathina qaluina alaha huwa almasihu ibnu maryama waqalalmasihu ya bani isra ila uabudu alaharabi warabakum inahu man yushrik bilahi faqadharama alahu alayhi aljanata wama wahuanaru wama liththaliminamin ansar
5:73	laqad kafara alathina qaluina alaha thalithu thalathatin wamamin ilahin ila ilahun wahidun wa inlam yantahu ama yaquluna layamasana alathinakafaru minhum athabun alim
5:74	afala yatubuna ila alahiwayastaghfirunahu walahu ghafurun rahim
5:75	ma almasihu ibnu maryama ilarasulun qad khalat min qablihi arusulu waumuhu sidiqatunkana ya kulani atamaunthur kayfa nubayinu lahumu al ayatithuma unthur ana yu fakun
5:76	qul atabuduna min duni alahi mala yamliku lakum daran wala nafan walahuhuwa asamiau alalim
5:77	qul ya ahla alkitabi lataghlu fi dinikum ghayra alhaqi wala tatabiauahwa qawmin qad dalu min qablu wadalukathiran wadalu an sawa i asabil
5:78	luaina alathina kafaru min banisra ila ala lisani dawudawaisa ibni maryama thalika bima asawakanu yatadun
5:79	kanu la yatanahawnan munkarin faluhu labi sa ma kanuyafalun
5:80	tara kathiran minhum yatawalawnalathina kafaru labi sa ma qadamat lahumanfusuhum an sakhita alahu alayhim wafi alathabihum khalidun
5:81	walaw kanu yu minuna bilahiwanabiyi wama unzila ilayhi ma itakhathuhumawliya walakina kathiran minhum fasiqun
5:82	latajidana ashada anasiadawatan lilathina amanu alyahuda walathinashraku walatajidana aqrabahum mawadatan lilathina amanualathina qalu ina nasara thalikabi ana minhum qisisina waruhbanan wanahum layastakbirun
5:83	wa itha samiau ma unzila ilarasuli tara ayunahum tafidu mina adamaimima arafu mina alhaqi yaquluna rabanamana faktubna ma ashshahidin
5:84	wama lana la nu minu bilahiwama jana mina alhaqi wanatmauan yudkhilana rabuna ma alqawmi asalihin
5:85	fathabahumu alahu bimaqalu janatin tajri min tahtihal anharu khalidina fiha wathalikajazau almuhsinin
5:86	walathina kafaru wakaththabubi ayatina ula ika as habualjahim
5:87	ya ayuha alathina amanula tuharimu tayibati ma ahalalahu lakum wala tatadu ina alaha layuhibu almuatadin
5:88	wakulu mima razaqakumu alahuhalalan tayiban wataqu alahalathi antum bihi mu minun
5:89	la yu akhithukumu alahubilaghwi fi aymanikum walakin yu akhithukumbima aqadtumu al aymana fakafaratuhu itamuasharati masakina min awsati ma tutaimunahlikum aw kiswatuhum aw tahriru raqabatin faman lamyajid fasiyamu thalathati ayamin thalikakafaratu aymanikum itha halaftum wahfathuaymanakum kathalika yubayinu alahu lakum ayatihilalakum tashkurun
5:90	ya ayuha alathina amanuinama alkhamru walmaysiru wal ansabuwal azlamu rijsun min amali ashshaytanifajtanibuhu lalakum tuflihun
5:91	inama yuridu ashshaytanuan yuqia baynakumu aladawata walbaghdafi alkhamri walmaysiri wayasudakum an thikrialahi wani asalati fahal antumuntahun
5:92	watiau alaha watiauarasula wahtharu fa in tawalaytum falamuanama ala rasulina albalaghualmubin
5:93	laysa ala alathina amanuwamilu asalihati junahun fimataimu itha ma itaqaw wamanuwamilu asalihati thuma itaqaw wamanuthuma itaqaw wahsanu walahu yuhibualmuhsinin
5:94	ya ayuha alathina amanulayabluwanakumu alahu bishay in mina asayditanaluhu aydikum warimahukum liyalama alahuman yakhafuhu bilghaybi famani iatadabada thalika falahu athabun alim
5:95	ya ayuha alathina amanula taqtulu asayda wantum hurumunwaman qatalahu minkum mutamidan fajazaun mithlu maqatala mina anami yahkumu bihi thawadlin minkum hadyan baligha alkabati aw kafaratuntamu masakina aw adlu thalika siyamanliyathuqa wabala amrihi afa alahuama salafa waman ada fayantaqimu alahuminhu walahu azizun thu intiqam
5:96	uhila lakum saydu albahriwatamuhu matan lakum walisayaratiwahurima alaykum saydu albari ma dumtum hurumanwataqu alaha alathi ilayhi tuhsharun
5:97	jala alahu alkabata albayta alharamaqiyaman linasi washshahra alharamawalhadya walqala ida thalikalitalamu ana alaha yalamu ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi wana alaha bikulishay in alim
5:98	ialamu ana alaha shadidu alaiqabiwana alaha ghafurun rahim
5:99	ma ala arasuli ilalbalaghu walahu yalamu matubduna wama taktumun
5:100	qul la yastawi alkhabithu watayibuwalaw ajabaka kathratu alkhabithi fataqu alahaya uli al albabi lalakum tuflihun
5:101	ya ayuha alathina amanula tas alu an ashya in tubda lakum tasu kumwa in tas alu anha hina yunazalu alqur anutubda lakum afa alahu anha walahughafurun halim
5:102	qad salaha qawmun min qablikumthuma asbahu biha kafirin
5:103	ma jala alahu min bahiratinwala sa ibatin wala wasilatin walahamin walakina alathina kafaruyaftaruna ala alahi alkathibawaktharuhum la yaqilun
5:104	wa itha qila lahum talawila ma anzala alahu wa ila arasuliqalu hasbuna ma wajadnalayhi abana awa law kana abauhumla yalamuna shay an wala yahtadun
5:105	ya ayuha alathina amanualaykum anfusakum la yadurukum man dalaitha ihtadaytum ila alahi marjiaukumjamian fayunabi ukum bima kuntum tamalun
5:106	ya ayuha alathina amanushahadatu baynikum itha hadara ahadakumualmawtu hina alwasiyati ithnani thawadlin minkum aw akharani min ghayrikum in antum darabtumfi al ardi fasabatkum musibatu almawti tahbisunahumamin badi asalati fayuqsimani bilahini irtabtum la nashtari bihi thamanan walaw kana thaqurba wala naktumu shahadata alahina ithan lamina al athimin
5:107	fa in authira ala anahumaistahaqa ithman fakharani yaqumanimaqamahuma mina alathina istahaqalayhimu al awlayani fayuqsimani bilahilashahadatuna ahaqu min shahadatihimawama iatadayna ina ithan lamina aththalimin
5:108	thalika adna an ya tu bishshahadatiala wajhiha aw yakhafu an turada aymanunbada aymanihim wataqu alaha wasmauwalahu la yahdi alqawma alfasiqin
5:109	yawma yajmau alahu arusulafayaqulu matha ujibtum qalu la ailma lanainaka anta alamu alghuyub
5:110	ith qala alahu yaisa ibna maryama uthkur niamati alaykawala walidatika ith ayadtuka biruhialqudusi tukalimu anasa fi almahdi wakahlanwa ith alamtuka alkitaba walhikmatawatawrata wal injila wa ithtakhluqu mina atini kahay ati atayribi ithni fatanfukhu fiha fatakunu tayranbi ithni watubri u al akmaha wal abrasabi ithni wa ith tukhriju almawta bi ithniwa ith kafaftu bani isra ila anka ithji tahum bilbayinati faqala alathinakafaru minhum in hatha ila sihrun mubin
5:111	wa ith awhaytu ila alhawariyinan aminu bi wabirasuli qalu amanawashad bi anana muslimun
5:112	ith qala alhawariyunaya aisa ibna maryama hal yastatiaurabuka an yunazila alayna ma idatan mina asama iqala itaqu alaha in kuntum mu minin
5:113	qalu nuridu an na kula minhawatatma ina qulubuna wanalama an qad sadaqtanawanakuna alayha mina ashshahidin
5:114	qala aisa ibnu maryama alahumarabana anzil alayna ma idatan mina asama itakunu lana aidan li awalina wakhirinawayatan minka warzuqna wanta khayru araziqin
5:115	qala alahu ini munaziluhalaykum faman yakfur badu minkum fa ini uaththibuhuathaban la uaththibuhu ahadanmina alalamin
5:116	wa ith qala alahu yaisa ibna maryama anta qulta linasitakhithuni waumiya ilahayni min duni alahiqala subhanaka ma yakunu li an aqula malaysa li bihaqin in kuntu qultuhu faqad alimtahutalamu ma fi nafsi wala alamu ma finafsika inaka anta alamu alghuyub
5:117	ma qultu lahum ila mamartani bihi ani uabudu alaha rabi warabakumwakuntu alayhim shahidan ma dumtu fihim falamatawafaytani kunta anta araqiba alayhim wanta alakuli shay in shahid
5:118	in tuaththibhum fa inahum aibadukawa in taghfir lahum fa inaka anta alazizu alhakim
5:119	qala alahu hathayawmu yanfau asadiqina sidquhum lahumjanatun tajri min tahtiha al anharukhalidina fiha abadan radiya alahuanhum waradu anhu thalika alfawzu alathim
5:120	lilahi mulku asamawatiwal ardi wama fihina wahuwa alakuli shay in qadir
6:1	alhamdu lilahi alathikhalaqa asamawati wal ardawajala aththulumati wanurathuma alathina kafaru birabihim yadilun
6:2	huwa alathi khalaqakum min tininthuma qada ajalan wajalun musaman aindahu thuma antumtamtarun
6:3	wahuwa alahu fi asamawatiwafi al ardi yalamu sirakum wajahrakum wayalamu mataksibun
6:4	wama ta tihim min ayatin min ayatirabihim ila kanu anha muaridin
6:5	faqad kaththabu bilhaqilama jahum fasawfa ya tihim anbau makanu bihi yastahzi un
6:6	alam yaraw kam ahlakna min qablihimin qarnin makanahum fi al ardi ma lamnumakin lakum warsalna asama alayhimidraran wajalna al anhara tajri min tahtihimfahlaknahum bithunubihim wansha na minbadihim qarnan akharin
6:7	walaw nazalna alayka kitabanfi qirtasin falamasuhu bi aydihim laqala alathinakafaru in hatha ila sihrun mubin
6:8	waqalu lawla unzila alayhimalakun walaw anzalna malakan laqudiya al amruthuma la yuntharun
6:9	walaw jalnahu malakan lajalnahurajulan walalabasna alayhim ma yalbisun
6:10	walaqadi istuhzi a birusulin min qablika fahaqabilathina sakhiru minhum ma kanubihi yastahzi un
6:11	qul siru fi al ardi thuma unthurukayfa kana aqibatu almukaththibin
6:12	qul liman ma fi asamawatiwal ardi qul lilahi kataba alanafsihi arahmata layajmanakum ilayawmi alqiyamati la rayba fihi alathinakhasiru anfusahum fahum la yu minun
6:13	walahu ma sakana fi alayli wanahariwahuwa asamiau alalim
6:14	qul aghayra alahi atakhithuwaliyan fatiri asamawati wal ardiwahuwa yutaimu wala yutamu qul iniumirtu an akuna awala man aslama wala takunana minalmushrikin
6:15	qul ini akhafu in asayturabi athaba yawmin athim
6:16	man yusraf anhu yawma ithinfaqad rahimahu wathalika alfawzu almubin
6:17	wa in yamsaska alahu bidurinfala kashifa lahu ila huwa wa in yamsaskabikhayrin fahuwa ala kuli shay in qadir
6:18	wahuwa alqahiru fawqa aibadihiwahuwa alhakimu alkhabir
6:19	qul ayu shay in akbaru shahadatanquli alahu shahidun bayni wabaynakum wauhiyailaya hatha alqur anu li unthirakum bihiwaman balagha a inakum latashaduna ana ma alahi alihatanukhra qul la ashadu qul inama huwa ilahunwahidun wa inani bari un mima tushrikun
6:20	alathina ataynahumualkitaba yarifunahu kama yarifuna abnahumalathina khasiru anfusahum fahum la yu minun
6:21	waman athlamu mimani iftarala alahi kathiban aw kaththababi ayatihi inahu la yuflihu aththalimun
6:22	wayawma nahshuruhum jamian thumanaqulu lilathina ashraku ayna shurakaukumu alathinakuntum tazaumun
6:23	thuma lam takun fitnatuhum ila an qaluwalahi rabina ma kunamushrikin
6:24	unthur kayfa kathabuala anfusihim wadala anhum ma kanuyaftarun
6:25	waminhum man yastamiau ilayka wajalnala qulubihim akinatan an yafqahuhu wafi athanihimwaqran wa in yaraw kula ayatin la yu minu bihahata itha jauka yujadilunakayaqulu alathina kafaru in hatha ila asatirual awalin
6:26	wahum yanhawna anhu wayan awna anhuwa in yuhlikuna ila anfusahum wama yashaurun
6:27	walaw tara ith wuqifu alanari faqalu ya laytananuradu wala nukaththiba bi ayatirabina wanakuna mina almu minin
6:28	bal bada lahum ma kanuyukhfuna min qablu walaw rudu ladu limanuhu anhu wa inahum lakathibun
6:29	waqalu in hiya ila hayatunadunya wama nahnu bimabauthin
6:30	walaw tara ith wuqifu alarabihim qala alaysa hatha bilhaqiqalu bala warabina qala fathuqualathaba bima kuntum takfurun
6:31	qad khasira alathina kaththabubiliqa i alahi hata itha jat humuasatu baghtatan qalu ya hasratanala ma faratna fiha wahumyahmiluna awzarahum ala thuhurihimala sa ma yazirun
6:32	wama alhayatu adunyaila laibun walahwun waladaru al akhiratikhayrun lilathina yataquna afala taqilun
6:33	qad nalamu inahu layahzunuka alathiyaquluna fa inahum la yukaththibunaka walakinaththalimina bi ayatialahi yajhadun
6:34	walaqad kuththibat rusulun minqablika fasabaru ala ma kuththibuwauthu hata atahum nasrunawala mubadila likalimati alahi walaqad jakamin naba i almursalin
6:35	wa in kana kabura alayka iaraduhumfa ini istatata an tabtaghiya nafaqan fi al ardiaw sulaman fi asama i fata tiyahum bi ayatinwalaw sha alahu lajamahum ala alhudafala takunana mina aljahilin
6:36	inama yastajibu alathinayasmauna walmawta yabathuhumu alahuthuma ilayhi yurjaun
6:37	waqalu lawla nuzila alayhiayatun min rabihi qul ina alaha qadirunala an yunazila ayatan walakinaktharahum la yalamun
6:38	wama min dabatin fi al ardiwala ta irin yatiru bijanahayhi ilaumamun amthalukum ma faratna fialkitabi min shay in thuma ila rabihim yuhsharun
6:39	walathina kaththabubi ayatina sumun wabukmun fi aththulumatiman yasha i alahu yudlilhu waman yasha yajalhuala siratin mustaqim
6:40	qul araytakum in atakum athabualahi aw atatkumu asatu aghayra alahitadauna in kuntum sadiqin
6:41	bal iyahu tadauna fayakshifu matadauna ilayhi in sha watansawna ma tushrikun
6:42	walaqad arsalna ila umamin minqablika fakhathnahum bilba sa i wadara ilalahum yatadaraun
6:43	falawla ith jahumba suna tadarau walakin qasat qulubuhumwazayana lahumu ashshaytanu ma kanuyamalun
6:44	falama nasu ma thukirubihi fatahna alayhim abwaba kuli shay in hataitha farihu bima utu akhathnahumbaghtatan fa itha hum mublisun
6:45	faqutia dabiru alqawmi alathinathalamu walhamdu lilahirabi alalamin
6:46	qul araytum in akhatha alahusamakum wabsarakum wakhatama ala qulubikuman ilahun ghayru alahi ya tikum bihi unthurkayfa nusarifu al ayati thuma hum yasdifun
6:47	qul araytakum in atakum athabualahi baghtatan aw jahratan hal yuhlaku ilalqawmu aththalimun
6:48	wama nursilu almursalina ilamubashshirina wamunthirina faman amana waslahafala khawfun alayhim wala hum yahzanun
6:49	walathina kaththabubi ayatina yamasuhumu alathabu bimakanu yafsuqun
6:50	qul la aqulu lakum aindi khaza inualahi wala alamu alghayba wala aqululakum ini malakun in atabiau ila ma yuhailaya qul hal yastawi al ama walbasiruafala tatafakarun
6:51	wanthir bihi alathina yakhafunan yuhsharu ila rabihim laysa lahum min dunihiwaliyun wala shafiaun lalahum yataqun
6:52	wala tatrudi alathinayadauna rabahum bilghadati walashiyiyuriduna wajhahu ma alayka min hisabihimin shay in wama min hisabika alayhim minshay in fatatrudahum fatakuna mina aththalimin
6:53	wakathalika fatana badahumbibadin liyaqulu ahaula i mana alahualayhim min baynina alaysa alahu bi alama bishshakirin
6:54	wa itha jaka alathinayu minuna bi ayatina faqul salamunalaykum kataba rabukum ala nafsihi arahmatanahu man amila minkum su an bijahalatin thuma tabamin badihi waslaha fanahu ghafurun rahim
6:55	wakathalika nufasilu al ayatiwalitastabina sabilu almujrimin
6:56	qul ini nuhitu an abuda alathinatadauna min duni alahi qul la atabiau ahwakumqad dalaltu ithan wama ana minalmuhtadin
6:57	qul ini ala bayinatin min rabiwakaththabtum bihi ma aindi matastajiluna bihi ini alhukmu ila lilahiyaqusu alhaqa wahuwa khayru alfasilin
6:58	qul law ana aindi matastajiluna bihi laqudiya al amru bayni wabaynakum walahualamu biththalimin
6:59	waindahu mafatihu alghaybi layalamuha ila huwa wayalamu ma fialbari walbahri wama tasqutu minwaraqatin ila yalamuha wala habatinfi thulumati al ardi wala ratbinwala yabisin ila fi kitabin mubin
6:60	wahuwa alathi yatawafakumbilayli wayalamu ma jarahtum binaharithuma yabathukum fihi liyuqda ajalun musaman thumailayhi marjiaukum thuma yunabi ukum bima kuntumtamalun
6:61	wahuwa alqahiru fawqa aibadihiwayursilu alaykum hafathatan hataitha ja ahadakumu almawtu tawafat hurusuluna wahum la yufaritun
6:62	thuma rudu ila alahi mawlahumualhaqi ala lahu alhukmu wahuwa asrau alhasibin
6:63	qul man yunajikum min thulumatialbari walbahri tadaunahu tadaruanwakhufyatan la in anjana min hathihilanakunana mina ashshakirin
6:64	quli alahu yunajikum minhawamin kuli karbin thuma antum tushrikun
6:65	qul huwa alqadiru ala anyabatha alaykum athaban min fawqikum aw min tahtiarjulikum aw yalbisakum shiyan wayuthiqa badakumba sa badin unthur kayfa nusarifual ayati lalahum yafqahun
6:66	wakaththaba bihi qawmuka wahuwa alhaququl lastu alaykum biwakil
6:67	likuli naba in mustaqarun wasawfatalamun
6:68	wa itha rayta alathinayakhuduna fi ayatina faridanhum hata yakhudu fi hadithinghayrihi wa ima yunsiyanaka ashshaytanufala taqaud bada aththikra malqawmi aththalimin
6:69	wama ala alathinayataquna min hisabihim min shay in walakinthikra lalahum yataqun
6:70	wathari alathina itakhathudinahum laiban walahwan wagharat humu alhayatuadunya wathakir bihi an tubsala nafsunbima kasabat laysa laha min duni alahiwaliyun wala shafiaun wa in tadil kula adlin layu khath minha ula ika alathinaubsilu bima kasabu lahum sharabun min hamiminwathabun alimun bima kanu yakfurun
6:71	qul anadau min duni alahi mala yanfauna wala yadurunawanuradu ala aqabina bada ithadana alahu kalathi istahwat huashshayatinu fi al ardi hayranalahu as habun yadaunahu ila alhudai tina qul ina huda alahi huwa alhudawaumirna linuslima lirabi alalamin
6:72	wan aqimu asalatawataquhu wahuwa alathi ilayhi tuhsharun
6:73	wahuwa alathi khalaqa asamawatiwal arda bilhaqi wayawma yaqulukun fayakunu qawluhu alhaqu walahu almulku yawmayunfakhu fi asuri alimu alghaybi washshahadatiwahuwa alhakimu alkhabir
6:74	wa ith qala ibrahimuli abihi azara atatakhithu asnaman alihatanini araka waqawmaka fi dalalin mubin
6:75	wakathalika nuri ibrahimamalakuta asamawati wal ardiwaliyakuna mina almuqinin
6:76	falama jana alayhi alaylu rakawkaban qala hatha rabi falama afala qalala uhibu al afilin
6:77	falama ra alqamara bazighanqala hatha rabi falama afala qalala in lam yahdini rabi lakunana mina alqawmi adalin
6:78	falama ra ashshamsabazighatan qala hatha rabi hathakbaru falama afalat qala ya qawmi inibari un mima tushrikun
6:79	ini wajahtu wajhiya lilathi fatarasamawati wal arda hanifanwama ana mina almushrikin
6:80	wahajahu qawmuhu qala atuhajunifi alahi waqad hadani wala akhafu matushrikuna bihi ila an yasha rabi shay anwasia rabi kula shay in ailman afala tatathakarun
6:81	wakayfa akhafu ma ashraktumwala takhafuna anakum ashraktum bilahima lam yunazil bihi alaykum sultanan fayualfariqayni ahaqu bil amni in kuntum talamun
6:82	alathina amanu walamyalbisu imanahum bithulmin ula ikalahumu al amnu wahum muhtadun
6:83	watilka hujatuna ataynahaibrahima ala qawmihi narfau darajatinman nashau ina rabaka hakimun alim
6:84	wawahabna lahu ishaqawayaquba kulan hadayna wanuhan hadaynamin qablu wamin thuriyatihi dawuda wasulaymanawayuba wayusufa wamusa waharuna wakathalikanajzi almuhsinin
6:85	wazakariya wayahyawaisa walyasa kulun mina asalihin
6:86	wa ismaila walyasawayunusa walutan wakulan fadalna alalalamin
6:87	wamin aba ihim wathuriyatihimwa ikhwanihim wajtabaynahum wahadaynahumila siratin mustaqim
6:88	thalika huda alahiyahdi bihi man yashau min aibadihi walaw ashrakulahabita anhum ma kanu yamalun
6:89	ula ika alathina ataynahumualkitaba walhukma wanubuwatafa in yakfur biha haula i faqad wakalnabiha qawman laysu biha bikafirin
6:90	ula ika alathina hadalahu fabihudahumu iqtadih qul la as alukumalayhi ajran in huwa ila thikra lilalamin
6:91	wama qadaru alaha haqaqadrihi ith qalu ma anzala alahuala basharin min shay in qul man anzala alkitabalathi ja bihi musa nuran wahudan linasitajalunahu qaratisa tubdunaha watukhfunakathiran waulimtum ma lam talamu antum wala abaukumquli alahu thuma tharhum fi khawdihimyalabun
6:92	wahatha kitabun anzalnahumubarakun musadiqu alathi bayna yadayhiwalitunthira uma alqura waman hawlahawalathina yu minuna bil akhiratiyu minuna bihi wahum ala salatihim yuhafithun
6:93	waman athlamu mimani iftarala alahi kathiban aw qala uhiyailaya walam yuha ilayhi shay un waman qalasaunzilu mithla ma anzala alahu walaw taraithi aththalimuna fi ghamaratialmawti walmala ikatu basituaydihim akhriju anfusakumu alyawma tujzawna athabalhuni bima kuntum taquluna ala alahighayra alhaqi wakuntum an ayatihitastakbirun
6:94	walaqad ji tumuna furadakama khalaqnakum awala maratin wataraktum makhawalnakum wara thuhurikum wamanara makum shufakumu alathinazamtum anahum fikum shurakau laqad taqatabaynakum wadala ankum ma kuntum tazaumun
6:95	ina alaha faliqu alhabiwanawa yukhriju alhaya mina almayitiwamukhriju almayiti mina alhayi thalikumu alahufana tu fakun
6:96	faliqu al isbahiwajala alayla sakanan washshamsa walqamara husbananthalika taqdiru alazizi alalim
6:97	wahuwa alathi jala lakumu anujumalitahtadu biha fi thulumati albariwalbahri qad fasalna al ayatiliqawmin yalamun
6:98	wahuwa alathi anshakum min nafsinwahidatin famustaqarun wamustawdaun qad fasalnal ayati liqawmin yafqahun
6:99	wahuwa alathi anzala mina asama iman fakhrajna bihi nabata kuli shay infakhrajna minhu khadiran nukhriju minhu habanmutarakiban wamina anakhli min talaihaqinwanun daniyatun wajanatin min anabinwazaytuna warumana mushtabihanwaghayra mutashabihin unthuru ilathamarihi itha athmara wayanaihi ina fi thalikumlayatin liqawmin yu minun
6:100	wajalu lilahi shurakaljina wakhalaqahum wakharaqu lahu banina wabanatinbighayri ailmin subhanahu watala amayasifun
6:101	badiau asamawatiwal ardi ana yakunu lahu waladun walamtakun lahu sahibatun wakhalaqa kula shay in wahuwabikuli shay in alim
6:102	thalikumu alahu rabukum lailaha ila huwa khaliqu kuli shay in fabuduhuwahuwa ala kuli shay in wakil
6:103	la tudrikuhu al absaru wahuwayudriku al absara wahuwa alatifu alkhabir
6:104	qad jakum basa iru minrabikum faman absara falinafsihi waman amiya falayhawama ana alaykum bihafith
6:105	wakathalika nusarifu al ayatiwaliyaqulu darasta walinubayinahu liqawmin yalamun
6:106	itabia ma uhiya ilayka minrabika la ilaha ila huwa waridani almushrikin
6:107	walaw sha alahu mashraku wama jalnaka alayhim hafithanwama anta alayhim biwakil
6:108	wala tasubu alathinayadauna min duni alahi fayasubu alahadwan bighayri ailmin kathalika zayana likuliumatin amalahum thuma ila rabihim marjiauhumfayunabi uhum bima kanu yamalun
6:109	waqsamu bilahi jahda aymanihimla in jat hum ayatun layu minuna biha qulinama al ayatu ainda alahi wamayushairukum anaha itha jat layu minun
6:110	wanuqalibu af idatahum wabsarahumkama lam yu minu bihi awala maratin wanatharuhumfi tughyanihim yamahun
6:111	walaw anana nazalnailayhimu almala ikata wakalamahumu almawta wahasharnalayhim kula shay in qubulan ma kanu liyu minuila an yasha alahu walakinaktharahum yajhalun
6:112	wakathalika jalna likulinabiyin aduwan shayatina al insi waljini yuhibaduhum ila badin zukhrufa alqawlighururan walaw sha rabuka ma faluhu fatharhumwama yaftarun
6:113	walitasgha ilayhi af idatualathina la yu minuna bil akhiratiwaliyardawhu waliyaqtarifu ma hum muqtarifun
6:114	afaghayra alahi abtaghi hakamanwahuwa alathi anzala ilaykumu alkitaba mufasalanwalathina ataynahumu alkitabayalamuna anahu munazalun min rabika bilhaqifala takunana mina almumtarin
6:115	watamat kalimatu rabika sidqanwadlan la mubadila likalimatihi wahuwa asamiaualalim
6:116	wa in tutia akthara man fi al ardiyudiluka an sabili alahi in yatabiauna ilaththana wa in hum ila yakhrusun
6:117	ina rabaka huwa alamu man yadiluan sabilihi wahuwa alamu bilmuhtadin
6:118	fakulu mima thukira ismualahi alayhi in kuntum bi ayatihimu minin
6:119	wama lakum ala ta kulu mimathukira ismu alahi alayhi waqad fasalalakum ma harama alaykum ila ma idturirtumilayhi wa ina kathiran layudiluna bi ahwa ihimbighayri ailmin ina rabaka huwa alamu bilmuatadin
6:120	watharu thahiral ithmi wabatinahu ina alathina yaksibunal ithma sayujzawna bima kanu yaqtarifun
6:121	wala ta kulu mima lam yuthkarismu alahi alayhi wa inahu lafisqun wa ina ashshayatinalayuhuna ila awliya ihim liyujadilukumwa in atatumuhum inakum lamushrikun
6:122	awa man kana maytan fahyaynahuwajalna lahu nuran yamshi bihi fi anasikaman mathaluhu fi aththulumatilaysa bikharijin minha kathalika zuyinalilkafirina ma kanu yamalun
6:123	wakathalika jalna fikuli qaryatin akabira mujrimiha liyamkuru fihawama yamkuruna ila bi anfusihim wamayashaurun
6:124	wa itha jat hum ayatunqalu lan nu mina hata nu ta mithlama utiya rusulu alahi alahu alamu haythuyajalu risalatahu sayusibu alathinajramu sagharun ainda alahi wathabunshadidun bima kanu yamkurun
6:125	faman yuridi alahu an yahdiyahuyashrah sadrahu lil islami waman yurid an yudilahuyajal sadrahu dayiqan harajan kanamayasadu fi asama i kathalikayajalu alahu arijsa ala alathinala yu minun
6:126	wahatha siratu rabikamustaqiman qad fasalna al ayatiliqawmin yaththakarun
6:127	lahum daru asalamiainda rabihim wahuwa waliyuhum bima kanuyamalun
6:128	wayawma yahshuruhum jamian yamashara aljini qadi istakthartum mina al insi waqalawliyauhum mina al insi rabana istamta badunabibadin wabalaghna ajalana alathiajalta lana qala anaru mathwakumkhalidina fiha ila ma shalahu ina rabaka hakimun alim
6:129	wakathalika nuwali bada aththaliminabadan bima kanu yaksibun
6:130	ya mashara aljini wal insialam ya tikum rusulun minkum yaqusuna alaykum ayatiwayunthirunakum liqa yawmikum hatha qalushahidna ala anfusina wagharat humu alhayatuadunya washahidu ala anfusihim anahumkanu kafirin
6:131	thalika an lam yakun rabuka muhlikalqura bithulmin wahluha ghafilun
6:132	walikulin darajatun mimamilu wama rabuka bighafilin amayamalun
6:133	warabuka alghaniyu thu arahmatin yasha yuthibkum wayastakhlif min badikum mayashau kama anshakum min thuriyati qawminakharin
6:134	ina ma tuaduna latinwama antum bimuajizin
6:135	qul ya qawmi iamalu alamakanatikum ini amilun fasawfa talamuna mantakunu lahu aqibatu adari inahu layuflihu aththalimun
6:136	wajalu lilahi mima tharamina alharthi wal anami nasibanfaqalu hatha lilahi bizamihim wahathalishuraka ina fama kana lishuraka ihimfala yasilu ila alahi wama kanalilahi fahuwa yasilu ila shuraka ihimsa ma yahkumun
6:137	wakathalika zayana likathirin minalmushrikina qatla awladihim shurakauhumliyurduhum waliyalbisu alayhim dinahum walaw sha alahuma faluhu fatharhum wama yaftarun
6:138	waqalu hathihi anamunwaharthun hijrun la yatamuhaila man nashau bizamihim wanamun hurimathuhuruha wanamun la yathkurunaisma alahi alayha iftiran alayhisayajzihim bima kanu yaftarun
6:139	waqalu ma fi butunihathihi al anami khalisatun lithukurinawamuharamun ala azwajina wa inyakun maytatan fahum fihi shurakau sayajzihim wasfahuminahu hakimun alim
6:140	qad khasira alathina qatalu awladahumsafahan bighayri ailmin waharamu ma razaqahumualahu iftiran ala alahi qad daluwama kanu muhtadin
6:141	wahuwa alathi ansha janatinmarushatin waghayra marushatin wanakhlawazara mukhtalifan ukuluhu wazaytuna warumanamutashabihan waghayra mutashabihin kulu minthamarihi itha athmara watu haqahu yawma hasadihiwala tusrifu inahu la yuhibu almusrifin
6:142	wamina al anami hamulatanwafarshan kulu mima razaqakumu alahu walatatabiau khutuwati ashshaytaninahu lakum aduwun mubin
6:143	thamaniyata azwajin mina ada nithnayni wamina almazi ithnayni qul aththakarayniharama ami alunthayayni ama ishtamalat alayhiarhamu alunthayayni nabi uni biailmin in kuntum sadiqin
6:144	wamina al ibili ithnayni wamina albaqarithnayni qul aththakarayni haramami alunthayayni ama ishtamalat alayhi arhamualunthayayni am kuntum shuhada ith wasakumualahu bihatha faman athlamu mimaniftara ala alahi kathiban liyudilanasa bighayri ailmin ina alaha layahdi alqawma aththalimin
6:145	qul la ajidu fima uhiyailaya muharaman ala taimin yatamuhuila an yakuna maytatan aw daman masfuhan aw lahmakhinzirin fa inahu rijsun aw fisqan uhila lighayri alahibihi famani idtura ghayra baghin wala adinfa ina rabaka ghafurun rahim
6:146	wala alathina haduharamna kula thi thufurinwamina albaqari walghanami haramnalayhim shuhumahuma ila ma hamalathuhuruhuma awi alhawayaw ma ikhtalata biathmin thalikajazaynahum bibaghyihim wa ina lasadiqun
6:147	fa in kaththabuka faqul rabukum thurahmatin wasiatin wala yuradu ba suhuani alqawmi almujrimin
6:148	sayaqulu alathina ashraku law shalahu ma ashrakna wala abaunawala haramna min shay in kathalikakaththaba alathina min qablihim hatathaqu ba sana qul hal aindakum min ailminfatukhrijuhu lana in tatabiauna ila aththanawa in antum ila takhrusun
6:149	qul falilahi alhujatu albalighatufalaw sha lahadakum ajmain
6:150	qul haluma shuhadakumu alathinayashaduna ana alaha harama hatha fa inshahidu fala tashad mahum wala tatabia ahwalathina kaththabu bi ayatinawalathina la yu minuna bil akhiratiwahum birabihim yadilun
6:151	qul talaw atlu ma haramarabukum alaykum ala tushriku bihi shay an wabilwalidaynihsanan wala taqtulu awladakum minimlaqin nahnu narzuqukum wa iyahum walataqrabu alfawahisha ma thahara minhawama batana wala taqtulu anafsalati harama alahu ila bilhaqithalikum wasakum bihi lalakum taqilun
6:152	wala taqrabu mala alyatimila bilati hiya ahsanu hatayablugha ashudahu wawfu alkayla walmizana bilqistila nukalifu nafsan ila wusaha wa ithaqultum fadilu walaw kana tha qurbawabiahdi alahi awfu thalikum wasakumbihi lalakum tathakarun
6:153	wana hatha siratimustaqiman fatabiauhu wala tatabiau asubulafatafaraqa bikum an sabilihi thalikum wasakumbihi lalakum tataqun
6:154	thuma atayna musalkitaba tamaman ala alathi ahsanawatafsilan likuli shay in wahudan warahmatanlalahum biliqa i rabihim yu minun
6:155	wahatha kitabun anzalnahumubarakun fatabiauhu wataqulalakum turhamun
6:156	an taqulu inama unzila alkitabuala ta ifatayni min qablina wa in kunan dirasatihim laghafilin
6:157	aw taqulu law ana unzila alaynalkitabu lakuna ahda minhum faqad jakumbayinatun min rabikum wahudan warahmatun faman athlamumiman kaththaba bi ayati alahi wasadafanha sanajzi alathina yasdifuna an ayatinasu a alathabi bima kanu yasdifun
6:158	hal yanthuruna ilan ta tiyahumu almala ikatu aw ya tiya rabuka aw ya tiyabadu ayati rabika yawma ya ti baduayati rabika la yanfau nafsan imanuhalam takun amanat min qablu aw kasabat fi imanihakhayran quli intathiru ina muntathirun
6:159	ina alathina faraqu dinahumwakanu shiyan lasta minhum fi shay in inamamruhum ila alahi thuma yunabi uhum bimakanu yafalun
6:160	man ja bilhasanatifalahu ashru amthaliha waman ja bisayi atifala yujza ila mithlaha wahum layuthlamun
6:161	qul inani hadani rabi ilasiratin mustaqimin dinan qiyaman milata ibrahimahanifan wama kana mina almushrikin
6:162	qul ina salati wanusukiwamahyaya wamamati lilahi rabialalamin
6:163	la sharika lahu wabithalikaumirtu wana awalu almuslimin
6:164	qul aghayra alahi abghi rabanwahuwa rabu kuli shay in wala taksibu kulu nafsin ilalayha wala taziru waziratun wizra ukhrathuma ila rabikum marjiaukum fayunabi ukum bimakuntum fihi takhtalifun
6:165	wahuwa alathi jalakum khala ifal ardi warafa badakum fawqa badindarajatin liyabluwakum fi ma atakumina rabaka sariau alaiqabi wa inahu laghafurun rahim
7:1	alif lam mim sad
7:2	kitabun unzila ilayka falayakun fi sadrika harajun minhu litunthirabihi wathikra lilmu minin
7:3	itabiau ma unzila ilaykum minrabikum wala tatabiau min dunihi awliyaqalilan ma tathakarun
7:4	wakam min qaryatin ahlaknahafajaha ba suna bayatan aw hum qa ilun
7:5	fama kana dawahum ithjahum ba suna ila an qalu inakuna thalimin
7:6	falanas alana alathina ursilailayhim walanas alana almursalin
7:7	falanaqusana alayhim biailmin wamakuna gha ibin
7:8	walwaznu yawma ithini alhaqufaman thaqulat mawazinuhu faula ika humu almuflihun
7:9	waman khafat mawazinuhu faula ikalathina khasiru anfusahum bima kanu bi ayatinayathlimun
7:10	walaqad makanakum fi al ardiwajalna lakum fiha mayisha qalilan matashkurun
7:11	walaqad khalaqnakum thuma sawarnakumthuma qulna lilmala ikati usjudu li adamafasajadu ila iblisa lam yakun mina asajidin
7:12	qala ma manaka alatasjuda ith amartuka qala ana khayrun minhukhalaqtani min narin wakhalaqtahu min tin
7:13	qala fahbit minhafama yakunu laka an tatakabara fiha fakhrujinaka mina asaghirin
7:14	qala anthirni ilayawmi yubathun
7:15	qala inaka mina almuntharin
7:16	qala fabima aghwaytanilaqaudana lahum sirataka almustaqim
7:17	thuma latiyanahum min bayniaydihim wamin khalfihim wan aymanihim wan shama ilihimwala tajidu aktharahum shakirin
7:18	qala ukhruj minha mathumanmadhuran laman tabiaka minhum lamlana jahanamaminkum ajmain
7:19	waya adamu uskun antawazawjuka aljanata fakula min haythu shi tumawala taqraba hathihi ashshajaratafatakuna mina aththalimin
7:20	fawaswasa lahuma ashshaytanuliyubdiya lahuma ma wuriya anhuma minsaw atihima waqala ma nahakumarabukuma an hathihi ashshajarati ilan takuna malakayni aw takuna mina alkhalidin
7:21	waqasamahuma ini lakumalamina anasihin
7:22	fadalahuma bighururin falamathaqa ashshajarata badat lahuma saw atuhumawatafiqa yakhsifani alayhimamin waraqi aljanati wanadahuma rabuhumalam anhakuma an tilkuma ashshajaratiwaqul lakuma ina ashshaytana lakumaduwun mubin
7:23	qala rabana thalamnanfusana wa in lam taghfir lana watarhamnalanakunana mina alkhasirin
7:24	qala ihbitu badukumlibadin aduwun walakum fi al ardi mustaqarunwamataun ila hin
7:25	qala fiha tahyawnawafiha tamutuna waminha tukhrajun
7:26	ya bani adama qad anzalnalaykum libasan yuwari saw atikumwarishan walibasu ataqwa thalikakhayrun thalika min ayati alahilalahum yaththakarun
7:27	ya bani adama layaftinanakumu ashshaytanu kama akhrajabawaykum mina aljanati yanziau anhuma libasahumaliyuriyahuma saw atihima inahu yarakumhuwa waqabiluhu min haythu la tarawnahum inajalna ashshayatina awliya lilathinala yu minun
7:28	wa itha falu fahishatan qaluwajadna alayha abana walahuamarana biha qul ina alaha laya muru bilfahsha i ataquluna alalahi ma la talamun
7:29	qul amara rabi bilqistiwaqimu wujuhakum ainda kuli masjidin wadauhumukhlisina lahu adina kama badakumtaudun
7:30	fariqan hada wafariqan haqalayhimu adalalatu inahumu itakhathuashshayatina awliya min duni alahiwayahsabuna anahum muhtadun
7:31	ya bani adama khuthuzinatakum ainda kuli masjidin wakulu washrabu walatusrifu inahu la yuhibu almusrifin
7:32	qul man harama zinata alahialati akhraja liaibadihi watayibatimina arizqi qul hiya lilathina amanufi alhayati adunya khalisatanyawma alqiyamati kathalika nufasilu al ayatiliqawmin yalamun
7:33	qul inama harama rabiyalfawahisha ma thahara minhawama batana wal ithma walbaghyabighayri alhaqi wan tushriku bilahi malam yunazil bihi sultanan wan taqulu ala alahima la talamun
7:34	walikuli umatin ajalun fa itha jajaluhum la yasta khiruna satan walayastaqdimun
7:35	ya bani adama imaya tiyanakum rusulun minkum yaqusuna alaykum ayatifamani itaqa waslaha fala khawfunalayhim wala hum yahzanun
7:36	walathina kaththabubi ayatina wastakbaru anhaula ika as habu anari humfiha khalidun
7:37	faman athlamu mimani iftarala alahi kathiban aw kaththababi ayatihi ula ika yanaluhum nasibuhumina alkitabi hata itha jat humrusuluna yatawafawnahum qalu ayna makuntum tadauna min duni alahi qalu daluana washahidu ala anfusihim anahum kanukafirin
7:38	qala udkhulu fi umamin qad khalatmin qablikum mina aljini wal insi fi anarikulama dakhalat umatun lanat ukhtaha hataitha idaraku fiha jamian qalatukhrahum li ulahum rabana haula iadaluna fatihim athaban diafanmina anari qala likulin diafunwalakin la talamun
7:39	waqalat ulahum li ukhrahumfama kana lakum alayna min fadlinfathuqu alathaba bima kuntum taksibun
7:40	ina alathina kaththabu bi ayatinawastakbaru anha la tufatahulahum abwabu asama i walayadkhuluna aljanata hata yalija aljamalu fisami alkhiyati wakathalika najzi almujrimin
7:41	lahum min jahanama mihadun waminfawqihim ghawashin wakathalika najzi aththalimin
7:42	walathina amanuwamilu asalihati la nukalifunafsan ila wusaha ula ika as habualjanati hum fiha khalidun
7:43	wanazana ma fi sudurihimin ghilin tajri min tahtihimu al anharu waqalualhamdu lilahi alathi hadanalihatha wama kuna linahtadiya lawlan hadana alahu laqad jat rusulurabina bilhaqi wanudu an tilkumualjanatu urithtumuha bima kuntum tamalun
7:44	wanada as habualjanati as haba anari an qadwajadna ma wadana rabuna haqanfahal wajadtum ma wada rabukum haqan qalunam faththana mu aththinun baynahum an lanatualahi ala aththalimin
7:45	alathina yasuduna ansabili alahi wayabghunaha aiwajan wahum bil akhiratikafirun
7:46	wabaynahuma hijabunwala al arafi rijalun yarifunakulan bisimahum wanadaw as habaljanati an salamun alaykum lam yadkhuluhawahum yatmaun
7:47	wa itha surifat absaruhumtilqa as habi anari qalurabana la tajalna ma alqawmi aththalimin
7:48	wanada as habual arafi rijalan yarifunahum bisimahumqalu ma aghna ankum jamaukum wamakuntum tastakbirun
7:49	ahaula i alathinaqsamtum la yanaluhumu alahu birahmatinudkhulu aljanata la khawfun alaykum wala antumtahzanun
7:50	wanada as habu anarias haba aljanati an afidu alaynamina alma i aw mima razaqakumu alahu qaluina alaha haramahuma ala alkafirin
7:51	alathina itakhathudinahum lahwan walaiban wagharat humu alhayatuadunya falyawma nansahum kamanasu liqa yawmihim hatha wama kanubi ayatina yajhadun
7:52	walaqad ji nahum bikitabin fasalnahuala ailmin hudan warahmatan liqawmin yu minun
7:53	hal yanthuruna ilata wilahu yawma ya ti ta wiluhu yaqulu alathinanasuhu min qablu qad jat rusulu rabina bilhaqifahal lana min shufa fayashfau lanaw nuradu fanamala ghayra alathi kunanamalu qad khasiru anfusahum wadala anhum makanu yaftarun
7:54	ina rabakumu alahu alathikhalaqa asamawati wal ardafi sitati ayamin thuma istawa alalarshi yughshi alayla anahara yatlubuhuhathithan washshamsa walqamara wanujumamusakhkharatin bi amrihi ala lahu alkhalqu wal amrutabaraka alahu rabu alalamin
7:55	udau rabakum tadaruanwakhufyatan inahu la yuhibu almuatadin
7:56	wala tufsidu fi al ardibada islahiha wadauhu khawfan watamanina rahmata alahi qaribun mina almuhsinin
7:57	wahuwa alathi yursilu ariyahabushran bayna yaday rahmatihi hata ithaqalat sahaban thiqalan suqnahu libaladinmayitin fanzalna bihi alma fakhrajnabihi min kuli aththamarati kathalikanukhriju almawta lalakum tathakarun
7:58	walbaladu atayibuyakhruju nabatuhu bi ithni rabihi walathikhabutha la yakhruju ila nakidan kathalikanusarifu al ayati liqawmin yashkurun
7:59	laqad arsalna nuhan ilaqawmihi faqala ya qawmi uabudu alaha malakum min ilahin ghayruhu ini akhafu alaykumathaba yawmin athim
7:60	qala almalau min qawmihi inalanaraka fi dalalin mubin
7:61	qala ya qawmi laysa bi dalalatunwalakini rasulun min rabi alalamin
7:62	ubalighukum risalati rabiwansahu lakum walamu mina alahi mala talamun
7:63	awa ajibtum an jakum thikrunmin rabikum ala rajulin minkum liyunthirakumwalitataqu walalakum turhamun
7:64	fakaththabuhu fanjaynahu walathinamahu fi alfulki waghraqna alathina kaththabubi ayatina inahum kanu qawmanamin
7:65	wa ila adin akhahumhudan qala ya qawmi uabudu alaha malakum min ilahin ghayruhu afala tataqun
7:66	qala almalau alathinakafaru min qawmihi ina lanaraka fi safahatinwa ina lanathunuka mina alkathibin
7:67	qala ya qawmi laysa bi safahatunwalakini rasulun min rabi alalamin
7:68	ubalighukum risalati rabiwana lakum nasihun amin
7:69	awa ajibtum an jakum thikrunmin rabikum ala rajulin minkum liyunthirakum wathkuruith jalakum khulafa min badi qawmi nuhinwazadakum fi alkhalqi bastatan fathkuruala alahi lalakum tuflihun
7:70	qalu aji tana linabuda alahawahdahu wanathara ma kana yabudu abaunafa tina bima taiduna in kunta mina asadiqin
7:71	qala qad waqa alaykum minrabikum rijsun waghadabun atujadilunani fi asma insamaytumuha antum wabaukum manazala alahu biha min sultanin fantathiruini makum mina almuntathirin
7:72	fanjaynahu walathinamahu birahmatin mina waqatana dabiralathina kaththabu bi ayatinawama kanu mu minin
7:73	wa ila thamuda akhahum salihanqala ya qawmi uabudu alaha malakum min ilahin ghayruhu qad jatkum bayinatunmin rabikum hathihi naqatu alahi lakum ayatanfatharuha ta kul fi ardi alahi walatamasuha bisu in faya khuthakum athabunalim
7:74	wathkuru ithjalakum khulafa min badi adin wabawakumfi al ardi tatakhithuna min suhuliha qusuranwatanhituna aljibala buyutan fathkuruala alahi wala tathaw fi al ardimufsidin
7:75	qala almalau alathinaistakbaru min qawmihi lilathina istudaifuliman amana minhum atalamuna ana salihanmursalun min rabihi qalu ina bima ursilabihi mu minun
7:76	qala alathina istakbaru inabilathi amantum bihi kafirun
7:77	faqaru anaqata watawan amri rabihim waqalu ya salihui tina bima taiduna in kunta minalmursalin
7:78	fakhathat humu arajfatu fasbahufi darihim jathimin
7:79	fatawala anhum waqala yaqawmi laqad ablaghtukum risalata rabi wanasahtulakum walakin la tuhibuna anasihin
7:80	walutan ith qalaliqawmihi ata tuna alfahishata ma sabaqakum bihamin ahadin mina alalamin
7:81	inakum lata tuna arijalashahwatan min duni anisa i bal antum qawmunmusrifun
7:82	wama kana jawabaqawmihi ila an qalu akhrijuhum min qaryatikuminahum unasun yatataharun
7:83	fanjaynahu wahlahu ilaimratahu kanat mina alghabirin
7:84	wamtarna alayhim mataranfanthur kayfa kana aqibatualmujrimin
7:85	wa ila madyana akhahumshuayban qala ya qawmi uabudu alaha malakum min ilahin ghayruhu qad jatkum bayinatunmin rabikum fawfu alkayla walmizana walatabkhasu anasa ashyahum walatufsidu fi al ardi bada islahiha thalikumkhayrun lakum in kuntum mu minin
7:86	wala taqaudu bikuli siratintuaiduna watasuduna an sabili alahi man amanabihi watabghunaha aiwajan wathkuru ithkuntum qalilan fakaththarakum wanthurukayfa kana aqibatu almufsidin
7:87	wa in kana ta ifatun minkum amanubilathi ursiltu bihi wata ifatun lamyu minu fasbiru hata yahkumalahu baynana wahuwa khayru alhakimin
7:88	qala almalau alathinaistakbaru min qawmihi lanukhrijanaka ya shuaybu walathinamanu maka min qaryatina aw latauduna fimilatina qala awa law kuna karihin
7:89	qadi iftarayna ala alahikathiban in audna fi milatikum bada ithnajana alahu minha wamayakunu lana an nauda fiha ila an yashalahu rabuna wasia rabuna kula shay inailman ala alahi tawakalna rabanaiftah baynana wabayna qawmina bilhaqiwanta khayru alfatihin
7:90	waqala almalau alathinakafaru min qawmihi la ini itabatum shuayban inakum ithanlakhasirun
7:91	fakhathat humu arajfatu fasbahufi darihim jathimin
7:92	alathina kaththabushuayban kan lam yaghnaw fiha alathina kaththabushuayban kanu humu alkhasirin
7:93	fatawala anhum waqala yaqawmi laqad ablaghtukum risalati rabi wanasahtulakum fakayfa asa ala qawmin kafirin
7:94	wama arsalna fi qaryatin minabiyin ila akhathna ahlaha bilba sa iwadara i lalahum yadaraun
7:95	thuma badalna makana asayi atialhasanata hata afaw waqalu qadmasa abana adarauwasarau fakhathnahum baghtatanwahum la yashaurun
7:96	walaw ana ahla alqura amanuwataqaw lafatahna alayhim barakatinmina asama i wal ardi walakinkaththabu fakhathnahum bima kanuyaksibun
7:97	afamina ahlu alqura an ya tiyahumba suna bayatan wahum na imun
7:98	awa amina ahlu alqura an ya tiyahumba suna duhan wahum yalabun
7:99	afaminu makra alahi falaya manu makra alahi ila alqawmu alkhasirun
7:100	awa lam yahdi lilathina yarithunal arda min badi ahliha an law nashau asabnahumbithunubihim wanatbau ala qulubihimfahum la yasmaun
7:101	tilka alqura naqusu alaykamin anba iha walaqad jat hum rusuluhum bilbayinatifama kanu liyu minu bima kaththabumin qablu kathalika yatbau alahu alaqulubi alkafirin
7:102	wama wajadna li aktharihimin ahdin wa in wajadna aktharahum lafasiqin
7:103	thuma bathna min badihim musabi ayatina ila firawna wamala ihifathalamu biha fanuthurkayfa kana aqibatu almufsidin
7:104	waqala musa yafirawnu ini rasulun min rabi alalamin
7:105	haqiqun ala an laqula ala alahi ila alhaqa qadji tukum bibayinatin min rabikum farsil maiya bani isra il
7:106	qala in kunta ji ta bi ayatinfa ti biha in kunta mina asadiqin
7:107	falqa asahu fa ithahiya thuabanun mubin
7:108	wanaza yadahu fa itha hiya baydaulinathirin
7:109	qala almalau min qawmi firawnaina hatha lasahirun alim
7:110	yuridu an yukhrijakum min ardikumfamatha ta murun
7:111	qalu arjih wakhahu warsilfi almada ini hashirin
7:112	ya tuka bikuli sahirin alim
7:113	waja asaharatufirawna qalu ina lana lajran in kunanahnu alghalibin
7:114	qala nam wa inakum laminalmuqarabin
7:115	qalu ya musa iman tulqiya wa ima an nakuna nahnu almulqin
7:116	qala alqu falama alqaw saharuayuna anasi wastarhabuhum wajaubisihrin athim
7:117	wawhayna ila musan alqi asaka fa itha hiya talqafu maya fikun
7:118	fawaqa alhaqu wabatala makanu yamalun
7:119	faghulibu hunalika wanqalabusaghirin
7:120	waulqiya asaharatu sajidin
7:121	qalu amana birabialalamin
7:122	rabi musa waharun
7:123	qala firawnu amantum bihiqabla an athana lakum ina hatha lamakrunmakartumuhu fi almadinati litukhriju minha ahlahafasawfa talamun
7:124	lauqatiana aydiyakum warjulakumin khilafin thuma lausalibanakum ajmain
7:125	qalu ina ila rabinamunqalibun
7:126	wama tanqimu mina ilan amana bi ayati rabinalama jatna rabana afrigh alaynasabran watawafana muslimin
7:127	waqala almalau min qawmi firawnatatharu musa waqawmahu liyufsidu fi al ardiwayatharaka walihataka qala sanuqatilu abnahumwanastahyi nisahum wa ina fawqahum qahirun
7:128	qala musa liqawmihistainu bilahi wasbiru inal arda lilahi yurithuha man yashaumin aibadihi walaqibatu lilmutaqin
7:129	qalu uthina minqabli an ta tiyana wamin badi ma ji tana qalasa rabukum an yuhlika aduwakum wayastakhlifakum fial ardi fayanthura kayfa tamalun
7:130	walaqad akhathna alafirawna bisinina wanaqsin mina aththamaratilalahum yaththakarun
7:131	fa itha jat humu alhasanatuqalu lana hathihi wa in tusibhumsayi atun yatayaru bimusa waman mahu alainama ta iruhum ainda alahi walakinaktharahum la yalamun
7:132	waqalu mahma ta tinabihi min ayatin litasharana biha famanahnu laka bimu minin
7:133	farsalna alayhimu atufanawaljarada walqumala wadafadiawadama ayatin mufasalatinfastakbaru wakanu qawman mujrimin
7:134	walama waqa alayhimu arijzuqalu ya musa udau lana rabaka bimahida aindaka la in kashafta ana arijzalanu minana laka walanursilana maka bani isra il
7:135	falama kashafna anhumu arijzaila ajalin hum balighuhu itha humyankuthun
7:136	fantaqamna minhum faghraqnahumfi alyami bi anahum kaththabu bi ayatinawakanu anha ghafilin
7:137	wawrathna alqawma alathinakanu yustadafuna mashariqa al ardiwamagharibaha alati barakna fihawatamat kalimatu rabika alhusna alabani isra ila bima sabaru wadamarnama kana yasnau firawnu waqawmuhu wamakanu yarishun
7:138	wajawazna bibani isra ilalbahra fataw ala qawmin yakufuna alasnamin lahum qalu ya musaijal lana ilahan kama lahum alihatunqala inakum qawmun tajhalun
7:139	ina haula i mutabarun mahum fihi wabatilun ma kanu yamalun
7:140	qala aghayra alahi abghikumilahan wahuwa fadalakum ala alalamin
7:141	wa ith anjaynakum min alifirawna yasumunakum su a alathabi yuqatiluna abnakumwayastahyuna nisakum wafi thalikum balaunmin rabikum athim
7:142	wawadna musa thalathinalaylatan watmamnaha biashrin fatama miqaturabihi arbaina laylatan waqala musa li akhihiharuna ukhlufni fi qawmi waslih walatatabia sabila almufsidin
7:143	walama ja musalimiqatina wakalamahu rabuhu qala rabiarini anthur ilayka qala lan taraniwalakini unthur ila aljabali fa inistaqara makanahu fasawfa tarani falamatajala rabuhu liljabali jalahu dakan wakhara musasaiqan falama afaqa qala subhanakatubtu ilayka wana awalu almu minin
7:144	qala ya musa ini istafaytukala anasi birisalatiwabikalami fakhuth ma ataytuka wakunmina ashshakirin
7:145	wakatabna lahu fi al alwahimin kuli shay in mawaithatan watafsilanlikuli shay in fakhutha biquwatin wa mur qawmakaya khuthu bi ahsaniha saurikum daralfasiqin
7:146	sasrifu an ayatiyalathina yatakabaruna fi al ardi bighayri alhaqiwa in yaraw kula ayatin la yu minu bihawa in yaraw sabila arushdi la yatakhithuhusabilan wa in yaraw sabila alghayi yatakhithuhusabilan thalika bi anahum kaththabu bi ayatinawakanu anha ghafilin
7:147	walathina kaththabubi ayatina waliqa i al akhiratihabitat amaluhum hal yujzawna ilama kanu yamalun
7:148	watakhatha qawmu musamin badihi min huliyihim aijlan jasadan lahu khuwarunalam yaraw anahu la yukalimuhum wala yahdihimsabilan itakhathuhu wakanu thalimin
7:149	walama suqita fi aydihimwaraw anahum qad dalu qalu la in lam yarhamnarabuna wayaghfir lana lanakunana mina alkhasirin
7:150	walama raja musa ilaqawmihi ghadbana asifan qala bi samakhalaftumuni min badi ajiltum amra rabikum walqa al alwahawakhatha bira si akhihi yajuruhu ilayhi qalaibna uma ina alqawma istadafuni wakaduyaqtulunani fala tushmit biya al ada walatajalni ma alqawmi aththalimin
7:151	qala rabi ighfir li wali akhiwadkhilna fi rahmatika wanta arhamu arahimin
7:152	ina alathina itakhathualaijla sayanaluhum ghadabun min rabihim wathilatunfi alhayati adunya wakathalikanajzi almuftarin
7:153	walathina amilu asayi atithuma tabu min badiha wamanu inarabaka min badiha laghafurun rahim
7:154	walama sakata an musalghadabu akhatha al alwaha wafi nuskhatihahudan warahmatun lilathina hum lirabihimyarhabun
7:155	wakhtara musaqawmahu sabaina rajulan limiqatina falamakhathat humu arajfatu qala rabi lawshi ta ahlaktahum min qablu wa iyaya atuhlikunabima fala asufahau mina in hiyaila fitnatuka tudilu biha man tashauwatahdi man tashau anta waliyuna faghfirlana warhamna wanta khayru alghafirin
7:156	waktub lana fi hathihiadunya hasanatan wafi al akhiratina hudna ilayka qala athabi usibubihi man ashau warahmati wasiat kula shay infasaktubuha lilathina yataquna wayu tuna azakatawalathina hum bi ayatinayu minun
7:157	alathina yatabiauna arasulanabiya al umiya alathi yajidunahumaktuban aindahum fi atawrati wal injiliya muruhum bilmarufi wayanhahum ani almunkariwayuhilu lahumu atayibati wayuharimualayhimu alkhaba itha wayadau anhum israhumwal aghlala alati kanat alayhim falathinamanu bihi wazaruhu wanasaruhu watabauanura alathi unzila mahu ula ikahumu almuflihun
7:158	qul ya ayuha anasuini rasulu alahi ilaykum jamian alathilahu mulku asamawati wal ardila ilaha ila huwa yuhyi wayumitufaminu bilahi warasulihi anabiyial umiyi alathi yu minu bilahi wakalimatihiwatabiauhu lalakum tahtadun
7:159	wamin qawmi musa umatun yahdunabilhaqi wabihi yadilun
7:160	waqatanahumu ithnatayashrata asbatan umaman wawhayna ilamusa ithi istasqahu qawmuhu ani idribiasaka alhajara fanbajasat minhu ithnatashrata aynan qad alima kulu unasin mashrabahum wathalalnalayhimu alghamama wanzalna alayhimu almanawasalwa kulu min tayibati marazaqnakum wama thalamuna walakinkanu anfusahum yathlimun
7:161	wa ith qila lahumu uskunu hathihialqaryata wakulu minha haythu shi tum waqulu hitatunwadkhulu albaba sujadan naghfir lakum khati atikumsanazidu almuhsinin
7:162	fabadala alathina thalamuminhum qawlan ghayra alathi qila lahum farsalnalayhim rijzan mina asama i bima kanuyathlimun
7:163	was alhum ani alqaryati alati kanathadirata albahri ith yaduna fi asabtith ta tihim hitanuhum yawma sabtihimshuran wayawma la yasbituna la ta tihim kathalikanabluhum bima kanu yafsuqun
7:164	wa ith qalat umatun minhumlima taithuna qawman alahu muhlikuhum awmuaththibuhum athaban shadidan qalumathiratan ila rabikum walalahum yataqun
7:165	falama nasu ma thukirubihi anjayna alathina yanhawna ani asu iwakhathna alathina thalamubiathabin ba isin bima kanu yafsuqun
