7:166	falama ataw an ma nuhuanhu qulna lahum kunu qiradatan khasi in
7:167	wa ith taththana rabukalayabathana alayhim ila yawmi alqiyamati manyasumuhum su a alathabi ina rabaka lasariau alaiqabiwa inahu laghafurun rahim
7:168	waqatanahum fi al ardiumaman minhumu asalihuna waminhum duna thalikawabalawnahum bilhasanati wasayi atilalahum yarjiaun
7:169	fakhalafa min badihim khalfun warithualkitaba ya khuthuna arada hathal adna wayaquluna sayughfaru lana wa in ya tihimaradun mithluhu ya khuthuhu alam yu khathalayhim mithaqu alkitabi an la yaquluala alahi ila alhaqa wadarasu mafihi wadaru al akhiratu khayrun lilathinayataquna afala taqilun
7:170	walathina yumasikuna bilkitabiwaqamu asalata ina lanudiau ajra almuslihin
7:171	wa ith nataqna aljabalafawqahum kanahu thulatun wathanuanahu waqiaun bihim khuthu ma ataynakumbiquwatin wathkuru ma fihi lalakumtataqun
7:172	wa ith akhatha rabuka minbani adama min thuhurihim thuriyatahumwashadahum ala anfusihim alastu birabikum qalubala shahidna an taqulu yawma alqiyamatina kuna an hatha ghafilin
7:173	aw taqulu inama ashraka abaunamin qablu wakuna thuriyatan min badihimafatuhlikuna bima fala almubtilun
7:174	wakathalika nufasilu al ayatiwalalahum yarjiaun
7:175	watlu alayhim naba alathiataynahu ayatina fansalakhaminha fatbahu ashshaytanu fakanamina alghawin
7:176	walaw shi na larafanahu bihawalakinahu akhlada ila al ardi watabahawahu famathaluhu kamathali alkalbi in tahmilalayhi yalhath aw tatruk hu yalhath thalika mathalualqawmi alathina kaththabu bi ayatinafaqsusi alqasasa lalahumyatafakarun
7:177	sa mathalan alqawmu alathinakaththabu bi ayatina wanfusahum kanuyathlimun
7:178	man yahdi alahu fahuwa almuhtadiwaman yudlil faula ika humu alkhasirun
7:179	walaqad thara na lijahanamakathiran mina aljini wal insi lahum qulubun layafqahuna biha walahum ayunun la yubsirunabiha walahum athanun la yasmauna bihaula ika kal anami bal hum adalula ika humu alghafilun
7:180	walilahi al asmau alhusnafadauhu biha watharu alathinayulhiduna fi asma ihi sayujzawna ma kanuyamalun
7:181	wamiman khalaqna umatun yahdunabilhaqi wabihi yadilun
7:182	walathina kaththabubi ayatina sanastadrijuhum min haythula yalamun
7:183	waumli lahum ina kaydi matin
7:184	awa lam yatafakaru ma bisahibihimin jinatin in huwa ila nathirun mubin
7:185	awalam yanthuru fimalakuti asamawati wal ardiwama khalaqa alahu min shay in wan asan yakuna qadi iqtaraba ajaluhum fabi ayi hadithinbadahu yu minun
7:186	man yudlili alahu falahadiya lahu wayatharuhum fi tughyanihimyamahun
7:187	yas alunaka ani asatiayana mursaha qul inama ailmuhainda rabi la yujaliha liwaqtiha ilahuwa thaqulat fi asamawati wal ardila ta tikum ila baghtatan yas alunaka kanaka hafiyunanha qul inama ailmuha ainda alahiwalakina akthara anasi layalamun
7:188	qul la amliku linafsi nafan waladaran ila ma sha alahuwalaw kuntu alamu alghayba lastakthartu mina alkhayriwama masaniya asu u in ana ilanathirun wabashirun liqawmin yu minun
7:189	huwa alathi khalaqakum min nafsinwahidatin wajala minha zawjaha liyaskunailayha falama taghashshaha hamalathamlan khafifan famarat bihi falama athqalatdawa alaha rabahuma la in ataytanasalihan lanakunana mina ashshakirin
7:190	falama atahumasalihan jala lahu shuraka fimatahuma fatala alahuama yushrikun
7:191	ayushrikuna ma la yakhluqushay an wahum yukhlaqun
7:192	wala yastatiauna lahum nasranwala anfusahum yansurun
7:193	wa in tadauhum ila alhuda layatabiaukum sawaun alaykum adawtumuhum am antum samitun
7:194	ina alathina tadauna min dunialahi aibadun amthalukum fadauhumfalyastajibu lakum in kuntum sadiqin
7:195	alahum arjulun yamshuna biha amlahum aydin yabtishuna biha am lahum ayunun yubsirunabiha am lahum athanun yasmauna biha quliudau shurakakum thuma kiduni fala tunthirun
7:196	ina waliyiya alahu alathinazala alkitaba wahuwa yatawala asalihin
7:197	walathina tadauna mindunihi la yastatiauna nasrakum walanfusahum yansurun
7:198	wa in tadauhum ila alhuda layasmau watarahum yanthuruna ilayka wahumla yubsirun
7:199	khuthi alafwa wa mur bilaurfiwarid ani aljahilin
7:200	wa ima yanzaghanaka mina ashshaytaninazghun fastaith bilahi inahusamiaun alim
7:201	ina alathina itaqaw ithamasahum ta ifun mina ashshaytani tathakarufa itha hum mubsirun
7:202	wa ikhwanuhum yamudunahum fialghayi thuma la yuqsirun
7:203	wa itha lam ta tihim bi ayatinqalu lawla ijtabaytaha qul inamatabiau ma yuha ilaya min rabi hathabasa iru min rabikum wahudan warahmatun liqawminyu minun
7:204	wa itha quri a alqur anu fastamiaulahu wansitu lalakum turhamun
7:205	wathkur rabaka fi nafsikatadaruan wakhifatan waduna aljahri mina alqawli bilghuduwiwal asali wala takun mina alghafilin
7:206	ina alathina ainda rabika layastakbiruna an aibadatihi wayusabihunahuwalahu yasjudun
8:1	yas alunaka ani al anfali qulial anfalu lilahi warasuli fataqualaha waslihu thata baynikum watiaualaha warasulahu in kuntum mu minin
8:2	inama almu minuna alathinaitha thukira alahu wajilat qulubuhum wa ithatuliyat alayhim ayatuhu zadat hum imananwala rabihim yatawakalun
8:3	alathina yuqimuna asalatawamima razaqnahum yunfiqun
8:4	ula ika humu almu minuna haqanlahum darajatun ainda rabihim wamaghfiratun warizqunkarim
8:5	kama akhrajaka rabuka min baytika bilhaqiwa ina fariqan mina almu minina lakarihun
8:6	yujadilunaka fi alhaqibada ma tabayana kanama yusaquna ilalmawti wahum yanthurun
8:7	wa ith yaidukumu alahu ihdata ifatayni anaha lakum watawadunana ghayra thati ashshawkati takunu lakumwayuridu alahu an yuhiqa alhaqa bikalimatihiwayaqta dabira alkafirin
8:8	liyuhiqa alhaqa wayubtilalbatila walaw kariha almujrimun
8:9	ith tastaghithuna rabakum fastajabalakum ani mumidukum bi alfin mina almala ikatimurdifin
8:10	wama jalahu alahu ilabushra walitatma ina bihi qulubukum wama anasruila min aindi alahi ina alaha azizunhakim
8:11	ith yughashshikumu anuasamanatan minhu wayunazilu alaykum mina asama iman liyutahirakum bihi wayuthiba ankumrijza ashshaytani waliyarbita alaqulubikum wayuthabita bihi al aqdam
8:12	ith yuhi rabuka ilalmala ikati ani makum fathabitu alathina amanusaulqi fi qulubi alathina kafaru aruaba fadribufawqa al anaqi wadribu minhum kula banan
8:13	thalika bi anahum shaqu alahawarasulahu waman yushaqiqi alaha warasulahufa ina alaha shadidu alaiqab
8:14	thalikum fathuquhu wanalilkafirina athaba anar
8:15	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha laqitumu alathina kafaru zahfan falatuwaluhumu al adbar
8:16	waman yuwalihim yawma ithin duburahuila mutaharifan liqitalin aw mutahayizanila fi atin faqad ba bighadabin mina alahiwama wahu jahanamu wabi sa almasir
8:17	falam taqtuluhum walakina alahaqatalahum wama ramayta ith ramayta walakinalaha rama waliyubliya almu minina minhu balanhasanan ina alaha samiaun alim
8:18	thalikum wana alaha muhinukaydi alkafirin
8:19	in tastaftihu faqad jakumualfathu wa in tantahu fahuwa khayrun lakum wa intaudu naud walan tughniya ankum fi atukum shay an walawkathurat wana alaha ma almu minin
8:20	ya ayuha alathina amanuatiau alaha warasulahu wala tawalawanhu wantum tasmaun
8:21	wala takunu kalathinaqalu samiana wahum la yasmaun
8:22	ina shara adawabi aindalahi asumu albukmu alathina layaqilun
8:23	walaw alima alahu fihim khayranlasmahum walaw asmahum latawalaw wahum muaridun
8:24	yayuha alathina amanuistajibu lilahi walirasuli itha dakumlima yuhyikum walamu ana alahayahulu bayna almar i waqalbihi wanahu ilayhi tuhsharun
8:25	wataqu fitnatan la tusibanalathina thalamu minkum khasatanwalamu ana alaha shadidu alaiqab
8:26	wathkuru ith antumqalilun mustadafuna fi al ardi takhafunan yatakhatafakumu anasu fawakumwayadakum binasrihi warazaqakum mina atayibatilalakum tashkurun
8:27	ya ayuha alathina amanula takhunu alaha warasula watakhunuamanatikum wantum talamun
8:28	walamu anama amwalukumwawladukum fitnatun wana alaha aindahu ajrunathim
8:29	ya ayuha alathina amanuin tataqu alaha yajal lakum furqananwayukafir ankum sayi atikum wayaghfir lakum walahuthu alfadli alathim
8:30	wa ith yamkuru bika alathinakafaru liyuthbituka aw yaqtuluka aw yukhrijuka wayamkurunawayamkuru alahu walahu khayru almakirin
8:31	wa itha tutla alayhim ayatunaqalu qad samiana law nashau laqulnamithla hatha in hatha ila asatirual awalin
8:32	wa ith qalu alahumain kana hatha huwa alhaqa min aindika famtiralayna hijaratan mina asama iawi i tina biathabin alim
8:33	wama kana alahuliyuaththibahum wanta fihim wama kanalahu muaththibahum wahum yastaghfirun
8:34	wama lahum ala yuaththibahumualahu wahum yasuduna ani almasjidi alharamiwama kanu awliyahu in awliyauhu ilalmutaquna walakina aktharahum la yalamun
8:35	wama kana salatuhumainda albayti ila mukan watasdiyatan fathuqualathaba bima kuntum takfurun
8:36	ina alathina kafaru yunfiqunamwalahum liyasudu an sabili alahifasayunfiqunaha thuma takunu alayhim hasratanthuma yughlabuna walathina kafaru ilajahanama yuhsharun
8:37	liyamiza alahu alkhabitha mina atayibiwayajala alkhabitha badahu ala badinfayarkumahu jamian fayajalahu fi jahanama ula ikahumu alkhasirun
8:38	qul lilathina kafaru in yantahuyughfar lahum ma qad salafa wa in yaudu faqad madatsunatu al awalin
8:39	waqatiluhum hata latakuna fitnatun wayakuna adinu kuluhu lilahifa ini intahaw fa ina alaha bima yamaluna basir
8:40	wa in tawalaw falamu ana alahamawlakum niama almawla waniama anasir
8:41	walamu anama ghanimtumin shay in fana lilahi khumusahu walirasuliwalithi alqurba walyatama walmasakiniwabni asabili in kuntum amantum bilahiwama anzalna ala abdina yawmalfurqani yawma iltaqa aljamani walahuala kuli shay in qadir
8:42	ith antum bilaudwati adunyawahum bilaudwati alquswa warakbuasfala minkum walaw tawadtum lakhtalaftum fialmiadi walakin liyaqdiya alahuamran kana mafaulan liyahlika man halaka an bayinatinwayahya man haya an bayinatin wa inalaha lasamiaun alim
8:43	ith yurikahumu alahu fi manamikaqalilan walaw arakahum kathiran lafashiltum walatanazatumfi al amri walakina alaha salama inahualimun bithati asudur
8:44	wa ith yurikumuhum ithiltaqaytum fi ayunikum qalilan wayuqalilukum fi ayunihimliyaqdiya alahu amran kana mafaulan wa ilalahi turjau al umur
8:45	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha laqitum fi atan fathbutu wathkurualaha kathiran lalakum tuflihun
8:46	watiau alaha warasulahuwala tanazau fatafshalu watathaba rihukumwasbiru ina alaha ma asabirin
8:47	wala takunu kalathinakharaju min diyarihim bataran wari a anasiwayasuduna an sabili alahi walahubima yamaluna muhit
8:48	wa ith zayana lahumu ashshaytanuamalahum waqala la ghaliba lakumualyawma mina anasi wa ini jarun lakumfalama tarati alfi atani nakasala aqibayhi waqala ini bari un minkum iniara ma la tarawna ini akhafu alahawalahu shadidu alaiqab
8:49	ith yaqulu almunafiquna walathinafi qulubihim maradun ghara haula idinuhum waman yatawakal ala alahi fa ina alahazizun hakim
8:50	walaw tara ith yatawafalathina kafaru almala ikatu yadribunawujuhahum wadbarahum wathuqu athaba alhariq
8:51	thalika bima qadamat aydikumwana alaha laysa bithalaminlilabid
8:52	kada bi ali firawna walathinamin qablihim kafaru bi ayati alahi fakhathahumualahu bithunubihim ina alaha qawiyunshadidu alaiqab
8:53	thalika bi ana alaha lamyaku mughayiran niamatan anamaha ala qawmin hatayughayiru ma bi anfusihim wana alaha samiaunalim
8:54	kada bi ali firawna walathinamin qablihim kaththabu bi ayati rabihimfahlaknahum bithunubihim waghraqna alafirawna wakulun kanu thalimin
8:55	ina shara adawabi aindalahi alathina kafaru fahum la yu minun
8:56	alathina ahadta minhumthuma yanquduna ahdahum fi kuli maratin wahum layataqun
8:57	fa ima tathqafanahum fi alharbifasharid bihim man khalfahum lalahum yaththakarun
8:58	wa ima takhafana min qawminkhiyanatan fanbith ilayhim alasawa in ina alaha la yuhibu alkha inin
8:59	wala yahsabana alathinakafaru sabaqu inahum la yuajizun
8:60	waidu lahum ma istatatumin quwatin wamin ribati alkhayli turhibuna bihiaduwa alahi waduwakum wakharina mindunihim la talamunahumu alahu yalamuhum wamatunfiqu min shay in fi sabili alahi yuwafa ilaykumwantum la tuthlamun
8:61	wa in janahu lisalmi fajnahlaha watawakal ala alahi inahu huwa asamiaualalim
8:62	wa in yuridu an yakhdauka fa ina hasbakalahu huwa alathi ayadaka binasrihi wabilmu minin
8:63	walafa bayna qulubihim law anfaqta mafi al ardi jamian ma alafta bayna qulubihimwalakina alaha alafa baynahum inahu azizun hakim
8:64	ya ayuha anabiyu hasbukalahu wamani itabaka mina almu minin
8:65	ya ayuha anabiyu haridialmu minina ala alqitali in yakun minkumaishruna sabiruna yaghlibu mi atayni wa in yakunminkum mi atun yaghlibu alfan mina alathina kafarubi anahum qawmun la yafqahun
8:66	al ana khafafa alahu ankumwalima ana fikum dafan fa in yakun minkum mi atun sabiratunyaghlibu mi atayni wa in yakun minkum alfun yaghlibu alfaynibi ithni alahi walahu ma asabirin
8:67	ma kana linabiyin an yakunalahu asra hata yuthkhina fi al ardituriduna arada adunya walahuyuridu al akhirata walahu azizun hakim
8:68	lawla kitabun mina alahisabaqa lamasakum fima akhathtum athabunathim
8:69	fakulu mima ghanimtum halalantayiban wataqu alaha ina alahaghafurun rahim
8:70	ya ayuha anabiyuqul liman fi aydikum mina al asra in yalami alahufi qulubikum khayran yu tikum khayran mima ukhithaminkum wayaghfir lakum walahu ghafurun rahim
8:71	wa in yuridu khiyanataka faqad khanualaha min qablu famkana minhum walahualimun hakim
8:72	ina alathina amanu wahajaruwajahadu bi amwalihim wanfusihim fi sabili alahiwalathina awaw wanasaru ula ikabaduhum awliyau badin walathinamanu walam yuhajiru ma lakum min walayatihimin shay in hata yuhajiru wa ini istansarukumfi adini falaykumu anasru ilala qawmin baynakum wabaynahum mithaqun walahubima tamaluna basir
8:73	walathina kafaru baduhumawliyau badin ila tafaluhu takunfitnatun fi al ardi wafasadun kabir
8:74	walathina amanu wahajaruwajahadu fi sabili alahi walathinawaw wanasaru ula ika humu almu minuna haqanlahum maghfiratun warizqun karim
8:75	walathina amanu minbadu wahajaru wajahadu makum faula ikaminkum waulu al arhami baduhum awlabibadin fi kitabi alahi ina alahabikuli shay in alim
9:1	baratun mina alahiwarasulihi ila alathina ahadtum minalmushrikin
9:2	fasihu fi al ardi arbatashurin walamu anakum ghayru muajizi alahiwana alaha mukhzi alkafirin
9:3	wathanun mina alahiwarasulihi ila anasi yawma alhajial akbari ana alaha bari un mina almushrikinawarasuluhu fa in tubtum fahuwa khayrun lakum wa in tawalaytumfalamu anakum ghayru muajizi alahiwabashshiri alathina kafaru biathabin alim
9:4	ila alathina ahadtumina almushrikina thuma lam yanqusukum shay an walam yuthahirualaykum ahadan fatimu ilayhim ahdahum ilamudatihim ina alaha yuhibu almutaqin
9:5	fa itha insalakha al ashuru alhurumufaqtulu almushrikina haythu wajadtumuhum wakhuthuhumwahsuruhum waqaudu lahum kula marsadinfa in tabu waqamu asalatawatawu azakata fakhalu sabilahum inalaha ghafurun rahim
9:6	wa in ahadun mina almushrikina istajarakafajirhu hata yasma kalama alahithuma ablighu ma manahu thalika bi anahum qawmun layalamun
9:7	kayfa yakunu lilmushrikina ahdun aindalahi wainda rasulihi ila alathina ahadtumainda almasjidi alharami fama istaqamulakum fastaqimu lahum ina alaha yuhibualmutaqin
9:8	kayfa wa in yatharu alaykumla yarqubu fikum ilan wala thimatan yurdunakumbi afwahihim wata ba qulubuhum waktharuhum fasiqun
9:9	ishtaraw bi ayati alahithamanan qalilan fasadu an sabilihi inahum sama kanu yamalun
9:10	la yarqubuna fi mu minin ilan walathimatan waula ika humu almuatadun
9:11	fa in tabu waqamu asalatawatawu azakata fa ikhwanukum fiadini wanufasilu al ayatiliqawmin yalamun
9:12	wa in nakathu aymanahum min badiahdihim watanu fi dinikum faqatilua imata alkufri inahum la aymana lahumlalahum yantahun
9:13	ala tuqatiluna qawmanakathu aymanahum wahamu bi ikhraji arasuliwahum badaukum awala maratin atakhshawnahum falahuahaqu an takhshawhu in kuntum mu minin
9:14	qatiluhum yuaththibhumu alahubi aydikum wayukhzihim wayansurkum alayhim wayashfi suduraqawmin mu minin
9:15	wayuthib ghaythaqulubihim wayatubu alahu ala man yashauwalahu alimun hakim
9:16	am hasibtum an tutraku walamayalami alahu alathina jahadu minkumwalam yatakhithu min duni alahi walarasulihi wala almu minina walijatan walahukhabirun bima tamalun
9:17	ma kana lilmushrikina anyamuru masajida alahi shahidina alanfusihim bilkufri ula ika habitatamaluhum wafi anari hum khalidun
9:18	inama yamuru masajida alahiman amana bilahi walyawmi al akhiriwaqama asalata watazakata walam yakhsha ila alahafasa ula ika an yakunu mina almuhtadin
9:19	ajaltum siqayata alhajiwaimarata almasjidi alharami kaman amanabilahi walyawmi al akhiri wajahadafi sabili alahi la yastawuna ainda alahiwalahu la yahdi alqawma aththalimin
9:20	alathina amanu wahajaruwajahadu fi sabili alahi bi amwalihimwanfusihim athamu darajatan ainda alahiwaula ika humu alfa izun
9:21	yubashshiruhum rabuhum birahmatinminhu waridwanin wajanatin lahum fihanaimun muqim
9:22	khalidina fiha abadan inalaha aindahu ajrun athim
9:23	ya ayuha alathina amanula tatakhithu abakum wa ikhwanakumawliya ini istahabu alkufra ala al imaniwaman yatawalahum minkum faula ika humu aththalimun
9:24	qul in kana abaukumwabnaukum wa ikhwanukum wazwajukumwashiratukum wamwalun iqtaraftumuha watijaratuntakhshawna kasadaha wamasakinu tardawnahahaba ilaykum mina alahi warasulihi wajihadinfi sabilihi fatarabasu hata ya tiya alahubi amrihi walahu la yahdi alqawma alfasiqin
9:25	laqad nasarakumu alahu fimawatina kathiratin wayawma hunaynin ithajabatkum kathratukum falam tughni ankum shay an wadaqatalaykumu al ardu bima rahubat thumawalaytum mudbirin
9:26	thuma anzala alahu sakinatahu alarasulihi wala almu minina wanzala junudan lamtarawha waththaba alathina kafaru wathalikajazau alkafirin
9:27	thuma yatubu alahu min badi thalikala man yashau walahu ghafurunrahim
9:28	ya ayuha alathina amanuinama almushrikuna najasun fala yaqrabualmasjida alharama bada amihim hathawa in khiftum aylatan fasawfa yughnikumu alahu min fadlihin sha ina alaha alimun hakim
9:29	qatilu alathina layu minuna bilahi wala bilyawmial akhiri wala yuharimuna ma haramalahu warasuluhu wala yadinuna dina alhaqimina alathina utu alkitaba hatayuatu aljizyata an yadin wahum saghirun
9:30	waqalati alyahudu auzayrun ibnu alahiwaqalati anasara almasihuibnu alahi thalika qawluhum bi afwahihim yudahi unaqawla alathina kafaru min qablu qatalahumu alahuana yu fakun
9:31	itakhathu ahbarahumwaruhbanahum arbaban min duni alahi walmasihaibna maryama wama umiru ila liyabudu ilahanwahidan la ilaha ila huwa subhanahuama yushrikun
9:32	yuriduna an yutfi u nura alahibi afwahihim waya ba alahu ila anyutima nurahu walaw kariha alkafirun
9:33	huwa alathi arsala rasulahu bilhudawadini alhaqi liyuthirahu ala adinikulihi walaw kariha almushrikun
9:34	ya ayuha alathina amanuina kathiran mina al ahbari waruhbanilaya kuluna amwala anasi bilbatiliwayasuduna an sabili alahi walathinayaknizuna aththahaba walfidatawala yunfiqunaha fi sabili alahifabashshirhum biathabin alim
9:35	yawma yuhma alayhafi nari jahanama fatukwa biha jibahuhumwajunubuhum wathuhuruhum hatha makanaztum li anfusikum fathuqu ma kuntum taknizun
9:36	ina aidata ashshuhuri ainda alahithna ashara shahran fi kitabi alahiyawma khalaqa asamawati wal ardaminha arbatun hurumun thalika adinualqayimu fala tathlimu fihina anfusakumwaqatilu almushrikina kafatan kama yuqatilunakumkafatan walamu ana alaha malmutaqin
9:37	inama anasi u ziyadatunfi alkufri yudalu bihi alathina kafaru yuhilunahuaman wayuharimunahu aman liyuwati uaidata ma harama alahu fayuhiluma harama alahu zuyina lahum su u amalihimwalahu la yahdi alqawma alkafirin
9:38	ya ayuha alathina amanuma lakum itha qila lakumu infiru fi sabili alahiththaqaltum ila al ardi araditum bilhayatiadunya mina al akhirati fama mataualhayati adunya fi al akhiratila qalil
9:39	ila tanfiru yuaththibkumathaban aliman wayastabdil qawman ghayrakum walataduruhu shay an walahu alakuli shay in qadir
9:40	ila tansuruhu faqad nasarahualahu ith akhrajahu alathina kafaru thaniyaithnayni ith huma fi alghari ithyaqulu lisahibihi la tahzan ina alahamana fanzala alahu sakinatahu alayhiwayadahu bijunudin lam tarawha wajala kalimata alathinakafaru asufla wakalimatu alahi hiyalaulya walahu azizun hakim
9:41	infiru khifafan wathiqalanwajahidu bi amwalikum wanfusikum fi sabili alahithalikum khayrun lakum in kuntum talamun
9:42	law kana aradan qaribanwasafaran qasidan latabauka walakinbaudat alayhimu ashshuqatu wasayahlifuna bilahilawi istatana lakharajna makumyuhlikuna anfusahum walahu yalamu inahum lakathibun
9:43	afa alahu anka lima athintalahum hata yatabayana laka alathina sadaquwatalama alkathibin
9:44	la yasta thinuka alathinayu minuna bilahi walyawmi al akhirian yujahidu bi amwalihim wanfusihim walahualimun bilmutaqin
9:45	inama yasta thinuka alathinala yu minuna bilahi walyawmi al akhiriwartabat qulubuhum fahum fi raybihimyataradadun
9:46	walaw aradu alkhuruja ladulahu audatan walakin kariha alahu inbiathahumfathabatahum waqila uqaudu ma alqaidin
9:47	law kharaju fikum ma zadukumila khabalan walawdau khilalakumyabghunakumu alfitnata wafikum samauna lahum walahualimun biththalimin
9:48	laqadi ibtaghawu alfitnata min qabluwaqalabu laka al umura hata ja alhaquwathahara amru alahi wahum karihun
9:49	waminhum man yaqulu i than li walataftini ala fi alfitnati saqatu wa inajahanama lamuhitatun bilkafirin
9:50	in tusibka hasanatun tasu humwa in tusibka musibatun yaqulu qad akhathnamrana min qablu wayatawalaw wahum farihun
9:51	qul lan yusibana ilama kataba alahu lana huwa mawlanawala alahi falyatawakali almu minun
9:52	qul hal tarabasuna bina ilaihda alhusnayayni wanahnu natarabasubikum an yusibakumu alahu biathabin minaindihi aw bi aydina fatarabasu inamakum mutarabisun
9:53	qul anfiqu tawan aw karhan lanyutaqabala minkum inakum kuntum qawman fasiqin
9:54	wama manahum an tuqbala minhumnafaqatuhum ila anahum kafaru bilahiwabirasulihi wala ya tuna asalataila wahum kusala wala yunfiquna ilawahum karihun
9:55	fala tuajibka amwaluhum walawladuhum inama yuridu alahu liyuaththibahumbiha fi alhayati adunyawatazhaqa anfusuhum wahum kafirun
9:56	wayahlifuna bilahinahum laminkum wama hum minkum walakinahumqawmun yafraqun
9:57	law yajiduna maljan aw magharatinaw mudakhalan lawalaw ilayhi wahum yajmahun
9:58	waminhum man yalmizuka fi asadaqatifa in uatu minha radu wa in lam yuatawminha itha hum yaskhatun
9:59	walaw anahum radu ma atahumualahu warasuluhu waqalu hasbunalahu sayu tina alahu min fadlihiwarasuluhu ina ila alahi raghibun
9:60	inama asadaqatulilfuqara i walmasakini walamilinalayha walmu alafati qulubuhum wafi ariqabiwalgharimina wafi sabili alahi wabniasabili faridatan mina alahi walahualimun hakimun
9:61	waminhumu alathina yu thunanabiya wayaquluna huwa uthunun qul uthunukhayrin lakum yu minu bilahi wayu minulilmu minina warahmatun lilathina amanuminkum walathina yu thuna rasula alahilahum athabun alim
9:62	yahlifuna bilahilakum liyurdukum walahu warasuluhu ahaquan yurduhu in kanu mu minin
9:63	alam yalamu anahu man yuhadidialaha warasulahu fana lahu nara jahanama khalidanfiha thalika alkhizyu alathim
9:64	yahtharu almunafiquna antunazala alayhim suratun tunabi uhum bima fiqulubihim quli istahzi u ina alaha mukhrijun matahtharun
9:65	wala in saltahum layaquluna inamakuna nakhudu wanalabu qul abilahiwayatihi warasulihi kuntum tastahzi un
9:66	la tatathiru qad kafartumbada imanikum in nafu an ta ifatin minkumnuaththib ta ifatan bi anahum kanumujrimin
9:67	almunafiquna walmunafiqatubaduhum min badin ya muruna bilmunkariwayanhawna ani almarufi wayaqbiduna aydiyahum nasualaha fanasiyahum ina almunafiqina humu alfasiqun
9:68	wada alahu almunafiqinawalmunafiqati walkufara narajahanama khalidina fiha hiya hasbuhumwalanahumu alahu walahum athabun muqim
9:69	kalathina min qablikum kanuashada minkum quwatan wakthara amwalan wawladanfastamtau bikhalaqihim fastamtatumbikhalaqikum kama istamta alathina minqablikum bikhalaqihim wakhudtum kalathikhadu ula ika habitat amaluhumfi adunya wal akhirati waula ikahumu alkhasirun
9:70	alam ya tihim nabau alathina minqablihim qawmi nuhin wadin wathamuda waqawmibrahima was habi madyana walmu tafikatiatat hum rusuluhum bilbayinati fama kanalahu liyathlimahum walakin kanuanfusahum yathlimun
9:71	walmu minuna walmu minatubaduhum awliyau badin ya muruna bilmarufiwayanhawna ani almunkari wayuqimuna asalatawayu tuna azakata wayutiauna alahawarasulahu ula ika sayarhamuhumu alahuina alaha azizun hakim
9:72	wada alahu almu minina walmu minatijanatin tajri min tahtiha al anharukhalidina fiha wamasakina tayibatanfi janati adnin waridwanun mina alahiakbaru thalika huwa alfawzu alathim
9:73	ya ayuha anabiyu jahidialkufara walmunafiqina waghluthalayhim wama wahum jahanamu wabi sa almasir
9:74	yahlifuna bilahi maqalu walaqad qalu kalimata alkufri wakafarubada islamihim wahamu bima lam yanaluwama naqamu ila an aghnahumu alahuwarasuluhu min fadlihi fa in yatubu yaku khayran lahumwa in yatawalaw yuaththibhumu alahu athabanaliman fi adunya wal akhiratiwama lahum fi al ardi min waliyin wala nasir
9:75	waminhum man ahada alahala in atana min fadlihi lanasadaqanawalanakunana mina asalihin
9:76	falama atahum min fadlihibakhilu bihi watawalaw wahum muaridun
9:77	faqabahum nifaqan fi qulubihimila yawmi yalqawnahu bima akhlafu alaha mawaduhu wabima kanu yakthibun
9:78	alam yalamu ana alaha yalamusirahum wanajwahum wana alaha alamualghuyub
9:79	alathina yalmizuna almutawiainamina almu minina fi asadaqati walathinala yajiduna ila juhdahum fayaskharuna minhumsakhira alahu minhum walahum athabun alim
9:80	istaghfir lahum aw la tastaghfirlahum in tastaghfir lahum sabaina maratan falan yaghfira alahulahum thalika bi anahum kafaru bilahiwarasulihi walahu la yahdi alqawma alfasiqin
9:81	fariha almukhalafuna bimaqadihimkhilafa rasuli alahi wakarihu an yujahidubi amwalihim wanfusihim fi sabili alahi waqalula tanfiru fi alhari qul naru jahanamashadu haran law kanu yafqahun
9:82	falyadhaku qalilan walyabkukathiran jazan bima kanu yaksibun
9:83	fa in rajaka alahu ila ta ifatinminhum fasta thanuka lilkhuruji faqul lantakhruju maiya abadan walan tuqatilu maiya aduwaninakum raditum bilquaudi awala maratin faqauduma alkhalifin
9:84	wala tusali ala ahadinminhum mata abadan wala taqum ala qabrihinahum kafaru bilahi warasulihi wamatuwahum fasiqun
9:85	wala tuajibka amwaluhum wawladuhuminama yuridu alahu an yuaththibahum bihafi adunya watazhaqa anfusuhum wahum kafirun
9:86	wa itha unzilat suratun an aminubilahi wajahidu ma rasulihi ista thanakaulu atawli minhum waqalu tharnanakun ma alqaidin
9:87	radu bi an yakunu ma alkhawalifiwatubia ala qulubihim fahum layafqahun
9:88	lakini arasulu walathinamanu mahu jahadu bi amwalihimwanfusihim waula ika lahumu alkhayratu waula ikahumu almuflihun
9:89	ada alahu lahum janatintajri min tahtiha al anharu khalidinafiha thalika alfawzu alathim
9:90	waja almuaththiruna minal arabi liyu thana lahum waqada alathinakathabu alaha warasulahu sayusibu alathinakafaru minhum athabun alim
9:91	laysa ala aduafa iwala ala almarda wala alalathina la yajiduna ma yunfiquna harajunitha nasahu lilahi warasulihi mala almuhsinina min sabilin walahughafurun rahim
9:92	wala ala alathina ithama atawka litahmilahum qulta la ajidu mahmilukum alayhi tawalaw wayunuhum tafidumina adamai hazanan ala yajidu mayunfiqun
9:93	inama asabilu alalathina yasta thinunaka wahum aghniyauradu bi an yakunu ma alkhawalifi watabalahu ala qulubihim fahum la yalamun
9:94	yatathiruna ilaykum itharajatum ilayhim qul la tatathiru lan nu minalakum qad nabana alahu min akhbarikumwasayara alahu amalakum warasuluhu thumaturaduna ila alimi alghaybi washshahadatifayunabi ukum bima kuntum tamalun
9:95	sayahlifuna bilahilakum itha inqalabtum ilayhim lituaridu anhumfaridu anhum inahum rijsun wama wahumjahanamu jazan bima kanu yaksibun
9:96	yahlifuna lakum litardawanhum fa in tardaw anhum fa ina alaha layarda ani alqawmi alfasiqin
9:97	al arabu ashadu kufran wanifaqanwajdaru ala yalamu hududa ma anzalalahu ala rasulihi walahualimun hakim
9:98	wamina al arabi man yatakhithuma yunfiqu maghraman wayatarabasu bikumu adawa iralayhim da iratu asaw i walahusamiaun alim
9:99	wamina al arabi man yu minu bilahiwalyawmi al akhiri wayatakhithu mayunfiqu qurubatin ainda alahi wasalawatiarasuli ala inaha qurbatun lahumsayudkhiluhumu alahu fi rahmatihi ina alahaghafurun rahim
9:100	wasabiquna al awalunamina almuhajirina wal ansari walathinaitabauhum bi ihsanin radiya alahuanhum waradu anhu wada lahum janatintajri tahtaha al anharu khalidinafiha abadan thalika alfawzu alathim
9:101	wamiman hawlakum mina al arabimunafiquna wamin ahli almadinati maradu ala anifaqila talamuhum nahnu nalamuhum sanuaththibuhumaratayni thuma yuraduna ila athabin athim
9:102	wakharuna iatarafu bithunubihimkhalatu amalan salihan wakharasayi an asa alahu an yatuba alayhim ina alahaghafurun rahim
9:103	khuth min amwalihim sadaqatantutahiruhum watuzakihim biha wasalialayhim ina salataka sakanun lahum walahusamiaun alim
9:104	alam yalamu ana alaha huwayaqbalu atawbata an aibadihi waya khuthuasadaqati wana alaha huwa atawabuarahim
9:105	waquli iamalu fasayara alahuamalakum warasuluhu walmu minuna wasaturaduna ilalimi alghaybi washshahadatifayunabi ukum bima kuntum tamalun
9:106	wakharuna murjawna li amri alahima yuaththibuhum wa ima yatubualayhim walahu alimun hakim
9:107	walathina itakhathumasjidan diraran wakufran watafriqan baynalmu minina wa irsadan liman haraba alahawarasulahu min qablu walayahlifuna in aradna ilalhusna walahu yashadu inahum lakathibun
9:108	la taqum fihi abadan lamasjidunusisa ala ataqwa min awali yawmin ahaquan taquma fihi fihi rijalun yuhibuna an yatataharuwalahu yuhibu almutahirin
9:109	afaman asasa bunyanahu alataqwa mina alahi waridwanin khayrunam man asasa bunyanahu ala shafa jurufinharin fanhara bihi fi narijahanama walahu la yahdi alqawma aththalimin
9:110	la yazalu bunyanuhumualathi banaw ribatan fi qulubihim ila an taqataqulubuhum walahu alimun hakim
9:111	ina alaha ishtara minalmu minina anfusahum wamwalahum bi ana lahumualjanata yuqatiluna fi sabili alahifayaqtuluna wayuqtaluna wadan alayhi haqan fi atawratiwal injili walqur ani waman awfabiahdihi mina alahi fastabshiru bibayaikumualathi bayatum bihi wathalika huwalfawzu alathim
9:112	ata ibuna alabidunalhamiduna asa ihuna arakiaunasajiduna al amiruna bilmarufiwanahuna ani almunkari walhafithunalihududi alahi wabashshiri almu minin
9:113	ma kana linabiyi walathinamanu an yastaghfiru lilmushrikina walaw kanuli qurba min badi ma tabayana lahum anahum as habualjahim
9:114	wama kana istighfaruibrahima li abihi ila an mawaidatin wadahaiyahu falama tabayana lahu anahu aduwun lilahitabara minhu ina ibrahima lawahun halim
9:115	wama kana alahu liyudilaqawman bada ith hadahum hatayubayina lahum ma yataquna ina alaha bikulishay in alim
9:116	ina alaha lahu mulku asamawatiwal ardi yuhyi wayumitu wama lakumin duni alahi min waliyin wala nasir
9:117	laqad taba alahu alanabiyi walmuhajirina wal ansarialathina itabauhu fi sati alausrati minbadi ma kada yazighu qulubu fariqin minhumthuma taba alayhim inahu bihim raufun rahim
9:118	wala aththalathatialathina khulifu hata itha daqatalayhimu al ardu bima rahubat wadaqatalayhim anfusuhum wathanu an la maljamina alahi ila ilayhi thuma tabalayhim liyatubu ina alaha huwa atawabuarahim
9:119	ya ayuha alathina amanuitaqu alaha wakunu ma asadiqin
9:120	ma kana li ahli almadinatiwaman hawlahum mina al arabi an yatakhalafuan rasuli alahi wala yarghabu bi anfusihiman nafsihi thalika bi anahum la yusibuhumthamaun wala nasabun walamakhmasatun fi sabili alahi wala yataunamawti an yaghithu alkufara walayanaluna min aduwin naylan ila kutiba lahumbihi amalun salihun ina alaha layudiau ajra almuhsinin
9:121	wala yunfiquna nafaqatan saghiratanwala kabiratan wala yaqtauna wadiyanila kutiba lahum liyajziyahumu alahu ahsanama kanu yamalun
9:122	wama kana almu minunaliyanfiru kafatan falawla nafara min kulifirqatin minhum ta ifatun liyatafaqahu fi adiniwaliyunthiru qawmahum itha rajau ilayhimlalahum yahtharun
9:123	ya ayuha alathina amanuqatilu alathina yalunakum mina alkufariwalyajidu fikum ghilthatan walamu analaha ma almutaqin
9:124	wa itha ma unzilat suratunfaminhum man yaqulu ayukum zadat hu hathihi imananfama alathina amanu fazadat humimanan wahum yastabshirun
9:125	wama alathina fiqulubihim maradun fazadat hum rijsan ilarijsihim wamatu wahum kafirun
9:126	awa la yarawna anahum yuftanunafi kuli amin maratan aw maratayni thuma layatubuna wala hum yaththakarun
9:127	wa itha ma unzilat suratunathara baduhum ila badinhal yarakum min ahadin thuma insarafu sarafalahu qulubahum bi anahum qawmun la yafqahun
9:128	laqad jakum rasulun min anfusikumazizun alayhi ma anitum harisunalaykum bilmu minina raufun rahim
9:129	fa in tawalaw faqul hasbiya alahula ilaha ila huwa alayhi tawakaltuwahuwa rabu alarshi alathim
10:1	alif lam ra tilka ayatualkitabi alhakim
10:2	akana linasi ajabanan awhayna ila rajulin minhum an anthirianasa wabashshiri alathina amanuana lahum qadama sidqin ainda rabihim qala alkafirunaina hatha lasahirun mubin
10:3	ina rabakumu alahu alathikhalaqa asamawati wal ardafi sitati ayamin thuma istawa alalarshi yudabiru al amra ma min shafiain ilamin badi ithnihi thalikumu alahu rabukumfabuduhu afala tathakarun
10:4	ilayhi marjiaukum jamian wada alahihaqan inahu yabdau alkhalqa thuma yuaiduhu liyajziyalathina amanu wamilu asalihatibilqisti walathina kafaru lahumsharabun min hamimin wathabun alimunbima kanu yakfurun
10:5	huwa alathi jala ashshamsadiyan walqamara nuran waqadarahu manazilalitalamu adada asinina walhisabama khalaqa alahu thalika ila bilhaqiyufasilu al ayati liqawmin yalamun
10:6	ina fi ikhtilafi alayli wanahariwama khalaqa alahu fi asamawatiwal ardi layatin liqawmin yataqun
10:7	ina alathina la yarjuna liqanawaradu bilhayati adunyawatmanu biha walathinahum an ayatina ghafilun
10:8	ula ika ma wahumu anarubima kanu yaksibun
10:9	ina alathina amanuwamilu asalihati yahdihim rabuhumbi imanihim tajri min tahtihimu al anharufi janati anaim
10:10	dawahum fiha subhanakalahuma watahiyatuhum fiha salamunwakhiru dawahum ani alhamdu lilahirabi alalamin
10:11	walaw yuajilu alahu linasiashshara istiajalahum bilkhayri laqudiyailayhim ajaluhum fanatharu alathina layarjuna liqana fi tughyanihimyamahun
10:12	wa itha masa al insana adurudana lijanbihi aw qaidan aw qa imanfalama kashafna anhu durahu mara kanlam yadauna ila durin masahu kathalikazuyina lilmusrifina ma kanu yamalun
10:13	walaqad ahlakna alquruna minqablikum lama thalamu wajat humrusuluhum bilbayinati wama kanuliyu minu kathalika najzi alqawma almujrimin
10:14	thuma jalnakum khala ifafi al ardi min badihim linanthura kayfatamalun
10:15	wa itha tutla alayhim ayatunabayinatin qala alathina layarjuna liqana i ti biqur anin ghayri hathaw badilhu qul ma yakunu li an ubadilahu min tilqa inafsi in atabiau ila ma yuha ilayaini akhafu in asaytu rabi athabayawmin athim
10:16	qul law sha alahu matalawtuhu alaykum wala adrakum bihi faqadlabithtu fikum aumuran min qablihi afala taqilun
10:17	faman athlamu mimani iftarala alahi kathiban aw kaththababi ayatihi inahu la yuflihualmujrimun
10:18	wayabuduna min duni alahi mala yaduruhum wala yanfauhum wayaqulunahaula i shufauna ainda alahiqul atunabi una alaha bima la yalamufi asamawati wala fi al ardisubhanahu watala ama yushrikun
10:19	wama kana anasuila umatan wahidatan fakhtalafu walawlakalimatun sabaqat min rabika laqudiya baynahum fimafihi yakhtalifun
10:20	wayaquluna lawla unzila alayhi ayatunmin rabihi faqul inama alghaybu lilahi fantathiruini makum mina almuntathirin
10:21	wa itha athaqna anasarahmatan min badi dara masat hum ithalahum makrun fi ayatina quli alahuasrau makran ina rusulana yaktubuna matamkurun
10:22	huwa alathi yusayirukum fialbari walbahri hata ithakuntum fi alfulki wajarayna bihim birihin tayibatinwafarihu biha jat ha rihunasifun wajahumu almawju min kuli makaninwathanu anahum uhita bihimdawu alaha mukhlisina lahu adinala in anjaytana min hathihi lanakunana mina ashshakirin
10:23	falama anjahum ithahum yabghuna fi al ardi bighayri alhaqi yayuha anasu inama baghyukum alanfusikum mata alhayati adunyathuma ilayna marjiaukum fanunabi ukum bimakuntum tamalun
10:24	inama mathalu alhayatiadunya kama in anzalnahu mina asama ifakhtalata bihi nabatu al ardi mimaya kulu anasu wal anamu hataitha akhathati al ardu zukhrufaha wazayanatwathana ahluha anahum qadirunalayha ataha amruna laylan aw naharanfajalnaha hasidan kan lam taghnabil amsi kathalika nufasilu al ayatiliqawmin yatafakarun
10:25	walahu yadau ila dariasalami wayahdi man yashau ila siratinmustaqim
10:26	lilathina ahsanu alhusnawaziyadatun wala yarhaqu wujuhahum qatarun walathilatun ula ika as habu aljanatihum fiha khalidun
10:27	walathina kasabu asayi atijazau sayi atin bimithliha watarhaquhum thilatunma lahum mina alahi min asimin kanamaughshiyat wujuhuhum qitan mina alayli muthlimanula ika as habu anari humfiha khalidun
10:28	wayawma nahshuruhum jamian thumanaqulu lilathina ashraku makanakum antumwashurakaukum fazayalna baynahum waqalashurakauhum ma kuntum iyanatabudun
10:29	fakafa bilahishahidan baynana wabaynakum in kuna an aibadatikumlaghafilin
10:30	hunalika tablu kulu nafsin maslafat warudu ila alahi mawlahumu alhaqiwadala anhum ma kanu yaftarun
10:31	qul man yarzuqukum mina asama iwal ardi aman yamliku asama wal absarawaman yukhriju alhaya mina almayiti wayukhriju almayitamina alhayi waman yudabiru al amra fasayaquluna alahufaqul afala tataqun
10:32	fathalikumu alahu rabukumualhaqu famatha bada alhaqi ila adalalufana tusrafun
10:33	kathalika haqat kalimaturabika ala alathina fasaqu anahum layu minun
10:34	qul hal min shuraka ikum man yabdaualkhalqa thuma yuaiduhu quli alahu yabdau alkhalqathuma yuaiduhu fana tu fakun
10:35	qul hal min shuraka ikum man yahdila alhaqi quli alahu yahdi lilhaqiafaman yahdi ila alhaqi ahaqu anyutaba aman la yahidi ila an yuhdafama lakum kayfa tahkumun
10:36	wama yatabiau aktharuhum ilathanan ina aththana layughni mina alhaqi shay an ina alaha alimunbima yafalun
10:37	wama kana hatha alqur anuan yuftara min duni alahi walakin tasdiqalathi bayna yadayhi watafsila alkitabi larayba fihi min rabi alalamin
10:38	am yaquluna iftarahu qul fa tubisuratin mithlihi wadau mani istatatum minduni alahi in kuntum sadiqin
10:39	bal kaththabu bima lam yuhitubiailmihi walama ya tihim ta wiluhu kathalika kaththabalathina min qablihim fanthurkayfa kana aqibatu aththalimin
10:40	waminhum man yu minu bihi waminhum man layu minu bihi warabuka alamu bilmufsidin
10:41	wa in kaththabuka faqul li amaliwalakum amalukum antum bari una mima amalu wanabari un mima tamalun
10:42	waminhum man yastamiauna ilayka afantatusmiau asuma walaw kanu layaqilun
10:43	waminhum man yanthuru ilaykafanta tahdi alaumya walaw kanu la yubsirun
10:44	ina alaha la yathlimuanasa shay an walakina anasanfusahum yathlimun
10:45	wayawma yahshuruhum kan lamyalbathu ila satan mina anahariyatarafuna baynahum qad khasira alathina kaththabubiliqa i alahi wama kanu muhtadin
10:46	wa ima nuriyanaka badalathi naiduhum aw natawafayanaka fa ilaynamarjiauhum thuma alahu shahidun ala mayafalun
10:47	walikuli umatin rasulun fa itha jarasuluhum qudiya baynahum bilqisti wahum layuthlamun
10:48	wayaquluna mata hathalwadu in kuntum sadiqin
10:49	qul la amliku linafsi daranwala nafan ila ma sha alahulikuli umatin ajalun itha ja ajaluhum falayasta khiruna satan wala yastaqdimun
10:50	qul araytum in atakum athabuhubayatan aw naharan matha yastajilu minhualmujrimun
10:51	athuma itha ma waqa amantumbihi al ana waqad kuntum bihi tastajilun
10:52	thuma qila lilathina thalamuthuqu athaba alkhuldi hal tujzawna ilabima kuntum taksibun
10:53	wayastanbi unaka ahaqun huwa qul iwarabi inahu lahaqun wama antum bimuajizin
10:54	walaw ana likuli nafsin thalamatma fi al ardi laftadat bihi wasaru anadamatalama rawu alathaba waqudiya baynahum bilqistiwahum la yuthlamun
10:55	ala ina lilahi ma fiasamawati wal ardi alaina wada alahi haqun walakinaktharahum la yalamun
10:56	huwa yuhyi wayumitu wa ilayhiturjaun
10:57	ya ayuha anasuqad jatkum mawaithatun min rabikum washifaunlima fi asuduri wahudan warahmatunlilmu minin
10:58	qul bifadli alahi wabirahmatihifabithalika falyafrahu huwa khayrun mimayajmaun
10:59	qul araytum ma anzala alahulakum min rizqin fajaltum minhu haraman wahalalanqul alahu athina lakum am ala alahitaftarun
10:60	wama thanu alathinayaftaruna ala alahi alkathiba yawmalqiyamati ina alaha lathu fadlinala anasi walakina aktharahum layashkurun
10:61	wama takunu fi sha nin wamatatlu minhu min qur anin wala tamaluna minamalin ila kuna alaykum shuhudan ithtufiduna fihi wama yazubu an rabika minmithqali tharatin fi al ardi walafi asama i wala asghara min thalikawala akbara ila fi kitabin mubin
10:62	ala ina awliya alahila khawfun alayhim wala hum yahzanun
10:63	alathina amanu wakanuyataqun
10:64	lahumu albushra fi alhayatiadunya wafi al akhirati latabdila likalimati alahi thalika huwalfawzu alathim
10:65	wala yahzunka qawluhum inalaizata lilahi jamian huwa asamiaualalim
10:66	ala ina lilahi man fi asamawatiwaman fi al ardi wama yatabiau alathinayadauna min duni alahi shuraka inyatabiauna ila aththana wa inhum ila yakhrusun
10:67	huwa alathi jala lakumu alaylalitaskunu fihi wanahara mubsiran inafi thalika layatin liqawmin yasmaun
10:68	qalu itakhatha alahuwaladan subhanahu huwa alghaniyu lahu ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi in aindakum min sultaninbihatha ataquluna ala alahi ma latalamun
10:69	qul ina alathina yaftaruna alalahi alkathiba la yuflihun
10:70	mataun fi adunyathuma ilayna marjiauhum thuma nuthiquhumu alathabashshadida bima kanu yakfurun
10:71	watlu alayhim naba nuhinith qala liqawmihi ya qawmi in kanakabura alaykum maqami watathkiri bi ayatialahi fala alahi tawakaltu fajmiauamrakum washurakakum thuma la yakun amrukumalaykum ghumatan thuma iqdu ilaya wala tunthirun
10:72	fa in tawalaytum fama saltukum minajrin in ajriya ila ala alahi waumirtuan akuna mina almuslimin
10:73	fakaththabuhu fanajaynahuwaman mahu fi alfulki wajalnahum khala ifawaghraqna alathina kaththabu bi ayatinafanthur kayfa kana aqibatualmuntharin
10:74	thuma bathna min badihi rusulanila qawmihim fajauhum bilbayinatifama kanu liyu minu bima kaththabubihi min qablu kathalika natbau alaqulubi almuatadin
10:75	thuma bathna min badihim musawaharuna ila firawna wamala ihi bi ayatinafastakbaru wakanu qawman mujrimin
10:76	falama jahumu alhaqumin aindina qalu ina hatha lasihrunmubin
10:77	qala musa ataquluna lilhaqilama jakum asihrun hatha walayuflihu asahirun
10:78	qalu aji tana litalfitanama wajadna alayhi abanawatakuna lakuma alkibriyau fi al ardi wamanahnu lakuma bimu minin
10:79	waqala firawnu i tuni bikuli sahirinalim
10:80	falama ja asaharatuqala lahum musa alqu ma antum mulqun
10:81	falama alqaw qala musama ji tum bihi asihru ina alahasayubtiluhu ina alaha la yuslihuamala almufsidin
10:82	wayuhiqu alahu alhaqabikalimatihi walaw kariha almujrimun
10:83	fama amana limusa ilathuriyatun min qawmihi ala khawfin minfirawna wamala ihim an yaftinahum wa ina firawna lalinfi al ardi wa inahu lamina almusrifin
10:84	waqala musa ya qawmin kuntum amantum bilahi falayhitawakalu in kuntum muslimin
10:85	faqalu ala alahitawakalna rabana la tajalnafitnatan lilqawmi aththalimin
10:86	wanajina birahmatika minalqawmi alkafirin
10:87	wawhayna ila musawakhihi an tabawa liqawmikuma bimisrabuyutan wajalu buyutakum qiblatan waqimu asalatawabashshiri almu minin
10:88	waqala musa rabanainaka atayta firawna wamalahu zinatan wamwalanfi alhayati adunya rabanaliyudilu an sabilika rabana itmis alamwalihim washdud ala qulubihim falayu minu hata yarawu alathaba al alim
10:89	qala qad ujibat dawatukumafastaqima wala tatabianisabila alathina la yalamun
10:90	wajawazna bibani isra ilalbahra fatbahum firawnu wajunuduhu baghyanwadwan hata itha adrakahu algharaqu qalamantu anahu la ilaha ila alathiamanat bihi banu isra ila wana minalmuslimin
10:91	al ana waqad asaytaqablu wakunta mina almufsidin
10:92	falyawma nunajika bibadanikalitakuna liman khalfaka ayatan wa ina kathiran mina anasian ayatina laghafilun
10:93	walaqad bawa na bani isra ilamubawa sidqin warazaqnahum mina atayibatifama ikhtalafu hata jahumualailmu ina rabaka yaqdi baynahum yawma alqiyamatifima kanu fihi yakhtalifun
10:94	fa in kunta fi shakin mima anzalnailayka fas ali alathina yaqrauna alkitabamin qablika laqad jaka alhaqu min rabika falatakunana mina almumtarin
10:95	wala takunana mina alathinakaththabu bi ayati alahi fatakunamina alkhasirin
10:96	ina alathina haqatalayhim kalimatu rabika la yu minun
10:97	walaw jat hum kulu ayatin hatayarawu alathaba al alim
10:98	falawla kanat qaryatun amanatfanafaha imanuha ila qawmayunusa lama amanu kashafna anhum athabalkhizyi fi alhayati adunyawamatanahum ila hin
10:99	walaw sha rabuka lamana manfi al ardi kuluhum jamian afanta tukrihu anasahata yakunu mu minin
10:100	wama kana linafsin an tu minaila bi ithni alahi wayajalu arijsala alathina la yaqilun
10:101	quli unthuru mathafi asamawati wal ardi wamatughni al ayatu wanuthuru anqawmin la yu minun
10:102	fahal yantathiruna ilamithla ayami alathina khalaw min qablihim qul fantathiruini makum mina almuntathirin
10:103	thuma nunaji rusulana walathinamanu kathalika haqan alaynanunji almu minin
10:104	qul ya ayuha anasuin kuntum fi shakin min dini fala abudu alathinatabuduna min duni alahi walakin abudu alahalathi yatawafakum waumirtu an akuna minalmu minin
10:105	wan aqim wajhaka lidini hanifanwala takunana mina almushrikin
10:106	wala tadau min duni alahima la yanfauka wala yaduruka fa infalta fa inaka ithan mina aththalimin
10:107	wa in yamsaska alahu bidurinfala kashifa lahu ila huwa wa in yuridkabikhayrin fala rada lifadlihi yusibubihi man yashau min aibadihi wahuwa alghafuru arahim
10:108	qul ya ayuha anasuqad jakumu alhaqu min rabikum famani ihtadafa inama yahtadi linafsihi waman dala fa inamayadilu alayha wama ana alaykumbiwakil
10:109	watabia ma yuhailayka wasbir hata yahkumalahu wahuwa khayru alhakimin
11:1	alif lam ra kitabun uhkimatayatuhu thuma fusilat min ladun hakiminkhabir
11:2	ala tabudu ila alahainani lakum minhu nathirun wabashir
11:3	wani istaghfiru rabakum thuma tubuilayhi yumatiakum matan hasanan ilajalin musaman wayu ti kula thi fadlin fadlahuwa in tawalaw fa ini akhafu alaykum athabayawmin kabir
11:4	ila alahi marjiaukum wahuwala kuli shay in qadir
11:5	ala inahum yathnuna sudurahumliyastakhfu minhu ala hina yastaghshuna thiyabahumyalamu ma yusiruna wama yualinuna inahualimun bithati asudur
11:6	wama min dabatin fi al ardila ala alahi rizquha wayalamumustaqaraha wamustawdaha kulun fi kitabinmubin
11:7	wahuwa alathi khalaqa asamawatiwal arda fi sitati ayamin wakanarshuhu ala alma i liyabluwakum ayukum ahsanuamalan wala in qulta inakum mabauthuna min badi almawtilayaqulana alathina kafaru in hatha ilasihrun mubin
11:8	wala in akhkharna anhumu alathabaila umatin madudatin layaquluna ma yahbisuhuala yawma ya tihim laysa masrufan anhum wahaqabihim ma kanu bihi yastahzi un
11:9	wala in athaqna al insanamina rahmatan thuma nazanahaminhu inahu layausun kafur
11:10	wala in athaqnahu namabada dara masat hu layaqulana thahabasayi atu ani inahu lafarihunfakhur
11:11	ila alathina sabaruwamilu asalihati ula ika lahumaghfiratun wajrun kabir
11:12	falalaka tarikun bada mayuha ilayka wada iqun bihi sadruka anyaqulu lawla unzila alayhi kanzun aw jamahu malakun inama anta nathirun walahuala kuli shay in wakil
11:13	am yaquluna iftarahu qul fa tubiashri suwarin mithlihi muftarayatin wadaumani istatatum min duni alahi in kuntum sadiqin
11:14	fa ilam yastajibu lakum falamuanama unzila biailmi alahi wan la ilahaila huwa fahal antum muslimun
11:15	man kana yuridu alhayatadunya wazinataha nuwafi ilayhim amalahumfiha wahum fiha la yubkhasun
11:16	ula ika alathina laysa lahumfi al akhirati ila anaru wahabitama sanau fiha wabatilun makanu yamalun
11:17	afaman kana ala bayinatinmin rabihi wayatluhu shahidun minhu wamin qablihi kitabumusa imaman warahmatan ula ikayu minuna bihi waman yakfur bihi mina al ahzabi fanarumawaiduhu fala taku fi miryatin minhu inahu alhaqumin rabika walakina akthara anasi layu minun
11:18	waman athlamu mimani iftarala alahi kathiban ula ika yuaradunala rabihim wayaqulu al ashadu haula ialathina kathabu ala rabihim alalanatu alahi ala aththalimin
11:19	alathina yasuduna ansabili alahi wayabghunaha aiwajan wahum bil akhiratihum kafirun
11:20	ula ika lam yakunu muajizina fial ardi wama kana lahum min duni alahimin awliya yudafu lahumu alathabu makanu yastatiauna asama wama kanuyubsirun
11:21	ula ika alathina khasiruanfusahum wadala anhum ma kanu yaftarun
11:22	la jarama anahum fi al akhiratihumu al akhsarun
11:23	ina alathina amanuwamilu asalihati wakhbatu ilarabihim ula ika as habu aljanati hum fihakhalidun
11:24	mathalu alfariqayni kalamawal asami walbasiri wasamiaihal yastawiyani mathalan afala tathakarun
11:25	walaqad arsalna nuhan ilaqawmihi ini lakum nathirun mubin
11:26	an la tabudu ila alahaini akhafu alaykum athaba yawmin alim
11:27	faqala almalau alathinakafaru min qawmihi ma naraka ila basharanmithlana wama naraka itabaka ilalathina hum arathiluna badiya ara yiwama nara lakum alayna min fadlinbal nathunukum kathibin
11:28	qala ya qawmi araytum inkuntu ala bayinatin min rabi watanirahmatan min aindihi faumiyat alaykum anulzimukumuhawantum laha karihun
11:29	waya qawmi la as alukumalayhi malan in ajriya ila ala alahiwama ana bitaridi alathina amanuinahum mulaqu rabihim walakini arakumqawman tajhalun
11:30	waya qawmi man yansuruni minalahi in taradtuhum afala tathakarun
11:31	wala aqulu lakum aindi khaza inualahi wala alamu alghayba wala aquluini malakun wala aqulu lilathina tazdariayunukum lan yu tiyahumu alahu khayran alahualamu bima fi anfusihim ini ithan lamina aththalimin
11:32	qalu ya nuhu qad jadaltanafaktharta jidalana fa tina bimataiduna in kunta mina asadiqin
11:33	qala inama ya tikum bihi alahuin sha wama antum bimuajizin
11:34	wala yanfaukum nushi inaradtu an ansaha lakum in kana alahuyuridu an yughwiyakum huwa rabukum wa ilayhi turjaun
11:35	am yaquluna iftarahu qul iniftaraytuhu falaya ijrami wana bari un mimatujrimun
11:36	wauhiya ila nuhinanahu lan yu mina min qawmika ila man qad amanafala tabta is bima kanu yafalun
11:37	wasnai alfulka bi ayuninawawahyina wala tukhatibni fi alathinathalamu inahum mughraqun
11:38	wayasnau alfulka wakulamamara alayhi malaun min qawmihi sakhiru minhu qala intaskharu mina fa ina naskharu minkum kamataskharun
11:39	fasawfa talamuna man ya tihi athabunyukhzihi wayahilu alayhi athabun muqim
11:40	hata itha jamruna wafara atanuru qulna ihmilfiha min kulin zawjayni ithnayni wahlaka ilaman sabaqa alayhi alqawlu waman amana wama amanamahu ila qalil
11:41	waqala irkabu fiha bismi alahimajraha wamursaha ina rabilaghafurun rahim
11:42	wahiya tajri bihim fi mawjin kaljibaliwanada nuhunu ibnahu wakana fimazilin ya bunaya irkab mana walatakun ma alkafirin
11:43	qala sawi ila jabalinyasimuni mina alma i qala la asimalyawma min amri alahi ila man rahima wahalabaynahuma almawju fakana mina almughraqin
11:44	waqila ya ardu iblai makiwaya samau aqliai waghida almauwaqudiya al amru wastawat ala aljudiyiwaqila buadan lilqawmi aththalimin
11:45	wanada nuhun rabahufaqala rabi ina ibni min ahli wa ina wadaka alhaquwanta ahkamu alhakimin
11:46	qala ya nuhu inahulaysa min ahlika inahu amalun ghayru salihin falatas alni ma laysa laka bihi ailmun ini aithukan takuna mina aljahilin
11:47	qala rabi ini authu bikan as alaka ma laysa li bihi ailmun wa ilataghfir li watarhamni akun mina alkhasirin
11:48	qila ya nuhu ihbitbisalamin mina wabarakatin alayka walaumamin miman maka waumamun sanumatiauhum thuma yamasuhumina athabun alim
11:49	tilka min anba i alghaybi nuhihailayka ma kunta talamuha anta wala qawmukamin qabli hatha fasbir ina alaqibatalilmutaqin
11:50	wa ila adin akhahumhudan qala ya qawmi uabudu alaha malakum min ilahin ghayruhu in antum ila muftarun
11:51	ya qawmi la as alukum alayhiajran in ajriya ila ala alathi fataraniafala taqilun
11:52	waya qawmi istaghfiru rabakumthuma tubu ilayhi yursili asama alaykumidraran wayazidkum quwatan ila quwatikum walatatawalaw mujrimin
11:53	qalu ya hudu maji tana bibayinatin wama nahnu bitarikialihatina an qawlika wama nahnulaka bimu minin
11:54	in naqulu ila iataraka badualihatina bisu in qala ini ushidu alahawashadu ani bari un mima tushrikun
11:55	min dunihi fakiduni jamian thuma latunthirun
11:56	ini tawakaltu ala alahirabi warabikum ma min dabatin ila huwa akhithunbinasiyatiha ina rabi ala siratinmustaqim
11:57	fa in tawalaw faqad ablaghtukum maursiltu bihi ilaykum wayastakhlifu rabi qawman ghayrakum walatadurunahu shay an ina rabi ala kulishay in hafith
11:58	walama ja amrunanajayna hudan walathina amanumahu birahmatin mina wanajaynahum minathabin ghalith
11:59	watilka adun jahadu bi ayatirabihim wasaw rusulahu watabau amra kulijabarin anid
11:60	wautbiau fi hathihi adunyalanatan wayawma alqiyamati ala ina adankafaru rabahum ala buadan liadin qawmi hud
11:61	wa ila thamuda akhahum salihanqala ya qawmi uabudu alaha malakum min ilahin ghayruhu huwa anshakum mina al ardiwastamarakum fiha fastaghfiruhu thumatubu ilayhi ina rabi qaribun mujib
11:62	qalu ya salihuqad kunta fina marjuwan qabla hatha atanhanan nabuda ma yabudu abaunawa inana lafi shakin mima tadaunailayhi murib
11:63	qala ya qawmi araytum inkuntu ala bayinatin min rabi wataniminhu rahmatan faman yansuruni mina alahin asaytuhu fama tazidunani ghayra takhsir
11:64	waya qawmi hathihi naqatualahi lakum ayatan fatharuha ta kulfi ardi alahi wala tamasuhabisu in faya khuthakum athabun qarib
11:65	faqaruha faqalatamatau fi darikum thalathata ayamin thalikawadun ghayru makthub
11:66	falama ja amrunanajayna salihan walathina amanumahu birahmatin mina wamin khizyi yawmi ithinina rabaka huwa alqawiyu alaziz
11:67	wakhatha alathina thalamuasayhatu fasbahu fi diyarihimjathimin
11:68	kan lam yaghnaw fiha alaina thamuda kafaru rabahum ala buadan lithamud
11:69	walaqad jat rusuluna ibrahimabilbushra qalu salaman qalasalamun fama labitha an ja biaijlin hanith
11:70	falama ra aydiyahum latasilu ilayhi nakirahum wawjasa minhum khifatan qalula takhaf ina ursilna ila qawmi lut
11:71	wamratuhu qa imatun fadahikatfabashsharnaha bi ishaqa wamin wara ishaqa yaqub
11:72	qalat ya waylata aliduwana ajuzun wahatha bali shaykhan ina hathalashay un ajib
11:73	qalu atajabina min amri alahirahmatu alahi wabarakatuhu alaykum ahlalbayti inahu hamidun majid
11:74	falama thahaba an ibrahimarawau wajat hu albushra yujadilunafi qawmi lut
11:75	ina ibrahima lahalimun awahunmunib
11:76	ya ibrahimu aridan hatha inahu qad ja amru rabika wa inahum atihimathabun ghayru mardud
11:77	walama jat rusulunalutan si a bihim wadaqa bihim tharan waqalahatha yawmun asib
11:78	wajahu qawmuhu yuhrauna ilayhiwamin qablu kanu yamaluna asayi ati qalaya qawmi haula i banati huna atharulakum fataqu alaha wala tukhzuni fi dayfialaysa minkum rajulun rashid
11:79	qalu laqad alimta ma lanafi banatika min haqin wa inaka latalamu manurid
11:80	qala law ana li bikum quwatan aw awila ruknin shadid
11:81	qalu ya lutu inarusulu rabika lan yasilu ilayka fasri bi ahlika biqitainmina alayli wala yaltafit minkum ahadun ilaimrataka inahu musibuha ma asabahumina mawaidahumu asubhu alaysa asubhubiqarib
11:82	falama ja amrunajalna aliyaha safilahawamtarna alayha hijaratanmin sijilin mandud
11:83	musawamatan ainda rabika wama hiyamina aththalimina bibaid
11:84	wa ila madyana akhahumshuayban qala ya qawmi uabudu alaha malakum min ilahin ghayruhu wala tanqusualmikyala walmizana ini arakumbikhayrin wa ini akhafu alaykum athaba yawminmuhit
11:85	waya qawmi awfu almikyala walmizanabilqisti wala tabkhasu anasashyahum wala tathaw fi al ardimufsidin
11:86	baqiyatu alahi khayrun lakum inkuntum mu minina wama ana alaykum bihafith
11:87	qalu ya shuaybu asalatukata muruka an natruka ma yabudu abaunaw an nafala fi amwalina ma nashauinaka lanta alhalimu arashid
11:88	qala ya qawmi araytum inkuntu ala bayinatin min rabi warazaqani minhu rizqanhasanan wama uridu an ukhalifakum ilama anhakum anhu in uridu ila al islahama istatatu wama tawfiqi ila bilahialayhi tawakaltu wa ilayhi unib
11:89	waya qawmi la yajrimanakumshiqaqi an yusibakum mithlu ma asabaqawma nuhin aw qawma hudin aw qawma salihinwama qawmu lutin minkum bibaid
11:90	wastaghfiru rabakum thuma tubuilayhi ina rabi rahimun wadud
11:91	qalu ya shuaybu manafqahu kathiran mima taqulu wa ina lanarakafina daifan walawla rahtukalarajamnaka wama anta alayna biaziz
11:92	qala ya qawmi arahtiazu alaykum mina alahi watakhathtumuhuwarakum thihriyan ina rabi bimatamaluna muhit
11:93	waya qawmi iamalu ala makanatikumini amilun sawfa talamuna man ya tihi athabunyukhzihi waman huwa kathibun wartaqibu inimakum raqib
11:94	walama ja amrunanajayna shuayban walathina amanumahu birahmatin mina wakhathati alathinathalamu asayhatu fasbahufi diyarihim jathimin
11:95	kan lam yaghnaw fiha alabuadan limadyana kama baidat thamud
11:96	walaqad arsalna musa bi ayatinawasultanin mubin
11:97	ila firawna wamala ihi fatabauamra firawna wama amru firawna birashid
11:98	yaqdumu qawmahu yawma alqiyamatifawradahumu anara wabi sa alwirdu almawrud
11:99	wautbiau fi hathihi lanatanwayawma alqiyamati bi sa arifdu almarfud
11:100	thalika min anba i alquranaqusuhu alayka minha qa imun wahasid
11:101	wama thalamnahumwalakin thalamu anfusahum famaghnat anhum alihatuhumu alati yadauna min duni alahimin shay in lama ja amru rabika wama zaduhumghayra tatbib
11:102	wakathalika akhthu rabika ithakhatha alqura wahiya thalimatunina akhthahu alimun shadid
11:103	ina fi thalika layatanliman khafa athaba al akhirati thalikayawmun majmuaun lahu anasu wathalikayawmun mashudun
11:104	wama nu akhkhiruhu ilali ajalin madud
11:105	yawma ya ti la takalamu nafsun ilabi ithnihi faminhum shaqiyun wasaid
11:106	fama alathina shaqufafi anari lahum fiha zafirun washahiq
11:107	khalidina fiha ma damatiasamawatu wal ardu ilama sha rabuka ina rabaka falun limayurid
11:108	wama alathina suaidufafi aljanati khalidina fiha ma damatiasamawatu wal ardu ilama sha rabuka atan ghayra majthuth
11:109	fala taku fi miryatin mimayabudu haula i ma yabuduna ilakama yabudu abauhum min qablu wa inalamuwafuhum nasibahum ghayra manqus
11:110	walaqad atayna musalkitaba fakhtulifa fihi walawla kalimatunsabaqat min rabika laqudiya baynahum wa inahum lafishakin minhu murib
11:111	wa ina kulan lamalayuwafiyanahum rabuka amalahum inahu bimayamaluna khabir
11:112	fastaqim kama umirta waman tabamaka wala tatghaw inahu bima tamalunabasir
11:113	wala tarkanu ila alathinathalamu fatamasakumu anaru wamalakum min duni alahi min awliya thuma latunsarun
11:114	waqimi asalata tarafayianahari wazulafan mina alayli ina alhasanatiyuthibna asayi ati thalika thikraliththakirin
11:115	wasbir fa ina alahala yudiau ajra almuhsinin
11:116	falawla kana mina alqurunimin qablikum ulu baqiyatin yanhawna ani alfasadi fial ardi ila qalilan miman anjayna minhumwataba alathina thalamu mautrifu fihi wakanu mujrimin
11:117	wama kana rabuka liyuhlikalqura bithulmin wahluha muslihun
11:118	walaw sha rabuka lajala anasaumatan wahidatan wala yazalunamukhtalifin
11:119	ila man rahima rabuka walithalikakhalaqahum watamat kalimatu rabika lamlana jahanama minaljinati wanasi ajmain
11:120	wakulan naqusu alayka min anba iarusuli ma nuthabitu bihi fu adaka wajakafi hathihi alhaqu wamawaithatun wathikralilmu minin
11:121	waqul lilathina layu minuna iamalu ala makanatikum inamilun
11:122	wantathiru inamuntathirun
11:123	walilahi ghaybu asamawatiwal ardi wa ilayhi yurjau al amru kuluhu fabudhuwatawakal alayhi wama rabuka bighafilin amatamalun
12:1	alif lam ra tilka ayatualkitabi almubin
12:2	ina anzalnahu qur ananarabiyan lalakum taqilun
12:3	nahnu naqusu alayka ahsanalqasasi bima awhayna ilayka hathalqur ana wa in kunta min qablihi lamina alghafilin
12:4	ith qala yusufu li abihi yabati ini raytu ahada ashara kawkaban washshamsawalqamara raytuhum li sajidin
12:5	qala ya bunaya la taqsusru yaka ala ikhwatika fayakidu laka kaydan inashshaytana lil insani aduwun mubin
12:6	wakathalika yajtabika rabukawayualimuka min ta wili al ahadithi wayutimuniamatahu alayka wala ali yaquba kamatamaha ala abawayka min qablu ibrahimawa ishaqa ina rabaka alimun hakim
12:7	laqad kana fi yusufa wa ikhwatihi ayatunlisa ilin
12:8	ith qalu layusufu wakhuhu ahabuila abina mina wanahnu ausbatunina abana lafi dalalin mubin
12:9	uqtulu yusufa awi itrahuhuardan yakhlu lakum wajhu abikum watakunu min badihiqawman salihin
12:10	qala qa ilun minhum lataqtulu yusufa walquhu fi ghayabati aljubi yaltaqithubadu asayarati in kuntum failin
12:11	qalu ya abana malaka la ta mana ala yusufa wa inalahu lanasihun
12:12	arsilhu mana ghadan yartawayalab wa ina lahu lahafithun
12:13	qala ini layahzununi an thathabubihi wakhafu an ya kulahu aththi buwantum anhu ghafilun
12:14	qalu la in akalahu aththi buwanahnu ausbatun ina ithan lakhasirun
12:15	falama thahabu bihiwajmau an yajaluhu fi ghayabati aljubi wawhaynailayhi latunabi anahum bi amrihim hatha wahum layashaurun
12:16	wajau abahum aishanyabkun
12:17	qalu ya abanaina thahabna nastabiqu wataraknayusufa ainda mataina fakalahu aththi buwama anta bimu minin lana walaw kuna sadiqin
12:18	wajau ala qamisihibidamin kathibin qala bal sawalat lakum anfusukumamran fasabrun jamilun walahu almustanuala ma tasifun
12:19	wajat sayaratun farsalu waridahumfadla dalwahu qala ya bushra hathaghulamun wasaruhu bidatan walahualimun bima yamalun
12:20	washarawhu bithamanin bakhsin darahimamadudatin wakanu fihi mina azahidin
12:21	waqala alathi ishtarahumin misra limratihi akrimi mathwahu asan yanfana aw natakhithahu waladan wakathalikamakana liyusufa fi al ardi walinualimahu minta wili al ahadithi walahu ghalibunala amrihi walakina akthara anasila yalamun
12:22	walama balagha ashudahu ataynahuhukman wailman wakathalika najzi almuhsinin
12:23	warawadat hu alati huwa fi baytihan nafsihi waghalaqati al abwaba waqalat haytalaka qala matha alahi inahu rabi ahsanamathwaya inahu la yuflihu aththalimun
12:24	walaqad hamat bihi wahama biha lawlan ra burhana rabihi kathalika linasrifanhu asu a walfahsha inahumin aibadina almukhlasin
12:25	wastabaqa albabawaqadat qamisahu min duburin walfaya sayidahalada albabi qalat ma jazau manarada bi ahlika su an ila an yusjana aw athabunalim
12:26	qala hiya rawadatni anafsi washahida shahidun min ahliha in kanaqamisuhu quda min qubulin fasadaqat wahuwa minalkathibin
12:27	wa in kana qamisuhu quda minduburin fakathabat wahuwa mina asadiqin
12:28	falama ra qamisahuquda min duburin qala inahu min kaydikuna inakaydakuna athim
12:29	yusufu arid an hatha wastaghfirilithanbiki inaki kunti mina alkhati in
12:30	waqala niswatun fi almadinatimratu alazizi turawidu fataha anafsihi qad shaghafaha huban ina lanarahafi dalalin mubin
12:31	falama samiat bimakrihina arsalatilayhina watadat lahuna mutakan watat kula wahidatinminhuna sikinan waqalati ukhruj alayhina falamaraynahu akbarnahu waqatana aydiyahuna waqulna hashalilahi ma hatha basharan in hathaila malakun karim
12:32	qalat fathalikuna alathilumtunani fihi walaqad rawadtuhu an nafsihi fastasamawala in lam yafal ma amuruhu layusjananawalayakunan mina asaghirin
12:33	qala rabi asijnu ahabuilaya mima yadaunani ilayhi wa ila tasrifani kaydahuna asbu ilayhina wakun mina aljahilin
12:34	fastajaba lahu rabuhu fasarafanhu kaydahuna inahu huwa asamiau alalim
12:35	thuma bada lahum min badi marawu al ayati layasjununahu hata hin
12:36	wadakhala mahu asijna fatayaniqala ahaduhuma ini arani asirukhamran waqala al akharu ini arani ahmilufawqa ra si khubzan ta kulu atayru minhu nabi nabita wilihi ina naraka mina almuhsinin
12:37	qala la ya tikuma tamunturzaqanihi ila naba tukuma bita wilihiqabla an ya tiyakuma thalikuma mimalamani rabi ini taraktu milata qawmin layu minuna bilahi wahum bil akhiratihum kafirun
12:38	watabatu milata aba ibrahima wa ishaqa wayaquba ma kanalana an nushrika bilahi min shay in thalikamin fadli alahi alayna wala anasiwalakina akthara anasi layashkurun
12:39	ya sahibayi asijniarbabun mutafariquna khayrun ami alahu alwahidualqahar
12:40	ma tabuduna min dunihi ilasman samaytumuha antum wabaukuma anzala alahu biha min sultaninini alhukmu ila lilahi amara alatabudu ila iyahu thalika adinualqayimu walakina akthara anasi layalamun
12:41	ya sahibayi asijniama ahadukuma fayasqi rabahu khamranwama al akharu fayuslabu fata kulu atayrumin ra sihi qudiya al amru alathi fihi tastaftiyan
12:42	waqala lilathi thananahu najin minhuma uthkurni aindarabika fansahu ashshaytanu thikrarabihi falabitha fi asijni bida sinin
12:43	waqala almaliku ini arasaba baqaratin simanin ya kuluhuna sabaun aijafunwasaba sunbulatin khudrin waukhara yabisatinya ayuha almalau aftuni fi ru yaya inkuntum liru ya taburun
12:44	qalu adghathu ahlaminwama nahnu bita wili al ahlami bialimin
12:45	waqala alathi najaminhuma wadakara bada umatin anaunabi ukum bita wilihi farsilun
12:46	yusufu ayuha asidiquaftina fi sabai baqaratin simaninya kuluhuna sabaun aijafun wasabai sunbulatinkhudrin waukhara yabisatin lali arjiauila anasi lalahum yalamun
12:47	qala tazrauna saba sininadaban fama hasadtum fatharuhu fisunbulihi ila qalilan mima ta kulun
12:48	thuma ya ti min badi thalikasabaun shidadun ya kulna ma qadamtum lahuna ilaqalilan mima tuhsinun
12:49	thuma ya ti min badi thalika amunfihi yughathu anasu wafihi yasirun
12:50	waqala almaliku i tuni bihi falamajahu arasulu qala irjia ilarabika fas alhu ma balu aniswatialati qatana aydiyahuna ina rabibikaydihina alim
12:51	qala ma khatbukuna ithrawadtuna yusufa an nafsihi qulna hasha lilahima alimna alayhi min su in qalatimratu alazizi al ana hashasa alhaquana rawadtuhu an nafsihi wa inahu lamina asadiqin
12:52	thalika liyalama ani lam akhunhubilghaybi wana alaha la yahdi kaydalkha inin
12:53	wama ubari u nafsi ina anafsalamaratun bisu i ila ma rahimarabi ina rabi ghafurun rahim
12:54	waqala almaliku i tuni bihiastakhlishu linafsi falama kalamahu qalainaka alyawma ladayna makinun amin
12:55	qala ijalni ala khaza inial ardi ini hafithun alim
12:56	wakathalika makana liyusufafi al ardi yatabawau minha haythu yashaunusibu birahmatina man nashau walanudiau ajra almuhsinin
12:57	walajru al akhirati khayrun lilathinamanu wakanu yataqun
12:58	waja ikhwatu yusufa fadakhalualayhi farafahum wahum lahu munkirun
12:59	walama jahazahum bijahazihimqala i tuni bi akhin lakum min abikum alatarawna ani ufi alkayla wana khayru almunzilin
12:60	fa in lam ta tuni bihi fala kaylalakum aindi wala taqrabun
12:61	qalu sanurawidu anhu abahuwa ina lafailun
12:62	waqala lifityanihi ijalu bidatahumfi rihalihim lalahum yarifunaha ithainqalabu ila ahlihim lalahum yarjiaun
12:63	falama rajau ila abihim qaluya abana munia mina alkaylufarsil mana akhana naktal wa inalahu lahafithun
12:64	qala hal amanukum alayhi ilakama amintukum ala akhihi min qablu falahukhayrun hafithan wahuwa arhamu arahimin
12:65	walama fatahu matahumwajadu bidatahum rudat ilayhim qalu yabana ma nabghi hathihi bidatunarudat ilayna wanamiru ahlana wanahfathuakhana wanazdadu kayla bairin thalikakaylun yasir
12:66	qala lan ursilahu makum hatatu tuni mawthiqan mina alahi lata tunani bihi ilan yuhata bikum falama atawhu mawthiqahum qalalahu ala ma naqulu wakil
12:67	waqala ya baniya latadkhulu min babin wahidin wadkhulu minabwabin mutafariqatin wama ughni ankum mina alahimin shay in ini alhukmu ila lilahialayhi tawakaltu walayhi falyatawakali almutawakilun
12:68	walama dakhalu min haythuamarahum abuhum ma kana yughni anhum mina alahimin shay in ila hajatan fi nafsi yaquba qadahawa inahu lathu ailmin lima alamnahuwalakina akthara anasi layalamun
12:69	walama dakhalu ala yusufawa ilayhi akhahu qala ini anakhuka fala tabta is bima kanu yamalun
12:70	falama jahazahum bijahazihimjala asiqayata fi rahli akhihi thumaththana mu aththinun ayatuha alairuinakum lasariqun
12:71	qalu waqbalu alayhim mathatafqidun
12:72	qalu nafqidu suwalmaliki waliman ja bihi himlu bairin wanabihi zaim
12:73	qalu talahi laqadalimtum ma ji na linufsida fi al ardi wamakuna sariqin
12:74	qalu fama jazauhu inkuntum kathibin
12:75	qalu jazauhu man wujida firahlihi fahuwa jazauhu kathalika najzi aththalimin
12:76	fabada bi awaiyatihim qabla wia iakhihi thuma istakhrajaha min wia i akhihi kathalikakidna liyusufa ma kana liya khuthakhahu fi dini almaliki ila an yasha alahunarfau darajatin man nashau wafawqa kuli thiailmin alim
12:77	qalu in yasriq faqad saraqa akhunlahu min qablu fasaraha yusufu fi nafsihi walam yubdihalahum qala antum sharun makanan walahualamu bima tasifun
12:78	qalu ya ayuhalazizu ina lahu aban shaykhan kabiran fakhuth ahadanamakanahu ina naraka mina almuhsinin
12:79	qala matha alahi ana khutha ila man wajadna matanaindahu ina ithan lathalimun
12:80	falama istay asu minhu khalasunajiyan qala kabiruhum alam talamu ana abakumqad akhatha alaykum mawthiqan mina alahi waminqablu ma faratum fi yusufa falan abrahal arda hata ya thana li abi aw yahkumalahu li wahuwa khayru alhakimin
12:81	irjiau ila abikum faqulu yabana ina ibnaka saraqa wama shahidnaila bima alimna wama kunalilghaybi hafithin
12:82	was ali alqaryata alati kunafiha walaira alati aqbalna fihawa ina lasadiqun
12:83	qala bal sawalat lakum anfusukumamran fasabrun jamilun asa alahu anya tiyani bihim jamian inahu huwa alalimu alhakim
12:84	watawala anhum waqala yasafa ala yusufa wabyadat aynahumina alhuzni fahuwa kathim
12:85	qalu talahi taftautathkuru yusufa hata takuna haradanaw takuna mina alhalikin
12:86	qala inama ashku baththi wahuznila alahi walamu mina alahi ma latalamun
12:87	ya baniya ithabu fatahasasumin yusufa wakhihi wala tay asu min rawhi alahinahu la yay asu min rawhi alahi ilalqawmu alkafirun
12:88	falama dakhalu alayhi qaluya ayuha alazizu masana wahlanaduru waji na bibidatin muzjatinfawfi lana alkayla watasadaq alayna inalaha yajzi almutasadiqin
12:89	qala hal alimtum mafaltum biyusufa wakhihi ith antum jahilun
12:90	qalu a inaka lanta yusufa qalana yusufu wahatha akhi qad mana alahualayna inahu man yataqi wayasbir fa ina alahala yudiau ajra almuhsinin
12:91	qalu talahi laqad atharakalahu alayna wa in kuna lakhati in
12:92	qala la tathriba alaykumualyawma yaghfiru alahu lakum wahuwa arhamu arahimin
12:93	ithabu biqamisi hathafalquhu ala wajhi abi ya ti basiran wa tunibi ahlikum ajmain
12:94	walama fasalati alairu qalabuhum ini lajidu riha yusufa lawla antufanidun
12:95	qalu talahi inakalafi dalalika alqadim
12:96	falama an ja albashiru alqahuala wajhihi fartada basiran qalalam aqul lakum ini alamu mina alahi ma latalamun
12:97	qalu ya abanaistaghfir lana thunubana ina kunakhati in
12:98	qala sawfa astaghfiru lakum rabinahu huwa alghafuru arahim
12:99	falama dakhalu ala yusufawa ilayhi abawayhi waqala udkhulu misrain sha alahu aminin
12:100	warafa abawayhi ala alarshiwakharu lahu sujadan waqala ya abati hathata wilu ru yaya min qablu qad jalaha rabi haqanwaqad ahsana bi ith akhrajani mina asijniwaja bikum mina albadwi min badi an nazagha ashshaytanubayni wabayna ikhwati ina rabi latifun limayashau inahu huwa alalimu alhakim
12:101	rabi qad ataytani mina almulkiwalamtani min ta wili al ahadithi fatira asamawatiwal ardi anta waliyi fi adunyawal akhirati tawafani musliman walhiqnibisalihin
12:102	thalika min anba i alghaybinuhihi ilayka wama kunta ladayhim ithajmau amrahum wahum yamkurun
12:103	wama aktharu anasiwalaw harasta bimu minin
12:104	wama tas aluhum alayhi min ajrinin huwa ila thikrun lilalamin
12:105	wakayin min ayatin fi asamawatiwal ardi yamuruna alayha wahum anhamuaridun
12:106	wama yu minu aktharuhum bilahila wahum mushrikun
12:107	afaminu an ta tiyahum ghashiyatunmin athabi alahi aw ta tiyahumu asatubaghtatan wahum la yashaurun
12:108	qul hathihi sabili adau ilalahi ala basiratin ana wamanitabani wasubhana alahi wama anamina almushrikin
12:109	wama arsalna min qablika ilarijalan nuhi ilayhim min ahli alqurafalam yasiru fi al ardi fayanthurukayfa kana aqibatu alathina min qablihimwaladaru al akhirati khayrun lilathinaitaqaw afala taqilun
12:110	hata itha istay asa arusuluwathanu anahum qad kuthibu jahumnasruna fanujiya man nashau walayuradu ba suna ani alqawmi almujrimin
12:111	laqad kana fi qasasihimaibratun li uli al albabi ma kana hadithanyuftara walakin tasdiqa alathibayna yadayhi watafsila kuli shay in wahudan warahmatanliqawmin yu minun
13:1	alif lam mim ra tilka ayatualkitabi walathi unzila ilayka minrabika alhaqu walakina akthara anasila yu minun
13:2	alahu alathi rafa asamawatibighayri amadin tarawnaha thuma istawa alalarshi wasakhkhara ashshamsa walqamara kulunyajri li ajalin musaman yudabiru al amra yufasilu al ayatilalakum biliqa i rabikum tuqinun
13:3	wahuwa alathi mada al ardawajala fiha rawasiya wanharan waminkuli aththamarati jala fiha zawjaynithnayni yughshi alayla anahara ina fi thalikalayatin liqawmin yatafakarun
13:4	wafi al ardi qitaun mutajawiratunwajanatun min anabin wazaraun wanakhilun sinwanunwaghayru sinwanin yusqa bima in wahidinwanufadilu badaha ala badinfi alukuli ina fi thalika layatinliqawmin yaqilun
13:5	wa in tajab fajabun qawluhum a ithakuna turaban a ina lafi khalqin jadidinula ika alathina kafaru birabihim waula ikal aghlalu fi anaqihim waula ika as habuanari hum fiha khalidun
13:6	wayastajilunaka bisayi atiqabla alhasanati waqad khalat min qablihimu almathulatuwa ina rabaka lathu maghfiratin linasiala thulmihim wa ina rabaka lashadidualaiqab
13:7	wayaqulu alathina kafaru lawlaunzila alayhi ayatun min rabihi inama anta munthirunwalikuli qawmin had
13:8	alahu yalamu ma tahmilukulu untha wama taghidu al arhamuwama tazdadu wakulu shay in aindahu bimiqdar
13:9	alimu alghaybi washshahadatialkabiru almutal
13:10	sawaun minkum man asara alqawlawaman jahara bihi waman huwa mustakhfin bilayli wasaribunbinahar
13:11	lahu muaqibatun min bayni yadayhiwamin khalfihi yahfathunahu min amri alahina alaha la yughayiru ma biqawmin hatayughayiru ma bi anfusihim wa itha aradalahu biqawmin su an fala marada lahu wamalahum min dunihi min wal
13:12	huwa alathi yurikumu albarqakhawfan wataman wayunshi u asahaba aththiqal
13:13	wayusabihu aradu bihamdihiwalmala ikatu min khifatihi wayursilu asawaiqafayusibu biha man yashau wahum yujadilunafi alahi wahuwa shadidu almihal
13:14	lahu dawatu alhaqi walathinayadauna min dunihi la yastajibuna lahum bishay in ilakabasiti kafayhi ila alma iliyablugha fahu wama huwa bibalighihi wamaduau alkafirina ila fi dalal
13:15	walilahi yasjudu man fi asamawatiwal ardi tawan wakarhan wathilaluhumbilghuduwi wal asal
13:16	qul man rabu asamawatiwal ardi quli alahu qul afatakhathtumin dunihi awliya la yamlikuna li anfusihimnafan wala daran qul hal yastawi al amawalbasiru am hal tastawi aththulumatuwanuru am jalu lilahi shurakakhalaqu kakhalqihi fatashabaha alkhalqu alayhim qulialahu khaliqu kuli shay in wahuwa alwahidualqahar
13:17	anzala mina asama i manfasalat awdiyatun biqadariha fahtamalasaylu zabadan rabiyan wamima yuqidunalayhi fi anari ibtigha hilyatinaw matain zabadun mithluhu kathalika yadribualahu alhaqa walbatila famazabadu fayathabu jufan wama mayanfau anasa fayamkuthu fi al ardi kathalikayadribu alahu al amthal
13:18	lilathina istajabulirabihimu alhusna walathina lamyastajibu lahu law ana lahum ma fi al ardijamian wamithlahu mahu laftadaw bihi ula ikalahum su u alhisabi wama wahum jahanamuwabi sa almihad
13:19	afaman yalamu anama unzila ilaykamin rabika alhaqu kaman huwa ama inamayatathakaru ulu al albab
13:20	alathina yufuna biahdi alahiwala yanquduna almithaq
13:21	walathina yasilunama amara alahu bihi an yusala wayakhshawnarabahum wayakhafuna su a alhisab
13:22	walathina sabaruibtigha wajhi rabihim waqamu asalatawanfaqu mima razaqnahum siran walaniyatanwayadrauna bilhasanati asayi ata ula ikalahum auqba adar
13:23	janatu adnin yadkhulunahawaman salaha min aba ihim wazwajihimwathuriyatihim walmala ikatuyadkhuluna alayhim min kuli bab
13:24	salamun alaykum bima sabartumfaniama auqba adar
13:25	walathina yanqudunahda alahi min badi mithaqihi wayaqtaunama amara alahu bihi an yusala wayufsidunafi al ardi ula ika lahumu alanatu walahum su uadar
13:26	alahu yabsutu arizqaliman yashau wayaqdiru wafarihu bilhayatiadunya wama alhayatu adunyafi al akhirati ila mata
13:27	wayaqulu alathina kafaru lawlaunzila alayhi ayatun min rabihi qul ina alahayudilu man yashau wayahdi ilayhi man anab
13:28	alathina amanu watatma inuqulubuhum bithikri alahi ala bithikrialahi tatma inu alqulub
13:29	alathina amanu wamilu asalihatituba lahum wahusnu mab
13:30	kathalika arsalnaka fiumatin qad khalat min qabliha umamun litatluwa alayhimualathi awhayna ilayka wahum yakfuruna birahmaniqul huwa rabi la ilaha ila huwa alayhitawakaltu wa ilayhi matab
13:31	walaw ana qur anan suyirat bihialjibalu aw qutiat bihi al ardu awkulima bihi almawta bal lilahi al amru jamianafalam yay asi alathina amanu an law yashaualahu lahada anasa jamian walayazalu alathina kafaru tusibuhum bimasanau qariatun aw tahulu qariban mindarihim hata ya tiya wadu alahina alaha la yukhlifu almiad
13:32	walaqadi istuhzi a birusulin min qablikafamlaytu lilathina kafaru thuma akhathtuhumfakayfa kana aiqab
13:33	afaman huwa qa imun alakuli nafsin bima kasabat wajalu lilahi shurakaqul samuhum am tunabi unahu bima la yalamufi al ardi am bithahirin minalqawli bal zuyina lilathina kafaru makruhum wasuduani asabili waman yudlili alahu famalahu min had
13:34	lahum athabun fi alhayatiadunya walathabu al akhiratiashaqu wama lahum mina alahi min waq
13:35	mathalu aljanati alati wuaidalmutaquna tajri min tahtiha al anharukuluha da imun wathiluhatilka auqba alathina itaqaw wauqba alkafirinanar
13:36	walathina ataynahumualkitaba yafrahuna bima unzila ilaykawamina al ahzabi man yunkiru badahu qulinama umirtu an abuda alaha wala ushrikabihi ilayhi adau wa ilayhi mab
13:37	wakathalika anzalnahu hukmanarabiyan wala ini itabata ahwahum bada majaka mina alailmi ma laka mina alahi minwaliyin wala waq
13:38	walaqad arsalna rusulan min qablikawajalna lahum azwajan wathuriyatan wamakana lirasulin an ya tiya bi ayatin ilabi ithni alahi likuli ajalin kitab
13:39	yamhu alahu ma yashauwayuthbitu waindahu umu alkitab
13:40	wa in ma nuriyanaka badalathi naiduhum aw natawafayanaka fa inamalayka albalaghu walayna alhisab
13:41	awa lam yaraw ana na ti al ardananqusuha min atrafiha walahuyahkumu la muaqiba lihukmihi wahuwasariau alhisab
13:42	waqad makara alathina min qablihimfalilahi almakru jamian yalamu ma taksibukulu nafsin wasayalamu alkufaru liman auqba adar
13:43	wayaqulu alathina kafaru lastamursalan qul kafa bilahi shahidan bayniwabaynakum waman aindahu ailmu alkitab
14:1	alif lam ra kitabunanzalnahu ilayka litukhrija anasa mina aththulumatila anuri bi ithni rabihim ila siratialazizi alhamid
14:2	alahi alathi lahu mafi asamawati wama fi al ardiwawaylun lilkafirina min athabin shadid
14:3	alathina yastahibuna alhayatadunya ala al akhirati wayasudunan sabili alahi wayabghunaha aiwajan ula ikafi dalalin baid
14:4	wama arsalna min rasulin ilabilisani qawmihi liyubayina lahum fayudilu alahuman yashau wayahdi man yashau wahuwa alazizu alhakim
14:5	walaqad arsalna musa bi ayatinan akhrij qawmaka mina aththulumatila anuri wathakirhum bi ayamialahi ina fi thalika layatinlikuli sabarin shakur
14:6	wa ith qala musaliqawmihi uthkuru niamata alahi alaykum ithanjakum min ali firawna yasumunakum su a alathabiwayuthabihuna abnakum wayastahyunanisakum wafi thalikum balaun min rabikumathim
14:7	wa ith taththana rabukumla in shakartum lazidanakum wala in kafartum ina athabilashadid
14:8	waqala musa in takfuru antumwaman fi al ardi jamian fa ina alahalaghaniyun hamid
14:9	alam ya tikum nabau alathina minqablikum qawmi nuhin wadin wathamuda walathinamin badihim la yalamuhum ila alahu jat humrusuluhum bilbayinati faradu aydiyahum fi afwahihimwaqalu ina kafarna bima ursiltumbihi wa ina lafi shakin mima tadaunanailayhi murib
14:10	qalat rusuluhum afi alahishakun fatiri asamawati wal ardiyadaukum liyaghfira lakum min thunubikumwayu akhkhirakum ila ajalin musaman qalu in antumila basharun mithluna turiduna an tasudunama kana yabudu abaunafa tuna bisultanin mubin
14:11	qalat lahum rusuluhum in nahnuila basharun mithlukum walakina alahayamunu ala man yashau min aibadihi wamakana lana an na tiyakum bisultanin ilabi ithni alahi wala alahifalyatawakali almu minun
14:12	wama lana alanatawakala ala alahi waqad hadanasubulana walanasbirana ala ma athaytumunawala alahi falyatawakali almutawakilun
14:13	waqala alathina kafarulirusulihim lanukhrijanakum min ardina awlatauduna fi milatina fawha ilayhim rabuhumlanuhlikana aththalimin
14:14	walanuskinanakumu al arda minbadihim thalika liman khafa maqami wakhafawaid
