14:15	wastaftahu wakhabakulu jabarin anid
14:16	min wara ihi jahanamu wayusqamin ma in sadid
14:17	yatajarauhu wala yakaduyusighuhu waya tihi almawtu min kuli makanin wamahuwa bimayitin wamin wara ihi athabun ghalith
14:18	mathalu alathina kafaru birabihimamaluhum karamadin ishtadat bihi arihufi yawmin asifin la yaqdiruna mimakasabu ala shay in thalika huwa adalalualbaid
14:19	alam tara ana alaha khalaqa asamawatiwal arda bilhaqi in yasha yuthibkumwaya ti bikhalqin jadid
14:20	wama thalika ala alahibiaziz
14:21	wabarazu lilahi jamian faqaladuafau lilathina istakbaruina kuna lakum taban fahal antum mughnunana min athabi alahi min shay in qalulaw hadana alahu lahadaynakum sawaunalayna ajaziana am sabarna malana min mahis
14:22	waqala ashshaytanulama qudiya al amru ina alaha wadakumwada alhaqi wawadtukum fakhlaftukum wama kanaliya alaykum min sultanin ila an dawtukum fastajabtumli fala talumuni walumu anfusakum ma anabimusrikhikum wama antum bimusrikhiya inikafartu bima ashraktumuni min qablu ina aththaliminalahum athabun alim
14:23	waudkhila alathina amanuwamilu asalihati janatintajri min tahtiha al anharu khalidinafiha bi ithni rabihim tahiyatuhum fihasalam
14:24	alam tara kayfa daraba alahumathalan kalimatan tayibatan kashajaratin tayibatinasluha thabitun wafarauha fi asama 
14:25	tu ti ukulaha kula hininbi ithni rabiha wayadribu alahual amthala linasi lalahum yatathakarun
14:26	wamathalu kalimatin khabithatinkashajaratin khabithatin ijtuththat min fawqi al ardi malaha min qarar
14:27	yuthabitu alahu alathina amanubilqawli aththabiti fi alhayatiadunya wafi al akhirati wayudilualahu aththaliminawayafalu alahu ma yasha 
14:28	alam tara ila alathinabadalu niamata alahi kufran wahalu qawmahumdara albawar
14:29	jahanama yaslawnaha wabi salqarar
14:30	wajalu lilahi andadanliyudilu an sabilihi qul tamatau fa ina masirakumila anar
14:31	qul liaibadiya alathina amanuyuqimu asalata wayunfiqu mimarazaqnahum siran walaniyatan min qabli anya tiya yawmun la bayaun fihi wala khilal
14:32	alahu alathi khalaqa asamawatiwal arda wanzala mina asama i manfakhraja bihi mina aththamarati rizqan lakumwasakhkhara lakumu alfulka litajriya fi albahri bi amrihiwasakhkhara lakumu al anhar
14:33	wasakhkhara lakumu ashshamsa walqamarada ibayni wasakhkhara lakumu alayla wanahar
14:34	watakum min kuli masaltumuhu wa in taudu niamata alahi la tuhsuhaina al insana lathalumun kafar
14:35	wa ith qala ibrahimurabi ijal hatha albalada aminan wajnubniwabaniya an nabuda al asnam
14:36	rabi inahuna adlalna kathiranmina anasi faman tabiani fa inahu miniwaman asani fa inaka ghafurun rahim
14:37	rabana ini askantu min thuriyatibiwadin ghayri thi zarain ainda baytika almuharamirabana liyuqimu asalata fajalaf idatan mina anasi tahwi ilayhim warzuqhumina aththamarati lalahum yashkurun
14:38	rabana inaka talamu manukhfi wama nualinu wama yakhfa alalahi min shay in fi al ardi wala fi asama 
14:39	alhamdu lilahi alathiwahaba li ala alkibari ismaila wa ishaqaina rabi lasamiau adua 
14:40	rabi ijalni muqima asalatiwamin thuriyati rabana wtaqabal dua 
14:41	rabana ighfir li waliwalidayawalilmu minina yawma yaqumu alhisab
14:42	wala tahsabana alahaghafilan ama yamalu aththalimunainama yu akhkhiruhum liyawmin tashkhasu fihial absar
14:43	muhtiaina muqniai rusihim layartadu ilayhim tarfuhum waf idatuhum hawa 
14:44	wanthiri anasa yawmaya tihimu alathabu fayaqulu alathina thalamurabana akhkhirna ila ajalin qaribin nujibdawataka wanatabiai arusula awa lam takunuaqsamtum min qablu ma lakum min zawal
14:45	wasakantum fi masakini alathinathalamu anfusahum watabayana lakum kayfa falnabihim wadarabna lakumu al amthal
14:46	waqad makaru makrahum wainda alahimakruhum wa in kana makruhum litazula minhu aljibal
14:47	fala tahsabana alahamukhlifa wadihi rusulahu ina alaha azizun thuintiqam
14:48	yawma tubadalu al ardu ghayra al ardiwasamawatu wabarazu lilahi alwahidialqahar

14:50	sarabiluhum min qatraninwataghsha wujuhahumu anar

15:1	alif lam ra tilka ayatualkitabi waqur anin mubin
15:2	rubama yawadu alathinakafaru law kanu muslimin

15:5	ma tasbiqu min umatin ajalahawama yasta khirun

15:44	laha sabatu abwabin likulibabin minhum juz un maqsum
15:45	ina almutaqina fi janatinwauyun

15:47	wanazana ma fi sudurihimin ghilin ikhwanan ala sururin mutaqabilin

15:50	wana athabi huwa alathabual alim

16:1	ata amru alahi falatastajiluhu subhanahu watala amayushrikun
16:2	yunazilu almala ikata biruhimin amrihi ala man yashau min aibadihi ananthiru anahu la ilaha ila anafataqun
16:3	khalaqa asamawati wal ardabilhaqi tala amayushrikun
16:4	khalaqa al insana min nutfatinfa itha huwa khasimun mubin
16:5	wal anama khalaqahalakum fiha dif un wamanafiau waminhata kulun
16:6	walakum fiha jamalun hinaturihuna wahina tasrahun
16:7	watahmilu athqalakum ilabaladin lam takunu balighihi ila bishiqial anfusi ina rabakum laraufun rahim
16:8	walkhayla walbighalawalhamira litarkabuha wazinatanwayakhluqu ma la talamun
16:9	wala alahi qasdu asabiliwaminha ja irun walaw sha lahadakumajmain
16:10	huwa alathi anzala mina asama iman lakum minhu sharabun waminhu shajarun fihitusimun
16:11	yunbitu lakum bihi azara wazaytunawanakhila wal anaba wamin kuli aththamaratina fi thalika layatan liqawmin yatafakarun
16:12	wasakhkhara lakumu alayla wanaharawashshamsa walqamara wanujumumusakhkharatun bi amrihi ina fi thalika layatinliqawmin yaqilun
16:13	wama thara lakum fi al ardimukhtalifan alwanuhu ina fi thalika layatanliqawmin yaththakarun
16:14	wahuwa alathi sakhkhara albahralita kulu minhu lahman tariyan watastakhrijuminhu hilyatan talbasunaha watara alfulkamawakhira fihi walitabtaghu min fadlihiwalalakum tashkurun
16:15	walqa fi al ardi rawasiyan tamida bikum wanharan wasubulan lalakum tahtadun
16:16	walamatin wabinajmihum yahtadun
16:17	afaman yakhluqu kaman la yakhluquafala tathakarun
16:18	wa in taudu niamata alahi latuhsuha ina alaha laghafurun rahim
16:19	walahu yalamu matusiruna wama tualinun
16:20	walathina yadauna minduni alahi la yakhluquna shay an wahumyukhlaqun
16:21	amwatun ghayru ahya inwama yashauruna ayana yubathun
16:22	ilahukum ilahun wahidunfalathina la yu minuna bil akhiratiqulubuhum munkiratun wahum mustakbirun
16:23	la jarama ana alaha yalamuma yusiruna wama yualinuna inahu la yuhibualmustakbirin
16:24	wa itha qila lahum mathanzala rabukum qalu asatiru al awalin
16:25	liyahmilu awzarahum kamilatanyawma alqiyamati wamin awzari alathina yudilunahumbighayri ailmin ala sa ma yazirun
16:26	qad makara alathina min qablihimfata alahu bunyanahum mina alqawaidifakhara alayhimu asaqfu min fawqihim watahumualathabu min haythu la yashaurun
16:27	thuma yawma alqiyamati yukhzihimwayaqulu ayna shuraka iya alathina kuntum tushaqunafihim qala alathina utu alailma ina alkhizyalyawma wasu a ala alkafirin
16:28	alathina tatawafahumu almala ikatuthalimi anfusihim falqawu asalamama kuna namalu min su in bala ina alahalimun bima kuntum tamalun
16:29	fadkhulu abwaba jahanama khalidinafiha falabi sa mathwa almutakabirin
16:30	waqila lilathina itaqaw mathanzala rabukum qalu khayran lilathina ahsanufi hathihi adunya hasanatun waladarual akhirati khayrun walaniama daru almutaqin
16:31	janatu adnin yadkhulunahatajri min tahtiha al anharu lahum fihama yashauna kathalika yajzi alahualmutaqin
16:32	alathina tatawafahumu almala ikatutayibina yaquluna salamun alaykumu udkhulualjanata bima kuntum tamalun
16:33	hal yanthuruna ila anta tiyahumu almala ikatu aw ya tiya amru rabika kathalikafala alathina min qablihim wama thalamahumualahu walakin kanu anfusahum yathlimun
16:34	fasabahum sayi atu mamilu wahaqa bihim ma kanu bihiyastahzi un
16:35	waqala alathina ashraku lawsha alahu ma abadna min dunihimin shay in nahnu wala abaunawala haramna min dunihi min shay in kathalikafala alathina min qablihim fahal ala arusulila albalaghu almubin
16:36	walaqad bathna fi kuli umatinrasulan ani uabudu alaha wajtanibu ataghutafaminhum man hada alahu waminhum man haqatalayhi adalalatu fasiru fi al ardifanthuru kayfa kana aqibatualmukaththibin
16:37	in tahris ala hudahumfa ina alaha la yahdi man yudilu wamalahum min nasirin
16:38	waqsamu bilahi jahda aymanihimla yabathu alahu man yamutu bala wadanalayhi haqan walakina akthara anasila yalamun
16:39	liyubayina lahumu alathiyakhtalifuna fihi waliyalama alathina kafaruanahum kanu kathibin
16:40	inama qawluna lishay initharadnahu an naqula lahu kun fayakun
16:41	walathina hajarufi alahi min badi ma thulimulanubawi anahum fi adunya hasanatanwalajru al akhirati akbaru law kanu yalamun
16:42	alathina sabaru walarabihim yatawakalun
16:43	wama arsalna min qablika ilarijalan nuhi ilayhim fas alu ahla aththikrin kuntum la talamun
16:44	bilbayinati wazuburiwanzalna ilayka aththikra litubayina linasima nuzila ilayhim walalahum yatafakarun
16:45	afamina alathina makaru asayi atian yakhsifa alahu bihimu al arda aw ya tiyahumualathabu min haythu la yashaurun
16:46	aw ya khuthahum fi taqalubihim famahum bimuajizin
16:47	aw ya khuthahum alatakhawufin fa ina rabakum laraufun rahim
16:48	awa lam yaraw ila ma khalaqalahu min shay in yatafayau thilaluhuani alyamini washshama ili sujadan lilahiwahum dakhirun
16:49	walilahi yasjudu ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi min dabatin walmala ikatuwahum la yastakbirun
16:50	yakhafuna rabahum min fawqihimwayafaluna ma yu marun
16:51	waqala alahu latatakhithu ilahayni ithnayni inama huwailahun wahidun fa iyaya farhabun
16:52	walahu ma fi asamawatiwal ardi walahu adinu wasibanafaghayra alahi tataqun
16:53	wama bikum min niamatin famina alahithuma itha masakumu aduru fa ilayhitaj arun
16:54	thuma itha kashafa adurankum itha fariqun minkum birabihim yushrikun
16:55	liyakfuru bima ataynahumfatamatau fasawfa talamun
16:56	wayajaluna lima layalamuna nasiban mima razaqnahum talahilatus aluna ama kuntum taftarun
16:57	wayajaluna lilahi albanatisubhanahu walahum ma yashtahun
16:58	wa itha bushshira ahaduhum bilunthathala wajhuhu muswadan wahuwa kathim
16:59	yatawara mina alqawmi minsu i ma bushshira bihi ayumsikuhu ala hunin amyadusuhu fi aturabi ala sa mayahkumun
16:60	lilathina la yu minuna bil akhiratimathalu asaw i walilahi almathalu al alawahuwa alazizu alhakim
16:61	walaw yu akhithu alahuanasa bithulmihim ma tarakalayha min dabatin walakin yu akhkhiruhumila ajalin musaman fa itha ja ajaluhum layasta khiruna satan wala yastaqdimun
16:62	wayajaluna lilahi mayakrahuna watasifu alsinatuhumu alkathiba analahumu alhusna la jarama ana lahumu anarawanahum mufratun
16:63	talahi laqad arsalnaila umamin min qablika fazayana lahumu ashshaytanuamalahum fahuwa waliyuhumu alyawma walahum athabunalim
16:64	wama anzalna alayka alkitabaila litubayina lahumu alathi ikhtalafu fihiwahudan warahmatan liqawmin yu minun
16:65	walahu anzala mina asama iman fahya bihi al arda badamawtiha ina fi thalika layatan liqawminyasmaun
16:66	wa ina lakum fi al anamilaibratan nusqikum mima fi butunihi min baynifarthin wadamin labanan khalisan sa ighan lishsharibin
16:67	wamin thamarati anakhili wal anabitatakhithuna minhu sakaran warizqan hasanan inafi thalika layatan liqawmin yaqilun
16:68	wawha rabuka ila anahliani itakhithi mina aljibali buyutan wamina ashshajariwamima yarishun
16:69	thuma kuli min kuli aththamaratifasluki subula rabiki thululan yakhruju min butunihasharabun mukhtalifun alwanuhu fihi shifaunlinasi ina fi thalika layatanliqawmin yatafakarun
16:70	walahu khalaqakum thumayatawafakum waminkum man yuradu ila arthalialaumuri likay la yalama bada ailmin shay an inalaha alimun qadir
16:71	walahu fadala badakumala badin fi arizqi fama alathinafudilu biradi rizqihim ala mamalakat aymanuhum fahum fihi sawaun afabiniamatialahi yajhadun
16:72	walahu jala lakum minanfusikum azwajan wajala lakum min azwajikumbanina wahafadatan warazaqakum mina atayibatiafabilbatili yu minuna wabiniamati alahihum yakfurun
16:73	wayabuduna min duni alahi mala yamliku lahum rizqan mina asamawatiwal ardi shay an wala yastatiaun
16:74	fala tadribu lilahial amthala ina alaha yalamu wantum latalamun
16:75	daraba alahu mathalan abdanmamlukan la yaqdiru ala shay in waman razaqnahumina rizqan hasanan fahuwa yunfiqu minhu siranwajahran hal yastawuna alhamdu lilahi balaktharuhum la yalamun
16:76	wadaraba alahu mathalanrajulayni ahaduhuma abkamu la yaqdiru alashay in wahuwa kalun ala mawlahu aynamayuwajihu la ya ti bikhayrin hal yastawi huwa wamanya muru biladli wahuwa ala siratinmustaqim
16:77	walilahi ghaybu asamawatiwal ardi wama amru asatila kalamhi albasari aw huwa aqrabu inalaha ala kuli shay in qadir
16:78	walahu akhrajakum min butuniumahatikum la talamuna shay an wajala lakumuasama wal absara wal af idatalalakum tashkurun
16:79	alam yaraw ila atayrimusakhkharatin fi jawi asama i mayumsikuhuna ila alahu ina fi thalika layatinliqawmin yu minun
16:80	walahu jala lakum minbuyutikum sakanan wajala lakum min juludi al anamibuyutan tastakhifunaha yawma thanikumwayawma iqamatikum wamin aswafihawawbariha washariha athathanwamatan ila hin
16:81	walahu jala lakum mimakhalaqa thilalan wajala lakum mina aljibaliaknanan wajala lakum sarabila taqikumu alharawasarabila taqikum ba sakum kathalika yutimuniamatahu alaykum lalakum tuslimun
16:82	fa in tawalaw fa inama alaykalbalaghu almubin
16:83	yarifuna niamata alahi thumayunkirunaha waktharuhumu alkafirun
16:84	wayawma nabathu min kuli umatinshahidan thuma la yu thanu lilathinakafaru wala hum yustatabun
16:85	wa itha ra alathina thalamualathaba fala yukhafafu anhum wala humyuntharun
16:86	wa itha ra alathinashraku shurakahum qalu rabana haula ishurakauna alathina kuna nadaumin dunika falqaw ilayhimu alqawla inakum lakathibun
16:87	walqaw ila alahi yawma ithinasalama wadala anhum ma kanuyaftarun
16:88	alathina kafaru wasaduan sabili alahi zidnahum athaban fawqalathabi bima kanu yufsidun
16:89	wayawma nabathu fi kuli umatinshahidan alayhim min anfusihim waji na bika shahidanala haula i wanazalna alaykalkitaba tibyanan likuli shay in wahudan warahmatanwabushra lilmuslimin
16:90	ina alaha ya muru biladliwal ihsani wa ita i thialqurba wayanha ani alfahsha i walmunkariwalbaghyi yaithukum lalakum tathakarun
16:91	wawfu biahdi alahi ithahadtum wala tanqudu al aymanabada tawkidiha waqad jaltumu alaha alaykumkafilan ina alaha yalamu ma tafalun
16:92	wala takunu kalati naqadatghazlaha min badi quwatin ankathan tatakhithunaymanakum dakhalan baynakum an takuna umatun hiya arbamin umatin inama yablukumu alahu bihiwalayubayinana lakum yawma alqiyamati ma kuntumfihi takhtalifun
16:93	walaw sha alahu lajalakumumatan wahidatan walakin yudilu man yashauwayahdi man yashau walatus aluna ama kuntumtamalun
16:94	wala tatakhithu aymanakumdakhalan baynakum fatazila qadamun bada thubutiha watathuquasu a bima sadadtum an sabili alahiwalakum athabun athim
16:95	wala tashtaru biahdi alahithamanan qalilan inama ainda alahi huwa khayrunlakum in kuntum talamun
16:96	ma aindakum yanfadu wamainda alahi baqin walanajziyana alathinasabaru ajrahum bi ahsani ma kanuyamalun
16:97	man amila salihan min thakarinaw untha wahuwa mu minun falanuhyiyanahu hayatantayibatan walanajziyanahum ajrahum bi ahsani makanu yamalun
16:98	fa itha qara ta alqur ana fastaithbilahi mina ashshaytani arajim
16:99	inahu laysa lahu sultanun alalathina amanu wala rabihimyatawakalun
16:100	inama sultanuhu alalathina yatawalawnahu walathina humbihi mushrikun
16:101	wa itha badalna ayatanmakana ayatin walahu alamu bimayunazilu qalu inama anta muftarin bal aktharuhumla yalamun
16:102	qul nazalahu ruhu alqudusi minrabika bilhaqi liyuthabita alathina amanuwahudan wabushra lilmuslimin
16:103	walaqad nalamu anahum yaquluna inamayualimuhu basharun lisanu alathi yulhidunailayhi ajamiyun wahatha lisanun arabiyunmubin
16:104	ina alathina la yu minunabi ayati alahi la yahdihimu alahuwalahum athabun alim
16:105	inama yaftari alkathibalathina la yu minuna bi ayati alahiwaula ika humu alkathibun
16:106	man kafara bilahi minbadi imanihi ila man ukriha waqalbuhu mutma inunbil imani walakin man sharaha bilkufrisadran falayhim ghadabun mina alahiwalahum athabun athim
16:107	thalika bi anahumu istahabualhayata adunya ala al akhiratiwana alaha la yahdi alqawma alkafirin
16:108	ula ika alathina tabalahu ala qulubihim wasamaihim wabsarihimwaula ika humu alghafilun
16:109	la jarama anahum fi al akhiratihumu alkhasirun
16:110	thuma ina rabaka lilathina hajarumin badi ma futinu thuma jahadu wasabaruina rabaka min badiha laghafurun rahim
16:111	yawma ta ti kulu nafsin tujadiluan nafsiha watuwafa kulu nafsin mamilat wahum la yuthlamun
16:112	wadaraba alahu mathalanqaryatan kanat aminatan mutma inatanya tiha rizquha raghadan min kuli makaninfakafarat bi anaumi alahi fathaqaha alahulibasa aljuai walkhawfi bima kanuyasnaun
16:113	walaqad jahum rasulun minhum fakaththabuhufakhathahumu alathabu wahum thalimun
16:114	fakulu mima razaqakumu alahuhalalan tayiban washkuru niamatalahi in kuntum iyahu tabudun
16:115	inama harama alaykumualmaytata wadama walahma alkhinziri wamauhila lighayri alahi bihi famani idtura ghayra baghinwala adin fa ina alaha ghafurun rahim
16:116	wala taqulu lima tasifualsinatukumu alkathiba hatha halalunwahatha haramun litaftaru ala alahialkathiba ina alathina yaftaruna alalahi alkathiba la yuflihun
16:117	mataun qalilun walahum athabunalim
16:118	wala alathina haduharamna ma qasasnalayka min qablu wama thalamnahumwalakin kanu anfusahum yathlimun
16:119	thuma ina rabaka lilathinamilu asu a bijahalatin thuma tabumin badi thalika waslahu ina rabakamin badiha laghafurun rahim
16:120	ina ibrahima kana umatan qanitanlilahi hanifan walam yaku mina almushrikin
16:121	shakiran li anaumihi ijtabahuwahadahu ila siratin mustaqim
16:122	wataynahu fi adunyahasanatan wa inahu fi al akhirati lamina asalihin
16:123	thuma awhayna ilayka anitabia milata ibrahima hanifan wama kanamina almushrikin
16:124	inama juaila asabtu alalathina ikhtalafu fihi wa ina rabaka layahkumubaynahum yawma alqiyamati fima kanu fihiyakhtalifun
16:125	udau ila sabili rabika bilhikmatiwalmawaithati alhasanati wajadilhumbilati hiya ahsanu ina rabaka huwa alamubiman dala an sabilihi wahuwa alamu bilmuhtadin
16:126	wa in aqabtum faqibubimithli ma auqibtum bihi wala in sabartum lahuwakhayrun lisabirin
16:127	wasbir wama sabrukaila bilahi wala tahzanalayhim wala taku fi dayqin mimayamkurun
16:128	ina alaha ma alathinaitaqaw walathina hum muhsinun
17:1	subhana alathi asrabiabdihi laylan mina almasjidi alharami ilalmasjidi al aqsa alathi barakna hawlahulinuriyahu min ayatina inahu huwa asamiaualbasir
17:2	watayna musa alkitabawajalnahu hudan libani isra ila alatatakhithu min duni wakila
17:3	thuriyata man hamalnama nuhin inahu kana abdan shakura
17:4	waqadayna ila bani isra ilafi alkitabi latufsiduna fi al ardi maratayniwalataluna auluwan kabira
17:5	fa itha ja wadu ulahumabathna alaykum aibadan lana uliba sin shadidin fajasu khilala adiyariwakana wadan mafaula
17:6	thuma radadna lakumu alkaratalayhim wamdadnakum bi amwalin wabaninawajalnakum akthara nafira
17:7	in ahsantum ahsantumli anfusikum wa in asa tum falaha fa itha jawadu al akhirati liyasu u wujuhakum waliyadkhulualmasjida kama dakhaluhu awala maratin waliyutabiru malaw tatbira
17:8	asa rabukum an yarhamakumwa in audtum audna wajalna jahanama lilkafirinahasira
17:9	ina hatha alqur ana yahdililati hiya aqwamu wayubashshiru almu minina alathinayamaluna asalihati ana lahum ajrankabira
17:10	wana alathina layu minuna bil akhirati atadna lahum athabanalima
17:11	wayadau al insanu bishshariduahu bilkhayri wakana al insanuajula
17:12	wajalna alayla wanaharayatayni famahawna ayata alayliwajalna ayata anahari mubsiratanlitabtaghu fadlan min rabikum walitalamu adada asininawalhisaba wakula shay in fasalnahutafsila
17:13	wakula insanin alzamnahu ta irahufi aunuqihi wanukhriju lahu yawma alqiyamati kitabanyalqahu manshura
17:14	iqra kitabaka kafa binafsikalyawma alayka hasiba
17:15	mani ihtada fa inama yahtadilinafsihi waman dala fa inama yadilualayha wala taziru waziratun wizra ukhrawama kuna muaththibina hatanabatha rasula
17:16	wa itha aradna an nuhlikaqaryatan amarna mutrafiha fafasaqu fihafahaqa alayha alqawlu fadamarnahatadmira
17:17	wakam ahlakna mina alquruni minbadi nuhin wakafa birabika bithunubiaibadihi khabiran basira
17:18	man kana yuridu alajilatajalna lahu fiha ma nashau limanuridu thuma jalna lahu jahanama yaslahamathmuman madhura
17:19	waman arada al akhirata wasalaha sayaha wahuwa mu minun faula ika kanasayuhum mashkura
17:20	kulan numidu haula i wahaula imin ata i rabika wama kana ataurabika mahthura
17:21	unthur kayfa fadalnabadahum ala badin walal akhiratuakbaru darajatin wakbaru tafdila
17:22	la tajal ma alahi ilahanakhara fataqauda mathmuman makhthula
17:23	waqada rabuka ala tabuduila iyahu wabilwalidayni ihsananima yablughana aindaka alkibara ahaduhumaw kilahuma fala taqul lahuma ufinwala tanharhuma waqul lahuma qawlan karima
17:24	wakhfid lahuma janahaththuli mina arahmati waqulrabi irhamhuma kama rabayani saghira
17:25	rabukum alamu bima fi nufusikumin takunu salihina fa inahu kana lil awabinaghafura
17:26	wati tha alqurba haqahuwalmiskina wabna asabili walatubaththir tabthira
17:27	ina almubaththirina kanuikhwana ashshayatini wakana ashshaytanulirabihi kafura
17:28	wa ima tuaridana anhumuibtigha rahmatin min rabika tarjuha faqulahum qawlan maysura
17:29	wala tajal yadaka maghlulatan ilaunuqika wala tabsutha kula albastifataqauda maluman mahsura
17:30	ina rabaka yabsutu arizqaliman yashau wayaqdiru inahu kana biaibadihikhabiran basira
17:31	wala taqtulu awladakumkhashyata imlaqin nahnu narzuquhum wa iyakumina qatlahum kana khit an kabira
17:32	wala taqrabu azinainahu kana fahishatan wasa sabila
17:33	wala taqtulu anafsa alatiharama alahu ila bilhaqiwaman qutila mathluman faqad jalnaliwaliyihi sultanan fala yusrif fi alqatli inahukana mansura
17:34	wala taqrabu mala alyatimila bilati hiya ahsanu hatayablugha ashudahu wawfu bilahdi ina alahda kanamas ula
17:35	wawfu alkayla itha kiltum wazinubilqistasi almustaqimi thalika khayrun wahsanuta wila
17:36	wala taqfu ma laysa laka bihiailmun ina asama walbasara walfu adakulu ula ika kana anhu mas ula
17:37	wala tamshi fi al ardi marahaninaka lan takhriqa al arda walan tablugha aljibalatula
17:38	kulu thalika kana sayi uhuainda rabika makruha
17:39	thalika mima awhailayka rabuka mina alhikmati wala tajal malahi ilahan akhara fatulqa fijahanama maluman madhura
17:40	afasfakum rabukum bilbaninawatakhatha mina almala ikati inathaninakum lataquluna qawlan athima
17:41	walaqad sarafna fi hathalqur ani liyaththakaru wama yaziduhumila nufura
17:42	qul law kana mahu alihatunkama yaquluna ithan labtaghaw ila thialarshi sabila
17:43	subhanahu watalama yaquluna auluwan kabira
17:44	tusabihu lahu asamawatuasabau wal ardu waman fihina wa in minshay in ila yusabihu bihamdihi walakinla tafqahuna tasbihahum inahu kana halimanghafura
17:45	wa itha qara ta alqur anajalna baynaka wabayna alathina layu minuna bil akhirati hijabanmastura
17:46	wajalna ala qulubihimakinatan an yafqahuhu wafi athanihim waqran wa ithathakarta rabaka fi alqur ani wahdahuwalaw ala adbarihim nufura
17:47	nahnu alamu bimayastamiauna bihi ith yastamiauna ilayka wa ithum najwa ith yaqulu aththalimunain tatabiauna ila rajulan mashura
17:48	unthur kayfa darabulaka al amthala fadalu fala yastatiaunasabila
17:49	waqalu a itha kunaithaman warufatan a inalamabauthuna khalqan jadida
17:50	qul kunu hijaratan aw hadida
17:51	aw khalqan mima yakburu fi sudurikumfasayaquluna man yuaiduna quli alathi fatarakumawala maratin fasayunghiduna ilayka rusahumwayaquluna mata huwa qul asa an yakuna qariba
17:52	yawma yadaukum fatastajibuna bihamdihiwatathununa in labithtum ila qalila
17:53	waqul liaibadi yaqulu alatihiya ahsanu ina ashshaytana yanzaghubaynahum ina ashshaytana kana lil insaniaduwan mubina
17:54	rabukum alamu bikum in yasha yarhamkumaw in yasha yuaththibkum wama arsalnakalayhim wakila
17:55	warabuka alamu biman fi asamawatiwal ardi walaqad fadalna badanabiyina ala badin wataynadawuda zabura
17:56	quli udau alathina zamtum mindunihi fala yamlikuna kashfa aduriankum wala tahwila
17:57	ula ika alathina yadaunayabtaghuna ila rabihimu alwasilata ayuhum aqrabuwayarjuna rahmatahu wayakhafuna athabahuina athaba rabika kana mahthura
17:58	wa in min qaryatin ila nahnumuhlikuha qabla yawmi alqiyamati aw muaththibuhathaban shadidan kana thalika fi alkitabimastura
17:59	wama manana an nursila bil ayatila an kaththaba biha al awaluna wataynathamuda anaqata mubsiratan fathalamubiha wama nursilu bil ayatila takhwifa
17:60	wa ith qulna laka ina rabakahata binasi wama jalnaru ya alati araynaka ilafitnatan linasi washshajaratalmalaunata fi alqur ani wanukhawifuhum famayaziduhum ila tughyanan kabira
17:61	wa ith qulna lilmala ikatiusjudu li adama fasajadu ila iblisa qalasjudu liman khalaqta tina
17:62	qala araytaka hatha alathikaramta alaya la in akhkhartani ila yawmi alqiyamatilahtanikana thuriyatahu ila qalila
17:63	qala ithab faman tabiakaminhum fa ina jahanama jazaukum jazan mawfura
17:64	wastafziz mani istatataminhum bisawtika wajlib alayhim bikhaylika warajlikawasharik hum fi al amwali wal awladiwaidhum wama yaiduhumu ashshaytanu ilaghurura
17:65	ina aibadi laysa laka alayhimsultanun wakafa birabika wakila
17:66	rabukumu alathi yuzji lakumualfulka fi albahri litabtaghu min fadlihi inahukana bikum rahima
17:67	wa itha masakumu adurufi albahri dala man tadauna ila iyahufalama najakum ila albari aradtumwakana al insanu kafura
17:68	afamintum an yakhsifa bikum janibalbari aw yursila alaykum hasiban thuma latajidu lakum wakila
17:69	am amintum an yuaidakum fihi taratanukhra fayursila alaykum qasifan mina arihifayughriqakum bima kafartum thuma la tajidu lakumalayna bihi tabia
17:70	walaqad karamna bani adamawahamalnahum fi albari walbahriwarazaqnahum mina atayibati wafadalnahumala kathirin miman khalaqna tafdila
17:71	yawma nadau kula unasin bi imamihimfaman utiya kitabahu biyaminihi faula ikayaqrauna kitabahum wala yuthlamunafatila
17:72	waman kana fi hathihi amafahuwa fi al akhirati ama wadalu sabila
17:73	wa in kadu layaftinunaka ani alathiawhayna ilayka litaftariya alayna ghayrahuwa ithan latakhathuka khalila
17:74	walawla an thabatnaka laqadkidta tarkanu ilayhim shay an qalila
17:75	ithan lathaqnaka diafalhayati wadiafa almamati thuma latajidu laka alayna nasira
17:76	wa in kadu layastafizunaka minal ardi liyukhrijuka minha wa ithan layalbathuna khilafaka ila qalila
17:77	sunata man qad arsalna qablaka minrusulina wala tajidu lisunatina tahwila
17:78	aqimi asalataliduluki ashshamsi ila ghasaqi alayli waqur analfajri ina qur ana alfajri kana mashuda
17:79	wamina alayli fatahajad bihi nafilatanlaka asa an yabathaka rabuka maqaman mahmuda
17:80	waqul rabi adkhilni mudkhala sidqinwakhrijni mukhraja sidqin wajal li minladunka sultanan nasira
17:81	waqul ja alhaqu wazahaqa albatiluina albatila kana zahuqa
17:82	wanunazilu mina alqur ani mahuwa shifaun warahmatun lilmu minina walayazidu aththalimina ilakhasara
17:83	wa itha anamna alal insani arada wana bijanibihiwa itha masahu ashsharu kana yausa
17:84	qul kulun yamalu ala shakilatihifarabukum alamu biman huwa ahda sabila
17:85	wayas alunaka ani aruhiquli aruhu min amri rabi wama utitumina alailmi ila qalila
17:86	wala in shi na lanathabanabilathi awhayna ilayka thuma latajidu laka bihi alayna wakila
17:87	ila rahmatan min rabika inafadlahu kana alayka kabira
17:88	qul la ini ijtamati al insu waljinuala an ya tu bimithli hatha alqur ani laya tuna bimithlihi walaw kana baduhum libadinthahira
17:89	walaqad sarafna linasifi hatha alqur ani min kuli mathalin fabaktharu anasi ila kufura
17:90	waqalu lan nu mina laka hatatafjura lana mina al ardi yanbua
17:91	aw takuna laka janatun min nakhilinwainabin fatufajira al anhara khilalahatafjira
17:92	aw tusqita asama kamazamta alayna kisafan aw ta tiya bilahiwalmala ikati qabila
17:93	aw yakuna laka baytun min zukhrufin aw tarqafi asama i walan nu mina liruqiyika hatatunazila alayna kitaban naqrauhu qul subhanarabi hal kuntu ila basharan rasula
17:94	wama mana anasa anyu minu ith jahumu alhuda ila an qaluabatha alahu basharan rasula
17:95	qul law kana fi al ardi mala ikatunyamshuna mutma inina lanazalna alayhim mina asama imalakan rasula
17:96	qul kafa bilahishahidan bayni wabaynakum inahu kana biaibadihikhabiran basira
17:97	waman yahdi alahu fahuwa almuhtadiwaman yudlil falan tajida lahum awliya min dunihiwanahshuruhum yawma alqiyamati alawujuhihim aumyan wabukman wasuman ma wahumjahanamu kulama khabat zidnahum saira
17:98	thalika jazauhum bi anahumkafaru bi ayatina waqalu a ithakuna aithaman warufatana ina lamabauthuna khalqan jadida
17:99	awa lam yaraw ana alaha alathikhalaqa asamawati wal ardaqadirun ala an yakhluqa mithlahum wajala lahumajalan la rayba fihi faba aththalimunaila kufura
17:100	qul law antum tamlikuna khaza inarahmati rabi ithan lamsaktum khashyata al infaqiwakana al insanu qatura
17:101	walaqad atayna musatisa ayatin bayinatin fas albani isra ila ith jahum faqalalahu firawnu ini lathunuka ya musamashura
17:102	qala laqad alimta ma anzalahaula i ila rabu asamawatiwal ardi basa ira wa ini lathunukaya firawnu mathbura
17:103	farada an yastafizahum mina al ardifaghraqnahu waman mahu jamia
17:104	waqulna min badihi libani isra ilauskunu al arda fa itha ja wadu al akhiratiji na bikum lafifa
17:105	wabilhaqi anzalnahuwabilhaqi nazala wama arsalnaka ilamubashshiran wanathira
17:106	waqur anan faraqnahulitaqrahu ala anasi alamukthin wanazalnahu tanzila
17:107	qul aminu bihi aw latu minu ina alathina utu alailma min qablihi ithayutla alayhim yakhiruna lil athqanisujada
17:108	wayaquluna subhana rabinain kana wadu rabina lamafaula
17:109	wayakhiruna lil athqaniyabkuna wayaziduhum khushua
17:110	quli udau alaha awi udau arahmanayan ma tadau falahu al asmau alhusnawala tajhar bisalatika wala tukhafitbiha wabtaghi bayna thalika sabila
17:111	waquli alhamdu lilahi alathilam yatakhith waladan walam yakun lahu sharikun fialmulki walam yakun lahu waliyun mina aththuliwakabirhu takbira
18:1	alhamdu lilahi alathianzala ala abdihi alkitaba walam yajal lahuaiwaja
18:2	qayiman liyunthira ba san shadidanmin ladunhu wayubashshira almu minina alathinayamaluna asalihati ana lahum ajran hasana
18:3	makithina fihi abada
18:4	wayunthira alathina qaluitakhatha alahu walada
18:5	ma lahum bihi min ailmin walali aba ihim kaburat kalimatan takhruju min afwahihimin yaquluna ila kathiba
18:6	falalaka bakhiaun nafsaka alatharihim in lam yu minu bihatha alhadithiasafa
18:7	ina jalna ma alal ardi zinatan laha linabluwahum ayuhum ahsanuamala
18:8	wa ina lajailuna malayha saidan juruza
18:9	am hasibta ana as habalkahfi waraqimi kanu min ayatinajaba
18:10	ith awa alfityatu ilalkahfi faqalu rabana atina minladunka rahmatan wahayi lana min amrinarashada
18:11	fadarabna ala athanihimfi alkahfi sinina adada
18:12	thuma bathnahum linalama ayualhizbayni ahsa lima labithu amada
18:13	nahnu naqusu alaykanabahum bilhaqi inahum fityatun amanubirabihim wazidnahum huda
18:14	warabatna alaqulubihim ith qamu faqalu rabunarabu asamawati wal ardilan nadauwa min dunihi ilahan laqad qulna ithanshatata
18:15	haula i qawmuna itakhathumin dunihi alihatan lawla ya tuna alayhim bisultaninbayinin faman athlamu mimani iftara alalahi kathiba
18:16	wa ithi iatazaltumuhum wamayabuduna ila alaha fa wu ila alkahfiyanshur lakum rabukum min rahmatihi wayuhayi lakum minamrikum mirfaqa
18:17	watara ashshamsa itha talatazawaru an kahfihim thata alyamini wa ithagharabat taqriduhum thata ashshimaliwahum fi fajwatin minhu thalika min ayatialahi man yahdi alahu fahuwa almuhtadi waman yudlilfalan tajida lahu waliyan murshida
18:18	watahsabuhum ayqathanwahum ruqudun wanuqalibuhum thata alyamini wathatashshimali wakalbuhum basitun thirayhibilwasidi lawi italata alayhimlawalayta minhum firaran walamuli ta minhum ruaba
18:19	wakathalika bathnahumliyatasalu baynahum qala qa ilun minhumkam labithtum qalu labithna yawman aw badayawmin qalu rabukum alamu bima labithtum fabathuahadakum biwariqikum hathihi ila almadinatifalyanthur ayuha azka tamanfalya tikum birizqin minhu walyatalataf walayushairana bikum ahada
18:20	inahum in yatharu alaykumyarjumukum aw yuaidukum fi milatihim walan tuflihuithan abada
18:21	wakathalika atharnalayhim liyalamu ana wada alahi haqunwana asata la rayba fiha ithyatanazauna baynahum amrahum faqalu ibnualayhim bunyanan rabuhum alamu bihim qala alathinaghalabu ala amrihim lanatakhithana alayhimasjida
18:22	sayaquluna thalathatun rabiauhumkalbuhum wayaquluna khamsatun sadisuhum kalbuhum rajmanbilghaybi wayaquluna sabatun wathaminuhumkalbuhum qul rabi alamu biaidatihim ma yalamuhumila qalilun fala tumari fihim ilamiran thahiran wala tastaftifihim minhum ahada
18:23	wala taqulana lishay in ini failunthalika ghada
18:24	ila an yasha alahuwathkur rabaka itha nasita waqul asan yahdiyani rabi li aqraba min hatha rashada
18:25	walabithu fi kahfihim thalathami atin sinina wazdadu tisa
18:26	quli alahu alamu bimalabithu lahu ghaybu asamawati wal ardiabsir bihi wasmia ma lahum min dunihi minwaliyin wala yushriku fi hukmihi ahada
18:27	watlu ma uhiya ilaykamin kitabi rabika la mubadila likalimatihiwalan tajida min dunihi multahada
18:28	wasbir nafsaka ma alathinayadauna rabahum bilghadati walashiyiyuriduna wajhahu wala tadu aynaka anhumturidu zinata alhayati adunya walatutia man aghfalna qalbahu an thikrinawataba hawahu wakana amruhu furuta
18:29	waquli alhaqu min rabikum faman shafalyu min waman sha falyakfur ina atadnaliththalimina naran ahatabihim suradiquha wa in yastaghithu yughathubima in kalmuhli yashwi alwujuha bi sa ashsharabuwasat murtafaqa
18:30	ina alathina amanuwamilu asalihati ina lanudiau ajra man ahsana amala
18:31	ula ika lahum janatu adnintajri min tahtihimu al anharu yuhalawnafiha min asawira min thahabin waylbasunathiyaban khudran min sundusin wa istabraqinmutaki ina fiha ala al ara iki niama aththawabuwahasunat murtafaqa
18:32	wadrib lahum mathalanrajulayni jalna li ahadihima janataynimin anabin wahafafnahuma binakhlinwajalna baynahuma zara
18:33	kilta aljanatayni atat ukulahawalam tathlim minhu shay an wafajarna khilalahumanahara
18:34	wakana lahu thamarun faqala lisahibihiwahuwa yuhawiruhu ana aktharu minka malanwazu nafara
18:35	wadakhala janatahu wahuwa thalimunlinafsihi qala ma athunu an tabidahathihi abada
18:36	wama athunu asataqa imatan wala in rudidtu ila rabi lajidanakhayran minha munqalaba
18:37	qala lahu sahibuhu wahuwa yuhawiruhuakafarta bilathi khalaqaka min turabinthuma min nutfatin thuma sawaka rajula
18:38	lakina huwa alahu rabi walaushriku birabi ahada
18:39	walawla ith dakhalta janatakaqulta ma sha alahu la quwata ilabilahi in tarani ana aqala minka malanwawalada
18:40	fasa rabi an yu tiyani khayranmin janatika wayursila alayha husbananmina asama i fatusbiha saidanzalaqa
18:41	aw yusbiha mauhaghawran falan tastatia lahu talaba
18:42	wauhita bithamarihi fasbahayuqalibu kafayhi ala ma anfaqa fihawahiya khawiyatun ala aurushihawayaqulu ya laytani lam ushrik birabi ahada
18:43	walam takun lahu fi atun yansurunahumin duni alahi wama kana muntasira
18:44	hunalika alwalayatu lilahialhaqi huwa khayrun thawaban wakhayrun auqba
18:45	wadrib lahum mathala alhayatiadunya kama in anzalnahu mina asama ifakhtalata bihi nabatu al ardi fasbahahashiman tathruhu ariyahu wakanalahu ala kuli shay in muqtadira
18:46	almalu walbanuna zinatu alhayatiadunya walbaqiyatu asalihatukhayrun ainda rabika thawaban wakhayrun amala

20:79	wadala firawnu qawmahu wamahada

20:81	kulu min tayibati marazaqnakum wala tatghaw fihi fayahilalaykum ghadabi waman yahlil alayhi ghadabifaqad hawa
20:82	wa ini laghafarun liman tabawamana wamila salihan thuma ihtada

20:87	qalu ma akhlafnamawaidaka bimalkina walakina humilnawzaran min zinati alqawmi faqathafnahafakathalika alqa asamiriy

20:98	inama ilahukumu alahualathi la ilaha ila huwa wasiakula shay in ailma

21:1	iqtaraba linasi hisabuhumwahum fi ghaflatin muaridun

21:25	wama arsalna min qablika minrasulin ila nuhi ilayhi anahu la ilahaila ana fabudun

21:27	la yasbiqunahu bilqawliwahum bi amrihi yamalun

22:1	ya ayuha anasuitaqu rabakum ina zalzalata asati shay unathim
22:2	yawma tarawnaha tathalu kulumurdiatin ama ardat watadaukulu thati hamlin hamlaha wataranasa sukara wama hum bisukarawalakina athaba alahi shadid
22:3	wamina anasi man yujadilufi alahi bighayri ailmin wayatabiau kula shaytaninmarid
22:4	kutiba alayhi anahu man tawalahufanahu yudiluhu wayahdihi ila athabi asair
22:5	ya ayuha anasuin kuntum fi raybin mina albathi fa ina khalaqnakumin turabin thuma min nutfatin thuma minalaqatin thuma min mudghatin mukhalaqatin waghayrimukhalaqatin linubayina lakum wanuqiru fi al arhami manashau ila ajalin musaman thuma nukhrijukum tiflanthuma litablughu ashudakum waminkum man yutawafawaminkum man yuradu ila arthali alaumuri likaylayalama min badi ailmin shay an watara al ardahamidatan fa itha anzalna alayhalma ihtazat warabat wanbatat min kuli zawjin bahij
22:6	thalika bi ana alaha huwa alhaquwanahu yuhyi almawta wanahu ala kulishay in qadir
22:7	wana asata atiyatunla rayba fiha wana alaha yabathu manfi alqubur
22:8	wamina anasi man yujadilufi alahi bighayri ailmin wala hudan walakitabin munir
22:9	thaniya aitfihi liyudilan sabili alahi lahu fi adunyakhizyun wanuthiquhu yawma alqiyamati athabalhariq
22:10	thalika bima qadamat yadakawana alaha laysa bithalaminlilabid
22:11	wamina anasi man yabudualaha ala harfin fa in asabahukhayrun itmana bihi wa in asabat hu fitnatuninqalaba ala wajhihi khasira adunya wal akhiratathalika huwa alkhusranu almubin
22:12	yadau min duni alahi ma layaduruhu wama la yanfauhu thalikahuwa adalalu albaid
22:13	yadau laman daruhu aqrabu minafaihi labi sa almawla walabi sa alashir
22:14	ina alaha yudkhilu alathinamanu wamilu asalihati janatintajri min tahtiha al anharu ina alahayafalu ma yurid
22:15	man kana yathunu anlan yansurahu alahu fi adunya wal akhiratifalyamdud bisababin ila asama i thumaliyaqta falyanthur hal yuthibanakayduhu ma yaghith
22:16	wakathalika anzalnahu ayatinbayinatin wana alaha yahdi man yurid
22:17	ina alathina amanu walathinahadu wasabi ina wanasarawalmajusa walathina ashraku ina alahayafsilu baynahum yawma alqiyamati ina alahala kuli shay in shahid
22:18	alam tara ana alaha yasjudu lahuman fi asamawati waman fi al ardiwashshamsu walqamaru wanujumu waljibaluwashshajaru wadawabu wakathirun minanasi wakathirun haqa alayhi alathabuwaman yuhini alahu fama lahu min mukrimin ina alahayafalu ma yasha 
22:19	hathani khasmani ikhtasamufi rabihim falathina kafaru qutiatlahum thiyabun min narin yusabu min fawqirusihimu alhamim
22:20	yusharu bihi ma fi butunihimwaljulud
22:21	walahum maqamiau min hadid
22:22	kulama aradu an yakhrujuminha min ghamin uaidu fiha wathuquathaba alhariq
22:23	ina alaha yudkhilu alathinamanu wamilu asalihati janatintajri min tahtiha al anharu yuhalawnafiha min asawira min thahabin walu lu anwalibasuhum fiha harir
22:24	wahudu ila atayibimina alqawli wahudu ila sirati alhamid
22:25	ina alathina kafaru wayasudunan sabili alahi walmasjidi alharamialathi jalnahu linasi sawanalakifu fihi walbadi waman yurid fihibi ilhadin bithulmin nuthiqhu min athabinalim
22:26	wa ith bawa na li ibrahimamakana albayti an la tushrik bi shay an watahirbaytiya lita ifina walqa iminawarukai asujud
22:27	waththin fi anasibilhaji ya tuka rijalan walakuli damirin ya tina min kuli fajin amiq
22:28	liyashadu manafia lahum wayathkuruisma alahi fi ayamin malumatin alama razaqahum min bahimati al anami fakulu minhawataimu alba isa alfaqir
22:29	thuma lyaqdu tafathahum walyufunuthurahum walyatawafu bilbaytialatiq
22:30	thalika waman yuaththimhurumati alahi fahuwa khayrun lahu aindarabihi wauhilat lakumu al anamu ila mayutla alaykum fajtanibu arijsa minal awthani wajtanibu qawla azur
22:31	hunafa lilahi ghayramushrikina bihi waman yushrik bilahi fakanamakhara mina asama i fatakhtafuhu atayruaw tahwi bihi arihu fi makanin sahiq
22:32	thalika waman yuaththimsha ira alahi fa inaha min taqwalqulub
22:33	lakum fiha manafiau ilajalin musaman thuma mahiluha ila albaytialatiq
22:34	walikuli umatin jalna mansakanliyathkuru isma alahi ala marazaqahum min bahimati al anami fa ilahukum ilahunwahidun falahu aslimu wabashshiri almukhbitin
22:35	alathina itha thukiralahu wajilat qulubuhum wasabirinala ma asabahum walmuqimi asalatiwamima razaqnahum yunfiqun
22:36	walbudna jalnahalakum min sha iri alahi lakum fihakhayrun fathkuru isma alahi alayhasawafa fa itha wajabat junubuhafakulu minha wataimu alqania walmuatarakathalika sakhkharnaha lakum lalakumtashkurun
22:37	lan yanala alaha luhumuhawala dimauha walakin yanaluhuataqwa minkum kathalika sakhkharahalakum litukabiru alaha ala ma hadakumwabashshiri almuhsinin
22:38	ina alaha yudafiau anialathina amanu ina alaha la yuhibukula khawanin kafur

22:41	alathina in makanahum fial ardi aqamu asalata watawuazakata wamaru bilmarufi wanahawani almunkari walilahi aqibatu al umur

22:62	thalika bi ana alaha huwa alhaquwana ma yadauna min dunihi huwa albatiluwana alaha huwa alaliyu alkabir

22:66	wahuwa alathi ahyakumthuma yumitukum thuma yuhyikum ina al insanalakafur
22:67	likuli umatin jalna mansakan humnasikuhu fala yunaziaunaka fi al amri wadauila rabika inaka lala hudan mustaqim
22:68	wa in jadaluka faquli alahualamu bima tamalun
22:69	alahu yahkumu baynakum yawmalqiyamati fima kuntum fihi takhtalifun
22:70	alam talam ana alaha yalamu mafi asama i wal ardi ina thalikafi kitabin ina thalika ala alahiyasir

24:1	suratun anzalnaha wafaradnahawanzalna fiha ayatin bayinatinlalakum tathakarun
24:2	azaniyatu wazanifajlidu kula wahidin minhuma mi atajaldatin wala ta khuthkum bihima ra fatunfi dini alahi in kuntum tu minuna bilahiwalyawmi al akhiri walyashad athabahumata ifatun mina almu minin
24:3	azani la yankihuila zaniyatan aw mushrikatan wazaniyatula yankihuha ila zanin awmushrikun wahurima thalika alalmu minin
24:4	walathina yarmuna almuhsanatithuma lam ya tu bi arbati shuhada fajliduhumthamanina jaldatan wala taqbalu lahum shahadatanabadan waula ika humu alfasiqun
24:5	ila alathina tabumin badi thalika waslahu fa ina alahaghafurun rahim
24:6	walathina yarmuna azwajahumwalam yakun lahum shuhadau ila anfusuhum fashahadatuahadihim arbau shahadatin bilahinahu lamina asadiqin

24:9	walkhamisata ana ghadabalahi alayha in kana mina asadiqin
24:10	walawla fadlu alahialaykum warahmatuhu wana alaha tawabunhakim
24:11	ina alathina jau bil ifkiausbatun minkum la tahsabuhu sharan lakumbal huwa khayrun lakum likuli imri in minhum ma iktasabamina al ithmi walathi tawala kibrahuminhum lahu athabun athim
24:12	lawla ith samiatumuhu thanalmu minuna walmu minatu bi anfusihim khayran waqaluhatha ifkun mubin
24:13	lawla jau alayhibi arbati shuhada fa ith lam ya tu bishshuhada ifaula ika ainda alahi humu alkathibun
24:14	walawla fadlu alahialaykum warahmatuhu fi adunya wal akhiratilamasakum fi ma afadtum fihi athabunathim
24:15	ith talaqawnahu bi alsinatikumwataquluna bi afwahikum ma laysa lakum bihiailmun watahsabunahu hayinan wahuwa ainda alahiathim
24:16	walawla ith samiatumuhuqultum ma yakunu lana an natakalama bihathasubhanaka hatha buhtanun athim
24:17	yaithukumu alahu antaudu limithlihi abadan in kuntum mu minin

24:20	walawla fadlu alahialaykum warahmatuhu wana alaha raufun rahim
24:21	ya ayuha alathina amanula tatabiau khutuwati ashshaytaniwaman yatabia khutuwati ashshaytanifa inahu ya muru bilfahsha i walmunkariwalawla fadlu alahi alaykum warahmatuhuma zaka minkum min ahadin abadan walakinalaha yuzaki man yashau walahusamiaun alim
24:22	wala ya tali ulu alfadliminkum wasati an yu tu uli alqurba walmasakinawalmuhajirina fi sabili alahiwalyafu walyasfahu ala tuhibunan yaghfira alahu lakum walahu ghafurunrahim
24:23	ina alathina yarmuna almuhsanatialghafilati almu minati luainu fi adunyawal akhirati walahum athabun athim
24:24	yawma tashadu alayhim alsinatuhumwaydihim warjuluhum bima kanu yamalun

24:26	alkhabithatu lilkhabithina walkhabithunalilkhabithati watayibatu litayibinawatayibuna litayibatiula ika mubarauna mima yaquluna lahumaghfiratun warizqun karim
24:27	ya ayuha alathina amanula tadkhulu buyutan ghayra buyutikum hatatasta nisu watusalimu ala ahliha thalikumkhayrun lakum lalakum tathakarun
24:28	fa in lam tajidu fiha ahadanfala tadkhuluha hata yu thanalakum wa in qila lakumu irjiau farjiau huwa azkalakum walahu bima tamaluna alim
24:29	laysa alaykum junahun an tadkhulubuyutan ghayra maskunatin fiha mataun lakum walahuyalamu ma tubduna wama taktumun
24:30	qul lilmu minina yaghudu min absarihumwayahfathu furujahum thalika azkalahum ina alaha khabirun bima yasnaun
24:31	waqul lilmu minati yaghdudnamin absarihina wayahfathnafurujahuna wala yubdina zinatahuna ila mathahara minha walyadribnabikhumurihina ala juyubihina wala yubdinazinatahuna ila libuaulatihina aw aba ihinaw aba i buaulatihina aw abna ihina awabna i buaulatihina aw ikhwanihina aw banikhwanihina aw bani akhawatihina aw nisa ihinaw ma malakat aymanuhuna awi atabiainaghayri uli al irbati mina arijali awi atiflialathina lam yatharu ala awratianisa i wala yadribnabi arjulihina liyualama ma yukhfina min zinatihinawatubu ila alahi jamian ayuhalmu minuna lalakum tuflihun
24:32	wankihu al ayamaminkum wasalihina min aibadikumwa ima ikum in yakunu fuqara yughnihimu alahumin fadlihi walahu wasiaun alim
24:33	walyastafifi alathina layajiduna nikahan hata yughniyahumu alahumin fadlihi walathina yabtaghuna alkitabamima malakat aymanukum fakatibuhum inalimtum fihim khayran watuhum min mali alahialathi atakum wala tukrihu fatayatikumala albigha i in aradna tahasunanlitabtaghu arada alhayati adunyawaman yukrihuna fa ina alaha min badi ikrahihinaghafurun rahim
24:34	walaqad anzalna ilaykum ayatinmubayinatin wamathalan mina alathina khalaw minqablikum wamawaithatan lilmutaqin

27:1	ta sin tilka ayatualqur ani wakitabin mubin
27:2	hudan wabushra lilmu minin

27:5	ula ika alathina lahum su ualathabi wahum fi al akhirati humu al akhsarun

27:8	falama jaha nudiya anburika man fi anari waman hawlahawasubhana alahi rabi alalamin

27:12	wadkhil yadaka fi jaybika takhruj baydamin ghayri su in fi tisai ayatin ilafirawna waqawmihi inahum kanu qawman fasiqin

27:14	wajahadu biha wastayqanat hanfusuhum thulman wauluwan fanthurkayfa kana aqibatu almufsidin

27:16	wawaritha sulaymanu dawudawaqala ya ayuha anasuaulimna mantiqa atayri wautinamin kuli shay in ina hatha lahuwa alfadlualmubin
27:17	wahushira lisulaymanajunuduhu mina aljini wal insi watayrifahum yuzaun
27:18	hata itha ataw alawadi anamli qalat namlatun ya ayuhanamlu udkhulu masakinakum la yahtimanakumsulaymanu wajunuduhu wahum la yashaurun
27:19	fatabasama dahikan min qawlihawaqala rabi awziani an ashkura niamataka alatianamta alaya wala walidaya wan amala salihantardahu wadkhilni birahmatika fi aibadikasalihin
27:20	watafaqada atayra faqalama liya la ara alhudhuda am kana minalgha ibin
27:21	lauaththibanahu athabanshadidan aw lathbahanahu aw laya tiyani bisultaninmubin
27:22	famakatha ghayra baidin faqala ahatubima lam tuhit bihi waji tuka min saba inbinaba in yaqin

27:33	qalu nahnu ulu quwatinwaulu ba sin shadidin wal amru ilayki fanthurimatha ta murin
27:34	qalat ina almuluka ithadakhalu qaryatan afsaduha wajalu aizata ahlihathilatan wakathalika yafalun
27:35	wa ini mursilatun ilayhim bihadiyatin fanathiratunbima yarjiau almursalun
27:36	falama ja sulaymana qalatumidunani bimalin fama ataniyalahu khayrun mima atakum bal antumbihadiyatikum tafrahun
27:37	irjia ilayhim falana tiyanahum bijunudinla qibala lahum biha walanukhrijanahum minhathilatan wahum saghirun

27:60	aman khalaqa asamawatiwal arda wanzala lakum mina asama iman fanbatna bihi hada iqa thatabahjatin ma kana lakum an tunbitu shajaraha ilahun ma alahi bal hum qawmun yadilun
27:61	aman jala al arda qararanwajala khilalaha anharan wajala laharawasiya wajala bayna albahrayni hajizana ilahun ma alahi bal aktharuhum layalamun
27:62	aman yujibu almudtara ithadahu wayakshifu asu a wayajalukum khulafal ardi a ilahun ma alahi qalilan matathakarun
27:63	aman yahdikum fi thulumatialbari walbahri waman yursilu ariyahabushran bayna yaday rahmatihi a ilahun ma alahitala alahu ama yushrikun

28:2	tilka ayatu alkitabialmubin
28:3	natlu alayka min naba i musawafirawna bilhaqi liqawmin yu minun

28:7	wawhayna ila umi musan ardiaihi fa itha khifti alayhi falqihi fialyami wala takhafi wala tahzanina raduhu ilayki wajailuhu minalmursalin
28:8	faltaqatahu alufirawna liyakuna lahum aduwan wahazanan inafirawna wahamana wajunudahuma kanukhati in
28:9	waqalati imratu firawna quratuaynin li walaka la taqtuluhu asa an yanfanaw natakhithahu waladan wahum la yashaurun
28:10	wasbaha fu adu umimusa farighan in kadat latubdi bihi lawlan rabatna ala qalbiha litakunamina almu minin
28:11	waqalat li ukhtihi qusihifabasurat bihi an junubin wahum la yashaurun
28:12	waharamna alayhi almaradiamin qablu faqalat hal adulukum ala ahli baytinyakfulunahu lakum wahum lahu nasihun
28:13	faradadnahu ila umihi kaytaqara aynuha wala tahzana walitalamana wada alahi haqun walakinaktharahum la yalamun
28:14	walama balagha ashudahu wastawataynahu hukman wailman wakathalikanajzi almuhsinin
28:15	wadakhala almadinata ala hinighaflatin min ahliha fawajada fiha rajulayniyaqtatilani hatha min shiatihi wahathamin aduwihi fastaghathahu alathi minshiatihi ala alathi min aduwihifawakazahu musa faqada alayhi qala hathamin amali ashshaytani inahu aduwun mudilunmubin
28:16	qala rabi ini thalamtunafsi faghfir li faghafara lahu inahu huwa alghafuruarahim
28:17	qala rabi bima anamtalaya falan akuna thahiran lilmujrimin
28:18	fasbaha fi almadinati kha ifanyataraqabu fa itha alathi istansarahu bil amsiyastasrikhuhu qala lahu musa inakalaghawiyun mubin
28:19	falama an arada an yabtishabilathi huwa aduwun lahuma qalaya musa aturidu an taqtulani kama qataltanafsan bil amsi in turidu ila an takuna jabaranfi al ardi wama turidu an takuna mina almuslihin
28:20	waja rajulun min aqsalmadinati yasa qala ya musa inalmala ya tamiruna bika liyaqtuluka fakhruj ini lakamina anasihin
28:21	fakharaja minha kha ifanyataraqabu qala rabi najini mina alqawmi aththalimin
28:22	walama tawajaha tilqamadyana qala asa rabi an yahdiyani sawasabil
28:23	walama warada ma madyanawajada alayhi umatan mina anasi yasqunawawajada min dunihimu imratayni tathudani qalama khatbukuma qalata lanasqi hata yusdira ariauwabuna shaykhun kabir
28:24	fasaqa lahuma thuma tawalaila aththili faqala rabini lima anzalta ilaya min khayrin faqir
28:25	fajat hu ihdahumatamshi ala istihya in qalat inabi yadauka liyajziyaka ajra ma saqayta lanafalama jahu waqasa alayhi alqasasaqala la takhaf najawta mina alqawmi aththalimin
28:26	qalat ihdahuma yabati ista jirhu ina khayra mani ista jarta alqawiyu al amin
28:27	qala ini uridu an unkihakaihda ibnataya hatayni ala anta jurani thamaniya hijajin fa in atmamta ashranfamin aindika wama uridu an ashuqa alayka satajidunin sha alahu mina asalihin
28:28	qala thalika bayni wabaynakayama al ajalayni qadaytu fala audwanalaya walahu ala ma naquluwakil
28:29	falama qada musal ajala wasara bi ahlihi anasa min janibi aturinaran qala li ahlihi umkuthu ini anastu naranlali atikum minha bikhabarin aw jathwatinmina anari lalakum tastalun
28:30	falama ataha nudiyamin shati i alwadi al aymani fi albuqati almubarakatimina ashshajarati an ya musa ini analahu rabu alalamin
28:31	wan alqi asaka falama rahatahtazu kanaha janun wala mudbiranwalam yuaqib ya musa aqbil wala takhafinaka mina al aminin
28:32	usluk yadaka fi jaybika takhruj baydamin ghayri su in wadmum ilayka janahakamina arahbi fathanika burhanani minrabika ila firawna wamala ihi inahum kanuqawman fasiqin
28:33	qala rabi ini qataltu minhumnafsan fakhafu an yaqtulun
28:34	wakhi harunu huwa afsahumini lisanan farsilhu maiya rid an yusadiqunini akhafu an yukaththibun
28:35	qala sanashudu adudakabi akhika wanajalu lakuma sultanan falayasiluna ilaykuma bi ayatinantuma wamani itabakuma alghalibun
28:36	falama jahum musabi ayatina bayinatin qalu mahatha ila sihrun muftaran wamasamiana bihatha fi aba inal awalin
28:37	waqala musa rabi alamubiman ja bilhuda min aindihi wamantakunu lahu aqibatu adari inahu layuflihu aththalimun
28:38	waqala firawnu ya ayuhalmalau ma alimtu lakum min ilahin ghayrifawqid li ya hamanu ala atinifajal li sarhan lali ataliauila ilahi musa wa ini lathunuhumina alkathibin
28:39	wastakbara huwa wajunuduhu fial ardi bighayri alhaqi wathanuanahum ilayna la yurjaun
28:40	fakhathnahu wajunudahufanabathnahum fi alyami fanthurkayfa kana aqibatu aththalimin
28:41	wajalnahum a imatan yadauna ilanari wayawma alqiyamati la yunsarun
28:42	watbanahum fi hathihi adunyalanatan wayawma alqiyamati hum mina almaqbuhin
28:43	walaqad atayna musalkitaba min badi ma ahlakna alqurunal ula basa ira linasi wahudanwarahmatan lalahum yatathakarun
28:44	wama kunta bijanibialgharbiyi ith qadayna ila musal amra wama kunta mina ashshahidin
28:45	walakina ansha naqurunan fatatawala alayhimu alaumuru wama kuntathawiyan fi ahli madyana tatlu alayhim ayatinawalakina kuna mursilin
28:46	wama kunta bijanibi aturith nadayna walakin rahmatanmin rabika litunthira qawman ma atahum minathirin min qablika lalahum yatathakarun
28:47	walawla an tusibahum musibatunbima qadamat aydihim fayaqulu rabana lawlarsalta ilayna rasulan fanatabia ayatikawanakuna mina almu minin
28:48	falama jahumu alhaqumin aindina qalu lawla utiya mithla mautiya musa awa lam yakfuru bima utiya musamin qablu qalu sihrani tathaharawaqalu ina bikulin kafirun
28:49	qul fa tu bikitabin min aindi alahihuwa ahda minhuma atabiahu in kuntum sadiqin
28:50	fa in lam yastajibu laka falamanama yatabiauna ahwahum waman adalumimani itaba hawahu bighayri hudan mina alahina alaha la yahdi alqawma aththalimin
28:51	walaqad wasalna lahumualqawla lalahum yatathakarun
28:52	alathina ataynahumualkitaba min qablihi hum bihi yu minun
28:53	wa itha yutla alayhim qaluamana bihi inahu alhaqu min rabinaina kuna min qablihi muslimin
28:54	ula ika yu tawna ajrahum marataynibima sabaru wayadrauna bilhasanatiasayi ata wamima razaqnahum yunfiqun
28:55	wa itha samiau alaghwa araduanhu waqalu lana amaluna walakumamalukum salamun alaykum la nabtaghialjahilin
28:56	inaka la tahdi man ahbabtawalakina alaha yahdi man yashau wahuwalamu bilmuhtadin
28:57	waqalu in natabiai alhudamaka nutakhataf min ardina awa lamnumakin lahum haraman aminan yujba ilayhithamaratu kuli shay in rizqan min laduna walakinaktharahum la yalamun
28:58	wakam ahlakna min qaryatin batiratmaishataha fatilka masakinuhum lam tuskan minbadihim ila qalilan wakuna nahnu alwarithin
28:59	wama kana rabuka muhlikalqura hata yabatha fi umiharasulan yatlu alayhim ayatina wamakuna muhliki alqura ila wahluha thalimun
28:60	wama utitum min shay in famataualhayati adunya wazinatuhawama ainda alahi khayrun wabqa afalataqilun
28:61	afaman wadnahu wadan hasananfahuwa laqihi kaman matanahu mata alhayatiadunya thuma huwa yawma alqiyamati minalmuhdarin
28:62	wayawma yunadihim fayaqulu aynashuraka iya alathina kuntum tazaumun
28:63	qala alathina haqalayhimu alqawlu rabana haula i alathinaghwayna aghwaynahum kama ghawaynatabara na ilayka ma kanu iyanayabudun
28:64	waqila udau shurakakumfadawhum falam yastajibu lahum warawu alathabalaw anahum kanu yahtadun
28:65	wayawma yunadihim fayaqulu mathajabtumu almursalin
28:66	famiyat alayhimu al anbauyawma ithin fahum la yatasalun
28:67	fama man taba wamanawamila salihan fasa an yakuna minalmuflihin
28:68	warabuka yakhluqu ma yashauwayakhtaru ma kana lahumu alkhiyaratu subhanalahi watala ama yushrikun
28:69	warabuka yalamu ma tukinu suduruhumwama yualinun
28:70	wahuwa alahu la ilahaila huwa lahu alhamdu fi al ula wal akhiratiwalahu alhukmu wa ilayhi turjaun
28:71	qul araytum in jala alahualaykumu alayla sarmadan ila yawmi alqiyamatiman ilahun ghayru alahi ya tikum bidiya inafala tasmaun
28:72	qul araytum in jala alahualaykumu anahara sarmadan ila yawmialqiyamati man ilahun ghayru alahiya tikum bilaylin taskununa fihi afala tubsirun
28:73	wamin rahmatihi jala lakumualayla wanahara litaskunu fihi walitabtaghumin fadlihi walalakum tashkurun
28:74	wayawma yunadihim fayaqulu aynashuraka iya alathina kuntum tazaumun
28:75	wanazana min kuli umatinshahidan faqulna hatu burhanakumfalimu ana alhaqa lilahi wadalanhum ma kanu yaftarun
28:76	ina qaruna kana min qawmimusa fabagha alayhim wataynahumina alkunuzi ma ina mafatihahu latanu ubilausbati uli alquwati ith qalalahu qawmuhu la tafrah ina alaha layuhibu alfarihin
28:77	wabtaghi fima atakalahu adara al akhirata walatansa nasibaka mina adunya wahsinkama ahsana alahu ilayka wala tabghialfasada fi al ardi ina alaha layuhibu almufsidin
28:78	qala inama utituhu alailmin aindi awa lam yalam ana alaha qad ahlaka minqablihi mina alquruni man huwa ashadu minhu quwatan waktharujaman wala yus alu an thunubihimu almujrimun
28:79	fakharaja ala qawmihi fi zinatihiqala alathina yuriduna alhayata adunyaya layta lana mithla ma utiya qarunuinahu lathu haththin athim
28:80	waqala alathina utualailma waylakum thawabu alahi khayrun liman amanawamila salihan wala yulaqahaila asabirun
28:81	fakhasafna bihi wabidarihial arda fama kana lahu min fi atin yansurunahumin duni alahi wama kana mina almuntasirin
28:82	wasbaha alathinatamanaw makanahu bil amsi yaquluna waykanalaha yabsutu arizqa liman yashaumin aibadihi wayaqdiru lawla an mana alahualayna lakhasafa bina waykanahu la yuflihualkafirun
28:83	tilka adaru al akhiratunajaluha lilathina la yuridunauluwan fi al ardi wala fasadan walaqibatulilmutaqin
28:84	man ja bilhasanatifalahu khayrun minha waman ja bisayi atifala yujza alathina amilu asayi atila ma kanu yamalun
28:85	ina alathi farada alaykalqur ana laraduka ila madin qulrabi alamu man ja bilhuda waman huwafi dalalin mubin
28:86	wama kunta tarju an yulqailayka alkitabu ila rahmatan min rabikafala takunana thahiran lilkafirin
28:87	wala yasudunaka an ayatialahi bada ith unzilat ilayka wadau ilarabika wala takunana mina almushrikin
28:88	wala tadau ma alahi ilahanakhara la ilaha ila huwa kulushay in halikun ila wajhahu lahu alhukmuwa ilayhi turjaun
29:1	alif lam mim
29:2	ahasiba anasu anyutraku an yaqulu amana wahum layuftanun
29:3	walaqad fatana alathina minqablihim falayalamana alahu alathina sadaquwalayalamana alkathibin
29:4	am hasiba alathinayamaluna asayi ati an yasbiquna sama yahkumun
29:5	man kana yarju liqa alahifa ina ajala alahi latin wahuwa asamiaualalim
29:6	waman jahada fa inama yujahidulinafsihi ina alaha laghaniyun ani alalamin
29:7	walathina amanuwamilu asalihati lanukafirananhum sayi atihim walanajziyanahum ahsana alathikanu yamalun
29:8	wawasayna al insana biwalidayhihusnan wa in jahadaka litushrika bi malaysa laka bihi ailmun fala tutiahumailaya marjiaukum faunabi ukum bima kuntum tamalun
29:9	walathina amanuwamilu asalihati lanudkhilanahum fiasalihin
29:10	wamina anasi man yaqulu amanabilahi fa itha uthiya fi alahijala fitnata anasi kathabi alahiwala in ja nasrun min rabika layaquluna inakuna makum awa laysa alahu bi alama bimafi suduri alalamin
29:11	walayalamana alahu alathinamanu walayalamana almunafiqin
29:12	waqala alathina kafarulilathina amanu itabiau sabilanawalnahmil khatayakum wama hum bihamilinamin khatayahum min shay in inahum lakathibun
29:13	walayahmiluna athqalahumwathqalan ma athqalihim walayus aluna yawmalqiyamati ama kanu yaftarun
29:14	walaqad arsalna nuhan ilaqawmihi falabitha fihim alfa sanatin ila khamsina amanfakhathahumu atufanu wahum thalimun
29:15	fanjaynahu was haba asafinatiwajalnaha ayatan lilalamin
29:16	wa ibrahima ith qalaliqawmihi uabudu alaha wataquhu thalikumkhayrun lakum in kuntum talamun
29:17	inama tabuduna min duni alahiawthanan watakhluquna ifkan ina alathinatabuduna min duni alahi la yamlikuna lakumrizqan fabtaghu ainda alahi arizqa wabuduhuwashkuru lahu ilayhi turjaun
29:18	wa in tukaththibu faqad kaththabaumamun min qablikum wama ala arasulila albalaghu almubin
29:19	awa lam yaraw kayfa yubdi u alahualkhalqa thuma yuaiduhu ina thalika ala alahiyasir
29:20	qul siru fi al ardi fanthurukayfa bada alkhalqa thuma alahu yunshi u anash atal akhirata ina alaha ala kuli shay inqadir
29:21	yuaththibu man yashau wayarhamuman yashau wa ilayhi tuqlabun
29:22	wama antum bimuajizina fi al ardiwala fi asama i wama lakum minduni alahi min waliyin wala nasir
29:23	walathina kafaru bi ayatialahi waliqa ihi ula ika ya isu min rahmatiwaula ika lahum athabun alim
29:24	fama kana jawabaqawmihi ila an qalu uqtuluhu aw hariquhufanjahu alahu mina anari ina fithalika layatin liqawmin yu minun
29:25	waqala inama itakhathtumin duni alahi awthanan mawadata baynikum fi alhayatiadunya thuma yawma alqiyamati yakfurubadukum bibadin wayalanu badukumbadan wama wakumu anaru wamalakum min nasirin
29:26	famana lahu lutun waqalaini muhajirun ila rabi inahu huwa alazizualhakim
29:27	wawahabna lahu ishaqawayaquba wajalna fi thuriyatihi anubuwatawalkitaba wataynahu ajrahu fi adunyawa inahu fi al akhirati lamina asalihin
29:28	walutan ith qalaliqawmihi inakum lata tuna alfahishata masabaqakum biha min ahadin mina alalamin
29:29	a inakum lata tuna arijalawataqtauna asabila wata tuna fi nadikumualmunkara fama kana jawaba qawmihi ilan qalu i tina biathabi alahi inkunta mina asadiqin
29:30	qala rabi unsurni alalqawmi almufsidin
29:31	walama jat rusulunaibrahima bilbushra qalu inamuhliku ahli hathihi alqaryati ina ahlaha kanuthalimin
29:32	qala ina fiha lutanqalu nahnu alamu biman fihalanunajiyanahu wahlahu ila imratahu kanat minalghabirin
29:33	walama an jat rusulunalutan si a bihim wadaqa bihim tharan waqalula takhaf wala tahzan ina munajukawahlaka ila imrataka kanat mina alghabirin
29:34	ina munziluna ala ahli hathihialqaryati rijzan mina asama i bima kanuyafsuqun
29:35	walaqad tarakna minha ayatanbayinatan liqawmin yaqilun
29:36	wa ila madyana akhahumshuayban faqala ya qawmi uabudu alahawarju alyawma al akhira wala tathaw fial ardi mufsidin
29:37	fakaththabuhu fakhathat humuarajfatu fasbahu fi darihim jathimin
29:38	wadan wathamuda waqad tabayanalakum min masakinihim wazayana lahumu ashshaytanuamalahum fasadahum ani asabili wakanumustabsirin
29:39	waqaruna wafirawna wahamanawalaqad jahum musa bilbayinati fastakbarufi al ardi wama kanu sabiqin
29:40	fakulan akhathna bithanbihifaminhum man arsalna alayhi hasiban waminhum manakhathat hu asayhatu waminhum mankhasafna bihi al arda waminhum man aghraqnawama kana alahu liyathlimahumwalakin kanu anfusahum yathlimun
29:41	mathalu alathina itakhathumin duni alahi awliya kamathali alankabutitakhathat baytan wa ina awhana albuyuti labaytualankabuti law kanu yalamun
29:42	ina alaha yalamu mayadauna min dunihi min shay in wahuwa alazizu alhakim
29:43	watilka al amthalu nadribuhalinasi wama yaqiluha ilalalimun
29:44	khalaqa alahu asamawatiwal arda bilhaqi ina fi thalikalayatan lilmu minin
29:45	utlu ma uhiya ilayka minalkitabi waqimi asalata ina asalatatanha ani alfahsha i walmunkariwalathikru alahi akbaru walahuyalamu ma tasnaun
29:46	wala tujadilu ahla alkitabila bilati hiya ahsanu ila alathinathalamu minhum waqulu amana bilathiunzila ilayna waunzila ilaykum wa ilahunawa ilahukum wahidun wanahnu lahu muslimun
29:47	wakathalika anzalna ilaykalkitaba falathina ataynahumualkitaba yu minuna bihi wamin haula i manyu minu bihi wama yajhadu bi ayatinaila alkafirun
29:48	wama kunta tatlu min qablihi min kitabinwala takhutuhu biyaminika ithan lartabalmubtilun
29:49	bal huwa ayatun bayinatunfi suduri alathina utu alailma wamayajhadu bi ayatina ila aththalimun
29:50	waqalu lawla unzila alayhi ayatunmin rabihi qul inama al ayatu ainda alahiwa inama ana nathirun mubin
29:51	awa lam yakfihim ana anzalnalayka alkitaba yutla alayhim ina fi thalikalarahmatan wathikra liqawmin yu minun
29:52	qul kafa bilahibayni wabaynakum shahidan yalamu ma fi asamawatiwal ardi walathina amanubilbatili wakafaru bilahi ula ikahumu alkhasirun
29:53	wayastajilunaka bilathabiwalawla ajalun musaman lajahumu alathabuwalaya tiyanahum baghtatan wahum la yashaurun
29:54	yastajilunaka bilathabiwa ina jahanama lamuhitatun bilkafirin
29:55	yawma yaghshahumu alathabumin fawqihim wamin tahti arjulihim wayaqulu thuquma kuntum tamalun
29:56	ya aibadiya alathinamanu ina ardi wasiatun fa iyayafabudun
29:57	kulu nafsin tha iqatu almawti thumailayna turjaun
29:58	walathina amanuwamilu asalihati lanubawi anahumina aljanati ghurafan tajri min tahtiha al anharukhalidina fiha niama ajru alamilin
29:59	alathina sabaru walarabihim yatawakalun
29:60	wakayin min dabatin la tahmilurizqaha alahu yarzuquha wa iyakumwahuwa asamiau alalim
29:61	wala in saltahum man khalaqa asamawatiwal arda wasakhkhara ashshamsa walqamaralayaquluna alahu fana yu fakun
29:62	alahu yabsutu arizqaliman yashau min aibadihi wayaqdiru lahu ina alahabikuli shay in alim
29:63	wala in saltahum man nazala mina asama iman fahya bihi al arda min badimawtiha layaquluna alahu quli alhamdulilahi bal aktharuhum la yaqilun
29:64	wama hathihi alhayatuadunya ila lahwun walaibun wa ina adaral akhirata lahiya alhayawanu law kanuyalamun
29:65	fa itha rakibu fi alfulki dawualaha mukhlisina lahu adina falamanajahum ila albari itha hum yushrikun
29:66	liyakfuru bima ataynahumwaliyatamatau fasawfa yalamun
29:67	awa lam yaraw ana jalna haramanaminan wayutakhatafu anasu min hawlihimafabilbatili yu minuna wabiniamati alahiyakfurun

30:1	alif lam mim
30:2	ghulibati arum

30:5	binasri alahi yansuruman yashau wahuwa alazizu arahim
30:6	wada alahi la yukhlifu alahuwadahu walakina akthara anasi layalamun
30:7	yalamuna thahiranmina alhayati adunya wahum anial akhirati hum ghafilun
30:8	awa lam yatafakaru fi anfusihim makhalaqa alahu asamawati wal ardawama baynahuma ila bilhaqiwajalin musaman wa ina kathiran mina anasibiliqa i rabihim lakafirun
30:9	awa lam yasiru fi al ardi fayanthurukayfa kana aqibatu alathina min qablihimkanu ashada minhum quwatan watharu al ardawamaruha akthara mima amaruha wajat humrusuluhum bilbayinati fama kana alahuliyathlimahum walakin kanu anfusahumyathlimun
30:10	thuma kana aqibata alathinasau asu a an kaththabu bi ayatialahi wakanu biha yastahzi un

30:13	walam yakun lahum min shuraka ihimshufau wakanu bishuraka ihim kafirin
30:14	wayawma taqumu asatuyawma ithin yatafaraqun

30:16	wama alathina kafaru wakaththabubi ayatina waliqa i al akhiratifaula ika fi alathabi muhdarun
30:17	fasubhana alahi hinatumsuna wahina tusbihun
30:18	walahu alhamdu fi asamawatiwal ardi washiyan wahina tuthirun
30:19	yukhriju alhaya mina almayitiwayukhriju almayita mina alhayi wayuhyi al ardabada mawtiha wakathalika tukhrajun
30:20	wamin ayatihi an khalaqakumin turabin thuma itha antum basharun tantashirun
30:21	wamin ayatihi an khalaqa lakumin anfusikum azwajan litaskunu ilayha wajalabaynakum mawadatan warahmatan ina fi thalika layatinliqawmin yatafakarun
30:22	wamin ayatihi khalqu asamawatiwal ardi wakhtilafu alsinatikumwalwanikum ina fi thalika layatinlilalimin
30:23	wamin ayatihi manamukumbilayli wanahari wabtighaukumin fadlihi ina fi thalika layatinliqawmin yasmaun
30:24	wamin ayatihi yurikumualbarqa khawfan wataman wayunazilu mina asama iman fayuhyi bihi al arda bada mawtihaina fi thalika layatin liqawminyaqilun
30:25	wamin ayatihi an taquma asamauwal ardu bi amrihi thuma itha dakumdawatan mina al ardi itha antum takhrujun
30:26	walahu man fi asamawatiwal ardi kulun lahu qanitun
30:27	wahuwa alathi yabdau alkhalqathuma yuaiduhu wahuwa ahwanu alayhi walahu almathalu al alafi asamawati wal ardiwahuwa alazizu alhakim
30:28	daraba lakum mathalan min anfusikumhal lakum mima malakat aymanukum min shurakafi ma razaqnakum fantum fihi sawaun takhafunahumkakhifatikum anfusakum kathalika nufasilu al ayatiliqawmin yaqilun
30:29	bali itaba alathina thalamuahwahum bighayri ailmin faman yahdi man adalalahu wama lahum min nasirin
30:30	faqim wajhaka lidini hanifanfitrata alahi alati fatara anasalayha la tabdila likhalqi alahi thalikadinu alqayimu walakina akthara anasila yalamun
30:31	munibina ilayhi wataquhuwaqimu asalata wala takunumina almushrikin
30:32	mina alathina faraqu dinahum wakanushiyan kulu hizbin bima ladayhim farihun
30:33	wa itha masa anasa durundaw rabahum munibina ilayhi thuma itha athaqahuminhu rahmatan itha fariqun minhum birabihimyushrikun
30:34	liyakfuru bima ataynahumfatamatau fasawfa talamun
30:35	am anzalna alayhim sultananfahuwa yatakalamu bima kanu bihi yushrikun
30:36	watha athaqna anasarahmatan farihu biha wa in tusibhumsayi atun bima qadamat aydihim itha hum yaqnatun
30:37	awa lam yaraw ana alaha yabsutuarizqa liman yashau wayaqdiru ina fi thalikalayatin liqawmin yu minun
30:38	fati tha alqurba haqahuwalmiskina wabna asabili thalikakhayrun lilathina yuriduna wajha alahi waula ikahumu almuflihun
30:39	wama ataytum min ribanliyarbuwa fi amwali anasi falayarbu ainda alahi wama ataytum min zakatinturiduna wajha alahi faula ika humu almudaifun
30:40	alahu alathi khalaqakumthuma razaqakum thuma yumitukum thuma yuhyikum halmin shuraka ikum man yafalu min thalikum minshay in subhanahu watala amayushrikun
30:41	thahara alfasadu fialbari walbahri bima kasabat aydi anasiliyuthiqahum bada alathi amilulalahum yarjiaun
30:42	qul siru fi al ardi fanthurukayfa kana aqibatu alathina min qablu kanaktharuhum mushrikin
30:43	faqim wajhaka lidini alqayimimin qabli an ya tiya yawmun la marada lahu mina alahiyawma ithin yasadaun
30:44	man kafara falayhi kufruhu waman amila salihanfali anfusihim yamhadun
30:45	liyajziya alathina amanuwamilu asalihati min fadlihinahu la yuhibu alkafirin
30:46	wamin ayatihi an yursila ariyahamubashshiratin waliyuthiqakum min rahmatihiwalitajriya alfulku bi amrihi walitabtaghu min fadlihiwalalakum tashkurun
30:47	walaqad arsalna min qablika rusulanila qawmihim fajauhum bilbayinatifantaqamna mina alathina ajramu wakanahaqan alayna nasru almu minin
30:48	alahu alathi yursilu ariyahafatuthiru sahaban fayabsutuhu fi asama ikayfa yashau wayajaluhu kisafan fatara alwadqayakhruju min khilalihi fa itha asaba bihiman yashau min aibadihi itha humyastabshirun
30:49	wa in kanu min qabli an yunazalalayhim min qablihi lamublisin
30:50	fanthur ila atharirahmati alahi kayfa yuhyi al ardabada mawtiha ina thalika lamuhyi almawtawahuwa ala kuli shay in qadir
30:51	wala in arsalna rihanfarawhu musfaran lathalu min badihiyakfurun
30:52	fa inaka la tusmiau almawtawala tusmiau asuma aduaitha walaw mudbirin
30:53	wama anta bihadi alaumyi andalalatihim in tusmiau ila man yu minu bi ayatinafahum muslimun
30:54	alahu alathi khalaqakum mindafin thuma jala min badi dafin quwatanthuma jala min badi quwatin dafan washaybatanyakhluqu ma yashau wahuwa alalimu alqadir
30:55	wayawma taqumu asatuyuqsimu almujrimuna ma labithu ghayra satin kathalikakanu yu fakun
30:56	waqala alathina utualailma wal imana laqad labithtum fi kitabialahi ila yawmi albathi fahatha yawmualbathi walakinakum kuntum la talamun
30:57	fayawma ithin la yanfau alathinathalamu mathiratuhum wala humyustatabun
30:58	walaqad darabna linasifi hatha alqur ani min kuli mathalin wala inji tahum bi ayatin layaqulana alathina kafaruin antum ila mubtilun
30:59	kathalika yatbau alahuala qulubi alathina la yalamun
30:60	fasbir ina wada alahihaqun wala yastakhifanaka alathina layuqinun

31:2	tilka ayatu alkitabi alhakim
31:3	hudan warahmatan lilmuhsinin
31:4	alathina yuqimuna asalatawayu tuna azakata wahum bil akhiratihum yuqinun
31:5	ula ika ala hudan minrabihim waula ika humu almuflihun
31:6	wamina anasi man yashtarilahwa alhadithi liyudila an sabili alahibighayri ailmin wayatakhithaha huzuwan ula ikalahum athabun muhin
31:7	wa itha tutla alayhi ayatunawala mustakbiran kan lam yasmaha kana fi uthunayhiwaqran fabashshirhu biathabin alim
31:8	ina alathina amanuwamilu asalihati lahum janatuanaim
31:9	khalidina fiha wada alahihaqan wahuwa alazizu alhakim
31:10	khalaqa asamawatibighayri amadin tarawnaha walqa fi al ardirawasiya an tamida bikum wabaththa fiha min kulidabatin wanzalna mina asama i manfanbatna fiha min kuli zawjin karim
31:11	hatha khalqu alahi farunimatha khalaqa alathina min dunihi bali aththalimunafi dalalin mubin
31:12	walaqad atayna luqmanalhikmata ani ushkur lilahi waman yashkur fa inamayashkuru linafsihi waman kafara fa ina alaha ghaniyun hamid
31:13	wa ith qala luqmanu libnihiwahuwa yaithuhu ya bunaya latushrik bilahi ina ashshirka lathulmunathim
31:14	wawasayna al insanabiwalidayhi hamalat hu umuhu wahnan alawahnin wafisaluhu fi amayni ani ushkur li waliwalidaykailaya almasir
31:15	wa in jahadaka ala antushrika bi ma laysa laka bihi ailmun fala tutiahumawasahibhuma fi adunya marufanwatabia sabila man anaba ilaya thuma ilayamarjiaukum faunabi ukum bima kuntum tamalun
31:16	ya bunaya inaha in takumithqala habatin min khardalin fatakun fi sakhratinaw fi asamawati aw fi al ardiya ti biha alahu ina alaha latifunkhabir
31:17	ya bunaya aqimi asalatawa mur bilmarufi wanha ani almunkari wasbirala ma asabaka ina thalika minazmi al umur
31:18	wala tusair khadaka linasiwala tamshi fi al ardi marahan ina alahala yuhibu kula mukhtalin fakhur
31:19	waqsid fi mashyika waghdudmin sawtika ina ankara al aswati lasawtualhamir
31:20	alam taraw ana alaha sakhkharalakum ma fi asamawati wamafi al ardi wasbagha alaykum niamahu thahiratanwabatinatan wamina anasi man yujadilufi alahi bighayri ailmin wala hudan walakitabin munir
31:21	wa itha qila lahumu itabiau manzala alahu qalu bal natabiau ma wajadnalayhi abana awa law kana ashshaytanuyadauhum ila athabi asair
31:22	waman yuslim wajhahu ila alahiwahuwa muhsinun faqadi istamsaka bilaurwatialwuthqa wa ila alahi aqibatual umur
31:23	waman kafara fala yahzunkakufruhu ilayna marjiauhum fanunabi uhum bimamilu ina alaha alimun bithati asudur
31:24	numatiauhum qalilan thuma nadtaruhumila athabin ghalith
31:25	wala in saltahum man khalaqa asamawatiwal arda layaquluna alahu quli alhamdulilahi bal aktharuhum la yalamun
31:26	lilahi ma fi asamawatiwal ardi ina alaha huwa alghaniyu alhamid
31:27	walaw anama fi al ardi minshajaratin aqlamun walbahru yamuduhu minbadihi sabatu abhurin ma nafidat kalimatualahi ina alaha azizun hakim
31:28	ma khalqukum wala bathukumila kanafsin wahidatin ina alahasamiaun basir
31:29	alam tara ana alaha yuliju alaylafi anahari wayuliju anahara fialayli wasakhkhara ashshamsa walqamara kulunyajri ila ajalin musaman wana alaha bimatamaluna khabir
31:30	thalika bi ana alaha huwa alhaquwana ma yadauna min dunihi albatilu wana alahahuwa alaliyu alkabir
31:31	alam tara ana alfulka tajri fi albahribiniamati alahi liyuriyakum min ayatihina fi thalika layatin likuli sabarinshakur
31:32	wa itha ghashiyahum mawjun kaththulalidawu alaha mukhlisina lahu adinafalama najahum ila albari faminhum muqtasidunwama yajhadu bi ayatina ilakulu khatarin kafur
31:33	ya ayuha anasuitaqu rabakum wakhshaw yawman la yajzi walidunan waladihi wala mawludun huwa jazin an walidihishay an ina wada alahi haqun falataghuranakumu alhayatu adunya walayaghuranakum bilahi algharur
31:34	ina alaha aindahu ailmu asatiwayunazilu alghaytha wayalamu ma fi al arhamiwama tadri nafsun matha taksibu ghadan wamatadri nafsun bi ayi ardin tamutu ina alahalimun khabir
32:1	alif lam mim
32:2	tanzilu alkitabi la raybafihi min rabi alalamin
32:3	am yaquluna iftarahu bal huwa alhaqumin rabika litunthira qawman ma atahum minathirin min qablika lalahum yahtadun
32:4	alahu alathi khalaqa asamawatiwal arda wama baynahuma fi sitatiayamin thuma istawa ala alarshi malakum min dunihi min waliyin wala shafiain afalatatathakarun
32:5	yudabiru al amra mina asama ila al ardi thuma yaruju ilayhi fi yawmin kanamiqdaruhu alfa sanatin mima taudun
32:6	thalika alimu alghaybi washshahadatialazizu arahim
