32:10	waqalu a itha dalalnafi al ardi a ina lafi khalqin jadidin bal humbiliqa i rabihim kafirun
32:11	qul yatawafakum malaku almawti alathiwukila bikum thuma ila rabikum turjaun
32:12	walaw tara ithi almujrimuna nakisurusihim ainda rabihim rabana absarnawasamiana farjiana namal salihanina muqinun
32:13	walaw shi na lataynakula nafsin hudaha walakin haqalqawlu mini lamlana jahanama mina aljinati wanasiajmain
32:14	fathuqu bima nasitum liqayawmikum hatha ina nasinakum wathuquathaba alkhuldi bima kuntum tamalun
32:15	inama yu minu bi ayatinalathina itha thukiru bihakharu sujadan wasabahu bihamdi rabihim wahumla yastakbirun
32:16	tatajafa junubuhum ani almadajiaiyadauna rabahum khawfan wataman wamimarazaqnahum yunfiqun
32:17	fala talamu nafsun maukhfiya lahum min qurati ayunin jazan bima kanuyamalun
32:18	afaman kana mu minan kaman kanafasiqan la yastawun
32:19	ama alathina amanuwamilu asalihati falahum janatualma wa nuzulan bima kanu yamalun
32:20	wama alathina fasaqufama wahumu anaru kulama araduan yakhruju minha uaidu fiha waqila lahum thuquathaba anari alathi kuntum bihitukaththibun
32:21	walanuthiqanahum mina alathabial adna duna alathabi al akbari lalahumyarjiaun
32:22	waman athlamu miman thukirabi ayati rabihi thuma arada anhaina mina almujrimina muntaqimun
32:23	walaqad atayna musalkitaba fala takun fi miryatin min liqa ihiwajalnahu hudan libani isra il
32:24	wajalna minhum a imatan yahdunabi amrina lama sabaru wakanu bi ayatinayuqinun
32:25	ina rabaka huwa yafsilu baynahumyawma alqiyamati fima kanu fihiyakhtalifun
32:26	awa lam yahdi lahum kam ahlakna minqablihim mina alquruni yamshuna fi masakinihim ina fithalika layatin afala yasmaun
32:27	awa lam yaraw ana nasuqu almaila al ardi aljuruzi fanukhriju bihi zaranta kulu minhu anamuhum wanfusuhum afala yubsirun
32:28	wayaquluna mata hatha alfathuin kuntum sadiqin
32:29	qul yawma alfathi la yanfaualathina kafaru imanuhum wala hum yuntharun
32:30	farid anhum wantathirinahum muntathirun
33:1	ya ayuha anabiyuitaqi alaha wala tutiai alkafirinawalmunafiqina ina alaha kanaliman hakima
33:2	watabia ma yuhailayka min rabika ina alaha kana bimatamaluna khabira
33:3	watawakal ala alahi wakafabilahi wakila
33:4	ma jala alahu lirajulin minqalbayni fi jawfihi wama jala azwajakumu ala ituthahiruna minhuna umahatikum wamajala adaiyakum abnakum thalikumqawlukum bi afwahikum walahu yaqulu alhaqawahuwa yahdi asabil
33:5	udauhum li aba ihim huwa aqsatuainda alahi fa in lam talamu abahumfa ikhwanukum fi adini wamawalikumwalaysa alaykum junahun fima akhta tumbihi walakin ma tamadat qulubukum wakanalahu ghafuran rahima
33:6	anabiyu awla bilmu mininamin anfusihim wazwajuhu umahatuhum waulu al arhamibaduhum awla bibadin fi kitabialahi mina almu minina walmuhajirina ilan tafalu ila awliya ikum marufan kanathalika fi alkitabi mastura
33:7	wa ith akhathna mina anabiyinamithaqahum waminka wamin nuhin wa ibrahimawamusa waisa ibni maryama wakhathnaminhum mithaqan ghalitha
33:8	liyas ala asadiqina an sidqihimwada lilkafirina athaban alima
33:9	ya ayuha alathina amanuthkuru niamata alahi alaykum ith jatkumjunudun farsalna alayhim rihan wajunudan lamtarawha wakana alahu bimatamaluna basira
33:10	ith jaukum min fawqikum waminasfala minkum wa ith zaghati al absaruwabalaghati alqulubu alhanajira watathununabilahi aththununa
33:11	hunalika ibtuliya almu minunawazulzilu zilzalan shadida
33:12	wa ith yaqulu almunafiqunawalathina fi qulubihim maradun mawadana alahu warasuluhu ila ghurura
33:13	wa ith qalat ta ifatunminhum ya ahla yathriba la muqama lakum farjiauwayasta thinu fariqun minhumu anabiyayaquluna ina buyutana awratun wama hiyabiawratin in yuriduna ila firara
33:14	walaw dukhilat alayhim min aqtarihathuma su ilu alfitnata latawha wamatalabathu biha ila yasira
33:15	walaqad kanu ahadu alahamin qablu la yuwaluna al adbara wakanahdu alahi mas ula
33:16	qul lan yanfakumu alfiraru infarartum mina almawti awi alqatli wa ithan latumatauna ila qalila
33:17	qul man tha alathi yasimukumina alahi in arada bikum su an aw aradabikum rahmatan wala yajiduna lahum min duni alahiwaliyan wala nasira
33:18	qad yalamu alahu almuawiqinaminkum walqa ilina li ikhwanihim halumailayna wala ya tuna alba sa ila qalila
33:19	ashihatan alaykum fa itha jalkhawfu raytahum yanthuruna ilayka taduruayunuhum kalathi yughsha alayhi minalmawti fa itha thahaba alkhawfu salaqukumbi alsinatin hidadin ashihatan alalkhayri ula ika lam yu minu fahbata alahuamalahum wakana thalika ala alahiyasira
33:20	yahsabuna al ahzabalam yathabu wa in ya ti al ahzabu yawadulaw anahum baduna fi al arabi yas aluna ananba ikum walaw kanu fikum ma qataluila qalila
33:21	laqad kana lakum fi rasuli alahiuswatun hasanatun liman kana yarju alahawalyawma al akhira wathakara alahakathira
33:22	walama ra almu minuna al ahzabaqalu hatha ma wadana alahuwarasuluhu wasadaqa alahu warasuluhu wamazadahum ila imanan wataslima
33:23	mina almu minina rijalun sadaquma ahadu alaha alayhi faminhum man qadanahbahu waminhum man yantathiru wamabadalu tabdila
33:24	liyajziya alahu asadiqinabisidqihim wayuaththiba almunafiqina insha aw yatuba alayhim ina alaha kanaghafuran rahima
33:25	warada alahu alathinakafaru bighaythihim lam yanalu khayranwakafa alahu almu minina alqitala wakanalahu qawiyan aziza
33:26	wanzala alathina thaharuhumin ahli alkitabi min sayasihim waqathafafi qulubihimu aruaba fariqan taqtuluna wata sirunafariqa
33:27	wawrathakum ardahum wadiyarahumwamwalahum wardan lam tatauha wakanalahu ala kuli shay in qadira
33:28	ya ayuha anabiyuqul li azwajika in kuntuna turidna alhayatadunya wazinataha fatalaynaumatiakuna wausarihkuna sarahan jamila
33:29	wa in kuntuna turidna alahawarasulahu wadara al akhirata fa inalaha ada lilmuhsinati minkuna ajranathima
33:30	ya nisa anabiyi manya ti minkuna bifahishatin mubayinatin yudaflaha alathabu diafayni wakana thalikala alahi yasira
33:31	waman yaqnut minkuna lilahiwarasulihi watamal salihan nu tiha ajrahamaratayni watadna laha rizqan karima
33:32	ya nisa anabiyilastuna kahadin mina anisa i initaqaytuna fala takhdana bilqawli fayatmalathi fi qalbihi maradun waqulna qawlanmarufa
33:33	waqarna fi buyutikuna walatabarajna tabaruja aljahiliyati al ula waqimnasalata watina azakatawatiana alaha warasulahu inama yuridualahu liyuthiba ankumu arijsa ahlalbayti wayutahirakum tathira
33:34	wathkurna ma yutlafi buyutikuna min ayati alahi walhikmatina alaha kana latifan khabira
33:35	ina almuslimina walmuslimatiwalmu minina walmu minati walqanitinawalqanitati wasadiqina wasadiqatiwasabirina wasabiratiwalkhashiaina walkhashiatiwalmutasadiqina walmutasadiqatiwasa imina wasa imatiwalhafithina furujahum walhafithatiwaththakirina alaha kathiran waththakiratiada alahu lahum maghfiratan wajran athima
33:36	wama kana limu minin walamu minatin itha qada alahu warasuluhuamran an yakuna lahumu alkhiyaratu min amrihim waman yasialaha warasulahu faqad dala dalalanmubina
33:37	wa ith taqulu lilathianama alahu alayhi wanamta alayhi amsik alaykazawjaka wataqi alaha watukhfi fi nafsika malahu mubdihi watakhsha anasa walahuahaqu an takhshahu falama qadazaydun minha wataran zawajnakahalikay la yakuna ala almu minina harajunfi azwaji adaiya ihim itha qadawminhuna wataran wakana amru alahi mafaula
33:38	ma kana ala anabiyimin harajin fima farada alahu lahusunata alahi fi alathina khalaw min qablu wakanamru alahi qadaran maqdura
33:39	alathina yubalighuna risalatialahi wayakhshawnahu wala yakhshawna ahadanila alaha wakafa bilahi hasiba
33:40	ma kana muhamadun abahadin min rijalikum walakin rasula alahiwakhatama anabiyina wakana alahubikuli shay in alima
33:41	ya ayuha alathina amanuthkuru alaha thikran kathira
33:42	wasabihuhu bukratan wasila
33:43	huwa alathi yusalialaykum wamala ikatuhu liyukhrijakum mina aththulumatila anuri wakana bilmu minina rahima
33:44	tahiyatuhum yawma yalqawnahu salamunwada lahum ajran karima
33:45	ya ayuha anabiyuina arsalnaka shahidan wamubashshiran wanathira
33:46	wadaiyan ila alahibi ithnihi wasirajan munira
33:47	wabashshiri almu minina bi ana lahum minalahi fadlan kabira
33:48	wala tutiai alkafirinawalmunafiqina wada athahum watawakalala alahi wakafa bilahiwakila
33:49	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha nakahtumu almu minati thuma talaqtumuhunamin qabli an tamasuhuna fama lakum alayhina minaidatin tatadunaha famatiauhuna wasarihuhunasarahan jamila
33:50	ya ayuha anabiyuina ahlalna laka azwajaka alatiatayta ujurahuna wama malakat yaminuka mimafa alahu alayka wabanati amika wabanatiamatika wabanati khalika wabanatikhalatika alati hajarna maka wamratanmu minatan in wahabat nafsaha linabiyi in aradanabiyu an yastankihaha khalisatanlaka min duni almu minina qad alimna ma faradnalayhim fi azwajihim wama malakat aymanuhumlikayla yakuna alayka harajun wakana alahughafuran rahima
33:51	turji man tashau minhuna watu wilayka man tashau wamani ibtaghayta miman azalta falajunaha alayka thalika adna an taqarayunuhuna wala yahzana wayardayna bimataytahuna kuluhuna walahu yalamu mafi qulubikum wakana alahu aliman halima
33:52	la yahilu laka anisaumin badu wala an tabadala bihina min azwajinwalaw ajabaka husnuhuna ila ma malakatyaminuka wakana alahu ala kuli shay inraqiba
33:53	ya ayuha alathina amanula tadkhulu buyuta anabiyi ila an yu thanalakum ila tamin ghayra nathirinainahu walakin itha duaitum fadkhulufa itha taimtum fantashiru walamusta nisina lihadithin ina thalikum kanayu thi anabiya fayastahyi minkum walahula yastahyi mina alhaqi wa ithasaltumuhuna matan fas aluhuna min wara ihijabin thalikum atharu liqulubikumwaqulubihina wama kana lakum an tu thurasula alahi wala an tankihu azwajahumin badihi abadan ina thalikum kana ainda alahiathima
33:54	in tubdu shay an aw tukhfuhu fa ina alahakana bikuli shay in alima
33:55	la junaha alayhina fi aba ihinawala abna ihina wala ikhwanihinawala abna i ikhwanihina wala abna iakhawatihina wala nisa ihina wala mamalakat aymanuhuna wataqina alaha inalaha kana ala kuli shay in shahida
33:56	ina alaha wamala ikatahu yusalunala anabiyi ya ayuha alathinamanu salu alayhi wasalimu taslima
33:57	ina alathina yu thuna alahawarasulahu lanahumu alahu fi adunyawal akhirati wada lahum athabanmuhina
33:58	walathina yu thunalmu minina walmu minati bighayri maiktasabu faqadi ihtamalu buhtanan wa ithmanmubina
33:59	ya ayuha anabiyuqul li azwajika wabanatika wanisa ialmu minina yudnina alayhina min jalabibihina thalikadna an yuarafna fala yu thayna wakanalahu ghafuran rahima
33:60	la in lam yantahi almunafiquna walathinafi qulubihim maradun walmurjifuna fialmadinati lanughriyanaka bihim thuma la yujawirunakafiha ila qalila
33:61	malaunina ayna ma thuqifu ukhithuwaqutilu taqtila
33:62	sunata alahi fi alathinakhalaw min qablu walan tajida lisunati alahi tabdila
33:63	yas aluka anasu ani asatiqul inama ailmuha ainda alahi wamayudrika lala asata takunu qariba
33:64	ina alaha lana alkafirinawada lahum saira
33:65	khalidina fiha abadan layajiduna waliyan wala nasira
33:66	yawma tuqalabu wujuhuhum fi anariyaquluna ya laytana atana alahawatana arasula
33:67	waqalu rabana ina atanasadatana wakubarana fadalunasabila
33:68	rabana atihim diafaynimina alathabi walanhum lanan kabira
33:69	ya ayuha alathina amanula takunu kalathina athaw musafabarahu alahu mima qalu wakanainda alahi wajiha
33:70	ya ayuha alathina amanuitaqu alaha waqulu qawlan sadida
33:71	yuslih lakum amalakumwayaghfir lakum thunubakum waman yutiai alahawarasulahu faqad faza fawzan athima
33:72	ina aradna al amanatala asamawati wal ardiwaljibali fabayna an yahmilnahawashfaqna minha wahamalaha al insanuinahu kana thaluman jahula
33:73	liyuaththiba alahu almunafiqinawalmunafiqati walmushrikina walmushrikatiwayatuba alahu ala almu minina walmu minatiwakana alahu ghafuran rahima
34:1	alhamdu lilahi alathilahu ma fi asamawati wamafi al ardi walahu alhamdu fi al akhiratiwahuwa alhakimu alkhabir
34:2	yalamu ma yaliju fi al ardiwama yakhruju minha wama yanzilu mina asama iwama yaruju fiha wahuwa arahimualghafur
34:3	waqala alathina kafaru lata tina asatu qul bala warabilata tiyanakum alimi alghaybi la yazubu anhumithqalu tharatin fi asamawatiwala fi al ardi wala asgharu min thalikawala akbaru ila fi kitabin mubin
34:4	liyajziya alathina amanuwamilu asalihati ula ika lahumaghfiratun warizqun karim
34:5	walathina saw fi ayatinamuajizina ula ika lahum athabun minrijzin alim
34:6	wayara alathina utualailma alathi unzila ilayka min rabika huwa alhaqawayahdi ila sirati alazizi alhamid
34:7	waqala alathina kafaru halnadulukum ala rajulin yunabi ukum ithamuziqtum kula mumazaqin inakum lafi khalqin jadid
34:8	aftara ala alahi kathibanam bihi jinatun bali alathina la yu minuna bil akhiratifi alathabi wadalali albaid
34:9	afalam yaraw ila ma baynaydihim wama khalfahum mina asama i wal ardin nasha nakhsif bihimu al arda aw nusqitalayhim kisafan mina asama i ina fi thalikalayatan likuli abdin munib
34:10	walaqad atayna dawudamina fadlan ya jibalu awibimahu watayra walana lahu alhadid
34:11	ani iamal sabighatin waqadirfi asardi wamalu salihan inibima tamaluna basir
34:12	walisulaymana arihaghuduwuha shahrun warawahuha shahrunwasalna lahu ayna alqitri wamina aljini manyamalu bayna yadayhi bi ithni rabihi waman yazighminhum an amrina nuthiqhu min athabi asair
34:13	yamaluna lahu ma yashau minmahariba watamathila wajifanin kaljawabiwaqudurin rasiyatin iamalu ala dawudashukran waqalilun min aibadiya ashshakur
34:14	falama qadaynalayhi almawta ma dalahum ala mawtihi iladabatu al ardi ta kulu minsatahu falamakhara tabayanati aljinu an law kanu yalamunalghayba ma labithu fi alathabi almuhin
34:15	laqad kana lisaba in fi maskanihim ayatunjanatani an yaminin washimalin kulu min rizqirabikum washkuru lahu baldatun tayibatunwarabun ghafur
34:16	faradu farsalna alayhimsayla alarimi wabadalnahum bijanatayhim janatayni thawatayukulin khamtin wathlin washay in min sidrin qalil
34:17	thalika jazaynahum bimakafaru wahal nujazi ila alkafur
34:18	wajalna baynahum wabayna alquralati barakna fiha quran thahiratanwaqadarna fiha asayra siru fihalayaliya wayaman aminin
34:19	faqalu rabana baidbayna asfarina wathalamu anfusahumfajalnahum ahaditha wamazaqnahum kulamumazaqin ina fi thalika layatin likulisabarin shakur
34:20	walaqad sadaqa alayhim iblisu thanahufatabauhu ila fariqan mina almu minin
34:21	wama kana lahu alayhim minsultanin ila linalama man yu minu bil akhiratimiman huwa minha fi shakin warabuka ala kulishay in hafith
34:22	quli udau alathina zamtum minduni alahi la yamlikuna mithqala tharatinfi asamawati wala fi al ardiwama lahum fihima min shirkin wama lahuminhum min thahir
34:23	wala tanfau ashshafatuaindahu ila liman athina lahu hataitha fuzia an qulubihim qalu matha qalarabukum qalu alhaqa wahuwa alaliyu alkabir
34:24	qul man yarzuqukum mina asamawatiwal ardi quli alahu wa ina aw iyakumlala hudan aw fi dalalin mubin
34:25	qul la tus aluna amajramna wala nus alu ama tamalun
34:26	qul yajmau baynana rabunathuma yaftahu baynana bilhaqiwahuwa alfatahu alalim
34:27	qul aruniya alathina alhaqtumbihi shuraka kala bal huwa alahualazizu alhakim
34:28	wama arsalnaka ila kafatanlinasi bashiran wanathiran walakinakthara anasi la yalamun
34:29	wayaquluna mata hathalwadu in kuntum sadiqin
34:30	qul lakum miadu yawmin latasta khiruna anhu satan wala tastaqdimun
34:31	waqala alathina kafaru lanu mina bihatha alqur ani wala bilathibayna yadayhi walaw tara ithi aththalimunamawqufuna ainda rabihim yarjiau baduhum ilabadin alqawla yaqulu alathina istudaifulilathina istakbaru lawla antum lakunamu minin
34:32	qala alathina istakbarulilathina istudaifu anahnu sadadnakumani alhuda bada ith jakum bal kuntumujrimin
34:33	waqala alathina istudaifulilathina istakbaru bal makru alayli wanaharith ta murunana an nakfura bilahiwanajala lahu andadan wasaru anadamatalama rawu alathaba wajalna al aghlalafi anaqi alathina kafaru hal yujzawna ilama kanu yamalun
34:34	wama arsalna fi qaryatin minathirin ila qala mutrafuha inabima ursiltum bihi kafirun
34:35	waqalu nahnu aktharu amwalanwawladan wama nahnu bimuaththabin
34:36	qul ina rabi yabsutu arizqaliman yashau wayaqdiru walakina akthara anasila yalamun
34:37	wama amwalukum wala awladukumbilati tuqaribukum aindana zulfa ilaman amana wamila salihan faula ikalahum jazau adiafi bima amiluwahum fi alghurufati aminun
34:38	walathina yasawna fi ayatinamuajizina ula ika fi alathabi muhdarun
34:39	qul ina rabi yabsutu arizqaliman yashau min aibadihi wayaqdiru lahu wamanfaqtum min shay in fahuwa yukhlifuhu wahuwa khayru araziqin
34:40	wayawma yahshuruhum jamian thumayaqulu lilmala ikati ahaula i iyakumkanu yabudun
34:41	qalu subhanaka anta waliyunamin dunihim bal kanu yabuduna aljina aktharuhumbihim mu minun
34:42	falyawma la yamliku badukumlibadin nafan wala daran wanaqululilathina thalamu thuqu athabanari alati kuntum biha tukaththibun
34:43	wa itha tutla alayhim ayatunabayinatin qalu ma hatha ilarajulun yuridu an yasudakum ama kanayabudu abaukum waqalu ma hathaila ifkun muftaran waqala alathinakafaru lilhaqi lama jahum in hathaila sihrun mubin
34:44	wama ataynahum minkutubin yadrusunaha wama arsalna ilayhimqablaka min nathir
34:45	wakaththaba alathina minqablihim wama balaghu miashara ma ataynahumfakaththabu rusuli fakayfa kana nakir
34:46	qul inama aithukumbiwahidatin an taqumu lilahi mathna wafuradathuma tatafakaru ma bisahibikum min jinatin inhuwa ila nathirun lakum bayna yaday athabinshadid
34:47	qul ma saltukum min ajrin fahuwalakum in ajriya ila ala alahi wahuwala kuli shay in shahid
34:48	qul ina rabi yaqthifu bilhaqialamu alghuyub
34:49	qul ja alhaqu wamayubdi u albatilu wama yuaid
34:50	qul in dalaltu fa inama adiluala nafsi wa ini ihtadaytu fabima yuhilaya rabi inahu samiaun qarib
34:51	walaw tara ith faziau falafawta waukhithu min makanin qarib
34:52	waqalu amana bihiwana lahumu atanawushu min makaninbaid
34:53	waqad kafaru bihi min qablu wayaqthifunabilghaybi min makanin baid
34:54	wahila baynahum wabayna mayashtahuna kama fuaila bi ashyaihim min qabluinahum kanu fi shakin murib
35:1	alhamdu lilahi fatiri asamawatiwal ardi jaili almala ikatirusulan uli ajnihatin mathna wathulathawaruba yazidu fi alkhalqi ma yashauina alaha ala kuli shay in qadir
35:2	ma yaftahi alahu linasimin rahmatin fala mumsika laha wamayumsik fala mursila lahu min badihi wahuwa alazizu alhakim
35:3	ya ayuha anasuthkuru niamata alahi alaykum hal min khaliqinghayru alahi yarzuqukum mina asama i wal ardila ilaha ila huwa fana tu fakun
35:4	wa in yukaththibuka faqad kuththibatrusulun min qablika wa ila alahi turjau al umur
35:5	ya ayuha anasuina wada alahi haqun falataghuranakumu alhayatu adunya walayaghuranakum bilahi algharur
35:6	ina ashshaytana lakumaduwun fatakhithuhu aduwan inamayadau hizbahu liyakunu min as habi asair
35:7	alathina kafaru lahum athabunshadidun walathina amanu wamilu asalihatilahum maghfiratun wajrun kabir
35:8	afaman zuyina lahu su u amalihi farahuhasanan fa ina alaha yudilu man yashauwayahdi man yashau fala tathab nafsukalayhim hasaratin ina alaha alimunbima yasnaun
35:9	walahu alathi arsalariyaha fatuthiru sahaban fasuqnahuila baladin mayitin fahyayna bihi al ardabada mawtiha kathalika anushur
35:10	man kana yuridu alaizata falilahialaizatu jamian ilayhi yasadu alkalimu atayibuwalamalu asalihu yarfauhu walathinayamkuruna asayi ati lahum athabunshadidun wamakru ula ika huwa yabur
35:11	walahu khalaqakum min turabinthuma min nutfatin thuma jalakum azwajan wamatahmilu min untha wala tadau ilabiailmihi wama yuamaru min muamarin walayunqasu min aumurihi ila fi kitabin ina thalikala alahi yasir
35:12	wama yastawi albahranihatha athbun furatun sa ighun sharabuhuwahatha milhun ujajun wamin kulinta kuluna lahman tariyan watastakhrijuna hilyatantalbasunaha watara alfulka fihi mawakhiralitabtaghu min fadlihi walalakum tashkurun
35:13	yuliju alayla fi anahariwayuliju anahara fi alayli wasakhkhara ashshamsawalqamara kulun yajri li ajalin musaman thalikumualahu rabukum lahu almulku walathinatadauna min dunihi ma yamlikuna min qitmir
35:14	in tadauhum la yasmau duakumwalaw samiau ma istajabu lakum wayawma alqiyamatiyakfuruna bishirkikum wala yunabi uka mithlu khabir
35:15	ya ayuha anasuantumu alfuqarau ila alahi walahuhuwa alghaniyu alhamid
35:16	in yasha yuthibkum waya tibikhalqin jadid
35:17	wama thalika ala alahibiaziz
35:18	wala taziru waziratun wizraukhra wa in tadau muthqalatun ila himlihala yuhmal minhu shay un walaw kana thaqurba inama tunthiru alathinayakhshawna rabahum bilghaybi waqamu asalatawaman tazaka fa inama yatazaka linafsihiwa ila alahi almasir
35:19	wama yastawi al ama walbasir
35:20	wala aththulumatuwala anur
35:21	wala aththiluwala alharur
35:22	wama yastawi al ahyauwala al amwatu ina alaha yusmiau man yashauwama anta bimusmiain man fi alqubur
35:23	in anta ila nathir
35:24	ina arsalnaka bilhaqibashiran wanathiran wa in min umatin ila khalafiha nathir
35:25	wa in yukaththibuka faqad kaththabalathina min qablihim jat hum rusuluhum bilbayinatiwabizuburi wabilkitabi almunir
35:26	thuma akhathtu alathinakafaru fakayfa kana nakir
35:27	alam tara ana alaha anzala mina asama iman fakhrajna bihi thamaratin mukhtalifanalwanuha wamina aljibali judadun bidunwahumrun mukhtalifun alwanuha wagharabibusud
35:28	wamina anasi wadawabiwal anami mukhtalifun alwanuhu kathalikainama yakhsha alaha min aibadihialaulamau ina alaha azizun ghafur
35:29	ina alathina yatluna kitabalahi waqamu asalatawanfaqu mima razaqnahum siran walaniyatanyarjuna tijaratan lan tabur
35:30	liyuwafiyahum ujurahum wayazidahum min fadlihinahu ghafurun shakur
35:31	walathi awhaynailayka mina alkitabi huwa alhaqu musadiqanlima bayna yadayhi ina alaha biaibadihilakhabirun basir
35:32	thuma awrathna alkitaba alathinaistafayna min aibadina faminhum thalimunlinafsihi waminhum muqtasidun waminhum sabiqun bilkhayratibi ithni alahi thalika huwa alfadlualkabir
35:33	janatu adnin yadkhulunahayuhalawna fiha min asawira min thahabinwalu lu an walibasuhum fiha harir
35:34	waqalu alhamdu lilahialathi athaba ana alhazana inarabana laghafurun shakur
35:35	alathi ahalana daralmuqamati min fadlihi la yamasunafiha nasabun wala yamasuna fihalughub
35:36	walathina kafaru lahum narujahanama la yuqda alayhim fayamutu walayukhafafu anhum min athabiha kathalikanajzi kula kafur
35:37	wahum yastarikhuna fiharabana akhrijna namal salihanghayra alathi kuna namalu awa lam nuamirkuma yatathakaru fihi man tathakara wajakumuanathiru fathuqu fama liththaliminamin nasir
35:38	ina alaha alimu ghaybi asamawatiwal ardi inahu alimun bithati asudur
35:39	huwa alathi jalakum khala ifafi al ardi faman kafara falayhi kufruhu walayazidu alkafirina kufruhum ainda rabihim ilamaqtan wala yazidu alkafirina kufruhum ilakhasara
35:40	qul araytum shurakakumu alathinatadauna min duni alahi aruni matha khalaqumina al ardi am lahum shirkun fi asamawatiam ataynahum kitaban fahum alabayinatin minhu bal in yaidu aththalimunabaduhum badan ila ghurura
35:41	ina alaha yumsiku asamawatiwal arda an tazula wala in zalatain amsakahuma min ahadin min badihi inahu kanahaliman ghafura
35:42	waqsamu bilahi jahda aymanihimla in jahum nathirun layakununa ahda minihda al umami falama jahum nathirunma zadahum ila nufura
35:43	istikbaran fi al ardi wamakrasayi i wala yahiqu almakru asayi uila bi ahlihi fahal yanthuruna ilasunata al awalina falan tajida lisunati alahitabdilan walan tajida lisunati alahi tahwila
35:44	awa lam yasiru fi al ardi fayanthurukayfa kana aqibatu alathina min qablihimwakanu ashada minhum quwatan wama kanalahu liyuajizahu min shay in fi asamawatiwala fi al ardi inahu kana alimanqadira
35:45	walaw yu akhithu alahuanasa bima kasabu ma taraka alathahriha min dabatin walakinyu akhkhiruhum ila ajalin musaman fa itha jajaluhum fa ina alaha kana biaibadihi basira

38:1	sad walqur ani thiaththikr

39:1	tanzilu alkitabi mina alahialazizi alhakim
39:2	ina anzalna ilayka alkitababilhaqi fabudi alaha mukhlisanlahu adin
39:3	ala lilahi adinualkhalisu walathina itakhathumin dunihi awliya ma nabuduhum ilaliyuqaribuna ila alahi zulfa inalaha yahkumu baynahum fi ma hum fihiyakhtalifuna ina alaha la yahdi man huwa kathibunkafar
39:4	law arada alahu an yatakhithawaladan lastafa mima yakhluqu mayashau subhanahu huwa alahu alwahidualqahar
39:5	khalaqa asamawati wal ardabilhaqi yukawiru alayla ala anahariwayukawiru anahara ala alayliwasakhkhara ashshamsa walqamara kulun yajrili ajalin musaman ala huwa alazizu alghafar

39:9	aman huwa qanitun analayli sajidan waqa iman yahtharu al akhiratawayarju rahmata rabihi qul hal yastawi alathinayalamuna walathina la yalamunainama yatathakaru ulu al albab

39:18	alathina yastamiauna alqawlafayatabiauna ahsanahu ula ika alathinahadahumu alahu waula ika hum ulu al albab

39:20	lakini alathina itaqawrabahum lahum ghurafun min fawqiha ghurafun mabniyatuntajri min tahtiha al anharu wada alahila yukhlifu alahu almiad
39:21	alam tara ana alaha anzala mina asama iman fasalakahu yanabia fi al ardithuma yukhriju bihi zaran mukhtalifan alwanuhu thumayahiju fatarahu musfaran thuma yajaluhu hutamanina fi thalika lathikra li uli al albab
39:22	afaman sharaha alahu sadrahulil islami fahuwa ala nurin min rabihi fawaylunlilqasiyati qulubuhum min thikri alahi ula ikafi dalalin mubin
39:23	alahu nazala ahsana alhadithikitaban mutashabihan mathaniya taqshairuminhu juludu alathina yakhshawna rabahum thumatalinu juluduhum waqulubuhum ila thikri alahithalika huda alahi yahdi bihi man yashauwaman yudlili alahu fama lahu min had
39:24	afaman yataqi biwajhihi su a alathabiyawma alqiyamati waqila liththaliminathuqu ma kuntum taksibun
39:25	kaththaba alathina minqablihim fatahumu alathabu min haythu layashaurun
39:26	fathaqahumu alahu alkhizyafi alhayati adunya walathabual akhirati akbaru law kanu yalamun

39:30	inaka mayitun wa inahum mayitun
39:31	thuma inakum yawma alqiyamatiainda rabikum takhtasimun

39:33	walathi ja bisidqiwasadaqa bihi ula ika humu almutaqun
39:34	lahum ma yashauna aindarabihim thalika jazau almuhsinin
39:35	liyukafira alahu anhum aswa alathiamilu wayajziyahum ajrahum bi ahsani alathi kanuyamalun
39:36	alaysa alahu bikafin abdahuwayukhawifunaka bilathina min dunihi waman yudlilialahu fama lahu min had
39:37	waman yahdi alahu fama lahumin mudilin alaysa alahu biazizin thintiqam
39:38	wala in saltahum man khalaqa asamawatiwal arda layaquluna alahu qul afaraytuma tadauna min duni alahi in aradaniyalahu bidurin hal huna kashifatu durihiaw aradani birahmatin hal huna mumsikaturahmatihi qul hasbiya alahu alayhiyatawakalu almutawakilun
39:39	qul ya qawmi iamalu alamakanatikum ini amilun fasawfa talamun
39:40	man ya tihi athabun yukhzihi wayahilualayhi athabun muqim
39:41	ina anzalna alayka alkitabalinasi bilhaqi famani ihtadafalinafsihi waman dala fa inama yadilualayha wama anta alayhim biwakil
39:42	alahu yatawafa al anfusa hinamawtiha walati lam tamut fi manamihafayumsiku alati qada alayha almawta wayursilual ukhra ila ajalin musaman ina fi thalikalayatin liqawmin yatafakarun
39:43	ami itakhathu min duni alahishufa qul awa law kanu la yamlikunashay an wala yaqilun
39:44	qul lilahi ashshafatujamian lahu mulku asamawati wal ardithuma ilayhi turjaun
39:45	wa itha thukira alahuwahdahu ishmazat qulubu alathina layu minuna bil akhirati wa itha thukiralathina min dunihi itha hum yastabshirun
39:46	quli alahuma fatira asamawatiwal ardi alima alghaybi washshahadatianta tahkumu bayna aibadika fi ma kanufihi yakhtalifun
39:47	walaw ana lilathina thalamuma fi al ardi jamian wamithlahu mahu laftadawbihi min su i alathabi yawma alqiyamati wabadalahum mina alahi ma lam yakunu yahtasibun
39:48	wabada lahum sayi atu makasabu wahaqa bihim ma kanu bihiyastahzi un
39:49	fa itha masa al insana durundana thuma itha khawalnahuniamatan mina qala inama utituhu alailmin bal hiya fitnatun walakina aktharahum layalamun
39:50	qad qalaha alathinamin qablihim fama aghna anhum ma kanuyaksibun
39:51	fasabahum sayi atu makasabu walathina thalamu min haula isayusibuhum sayi atu ma kasabu wamahum bimuajizin
39:52	awa lam yalamu ana alaha yabsutuarizqa liman yashau wayaqdiru ina fi thalikalayatin liqawmin yu minun
39:53	qul ya aibadiya alathinasrafu ala anfusihim la taqnatu min rahmatialahi ina alaha yaghfiru aththunubajamian inahu huwa alghafuru arahim
39:54	wanibu ila rabikum waslimu lahumin qabli an ya tiyakumu alathabu thuma la tunsarun
39:55	watabiau ahsana maunzila ilaykum min rabikum min qabli an ya tiyakumu alathabubaghtatan wantum la tashaurun
39:56	an taqula nafsun ya hasratala ma faratu fi janbi alahiwa in kuntu lamina asakhirin
39:57	aw taqula law ana alaha hadanilakuntu mina almutaqin
39:58	aw taqula hina tara alathabalaw ana li karatan fakuna mina almuhsinin
39:59	bala qad jatka ayatifakaththabta biha wastakbarta wakunta minalkafirin
39:60	wayawma alqiyamati tara alathinakathabu ala alahi wujuhuhum muswadatunalaysa fi jahanama mathwan lilmutakabirin
39:61	wayunaji alahu alathinaitaqaw bimafazatihim la yamasuhumu asu uwala hum yahzanun
39:62	alahu khaliqu kuli shay inwahuwa ala kuli shay in wakil
39:63	lahu maqalidu asamawatiwal ardi walathina kafaru bi ayatialahi ula ika humu alkhasirun
39:64	qul afaghayra alahi ta muruniabudu ayuha aljahilun
39:65	walaqad uhiya ilayka wa ilalathina min qablika la in ashrakta layahbatanamaluka walatakunana mina alkhasirin
39:66	bali alaha fabud wakunmina ashshakirin
39:67	wama qadaru alaha haqaqadrihi wal ardu jamian qabdatuhu yawmalqiyamati wasamawatu matwiyatunbiyaminihi subhanahu watala amayushrikun
39:68	wanufikha fi asuri fasaiqaman fi asamawati waman fi al ardila man sha alahu thuma nufikha fihiukhra fa itha hum qiyamun yanthurun
39:69	washraqati al ardu binuri rabihawawudia alkitabu waji a binabiyinawashshuhada i waqudiya baynahum bilhaqiwahum la yuthlamun
39:70	wawufiyat kulu nafsin ma amilatwahuwa alamu bima yafalun
39:71	wasiqa alathina kafaru ilajahanama zumaran hata itha jauhafutihat abwabuha waqala lahumkhazanatuha alam ya tikum rusulun minkum yatlunalaykum ayati rabikum wayunthirunakumliqa yawmikum hatha qalu bala walakinhaqat kalimatu alathabi ala alkafirin
39:72	qila udkhulu abwaba jahanama khalidinafiha fabi sa mathwa almutakabirin
39:73	wasiqa alathina itaqaw rabahumila aljanati zumaran hata itha jauhawafutihat abwabuha waqala lahumkhazanatuha salamun alaykum tibtum fadkhuluhakhalidin
39:74	waqalu alhamdu lilahialathi sadaqana wadahu wawrathanal arda natabawau mina aljanati haythu nashaufaniama ajru alamilin

40:1	ha mim
40:2	tanzilu alkitabi mina alahialazizi alalim
40:3	ghafiri aththanbi waqabiliatawbi shadidi alaiqabi thi atawlila ilaha ila huwa ilayhi almasir
40:4	ma yujadilu fi ayatialahi ila alathina kafaru falayaghrurka taqalubuhum fi albilad
40:5	kaththabat qablahum qawmu nuhinwal ahzabu min badihim wahamat kulumatin birasulihim liya khuthuhu wajadalu bilbatililiyudhidu bihi alhaqa fakhathtuhumfakayfa kana aiqab
40:6	wakathalika haqat kalimaturabika ala alathina kafaru anahum as habuanar
40:7	alathina yahmiluna alarshawaman hawlahu yusabihuna bihamdi rabihimwayu minuna bihi wayastaghfiruna lilathina amanurabana wasiata kula shay in rahmatan wailmanfaghfir lilathina tabu watabausabilaka waqihim athaba aljahim
40:8	rabana wadkhilhum janatiadnin alati wadtahum waman salaha min aba ihimwazwajihim wathuriyatihim inaka antalazizu alhakim
40:9	waqihimu asayi ati wamantaqi asayi ati yawma ithin faqad rahimtahuwathalika huwa alfawzu alathim
40:10	ina alathina kafaru yunadawnalamaqtu alahi akbaru min maqtikum anfusakum ithtudawna ila al imani fatakfurun
40:11	qalu rabana amatanaithnatayni wahyaytana ithnatayni fatarafnabithunubina fahal ila khurujin min sabil
40:12	thalikum bi anahu ithaduaiya alahu wahdahu kafartum wa in yushrak bihitu minu falhukmu lilahi alaliyialkabir
40:13	huwa alathi yurikum ayatihiwayunazilu lakum mina asama i rizqan wamayatathakaru ila man yunib
40:14	fadau alaha mukhlisinalahu adina walaw kariha alkafirun
40:15	rafiau adarajati thualarshi yulqi aruha min amrihi alaman yashau min aibadihi liyunthira yawma atalaq
40:16	yawma hum barizuna la yakhfala alahi minhum shay un limani almulku alyawmalilahi alwahidi alqahar
40:17	alyawma tujza kulu nafsin bimakasabat la thulma alyawma ina alahasariau alhisab
40:18	wanthirhum yawma al azifati ithialqulubu lada alhanajiri kathiminama liththalimina min hamiminwala shafiain yuta
40:19	yalamu kha inata al ayuni wamatukhfi asudur
40:20	walahu yaqdi bilhaqiwalathina yadauna min dunihi la yaqdunabishay in ina alaha huwa asamiau albasir
40:21	awa lam yasiru fi al ardi fayanthurukayfa kana aqibatu alathina kanumin qablihim kanu hum ashada minhum quwatan watharanfi al ardi fakhathahumu alahu bithunubihimwama kana lahum mina alahi min waq
40:22	thalika bi anahum kanata tihim rusuluhum bilbayinati fakafaru fakhathahumualahu inahu qawiyun shadidu alaiqab
40:23	walaqad arsalna musa bi ayatinawasultanin mubin
40:24	ila firawna wahamanawaqaruna faqalu sahirun kaththab
40:25	falama jahum bilhaqimin aindina qalu uqtulu abna alathinamanu mahu wastahyu nisahumwama kaydu alkafirina ila fi dalal
40:26	waqala firawnu tharuniaqtul musa walyadau rabahu ini akhafu anyubadila dinakum aw an yuthira fi al ardialfasad
40:27	waqala musa ini authtubirabi warabikum min kuli mutakabirin la yu minubiyawmi alhisab
40:28	waqala rajulun mu minun min alifirawna yaktumu imanahu ataqtuluna rajulan an yaqularabiya alahu waqad jakum bilbayinatimin rabikum wa in yaku kathiban falayhi kathibuhuwa in yaku sadiqan yusibkum badu alathiyaidukum ina alaha la yahdi man huwa musrifunkaththab
40:29	ya qawmi lakumu almulku alyawma thahirinafi al ardi faman yansuruna min ba si alahin jana qala firawnu ma urikumila ma ara wama ahdikum ilasabila arashad
40:30	waqala alathi amana yaqawmi ini akhafu alaykum mithla yawmi al ahzab
40:31	mithla da bi qawmi nuhin wadinwathamuda walathina min badihim wamalahu yuridu thulman lilaibad
40:32	waya qawmi ini akhafualaykum yawma atanad
40:33	yawma tuwaluna mudbirina ma lakumina alahi min asimin waman yudlili alahufama lahu min had
40:34	walaqad jakum yusufu min qablu bilbayinatifama ziltum fi shakin mima jakum bihi hataitha halaka qultum lan yabatha alahu minbadihi rasulan kathalika yudilu alahuman huwa musrifun murtab
40:35	alathina yujadiluna fi ayatialahi bighayri sultanin atahum kaburamaqtan ainda alahi wainda alathina amanukathalika yatbau alahu ala kuliqalbi mutakabirin jabar
40:36	waqala firawnu ya hamanuibni li sarhan lali ablughu al asbab
40:37	asbaba asamawatifatalia ila ilahi musa wa inilathunuhu kathiban wakathalikazuyina lifirawna su u amalihi wasuda ani asabiliwama kaydu firawna ila fi tabab
40:38	waqala alathi amana yaqawmi itabiauni ahdikum sabila arashad
40:39	ya qawmi inama hathihialhayatu adunya mataunwa ina al akhirata hiya daru alqarar
40:40	man amila sayi atan fala yujzaila mithlaha waman amila salihanmin thakarin aw untha wahuwa mu minun faula ikayadkhuluna aljanata yurzaquna fiha bighayri hisab
40:41	waya qawmi mali adaukum ilanajati watadaunani ila anar
40:42	tadaunani li akfura bilahiwaushrika bihi ma laysa li bihi ailmun wanadaukum ila alazizi alghafar
40:43	la jarama anama tadaunanilayhi laysa lahu dawatun fi adunya walafi al akhirati wana maradana ila alahiwana almusrifina hum as habu anar
40:44	fasatathkuruna ma aqululakum waufawidu amri ila alahi ina alahabasirun bilaibad
40:45	fawaqahu alahu sayi atima makaru wahaqa bi ali firawna su ualathab
40:46	anaru yuaradunalayha ghuduwan washiyan wayawma taqumu asatuadkhilu ala firawna ashada alathab
40:47	wa ith yatahajuna fi anarifayaqulu aduafau lilathinaistakbaru ina kuna lakum taban fahal antumughnuna ana nasiban mina anar
40:48	qala alathina istakbaru inakulun fiha ina alaha qad hakama baynalaibad
40:49	waqala alathina fi anarilikhazanati jahanama udau rabakum yukhafif anayawman mina alathab
40:50	qalu awa lam taku ta tikumrusulukum bilbayinati qalu bala qalufadau wama duau alkafirina ilafi dalal
40:51	ina lanansuru rusulanawalathina amanu fi alhayatiadunya wayawma yaqumu al ashad
40:52	yawma la yanfau aththaliminamathiratuhum walahumu alanatu walahum su u adar
40:53	walaqad atayna musalhuda wawrathna bani isra ila alkitab
40:54	hudan wathikra li uli al albab
40:55	fasbir ina wada alahihaqun wastaghfir lithanbika wasabihbihamdi rabika bilashiyi wal ibkar
40:56	ina alathina yujadilunafi ayati alahi bighayri sultanin atahumin fi sudurihim ila kibrun ma hum bibalighihifastaith bilahi inahu huwa asamiaualbasir
40:57	lakhalqu asamawati wal ardiakbaru min khalqi anasi walakina aktharanasi la yalamun
40:58	wama yastawi al ama walbasiruwalathina amanu wamilu asalihatiwala almusi u qalilan ma tatathakarun
40:59	ina asata latiyatunla rayba fiha walakina akthara anasila yu minun
40:60	waqala rabukumu udauni astajiblakum ina alathina yastakbiruna an aibadatisayadkhuluna jahanama dakhirin
40:61	alahu alathi jala lakumualayla litaskunu fihi wanahara mubsiranina alaha lathu fadlin ala anasiwalakina akthara anasi layashkurun
40:62	thalikumu alahu rabukum khaliqukuli shay in la ilaha ila huwa fanatu fakun
40:63	kathalika yu faku alathina kanubi ayati alahi yajhadun
40:64	alahu alathi jala lakumual arda qararan wasama binanwasawarakum fahsana suwarakum warazaqakumina atayibati thalikumu alahurabukum fatabaraka alahu rabu alalamin
40:65	huwa alhayu la ilahaila huwa fadauhu mukhlisina lahu adinalhamdu lilahi rabi alalamin
40:66	qul ini nuhitu an abuda alathinatadauna min duni alahi lama janiyalbayinatu min rabi waumirtu an uslima lirabi alalamin
40:67	huwa alathi khalaqakum min turabinthuma min nutfatin thuma min alaqatin thumayukhrijukum tiflan thuma litablughu ashudakum thumalitakunu shuyukhan waminkum man yutawafa min qabluwalitablughu ajalan musaman walalakum taqilun
40:68	huwa alathi yuhyi wayumitufa itha qada amran fa inama yaqulu lahukun fayakun
40:69	alam tara ila alathina yujadilunafi ayati alahi ana yusrafun
40:70	alathina kaththabu bilkitabiwabima arsalna bihi rusulana fasawfayalamun
40:71	ithi al aghlalu fi anaqihimwasalasilu yushabun
40:72	fi alhamimi thuma fi anariyusjarun
40:73	thuma qila lahum ayna ma kuntumtushrikun
40:74	min duni alahi qalu daluana bal lam nakun nadau min qablu shay an kathalikayudilu alahu alkafirin
40:75	thalikum bima kuntum tafrahunafi al ardi bighayri alhaqi wabima kuntumtamrahun
40:76	udkhulu abwaba jahanama khalidinafiha fabi sa mathwa almutakabirin
40:77	fasbir ina wada alahihaqun fa ima nuriyanaka bada alathinaiduhum aw natawafayanaka fa ilayna yurjaun
40:78	walaqad arsalna rusulan min qablikaminhum man qasasna alayka waminhum man lamnaqsus alayka wama kana lirasulinan ya tiya bi ayatin ila bi ithni alahifa itha ja amru alahi qudiya bilhaqiwakhasira hunalika almubtilun
40:79	alahu alathi jala lakumual anama litarkabu minha waminhata kulun
40:80	walakum fiha manafiauwalitablughu alayha hajatan fi sudurikumwalayha wala alfulki tuhmalun
40:81	wayurikum ayatihi faya ayatialahi tunkirun
40:82	afalam yasiru fi al ardi fayanthurukayfa kana aqibatu alathina min qablihimkanu akthara minhum washada quwatan watharanfi al ardi fama aghna anhum ma kanuyaksibun
40:83	falama jat hum rusuluhum bilbayinatifarihu bima aindahum mina alailmi wahaqabihim ma kanu bihi yastahzi un
40:84	falama raw ba sana qaluamana bilahi wahdahuwakafarna bima kuna bihi mushrikin
40:85	falam yaku yanfauhum imanuhum lamaraw ba sana sunata alahi alati qad khalat fiaibadihi wakhasira hunalika alkafirun
41:1	ha mim
41:2	tanzilun mina arahmaniarahim
41:3	kitabun fusilat ayatuhuqur anan arabiyan liqawmin yalamun
41:4	bashiran wanathiran faradaktharuhum fahum la yasmaun
41:5	waqalu qulubuna fi akinatinmima tadauna ilayhi wafi athaninawaqrun wamin baynina wabaynika hijabun famalinana amilun
41:6	qul inama ana basharunmithlukum yuha ilaya anama ilahukum ilahunwahidun fastaqimu ilayhi wastaghfiruhuwawaylun lilmushrikin
41:7	alathina la yu tuna azakatawahum bil akhirati hum kafirun
41:8	ina alathina amanuwamilu asalihati lahum ajrun ghayrumamnun
41:9	qul a inakum latakfuruna bilathikhalaqa al arda fi yawmayni watajaluna lahu andadanthalika rabu alalamin
41:10	wajala fiha rawasiya minfawqiha wabaraka fiha waqadara fihaqwataha fi arbati ayamin sawanlisa ilin
41:11	thuma istawa ila asama iwahiya dukhanun faqala laha walil ardi tiya tawan aw karhan qalata ataynata iain
41:12	faqadahuna saba samawatinfi yawmayni wawha fi kuli sama in amrahawazayana asama adunyabimasabiha wahifthan thalikataqdiru alazizi alalim
41:13	fa in aradu fuqul anthartukumsaiqatan mithla saiqati adin wathamud
41:14	ith jat humu arusulumin bayni aydihim wamin khalfihim ala tabudu ilalaha qalu law sha rabunalanzala mala ikatan fa ina bima ursiltumbihi kafirun
41:15	fama adun fastakbarufi al ardi bighayri alhaqi waqalu manashadu mina quwatan awa lam yaraw ana alahalathi khalaqahum huwa ashadu minhum quwatan wakanubi ayatina yajhadun
41:16	farsalna alayhim rihan sarsaranfi ayamin nahisatin linuthiqahumathaba alkhizyi fi alhayati adunyawalathabu al akhirati akhza wahum layunsarun
41:17	wama thamudu fahadaynahumfastahabu alama ala alhudafakhathat hum saiqatu alathabi alhunibima kanu yaksibun
41:18	wanajayna alathina amanuwakanu yataqun
41:19	wayawma yuhsharu adau alahila anari fahum yuzaun
41:20	hata itha ma jauhashahida alayhim samauhum wabsaruhum wajuluduhum bimakanu yamalun
41:21	waqalu lijuludihim lima shahidtumalayna qalu antaqana alahualathi antaqa kula shay in wahuwa khalaqakumawala maratin wa ilayhi turjaun
41:22	wama kuntum tastatiruna an yashadalaykum samaukum wala absarukum walajuludukum walakin thanantum ana alahala yalamu kathiran mima tamalun
41:23	wathalikum thanukumualathi thanantum birabikum ardakumfasbahtum mina alkhasirin
41:24	fa in yasbiru fanarumathwan lahum wa in yastatibu fama hum minalmuatabin
41:25	waqayadna lahum quranafazayanu lahum ma bayna aydihim wama khalfahumwahaqa alayhimu alqawlu fi umamin qad khalat minqablihim mina aljini wal insi inahum kanu khasirin
41:26	waqala alathina kafaru latasmau lihatha alqur ani walghaw fihilalakum taghlibun
41:27	falanuthiqana alathinakafaru athaban shadidan walanajziyanahum aswa alathikanu yamalun
41:28	thalika jazau ada ialahi anaru lahum fiha darualkhuldi jazan bima kanu bi ayatinayajhadun
41:29	waqala alathina kafarurabana arina alathayni adalanamina aljini wal insi najalhuma tahta aqdaminaliyakuna mina al asfalin
41:30	ina alathina qalu rabunalahu thuma istaqamu tatanazalu alayhimualmala ikatu ala takhafu wala tahzanuwabshiru biljanati alati kuntum tuadun
41:31	nahnu awliyaukum fi alhayatiadunya wafi al akhirati walakum fihama tashtahi anfusukum walakum fiha matadaun
41:32	nuzulan min ghafurin rahim
41:33	waman ahsanu qawlan miman daila alahi wamila salihan waqalainani mina almuslimin
41:34	wala tastawi alhasanatu walasayi atu idfa bilati hiya ahsanufa itha alathi baynaka wabaynahu adawatunkanahu waliyun hamim
41:35	wama yulaqaha ilalathina sabaru wama yulaqahaila thu haththin athim
41:36	wa ima yanzaghanaka mina ashshaytaninazghun fastaith bilahi inahuhuwa asamiau alalim
41:37	wamin ayatihi alaylu wanaharuwashshamsu walqamaru la tasjudu lishshamsiwala lilqamari wasjudu lilahi alathikhalaqahuna in kuntum iyahu tabudun
41:38	fa ini istakbaru falathinainda rabika yusabihuna lahu bilayli wanahariwahum la yas amun
41:39	wamin ayatihi anaka taral arda khashiatan fa itha anzalnalayha alma ihtazat warabat ina alathiahyaha lamuhyi almawta inahuala kuli shay in qadir
41:40	ina alathina yulhiduna fiayatina la yakhfawna alaynafaman yulqa fi anari khayrun amanya ti aminan yawma alqiyamati iamalu mashi tum inahu bima tamaluna basir
41:41	ina alathina kafaru biththikrilama jahum wa inahu lakitabun aziz
41:42	la ya tihi albatilu min bayniyadayhi wala min khalfihi tanzilun min hakimin hamid
41:43	ma yuqalu laka ila maqad qila lirusuli min qablika ina rabaka lathumaghfiratin wathu aiqabin alim
41:44	walaw jalnahu qur ananajamiyan laqalu lawla fusilat ayatuhuajamiyun warabiyun qul huwa lilathina amanuhudan washifaun walathina layu minuna fi athanihim waqrun wahuwa alayhim aman ula ikayunadawna min makanin baid
41:45	walaqad atayna musalkitaba fakhtulifa fihi walawla kalimatunsabaqat min rabika laqudiya baynahum wa inahum lafishakin minhu murib
41:46	man amila salihanfalinafsihi waman asa falayha wamarabuka bithalamin lilabid
41:47	ilayhi yuradu ailmu asatiwama takhruju min thamaratin min akmamihawama tahmilu min untha wala tadauila biailmihi wayawma yunadihim ayna shuraka iqalu athanaka ma mina minshahid
41:48	wadala anhum ma kanuyadauna min qablu wathanu ma lahum minmahis
41:49	la yas amu al insanu min dua ialkhayri wa in masahu ashsharu fayausun qanut
41:50	wala in athaqnahu rahmatanmina min badi dara masat hulayaqulana hatha li wama athunu asataqa imatan wala in rujiatu ila rabi ina liaindahu lalhusna falanunabi ana alathinakafaru bima amilu walanuthiqanahum min athabinghalith
41:51	wa itha anamna alal insani arada wana bijanibihiwa itha masahu ashsharu fathu dua inarid
41:52	qul araytum in kana min aindi alahithuma kafartum bihi man adalu miman huwa fi shiqaqinbaid
41:53	sanurihim ayatina fial afaqi wafi anfusihim hatayatabayana lahum anahu alhaqu awa lam yakfi birabikanahu ala kuli shay in shahid
41:54	ala inahum fi miryatin min liqa irabihim ala inahu bikuli shay in muhit
42:1	ha mim
42:2	ayn sin qaf
42:3	kathalika yuhi ilayka wa ilalathina min qablika alahu alazizu alhakim
42:4	lahu ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi wahuwa alaliyu alathim
42:5	takadu asamawatuyatafatarna min fawqihina walmala ikatuyusabihuna bihamdi rabihim wayastaghfirunaliman fi al ardi ala ina alaha huwalghafuru arahim
42:6	walathina itakhathumin dunihi awliya alahu hafithunalayhim wama anta alayhim biwakil
42:7	wakathalika awhaynailayka qur anan arabiyan litunthira uma alqurawaman hawlaha watunthira yawma aljamai larayba fihi fariqun fi aljanati wafariqun fi asair
42:8	walaw sha alahu lajalahumumatan wahidatan walakin yudkhilu man yashaufi rahmatihi waththalimunama lahum min waliyin wala nasir
42:9	ami itakhathu min dunihi awliyafalahu huwa alwaliyu wahuwa yuhyi almawtawahuwa ala kuli shay in qadir
42:10	wama ikhtalaftum fihi min shay in fahukmuhuila alahi thalikumu alahu rabialayhi tawakaltu wa ilayhi unib
42:11	fatiru asamawatiwal ardi jala lakum min anfusikum azwajanwamina al anami azwajan yathraukum fihilaysa kamithlihi shay un wahuwa asamiau albasir
42:12	lahu maqalidu asamawatiwal ardi yabsutu arizqa liman yashauwayaqdiru inahu bikuli shay in alim
42:13	shara lakum mina adini mawasa bihi nuhan walathi awhaynailayka wama wasayna bihi ibrahimawamusa waisa an aqimu adina walatatafaraqu fihi kabura ala almushrikina matadauhum ilayhi alahu yajtabi ilayhi man yashauwayahdi ilayhi man yunib
42:14	wama tafaraqu ila minbadi ma jahumu alailmu baghyan baynahum walawlakalimatun sabaqat min rabika ila ajalin musaman laqudiyabaynahum wa ina alathina urithu alkitaba minbadihim lafi shakin minhu murib
42:15	falithalika fadau wastaqimkama umirta wala tatabia ahwahum waqul amantubima anzala alahu min kitabin waumirtuli adila baynakum alahu rabuna warabukum lanamaluna walakum amalukum la hujatabaynana wabaynakumu alahu yajmau baynanawa ilayhi almasir
42:16	walathina yuhajunafi alahi min badi ma istujiba lahu hujatuhumdahidatun ainda rabihim walayhim ghadabunwalahum athabun shadid
42:17	alahu alathi anzala alkitababilhaqi walmizani wamayudrika lala asata qarib
42:18	yastajilu biha alathina layu minuna biha walathina amanumushfiquna minha wayalamuna anaha alhaquala ina alathina yumaruna fi asatilafi dalalin baid
42:19	alahu latifun biaibadihiyarzuqu man yashau wahuwa alqawiyu alaziz
42:20	man kana yuridu hartha al akhiratinazid lahu fi harthihi waman kana yuridu harthadunya nu tihi minha wama lahu fial akhirati min nasib
42:21	am lahum shurakau sharau lahumina adini ma lam ya than bihi alahuwalawla kalimatu alfasli laqudiya baynahumwa ina aththalimina lahum athabunalim
42:22	tara aththaliminamushfiqina mima kasabu wahuwa waqiaun bihim walathinamanu wamilu asalihati fi rawdatialjanati lahum ma yashauna ainda rabihimthalika huwa alfadlu alkabir
42:23	thalika alathi yubashshirualahu aibadahu alathina amanuwamilu asalihati qul las alukum alayhi ajran ila almawadata fi alqurbawaman yaqtarif hasanatan nazid lahu fiha husnanina alaha ghafurun shakur
42:24	am yaquluna iftara ala alahikathiban fa in yasha i alahu yakhtim alaqalbika wayamhu alahu albatila wayuhiqualhaqa bikalimatihi inahu alimun bithatiasudur
42:25	wahuwa alathi yaqbalu atawbatan aibadihi wayafu ani asayi atiwayalamu ma tafalun
42:26	wayastajibu alathina amanuwamilu asalihati wayaziduhum min fadlihiwalkafiruna lahum athabun shadid
42:27	walaw basata alahu arizqaliaibadihi labaghaw fi al ardi walakinyunazilu biqadarin ma yashau inahu biaibadihikhabirun basir
42:28	wahuwa alathi yunazilu alghaythamin badi ma qanatu wayanshuru rahmatahuwahuwa alwaliyu alhamid
42:29	wamin ayatihi khalqu asamawatiwal ardi wama baththa fihima min dabatinwahuwa ala jamaihim itha yashau qadir
42:30	wama asabakum min musibatinfabima kasabat aydikum wayafu an kathir
42:31	wama antum bimuajizina fi al ardiwama lakum min duni alahi min waliyin walanasir
42:32	wamin ayatihi aljawarifi albahri kal alam
42:33	in yasha yuskini arihafayathlalna rawakida ala thahrihina fi thalika layatin likuli sabarinshakur
42:34	aw yubiqhuna bima kasabu wayafuan kathir
42:35	wayalama alathina yujadilunafi ayatina ma lahum min mahis
42:36	fama utitum min shay in famataualhayati adunya wama aindalahi khayrun wabqa lilathina amanuwala rabihim yatawakalun
42:37	walathina yajtanibuna kaba iral ithmi walfawahisha wa itha ma ghadibuhum yaghfirun

45:1	ha mim

45:18	thuma jalnaka alashariatin mina al amri fatabiaha walatatabia ahwa alathina la yalamun

45:23	afarayta mani itakhatha ilahahuhawahu wadalahu alahu alailmin wakhatama ala samaihi waqalbihi wajala alabasarihi ghishawatan faman yahdihi min badi alahiafala tathakarun
45:24	waqalu ma hiya ila hayatunadunya namutu wanahya wamayuhlikuna ila adahru wama lahum bithalikamin ailmin in hum ila yathunun
45:25	wa itha tutla alayhim ayatunabayinatin ma kana hujatahum ilan qalu i tu bi aba ina in kuntum sadiqin

46:1	ha mim
46:2	tanzilu alkitabi mina alahialazizi alhakim
46:3	ma khalaqna asamawatiwal arda wama baynahuma ilabilhaqi wajalin musaman walathinakafaru ama unthiru muaridun
46:4	qul araytum ma tadauna min dunialahi aruni matha khalaqu mina al ardiam lahum shirkun fi asamawati itunibikitabin min qabli hatha aw atharatin minailmin in kuntum sadiqin
46:5	waman adalu miman yadau min dunialahi man la yastajibu lahu ila yawmialqiyamati wahum an dua ihim ghafilun
46:6	wa itha hushira anasukanu lahum adan wakanu biaibadatihimkafirin
46:7	wa itha tutla alayhim ayatunabayinatin qala alathina kafaru lilhaqilama jahum hatha sihrun mubin
46:8	am yaquluna iftarahu qul iniftaraytuhu fala tamlikuna li mina alahi shay anhuwa alamu bima tufiduna fihi kafa bihishahidan bayni wabaynakum wahuwa alghafuru arahim
46:9	qul ma kuntu bidan mina arusuliwama adri ma yufalu bi wala bikum inatabiau ila ma yuha ilaya wamana ila nathirun mubin
46:10	qul araytum in kana min aindi alahiwakafartum bihi washahida shahidun min bani isra ilala mithlihi famana wastakbartum inalaha la yahdi alqawma aththalimin
46:11	waqala alathina kafarulilathina amanu law kana khayran masabaquna ilayhi wa ith lam yahtadu bihifasayaquluna hatha ifkun qadim
46:12	wamin qablihi kitabu musa imamanwarahmatan wahatha kitabun musadiqunlisanan arabiyan liyunthira alathina thalamuwabushra lilmuhsinin
46:13	ina alathina qalu rabunalahu thuma istaqamu fala khawfunalayhim wala hum yahzanun
46:14	ula ika as habualjanati khalidina fiha jazan bimakanu yamalun
46:15	wawasayna al insanabiwalidayhi ihsanan hamalat hu umuhukurhan wawadat hu kurhan wahamluhu wafisaluhuthalathuna shahran hata ithabalagha ashudahu wabalagha arbaina sanatan qala rabiawziani an ashkura niamataka alati anamta alaya walawalidaya wan amala salihan tardahuwaslih li fi thuriyati ini tubtuilayka wa ini mina almuslimin
46:16	ula ika alathina nataqabaluanhum ahsana ma amilu wanatajawazu ansayi atihim fi as habi aljanati wada asidqialathi kanu yuadun
46:17	walathi qala liwalidayhiufin lakuma ataidanini an ukhraja waqad khalatialqurunu min qabli wahuma yastaghithani alahawaylaka amin ina wada alahi haqunfayaqulu ma hatha ila asatirual awalin
46:18	ula ika alathina haqalayhimu alqawlu fi umamin qad khalat min qablihim minaljini wal insi inahum kanu khasirin
46:19	walikulin darajatun mimamilu waliyuwafiyahum amalahum wahum la yuthlamun
46:20	wayawma yuaradu alathinakafaru ala anari athabtum tayibatikumfi hayatikumu adunya wastamtatumbiha falyawma tujzawna athaba alhuni bimakuntum tastakbiruna fi al ardi bighayri alhaqiwabima kuntum tafsuqun
46:21	wathkur akha adinith anthara qawmahu bil ahqafiwaqad khalati anuthuru min bayni yadayhi waminkhalfihi ala tabudu ila alaha iniakhafu alaykum athaba yawmin athim
46:22	qalu aji tana lita fikanan alihatina fa tina bima taidunain kunta mina asadiqin
46:23	qala inama alailmu aindalahi waubalighukum ma ursiltu bihi walakiniarakum qawman tajhalun
46:24	falama rawhu aridanmustaqbila awdiyatihim qalu hatha aridunmumtiruna bal huwa ma istajaltum bihi rihunfiha athabun alim
46:25	tudamiru kula shay in bi amri rabihafasbahu la yura ila masakinuhumkathalika najzi alqawma almujrimin
46:26	walaqad makanahum fima inmakanakum fihi wajalna lahum saman wabsaranwaf idatan fama aghna anhum samauhum walabsaruhum wala af idatuhum min shay in ith kanuyajhaduna bi ayati alahi wahaqabihim ma kanu bihi yastahzi un
46:27	walaqad ahlakna ma hawlakumina alqura wasarafna al ayatilalahum yarjiaun
46:28	falawla nasarahumu alathinaitakhathu min duni alahi qurbanan alihatanbal dalu anhum wathalika ifkuhum wama kanuyaftarun
46:29	wa ith sarafna ilaykanafaran mina aljini yastamiauna alqur ana falamahadaruhu qalu ansitu falamaqudiya walaw ila qawmihim munthirin
46:30	qalu ya qawmana inasamiana kitaban unzila min badi musa musadiqanlima bayna yadayhi yahdi ila alhaqi wa ilatariqin mustaqim
46:31	ya qawmana ajibu daiyalahi waminu bihi yaghfir lakum min thunubikumwayujirkum min athabin alim
46:32	waman la yujib daiya alahifalaysa bimuajizin fi al ardi walaysa lahu min dunihiawliya ula ika fi dalalin mubin
46:33	awa lam yaraw ana alaha alathikhalaqa asamawati wal ardawalam yaya bikhalqihina biqadirin ala an yuhyiyalmawta bala inahu ala kuli shay inqadir
46:34	wayawma yuaradu alathinakafaru ala anari alaysa hatha bilhaqiqalu bala warabina qala fathuqualathaba bima kuntum takfurun
46:35	fasbir kama sabaraulu alazmi mina arusuli wala tastajil lahumkanahum yawma yarawna ma yuaduna lam yalbathu ilasatan min naharin balaghun fahal yuhlakuila alqawmu alfasiqun
47:1	alathina kafaru wasaduan sabili alahi adala amalahum
47:2	walathina amanuwamilu asalihati wamanu bimanuzila ala muhamadin wahuwa alhaqu minrabihim kafara anhum sayi atihim waslahabalahum
47:3	thalika bi ana alathinakafaru itabau albatila wana alathina amanuitabau alhaqa min rabihim kathalika yadribualahu linasi amthalahum
47:4	fa itha laqitumu alathinakafaru fadarba ariqabi hataitha athkhantumuhum fashudu alwathaqa fa imamanan badu wa ima fidan hata tadalharbu awzaraha thalika walaw yashaualahu lantasara minhum walakinliyabluwa badakum bibadin walathinaqutilu fi sabili alahi falan yudilamalahum
47:5	sayahdihim wayuslihu balahum
47:6	wayudkhiluhumu aljanata arafahalahum
47:7	ya ayuha alathina amanuin tansuru alaha yansurkum wayuthabit aqdamakum
47:8	walathina kafaru fatasanlahum wadala amalahum
47:9	thalika bi anahum karihu manzala alahu fahbata amalahum
47:10	afalam yasiru fi al ardi fayanthurukayfa kana aqibatu alathina min qablihimdamara alahu alayhim walilkafirina amthaluha
47:11	thalika bi ana alaha mawlalathina amanu wana alkafirina lamawla lahum
47:12	ina alaha yudkhilu alathinamanu wamilu asalihati janatintajri min tahtiha al anharu walathinakafaru yatamatauna waya kuluna kama ta kulu al anamuwanaru mathwan lahum
47:13	wakayin min qaryatin hiya ashadu quwatanmin qaryatika alati akhrajatka ahlaknahum fala nasiralahum
47:14	afaman kana ala bayinatinmin rabihi kaman zuyina lahu su u amalihi watabauahwahum
47:15	mathalu aljanati alati wuaidalmutaquna fiha anharun min ma in ghayri asininwanharun min labanin lam yataghayar tamuhuwanharun min khamrin laththatin lishsharibinawanharun min asalin musafan walahum fihamin kuli aththamarati wamaghfiratun min rabihimkaman huwa khalidun fi anari wasuqu manhamiman faqata amahum
47:16	waminhum man yastamiau ilayka hataitha kharaju min aindika qalu lilathinautu alailma matha qala anifan ula ikalathina taba alahu alaqulubihim watabau ahwahum
47:17	walathina ihtadaw zadahumhudan watahum taqwahum
47:18	fahal yanthuruna ilasata an ta tiyahum baghtatan faqad jashratuha fana lahum itha jat humthikrahum
47:19	falam anahu la ilahaila alahu wastaghfir lithanbikawalilmu minina walmu minati walahuyalamu mutaqalabakum wamathwakum
47:20	wayaqulu alathina amanulawla nuzilat suratun fa itha unzilat suratun muhkamatunwathukira fiha alqitalu rayta alathinafi qulubihim maradun yanthuruna ilayka natharalmaghshiyi alayhi mina almawti fawla lahum
47:21	tatun waqawlun marufun fa ithazama al amru falaw sadaqu alaha lakanakhayran lahum
47:22	fahal asaytum in tawalaytum an tufsidufi al ardi watuqatiau arhamakum
47:23	ula ika alathina lanahumualahu fasamahum wama absarahum
47:24	afala yatadabaruna alqur anam ala qulubin aqfaluha
47:25	ina alathina irtadu aladbarihim min badi ma tabayana lahumu alhudashshaytanu sawala lahum wamla lahum
47:26	thalika bi anahum qalu lilathinakarihu ma nazala alahu sanutiaukum fibadi al amri walahu yalamu israrahum
47:27	fakayfa itha tawafat humu almala ikatuyadribuna wujuhahum wadbarahum
47:28	thalika bi anahumu itabau maskhata alaha wakarihu ridwanahufahbata amalahum
47:29	am hasiba alathina fiqulubihim maradun an lan yukhrija alahu adghanahum
47:30	walaw nashau laraynakahumfalaraftahum bisimahum walatarifanahum fi lahnialqawli walahu yalamu amalakum
47:31	walanabluwanakum hatanalama almujahidina minkum wasabirinawanabluwa akhbarakum
47:32	ina alathina kafaru wasaduan sabili alahi washaqu arasula minbadi ma tabayana lahumu alhuda lan yadurualaha shay an wasayuhbitu amalahum
47:33	ya ayuha alathina amanuatiau alaha watiau arasulawala tubtilu amalakum
47:34	ina alathina kafaru wasaduan sabili alahi thuma matu wahum kufarunfalan yaghfira alahu lahum
47:35	fala tahinu watadau ila asalmiwantumu al alawna walahu makum walanyatirakum amalakum
47:36	inama alhayatu adunyalaibun walahwun wa in tu minu watataqu yu tikum ujurakumwala yas alkum amwalakum
47:37	in yas alkumuha fayuhfikumtabkhalu wayukhrij adghanakum
47:38	ha antum haula itudawna litunfiqu fi sabili alahi faminkum manyabkhalu waman yabkhal fa inama yabkhalu an nafsihi walahualghaniyu wantumu alfuqarau wa in tatawalaw yastabdilqawman ghayrakum thuma la yakunu amthalakum
48:1	ina fatahna laka fathanmubina
48:2	liyaghfira laka alahu mataqadama min thanbika wama takhkhara wayutimaniamatahu alayka wayahdiyaka siratan mustaqima
48:3	wayansuraka alahu nasranaziza
48:4	huwa alathi anzala asakinatafi qulubi almu minina liyazdadu imanan maimanihim walilahi junudu asamawatiwal ardi wakana alahu aliman hakima
48:5	liyudkhila almu minina walmu minatijanatin tajri min tahtiha al anharukhalidina fiha wayukafira anhum sayi atihimwakana thalika ainda alahi fawzan athima
48:6	wayuaththiba almunafiqina walmunafiqatiwalmushrikina walmushrikati aththaninabilahi thana asaw ialayhim da iratu asaw i waghadiba alahualayhim walanahum wada lahum jahanama wasat masira
48:7	walilahi junudu asamawatiwal ardi wakana alahu azizan hakima
48:8	ina arsalnaka shahidanwamubashshiran wanathira
48:9	litu minu bilahi warasulihiwatuaziruhu watuwaqiruhu watusabihuhu bukratan wasila
48:10	ina alathina yubayiaunakainama yubayiauna alaha yadu alahifawqa aydihim faman nakatha fa inama yankuthu alanafsihi waman awfa bima ahada alayhu alahafasayu tihi ajran athima
48:11	sayaqulu laka almukhalafuna mina al arabishaghalatna amwaluna wahluna fastaghfirlana yaquluna bi alsinatihim ma laysa fiqulubihim qul faman yamliku lakum mina alahi shay an inarada bikum daran aw arada bikum nafanbal kana alahu bima tamaluna khabira
48:12	bal thanantum an lan yanqalibarasulu walmu minuna ila ahlihimabadan wazuyina thalika fi qulubikum wathanantumthana asaw i wakuntum qawman bura
48:13	waman lam yu min bilahiwarasulihi fa ina atadna lilkafirinasaira
48:14	walilahi mulku asamawatiwal ardi yaghfiru liman yashau wayuaththibuman yashau wakana alahu ghafuran rahima
48:15	sayaqulu almukhalafuna itha intalaqtumila maghanima lita khuthuha tharunanatabiakum yuriduna an yubadilu kalama alahiqul lan tatabiauna kathalikum qala alahumin qablu fasayaquluna bal tahsudunana bal kanula yafqahuna ila qalila
48:16	qul lilmukhalafina mina al arabisatudawna ila qawmin uli ba sin shadidin tuqatilunahumaw yuslimuna fa in tutiau yu tikumu alahuajran hasanan wa in tatawalaw kama tawalaytum minqablu yuaththibkum athaban alima
48:17	laysa ala al ama harajunwala ala al araji harajun walala almaridi harajun waman yutiaialaha warasulahu yudkhilhu janatin tajri min tahtihal anharu waman yatawala yuaththibhu athabanalima
48:18	laqad radiya alahu anialmu minina ith yubayiaunaka tahta ashshajaratifalima ma fi qulubihim fanzala asakinatalayhim wathabahum fathan qariba
48:19	wamaghanima kathiratan ya khuthunahawakana alahu azizan hakima
48:20	wadakumu alahu maghanimakathiratan ta khuthunaha fajala lakum hathihiwakafa aydiya anasi ankum walitakuna ayatanlilmu minina wayahdiyakum siratan mustaqima
48:21	waukhra lam taqdiru alayhaqad ahata alahu biha wakana alahuala kuli shay in qadira
48:22	walaw qatalakumu alathinakafaru lawalawu al adbara thuma la yajidunawaliyan wala nasira
48:23	sunata alahi alati qad khalat minqablu walan tajida lisunati alahi tabdila
48:24	wahuwa alathi kafa aydiyahumankum waydiyakum anhum bibatni makata min badi anathfarakum alayhim wakana alahubima tamaluna basira
48:25	humu alathina kafaru wasadukumani almasjidi alharami walhadyamakufan an yablugha mahilahu walawla rijalunmu minuna wanisaun mu minatun lam talamuhum antatauhum fatusibakum minhum maratun bighayriailmin liyudkhila alahu fi rahmatihi man yashaulaw tazayalu laththabna alathinakafaru minhum athaban alima
48:26	ith jala alathina kafarufi qulubihimu alhamiyata hamiyata aljahiliyatifanzala alahu sakinatahu ala rasulihi walalmu minina walzamahum kalimata ataqwa wakanuahaqa biha wahlaha wakana alahubikuli shay in alima
48:27	laqad sadaqa alahu rasulahuaru ya bilhaqi latadkhulunalmasjida alharama in sha alahu amininamuhaliqina rusakum wamuqasirina latakhafuna falima ma lam talamu fajala minduni thalika fathan qariba
48:28	huwa alathi arsala rasulahu bilhudawadini alhaqi liyuthirahu ala adinikulihi wakafa bilahi shahida
48:29	muhamadun rasulu alahi walathinamahu ashidau ala alkufari ruhamaubaynahum tarahum rukan sujadan yabtaghuna fadlanmina alahi waridwanan simahum fiwujuhihim min athari asujudi thalika mathaluhumfi atawrati wamathaluhum fi al injilikazarain akhraja shat ahu fazarahu fastaghlathafastawa ala suqihi yuajibu azuraliyaghitha bihimu alkufara wada alahualathina amanu wamilu asalihatiminhum maghfiratan wajran athima
49:1	ya ayuha alathina amanula tuqadimu bayna yadayi alahi warasulihi wataqualaha ina alaha samiaun alim
49:2	ya ayuha alathina amanula tarfau aswatakum fawqa sawti anabiyiwala tajharu lahu bilqawli kajahri badikumlibadin an tahbata amalukumwantum la tashaurun
49:3	ina alathina yaghuduna aswatahumainda rasuli alahi ula ika alathinaimtahana alahu qulubahum litaqwalahum maghfiratun wajrun athim
49:4	ina alathina yunadunaka minwara i alhujurati aktharuhum layaqilun
49:5	walaw anahum sabaru hatatakhruja ilayhim lakana khayran lahum walahughafurun rahim
49:6	ya ayuha alathina amanuin jakum fasiqun binaba in fatabayanu an tusibuqawman bijahalatin fatusbihu ala mafaltum nadimin
49:7	walamu ana fikum rasula alahilaw yutiaukum fi kathirin mina al amri lanitum walakinalaha hababa ilaykumu al imanawazayanahu fi qulubikum wakaraha ilaykumu alkufra walfusuqawalaisyana ula ika humu arashidun
49:8	fadlan mina alahi waniamatanwalahu alimun hakim
49:9	wa in ta ifatani minalmu minina iqtatalu faslihu baynahumafa in baghat ihdahuma ala al ukhrafaqatilu alati tabghi hata tafi a ilamri alahi fa in fat faslihubaynahuma biladli waqsitu ina alahayuhibu almuqsitin
49:10	inama almu minuna ikhwatun faslihubayna akhawaykum wataqu alaha lalakum turhamun
49:11	ya ayuha alathina amanula yaskhar qawmun min qawmin asa an yakunukhayran minhum wala nisaun min nisa in asan yakuna khayran minhuna wala talmizu anfusakum walatanabazu bil alqabi bi sa al ismualfusuqu bada al imani waman lam yatub faula ikahumu aththalimun
49:12	ya ayuha alathina amanuijtanibu kathiran mina aththani inabada aththani ithmun walatajasasu wala yaghtab badukum badanayuhibu ahadukum an ya kula lahma akhihimaytan fakarihtumuhu wataqu alaha ina alahatawabun rahim
49:13	ya ayuha anasuina khalaqnakum min thakarin waunthawajalnakum shuauban waqaba ila litarafuina akramakum ainda alahi atqakum ina alahalimun khabir
49:14	qalati al arabu amanaqul lam tu minu walakin qulu aslamna walamayadkhuli al imanu fi qulubikum wa in tutiaualaha warasulahu la yalitkum min amalikumshay an ina alaha ghafurun rahim
49:15	inama almu minuna alathinamanu bilahi warasulihi thuma lam yartabuwajahadu bi amwalihim wanfusihim fi sabili alahiula ika humu asadiqun

49:18	ina alaha yalamu ghayba asamawatiwal ardi walahu basirunbima tamalun
