57:1	sabaha lilahi ma fi asamawatiwal ardi wahuwa alazizu alhakim
57:2	lahu mulku asamawatiwal ardi yuhyi wayumitu wahuwa alakuli shay in qadir
57:3	huwa al awalu wal akhiru waththahiruwalbatinu wahuwa bikuli shay in alim
57:4	huwa alathi khalaqa asamawatiwal arda fi sitati ayamin thuma istawala alarshi yalamu ma yaliju fi al ardiwama yakhruju minha wama yanzilu mina asama iwama yaruju fiha wahuwa makum ayna makuntum walahu bima tamaluna basir
57:5	lahu mulku asamawatiwal ardi wa ila alahi turjaual umur
57:6	yuliju alayla fi anahariwayuliju anahara fi alayi wahuwa alimun bithatiasudur
57:7	aminu bilahiwarasulihi wanfiqu mima jalakum mustakhlafinafihi falathina amanu minkum wanfaqulahum ajrun kabir
57:8	wama lakum la tu minuna bilahiwarasulu yadaukum litu minu birabikum waqad akhathamithaqakum in kuntum mu minin
57:9	huwa alathi yunazilu alabdihi ayatin bayinatin liyukhrijakumina aththulumati ila anuriwa ina alaha bikum laraufun rahim
57:10	wama lakum ala tunfiqu fisabili alahi walilahi mirathu asamawatiwal ardi la yastawi minkum man anfaqa minqabli alfathi waqatala ula ika athamudarajatan mina alathina anfaqu min badu waqataluwakulan wada alahu alhusna walahubima tamaluna khabir
57:11	man tha alathi yuqridualaha qardan hasanan fayudaifahulahu walahu ajrun karim
57:12	yawma tara almu minina walmu minatiyasa nuruhum bayna aydihim wabi aymanihim bushrakumualyawma janatun tajri min tahtiha al anharukhalidina fiha thalika huwa alfawzu alathim
57:13	yawma yaqulu almunafiquna walmunafiqatulilathina amanu unthurunanaqtabis min nurikum qila irjiau warakum faltamisunuran faduriba baynahum bisurin lahu babun batinuhufihi arahmatu wathahiruhumin qibalihi alathab
57:14	yunadunahum alam nakun makum qalubala walakinakum fatantum anfusakum watarabastumwartabtum wagharatkumu al amaniyu hataja amru alahi wagharakum bilahialgharur
57:15	falyawma la yu khathuminkum fidyatun wala mina alathina kafaru ma wakumuanaru hiya mawlakum wabi sa almasir
57:16	alam ya ni lilathina amanuan takhsha qulubuhum lithikri alahi wamanazala mina alhaqi wala yakunu kalathinautu alkitaba min qablu fatala alayhimu al amadufaqasat qulubuhum wakathirun minhum fasiqun
57:17	ialamu ana alaha yuhyial arda bada mawtiha qad bayana lakumual ayati lalakum taqilun
57:18	ina almusadiqina walmusadiqatiwaqradu alaha qardan hasanan yudafulahum walahum ajrun karim
57:19	walathina amanu bilahiwarusulihi ula ika humu asidiquna washshuhadauainda rabihim lahum ajruhum wanuruhum walathinakafaru wakaththabu bi ayatina ula ikas habu aljahim
57:20	ialamu anama alhayatuadunya laibun walahwun wazinatun watafakhurunbaynakum watakathurun fi al amwali wal awladikamathali ghaythin ajaba alkufara nabatuhuthuma yahiju fatarahu musfaran thuma yakunu hutamanwafi al akhirati athabun shadidun wamaghfiratunmina alahi waridwanun wama alhayatuadunya ila matau alghurur
57:21	sabiqu ila maghfiratin minrabikum wajanatin arduha kardi asama iwal ardi uaidat lilathina amanubilahi warusulihi thalika fadlu alahiyu tihi man yashau walahu thualfadli alathim
57:22	ma asaba min musibatinfi al ardi wala fi anfusikum ila fi kitabinmin qabli an nabraha ina thalika ala alahiyasir
57:23	likayla ta saw ala mafatakum wala tafrahu bima atakumwalahu la yuhibu kula mukhtalinfakhur
57:24	alathina yabkhaluna waya muruna anasabilbukhli waman yatawala fa ina alaha huwalghaniyu alhamid
57:25	laqad arsalna rusulana bilbayinatiwanzalna mahumu alkitaba walmizanaliyaquma anasu bilqisti wanzalnalhadida fihi ba sun shadidun wamanafiau linasiwaliyalama alahu man yansuruhu warusulahu bilghaybina alaha qawiyun aziz
57:26	walaqad arsalna nuhan wa ibrahimawajalna fi thuriyatihima anubuwatawalkitaba faminhum muhtadin wakathirun minhum fasiqun
57:27	thuma qafayna ala atharihimbirusulina waqafayna biaisa ibni maryamawataynahu al injila wajalna fi qulubialathina itabauhu ra fatan warahmatan warahbaniyatanibtadauha ma katabnaha alayhimila ibtigha ridwani alahifama rawha haqa riayatihafatayna alathina amanu minhumajrahum wakathirun minhum fasiqun
57:28	ya ayuha alathina amanuitaqu alaha waminu birasulihi yu tikumkiflayni min rahmatihi wayajal lakum nuran tamshunabihi wayaghfir lakum walahu ghafurun rahim
57:29	li ala yalama ahlu alkitabiala yaqdiruna ala shay in min fadli alahiwana alfadla biyadi alahi yu tihi man yashauwalahu thu alfadli alathim
58:1	qad samia alahu qawla alati tujadilukafi zawjiha watashtaki ila alahi walahuyasmau tahawurakuma ina alaha samiaunbasir
58:2	alathina yuthahirunaminkum min nisa ihim ma huna umahatihim inumahatuhum ila ala i waladnahumwa inahum layaquluna munkaran mina alqawli wazuran wa inalaha lafuwun ghafur
58:3	walathina yuthahirunamin nisa ihim thuma yauduna lima qalufatahriru raqabatin min qabli an yatamasa thalikumtuathuna bihi walahu bimatamaluna khabir
58:4	faman lam yajid fasiyamushahrayni mutatabiayni min qabli an yatamasafaman lam yastatia fa itamu sitinamiskinan thalika litu minu bilahiwarasulihi watilka hududu alahi walilkafirinathabun alim
58:5	ina alathina yuhaduna alahawarasulahu kubitu kama kubita alathina minqablihim waqad anzalna ayatin bayinatinwalilkafirina athabun muhin
58:6	yawma yabathuhumu alahu jamianfayunabi uhum bima amilu ahsahu alahuwanasuhu walahu ala kuli shay inshahid
58:7	alam tara ana alaha yalamu mafi asamawati wama fi al ardima yakunu min najwa thalathatin ilahuwa rabiauhum wala khamsatin ila huwa sadisuhumwala adna min thalika wala aktharaila huwa mahum ayna ma kanu thumayunabi uhum bima amilu yawma alqiyamati inalaha bikuli shay in alim
58:8	alam tara ila alathina nuhuani anajwa thuma yauduna lima nuhuanhu wayatanajawna bil ithmi walaudwaniwamasiyati arasuli wa itha jaukahayawka bima lam yuhayika bihi alahuwayaquluna fi anfusihim lawla yuaththibunalahu bima naqulu hasbuhum jahanamu yaslawnahafabi sa almasir
58:9	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha tanajaytum fala tatanajaw bil ithmiwalaudwani wamasiyati arasuliwatanajaw bilbiri wataqwa wataqualaha alathi ilayhi tuhsharun
58:10	inama anajwa mina ashshaytaniliyahzuna alathina amanu walaysa bidarihimshay an ila bi ithni alahi walalahi falyatawakali almu minun
58:11	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha qila lakum tafasahu fi almajalisifafsahu yafsahi alahu lakum wa ithaqila unshuzu fanshuzu yarfai alahu alathinamanu minkum walathina utu alailmadarajatin walahu bima tamalunakhabir
58:12	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha najaytumu arasula faqadimu baynayaday najwakum sadaqatan thalika khayrunlakum watharu fa in lam tajidu fa ina alahaghafurun rahim
58:13	ashfaqtum an tuqadimu bayna yaday najwakumsadaqatin fa ith lam tafalu watabalahu alaykum faqimu asalatawatu azakata watiau alahawarasulahu walahu khabirun bimatamalun
58:14	alam tara ila alathinatawalaw qawman ghadiba alahu alayhim mahum minkum wala minhum wayahlifuna alalkathibi wahum yalamun
58:15	ada alahu lahum athabanshadidan inahum sa ma kanu yamalun
58:16	itakhathu aymanahum junatanfasadu an sabili alahi falahum athabunmuhin
58:17	lan tughniya anhum amwaluhum walawladuhum mina alahi shay an ula ika as habuanari hum fiha khalidun
58:18	yawma yabathuhumu alahu jamianfayahlifuna lahu kama yahlifuna lakum wayahsabunanahum ala shay in ala inahum humu alkathibun
58:19	istahwatha alayhimu ashshaytanufansahum thikra alahi ula ika hizbuashshaytani ala ina hizba ashshaytanihumu alkhasirun
58:20	ina alathina yuhaduna alahawarasulahu ula ika fi al athalin
58:21	kataba alahu laghlibana anawarusuli ina alaha qawiyun aziz
58:22	la tajidu qawman yu minuna bilahiwalyawmi al akhiri yuwaduna man hadalaha warasulahu walaw kanu abahumaw abnahum aw ikhwanahum aw ashiratahum ula ikakataba fi qulubihimu al imana wayadahum biruhinminhu wayudkhiluhum janatin tajri min tahtihal anharu khalidina fiha radiya alahuanhum waradu anhu ula ika hizbu alahiala ina hizba alahi humu almuflihun
59:1	sabaha lilahi ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi wahuwa alazizu alhakim
59:2	huwa alathi akhraja alathinakafaru min ahli alkitabi min diyarihim li awalialhashri ma thanantum an yakhruju wathanuanahum maniatuhum husunuhum mina alahifatahumu alahu min haythu lam yahtasibuwaqathafa fi qulubihimu aruaba yukhribunabuyutahum bi aydihim waydi almu minina fatabiru yauli al absar
59:3	walawla an kataba alahualayhimu aljala laththabahum fi adunyawalahum fi al akhirati athabu anar
59:4	thalika bi anahum shaqu alahawarasulahu waman yushaqi alaha fa ina alahashadidu alaiqab
59:5	ma qatatum min linatin awtaraktumuha qa imatan ala usulihafabi ithni alahi waliyukhziya alfasiqin
59:6	wama afa alahu alarasulihi minhum fama awjaftum alayhi min khaylin walarikabin walakina alaha yusaliturusulahu ala man yashau walahuala kuli shay in qadir
59:7	ma afa alahu alarasulihi min ahli alqura falilahi walirasuliwalithi alqurba walyatama walmasakiniwabni asabili kay la yakuna dulatanbayna al aghniya i minkum wama atakumuarasulu fakhuthuhu wama nahakumanhu fantahu wataqu alaha ina alahashadidu alaiqab
59:8	lilfuqara i almuhajirina alathinaukhriju min diyarihim wamwalihim yabtaghuna fadlanmina alahi waridwanan wayansurunalaha warasulahu ula ika humu asadiqun
59:9	walathina tabawau adarawal imana min qablihim yuhibuna man hajarailayhim wala yajiduna fi sudurihim hajatanmima utu wayu thiruna ala anfusihim walaw kanabihim khasasatun waman yuqa shuha nafsihi faula ikahumu almuflihun
59:10	walathina jau minbadihim yaquluna rabana ighfir lana wali ikhwaninalathina sabaquna bil imani walatajal fi qulubina ghilan lilathina amanurabana inaka raufun rahim
59:11	alam tara ila alathina nafaquyaquluna li ikhwanihimu alathina kafaru minahli alkitabi la in ukhrijtum lanakhrujana makum walanutiau fikum ahadan abadan wa in qutiltum lanansuranakumwalahu yashadu inahum lakathibun
59:12	la in ukhriju la yakhrujunamahum wala in qutilu la yansurunahum wala inasaruhum layuwaluna al adbara thuma layunsarun
59:13	lantum ashadu rahbatan fi sudurihimina alahi thalika bi anahum qawmun layafqahun
59:14	la yuqatilunakum jamianila fi quran muhasanatin aw min wara ijudurin ba suhum baynahum shadidun tahsabuhum jamianwaqulubuhum shata thalika bi anahum qawmun layaqilun
59:15	kamathali alathina min qablihimqariban thaqu wabala amrihim walahum athabunalim
59:16	kamathali ashshaytani ithqala lil insani ukfur falama kafara qalaini bari un minka ini akhafu alaha raba alalamin
59:17	fakana aqibatahumanahuma fi anari khalidayni fihawathalika jazau aththalimin
59:18	ya ayuha alathina amanuitaqu alaha waltanthur nafsun maqadamat lighadin wataqu alaha ina alahakhabirun bima tamalun
59:19	wala takunu kalathinanasu alaha fansahum anfusahum ula ikahumu alfasiqun
59:20	la yastawi as habu anariwas habu aljanati as habu aljanatihumu alfa izun
59:21	law anzalna hatha alqur anala jabalin laraytahu khashian mutasadianmin khashyati alahi watilka al amthalu nadribuhalinasi lalahum yatafakarun
59:22	huwa alahu alathi lailaha ila huwa alimu alghaybi washshahadatihuwa arahmanu arahim
59:23	huwa alahu alathi lailaha ila huwa almaliku alqudusu asalamualmu minu almuhayminu alazizu aljabaru almutakabirusubhana alahi ama yushrikun
59:24	huwa alahu alkhaliqu albari ualmusawiru lahu al asmau alhusnayusabihu lahu ma fi asamawatiwal ardi wahuwa alazizu alhakim
60:1	ya ayuha alathina amanula tatakhithu aduwi waduwakum awliyatulquna ilayhim bilmawadati waqad kafaru bima jakumina alhaqi yukhrijuna arasula wa iyakuman tu minu bilahi rabikum in kuntum kharajtumjihadan fi sabili wabtigha mardatitusiruna ilayhim bilmawadati wana alamu bimakhfaytum wama alantum waman yafalhu minkum faqad dalasawa asabil
60:2	in yathqafukum yakunu lakam adanwayabsutu ilaykum aydiyahum walsinatahum bisu iwawadu law takfurun
60:3	lan tanfakum arhamukum walawladukum yawma alqiyamati yafsilu baynakumwalahu bima tamaluna basir
60:4	qad kanat lakum uswatun hasanatunfi ibrahima walathina mahu ithqalu liqawmihim ina burau minkum wamimatabuduna min duni alahi kafarna bikum wabadabaynana wabaynakumu aladawatu walbaghdauabadan hata tu minu bilahi wahdahuila qawla ibrahima li abihi lastaghfirana lakawama amliku laka mina alahi min shay in rabanalayka tawakalna wa ilayka anabna wa ilayka almasir
60:5	rabana la tajalnafitnatan lilathina kafaru waghfir lanarabana inaka anta alazizu alhakim
60:6	laqad kana lakum fihim uswatun hasanatunliman kana yarju alaha walyawma al akhirawaman yatawala fa ina alaha huwa alghaniyu alhamid
60:7	asa alahu an yajalabaynakum wabayna alathina adaytum minhumawadatan walahu qadirun walahughafurun rahim
60:8	la yanhakumu alahuani alathina lam yuqatilukum fi adiniwalam yukhrijukum min diyarikum an tabaruhum watuqsituilayhim ina alaha yuhibu almuqsitin
60:9	inama yanhakumu alahuani alathina qatalukum fi adiniwakhrajukum min diyarikum wathaharuala ikhrajikum an tawalawhum waman yatawalahumfaula ika humu aththalimun
60:10	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha jakumu almu minatu muhajiratinfamtahinuhuna alahu alamu bi imanihinafa in alimtumuhuna mu minatin falatarjiauhuna ila alkufari la huna hilunlahum wala hum yahiluna lahuna watuhuma anfaqu wala junaha alaykum an tankihuhunaitha ataytumuhuna ujurahuna walatumsiku biaisami alkawafiri was alu manfaqtum walyas alu ma anfaqu thalikum hukmualahi yahkumu baynakum walahualimun hakim
60:11	wa in fatakum shay un min azwajikumila alkufari faqabtum fatu alathinathahabat azwajuhum mithla ma anfaqu wataqualaha alathi antum bihi mu minun
60:12	ya ayuha anabiyu ithajaka almu minatu yubayianaka alan la yushrikna bilahi shay an walayasriqna wala yaznina wala yaqtulna awladahunawala ya tina bibuhtanin yaftarinahu baynaydihina warjulihina wala yasinaka fimarufin fabayiahuna wastaghfir lahuna alahaina alaha ghafurun rahim
60:13	ya ayuha alathina amanula tatawalaw qawman ghadiba alahualayhim qad ya isu mina al akhirati kama ya isalkufaru min as habi alqubur
61:1	sabaha lilahi ma fi asamawatiwama fi al ardi wahuwa alazizu alhakim
61:2	ya ayuha alathina amanulima taquluna ma la tafalun
61:3	kabura maqtan ainda alahi antaqulu ma la tafalun
61:4	ina alaha yuhibu alathinayuqatiluna fi sabilihi safan kanahum bunyanunmarsus
61:5	wa ith qala musaliqawmihi ya qawmi lima tu thunani waqadtalamuna ani rasulu alahi ilaykum falama zaghuazagha alahu qulubahum walahu layahdi alqawma alfasiqin
61:6	wa ith qala aisa ibnumaryama ya bani isra ila ini rasulu alahilaykum musadiqan lima bayna yadaya mina atawratiwamubashshiran birasulin ya ti min badi ismuhu ahmadufalama jahum bilbayinati qaluhatha sihrun mubin
61:7	waman athlamu mimani iftarala alahi alkathiba wahuwa yudaila al islami walahu layahdi alqawma aththalimin
61:8	yuriduna liyutfi u nura alahibi afwahihim walahu mutimu nurihi walawkariha alkafirun
61:9	huwa alathi arsala rasulahu bilhudawadini alhaqi liyuthirahu ala adinikulihi walaw kariha almushrikun
61:10	ya ayuha alathina amanuhal adulukum ala tijaratin tunjikum min athabinalim
61:11	tu minuna bilahiwarasulihi watujahiduna fi sabili alahi bi amwalikumwanfusikum thalikum khayrun lakum in kuntum talamun
61:12	yaghfir lakum thunubakumwayudkhilkum janatin tajri min tahtihal anharu wamasakina tayibatan fi janatiadnin thalika alfawzu alathim
61:13	waukhra tuhibunaha nasrunmina alahi wafathun qaribun wabashshirialmu minin
61:14	ya ayuha alathina amanukunu ansara alahi kama qala aisaibnu maryama lilhawariyina man ansari ilalahi qala alhawariyuna nahnuansaru alahi famanat ta ifatun minbani isra ila wakafarat ta ifatun fayadnalathina amanu ala aduwihim fasbahuthahirin
62:1	yusabihu lilahi ma fiasamawati wama fi al ardialmaliki alqudusi alazizi alhakim
62:2	huwa alathi batha fial umiyina rasulan minhum yatlu alayhim ayatihiwayuzakihim wayualimuhumu alkitaba walhikmatawa in kanu min qablu lafi dalalin mubin
62:3	wakharina minhum lama yalhaqubihim wahuwa alazizu alhakim
62:4	thalika fadlu alahiyu tihi man yashau walahu thualfadli alathim
62:5	mathalu alathina humilu atawratathuma lam yahmiluha kamathali alhimariyahmilu asfaran bi sa mathalu alqawmi alathinakaththabu bi ayati alahi walahula yahdi alqawma aththalimin
62:6	qul ya ayuha alathinahadu in zamtum anakum awliyau lilahimin duni anasi fatamanawu almawta in kuntum sadiqin
62:7	wala yatamanawnahu abadan bimaqadamat aydihim walahu alimun biththalimin
62:8	qul ina almawta alathi tafirunaminhu fa inahu mulaqikum thuma turaduna ila alimialghaybi washshahadati fayunabi ukum bimakuntum tamalun
62:9	ya ayuha alathina amanuitha nudiya lisalati min yawmialjumuati fasaw ila thikri alahiwatharu albaya thalikum khayrun lakum in kuntumtalamun
62:10	fa itha qudiyati asalatufantashiru fi al ardi wabtaghu min fadlialahi wathkuru alaha kathiranlalakum tuflihun
62:11	wa itha raw tijaratan awlahwan infadu ilayha watarakuka qa imanqul ma ainda alahi khayrun mina alahwi wamina atijaratiwalahu khayru araziqin
63:1	itha jaka almunafiqunaqalu nashadu inaka larasulu alahi walahuyalamu inaka larasuluhu walahu yashadu inalmunafiqina lakathibun
63:2	itakhathu aymanahum junatanfasadu an sabili alahi inahum sa makanu yamalun
63:3	thalika bi anahum amanuthuma kafaru fatubia ala qulubihim fahum layafqahun
63:4	wa itha raytahum tuajibuka ajsamuhumwa in yaqulu tasma liqawlihim kanahum khushubunmusanadatun yahsabuna kula sayhatinalayhim humu aladuwu fahtharhum qatalahumualahu ana yu fakun
63:5	wa itha qila lahum talawyastaghfir lakum rasulu alahi lawaw rusahumwaraytahum yasuduna wahum mustakbirun
63:6	sawaun alayhim astaghfarta lahum amlam tastaghfir lahum lan yaghfira alahu lahum ina alahala yahdi alqawma alfasiqin
63:7	humu alathina yaquluna latunfiqu ala man ainda rasuli alahi hatayanfadu walilahi khaza inu asamawatiwal ardi walakina almunafiqina layafqahun
63:8	yaquluna la in rajana ilalmadinati layukhrijana al azu minha al athalawalilahi alaizatu walirasulihi walilmu minina walakinalmunafiqina la yalamun
63:9	ya ayuha alathina amanula tulhikum amwalukum wala awladukuman thikri alahi waman yafal thalikafaula ika humu alkhasirun
63:10	wanfiqu min ma razaqnakumin qabli an ya tiya ahadakumu almawtu fayaqula rabilawla akhkhartani ila ajalin qaribin fasadaqawakun mina asalihin
63:11	walan yu akhkhira alahu nafsan ithaja ajaluha walahu khabirun bimatamalun
64:1	yusabihu lilahi ma fiasamawati wama fi al ardilahu almulku walahu alhamdu wahuwa ala kulishay in qadir
64:2	huwa alathi khalaqakum faminkum kafirunwaminkum mu minun walahu bima tamalunabasir
64:3	khalaqa asamawati wal ardabilhaqi wasawarakum fahsana suwarakumwa ilayhi almasir
64:4	yalamu ma fi asamawatiwal ardi wayalamu ma tusiruna wamatualinuna walahu alimun bithati asudur
64:5	alam ya tikum nabau alathina kafarumin qablu fathaqu wabala amrihim walahum athabunalim
64:6	thalika bi anahu kanata tihim rusuluhum bilbayinati faqaluabasharun yahdunana fakafaru watawalaw wastaghnalahu walahu ghaniyun hamid
64:7	zama alathina kafaru an lanyubathu qul bala warabi latubathuna thumalatunabauna bima amiltum wathalika alalahi yasir
64:8	faminu bilahiwarasulihi wanuri alathi anzalna walahubima tamaluna khabir
64:9	yawma yajmaukum liyawmi aljamai thalikayawmu ataghabuni waman yu min bilahiwayamal salihan yukafir anhu sayi atihiwayudkhilhu janatin tajri min tahtihal anharu khalidina fiha abadan thalikalfawzu alathim
64:10	walathina kafaru wakaththabubi ayatina ula ika as habuanari khalidina fiha wabi sa almasir
64:11	ma asaba min musibatinila bi ithni alahi waman yu min bilahiyahdi qalbahu walahu bikuli shay in alim
64:12	watiau alaha watiauarasula fa in tawalaytum fa inama alarasulina albalaghu almubin
64:13	alahu la ilaha ilahuwa wala alahi falyatawakali almu minun
64:14	ya ayuha alathina amanuina min azwajikum wawladikum aduwan lakum fahtharuhumwa in tafu watasfahu wataghfiru fa ina alahaghafurun rahim
64:15	inama amwalukum wawladukumfitnatun walahu aindahu ajrun athim
64:16	fataqu alaha maistatatum wasmau watiau wanfiqukhayran li anfusikum waman yuqa shuha nafsihi faula ikahumu almuflihun
64:17	in tuqridu alaha qardanhasanan yudaifhu lakum wayaghfir lakum walahushakurun halim
64:18	alimu alghaybi washshahadatialazizu alhakim
65:1	ya ayuha anabiyu ithatalaqtumu anisa fataliquhunaliaidatihina wahsu alaidata wataqu alaharabakum la tukhrijuhuna min buyutihina walayakhrujna ila an ya tina bifahishatinmubayinatin watilka hududu alahi wamanyatada hududa alahi faqad thalamanafsahu la tadri lala alaha yuhdithubada thalika amra
65:2	fa itha balaghna ajalahunafamsikuhuna bimarufin aw fariquhuna bimarufinwashidu thaway adlin minkum waqimu ashshahadatalilahi thalikum yuathu bihi man kanayu minu bilahi walyawmi al akhiriwaman yataqi alaha yajal lahu makhraja
65:3	wayarzuqhu min haythu la yahtasibuwaman yatawakal ala alahi fahuwa hasbuhuina alaha balighu amrihi qad jala alahulikuli shay in qadra
65:4	wala i ya isna mina almahidimin nisa ikum ini irtabtum faidatuhuna thalathatuashurin wala i lam yahidna waulatual ahmali ajaluhuna an yadana hamlahunawaman yataqi alaha yajal lahu min amrihi yusra
65:5	thalika amru alahi anzalahuilaykum waman yataqi alaha yukafir anhu sayi atihiwayuathim lahu ajra
65:6	askinuhuna min haythu sakantum minwujdikum wala tudaruhuna litudayiqualayhina wa in kuna ulati hamlin fanfiqualayhina hata yadana hamlahunafa in ardana lakum fatuhuna ujurahunawa tamiru baynakum bimarufin wa in tasartum fasaturdiaulahu ukhra
65:7	liyunfiq thu satin min satihiwaman qudira alayhi rizquhu falyunfiq mima atahualahu la yukalifu alahu nafsan ilama ataha sayajalu alahubada ausrin yusra
65:8	wakayin min qaryatin atat an amrirabiha warusulihi fahasabnaha hisabanshadidan waththabnaha athabanukra
65:9	fathaqat wabala amrihawakana aqibatu amriha khusra
65:10	ada alahu lahum athabanshadidan fataqu alaha ya uli al albabialathina amanu qad anzala alahu ilaykum thikra
65:11	rasulan yatlu alaykum ayatialahi mubayinatin liyukhrija alathina amanuwamilu asalihati mina aththulumatila anuri waman yu min bilahiwayamal salihan yudkhilhu janatin tajrimin tahtiha al anharu khalidina fihabadan qad ahsana alahu lahu rizqa
65:12	alahu alathi khalaqa sabasamawatin wamina al ardi mithlahunayatanazalu al amru baynahuna litalamu ana alahala kuli shay in qadirun wana alaha qad ahatabikuli shay in ailma
66:1	ya ayuha anabiyulima tuharimu ma ahala alahu lakatabtaghi mardata azwajika walahughafurun rahim
66:2	qad farada alahu lakum tahilataymanikum walahu mawlakum wahuwalalimu alhakim
66:3	wa ith asara anabiyu ilabadi azwajihi hadithan falamanabat bihi watharahu alahu alayhiarafa badahu warada an badinfalama nabaha bihi qalat man anbaka hathaqala nabaniya alalimu alkhabir
66:4	in tatuba ila alahifaqad saghat qulubukuma wa in tathaharalayhi fa ina alaha huwa mawlahu wajibrilu wasalihualmu minina walmala ikatu bada thalika thahir
66:5	asa rabuhu in talaqakunan yubdilahu azwajan khayran minkuna muslimatinmu minatin qanitatin ta ibatinabidatin sa ihatin thayibatinwabkara
66:6	ya ayuha alathina amanuqu anfusakum wahlikum naran waquduha anasuwalhijaratu alayha mala ikatunghilathun shidadun la yasunalaha ma amarahum wayafaluna mayu marun
66:7	ya ayuha alathinakafaru la tatathiru alyawma inamatujzawna ma kuntum tamalun
66:8	ya ayuha alathina amanutubu ila alahi tawbatan nasuhanasa rabukum an yukafira ankum sayi atikumwayudkhilakum janatin tajri min tahtihal anharu yawma la yukhzi alahu anabiyawalathina amanu mahu nuruhum yasabayna aydihim wabi aymanihim yaquluna rabanatmim lana nurana waghfir lanainaka ala kuli shay in qadir
66:9	ya ayuha anabiyu jahidialkufara walmunafiqina waghluthalayhim wama wahum jahanamu wabi sa almasir
66:10	daraba alahu mathalan lilathinakafaru imrata nuhin wamrata lutin kanatatahta abdayni min aibadina salihaynifakhanatahuma falam yughniya anhumamina alahi shay an waqila udkhula anarama adakhilin
66:11	wadaraba alahu mathalan lilathinamanu imrata firawna ith qalat rabibni li aindaka baytan fi aljanati wanajini min firawnawamalihi wanajini mina alqawmi aththalimin

67:1	tabaraka alathi biyadihialmulku wahuwa ala kuli shay in qadir
